# PREREG_AUTOPILOT (written BEFORE each cycle's first run)

## CYCLE 1 (registered 2026-10-15T21:58Z, before any cycle-1 run)
Common: baseline = live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) (1.46/2.83/2.30), robustness line also vs B.Cfg(). Spans clean 2023-08..2025-07 / H1 / H2; UNTOUCHED never read. Selection on clean only; H1/H2 read once. Costs 1 tick/side (ES/ZN/FX 0.5 where stated) + commission. FOUND gates 1-7 as in AUTOPILOT.md (amended gate 4: own timing-destroyed null p95 on standalone Sharpe + bootstrap CI of additive uplift > 0; shift null reported, gate only for hedge claims). Additive at 10/20/30/50% and constant-risk (16 contracts-eq).
- C1-F1 FIVE-MIN FAMILY CROSS-INSTRUMENT. Mechanism: NQ no-filter 5-min trend books are negatively correlated with the pair in bad weeks; if it is a market-structure effect it should appear on ES/RTY/GC/ZN/CL/6E/6J with the same rules. Prior 15%. Grid: the V5 P1 frozen rules (SPECS_5MIN.md) x instruments {ES,RTY,GC,ZN,CL,6E,6J} x timeframes {3,5,10} min, parameters frozen from NQ (no re-fit); also clean-only re-selection of top-5 per instrument. Null: random-entry same frequency/hold.
- C1-F2 ALLOCATION RULE pair vs 5-min NQ ensemble (V5 P1 5-book ensemble). Mechanism: shift risk toward the book with better trailing risk-adjusted contribution / lower trailing correlation. Prior 8%. Grid: trailing windows {13,26,52} wk x rules {inverse-vol, trailing-corr tilt, trailing-Sharpe tilt} x tilt cap {±25,±50}% around a 80/20 base; walk-forward, fit on clean. Null: random tilts same turnover.
- C1-F3 TERM-STRUCTURE CARRY AND CROSS-SECTIONAL MOMENTUM, daily, 26 markets. Mechanism: roll yield (v.0 vs v.1 log price spread, annualised) predicts returns (risk premium); XS 12-1 momentum. Prior 25% standalone edge, 10% to clear gates as an additive sleeve. Grid: carry sign/long-short quintile, rebalance {weekly, monthly}, vol target 1%/market, XS momentum lookback {6,12} mo, combos {carry, mom, both}; 1 tick/side + roll costs. Null: random-sign portfolios same turnover. Report 2013-2026 Sharpe.
- C1-F4 INDEX RELATIVE-VALUE SPREADS (NQ vs ES vs RTY vs YM, beta/dollar-neutral). Mechanism: spread mean reversion / trend, market-neutral, so near-zero correlation by construction. Prior 8%. Grid: pairs {NQ-ES, NQ-RTY, ES-RTY, ES-YM}, ratio z-score lookback {1d,3d,10d at 5-min; 20d,60d daily}, entry z {1.5,2,2.5}, exit z {0,0.5}, stop {3,4}z; both MR and trend variants. Null: random-entry.
- C1-F5 CROSS-ASSET CONDITIONING OF THE PAIR'S ENTRIES. Mechanism: ZN/6J/BTC moves over the prior {5,15,60} min as risk-on/off context; skip or half-size pair entries against the context. Prior 6%. Evaluated as entry filter on the corrected rows (keep SURVIVES key + matched random-removal null); clean-only fit of thresholds {tercile cuts}.
- C1-F6 VOL-REGIME SIZE SWITCH (VX term-structure from Track L series + NQ realised vol percentile). Mechanism: pair edge differs by vol regime; D3 S1/S6 note sizing sensitivity. Prior 5% (E1 vol targeting already NO). Grid: regime = VX1/VX2 backwardation flag, VX percentile {70,80,90}, NQ 20d-vol percentile; size x{0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5} in the flagged regime. Clean-only fit. Null: shuffled regime labels.
- C1-F7 MBO-RECIPE ML ON ES/RTY BAR FEATURES. Mechanism: as T3 S5 (NQ) which was NO; cross-instrument replication. Prior 3%. Grid: T3 S5 feature list and 18-cell grid frozen; walk-forward 6/1; >=20 surrogate runs. 
- Ruled out without running (logged): conditional day lock from observables (D3 S2: nothing separates costly lock days, family-wise p 0.09); GC trend hedge (V5 P4: NO).

## CYCLE 2 (registered 2026-10-15T22:03Z, before any cycle-2 run; same common block as cycle 1)
- C2-G1 EXIT MANAGEMENT OF THE PAIR (D3 S6: -10% avg loss = +93% net, +10% avg win = +106%). Mechanism: the fixed 60/60 bracket leaves path information unused; breakeven/trailing stops and time stops may raise win rate/avg win or cut avg loss. Prior 10%. Grid (applied to every pair trade on the 1-second path, clean-only choice): breakeven stop after +{20,30,40} pts (offset 0/+2), trail {20,30} pts after +{30,40}, scale-out half at {30,40} with the rest to 60 (E6 tested static tranches, not path-dependent), time stop {60,120,240} min, partial combos; 8 contracts per book split as needed. Null: same rules applied to a time-shifted copy of the paths (path-destroyed) and matched random exits. Baseline live. FOUND gates adapted for an exit change on the pair itself (weekly Sharpe vs live up >=0.10 on each span, worst week not worse, own null p95, plateau, 2x slippage).
- C2-G2 META-LABELING OF THE PAIR'S ENTRIES. Mechanism: a classifier on features known at the flip predicts trade outcome better than the single variables in Track 1c/E2; skip or half-size low-probability entries. Prior 8%. Features (frozen): nt8sim FEAT (st_dist, hm_dist, ages, adr20, st_tr, hm_tr, ema), time of day, prior-day pair P&L, trailing 1-min/5-min vol, range position, VWAP distance, last-N-trade outcomes. Model: HistGradientBoosting/LightGBM, walk-forward expanding window, retrain monthly, first train on 2023-02..2023-07 + clean-fold; threshold (skip bottom {10,20,30}% / half-size) chosen on clean only; H1/H2 read untouched. Null: shuffled labels within month (>=100) and matched random removal.
- C2-G3 EVENT-DAY BREAKOUT SLEEVE (NQ). Mechanism: scheduled-release days (FOMC, NFP, CPI) have directional first-hour moves at times the pair is blocked/weak (09:00-09:58 entries disallowed in the pair); low correlation by construction. Prior 6%. Grid: event {FOMC decision (14:00 ET), NFP/CPI (08:30 ET)} x entry {break of first {1,5,15}-min range after release} x stop opposite side x target {1,1.5,2}R x exit {60 min, 15:55 CT}. Event dates: FOMC from the published schedule (verify; flag uncertain), NFP = first Friday rule with holiday exceptions, CPI dates approximate -> flag as unverified and run FOMC+NFP as the primary cells. Null: random non-event days same time-of-day. Small-sample flag expected (n events ~ 29 FOMC, ~40 NFP in the pair window).
- C2-G4 CALENDAR / SEASONAL SLEEVES ACROSS THE DAILY UNIVERSE (ex indices, done in T4 B). Mechanism: documented seasonal/roll-cycle effects (turn-of-month in bonds, month-of-year in energy/grains/metals, day-of-week) with long evidence 2013-2026. Prior 5%. Grid: instruments {ZN,ZB,ZF,6E,6J,GC,SI,CL,NG,ZC,ZS,ZW} x rule {turn-of-month window, month-of-year pre-registered a priori list (NG winter long Oct-Dec, grains Jun-Jul short..., stated before the run), Monday/Friday patterns}; rule list frozen BEFORE looking at data; selection on 2013-2022 + clean, H1/H2 read once. Null: random-date same frequency/holding time. Report long-history Sharpe; small-sample flags.

## C2-G4 FROZEN RULES (written 2026-10-15T22:10Z BEFORE any return data was loaded for the test; only a loader smoke test on prices follows)
Windows are defined on TRADING-DAY positions of the instrument's own UTC-day bar series (Databento daily bars). A window [s,e] means: enter at the OPEN of trading day s, exit at the CLOSE of trading day e (position earns open->close on s..e and the close->open gaps strictly inside). Long = +1, short = -1. Sizing: ATR20 vol target (t4_a_core.sizes: risk $ per ATR via 1% of $700k), fractional contracts; cost = 1 tick/side + per-product commission (t4_a_core.SPEC) + one extra round trip if a roll falls inside the window. The sleeve series is then scaled to the pair's CLEAN weekly volatility (as in T3 S6/T4 A; scale factor and implied contracts/margin reported).
Rules (14 instrument-rule sleeves; rationale in brackets; NO other rules will be examined; there is NO tuning of any date):
 R1 BOND MONTH-END: long ZN, ZB, ZF, window = 3rd-to-last trading day of each month .. last trading day of month. [index-extension / duration-buying demand at month end]
 R2 WINTER ENERGY BUILD: long NG, HO, window = first trading day of October .. last trading day of December. [heating-demand risk premium]
 R3 DRIVING SEASON: long RB, CL, window = first trading day of February .. last trading day of April. [pre-driving-season refinery/gasoline buying]
 R4 GRAIN WEATHER/HARVEST: ZC, ZS, ZW: LONG leg = first trading day of March .. 15th calendar day of June (last trading day <= Jun 15); SHORT leg = first trading day of July .. last trading day of September. [new-crop weather premium into June; harvest pressure after]. Legs reported separately and combined.
 R5 GOLD/SILVER AUTUMN DEMAND: long GC, SI, window = first trading day of September .. last trading day of November. [Indian festival/wedding and Q4 jewelry demand]
 R6 LEAN HOGS: long HE, window = first trading day of February .. last trading day of June. [seasonal low in winter to summer peak]
 R7 GOLD MONDAY (weekend effect): short GC on Mondays, enter Monday open, exit Monday close (window = each Monday, 1 day). [documented weekend effect; expected weak/decayed -- included as the day-of-week control the directive asked for]
Multiplicity: 14 sleeves are examined in total, all reported. Each sleeve is judged against ITS OWN random-date null (>=500 draws: same number of windows per calendar year, same holding length in trading days, start dates uniformly random within each year), at p95, on standalone weekly Sharpe in (a) 2013-2022 (the a-priori untouched holdout for these a-priori rules), (b) clean, (c) H1, (d) H2, (e) pair window all-in. A sleeve counts only if (a) AND (e) beat p95 and the additive/constant-risk gates hold (T3 gates incl. worst-decile corr <= +0.30, bootstrap CI > 0), then plateau (start and end shifted by +-1 and +-2 trading days: >=75% of the 24 neighbours keep >=75% of the all-in Sharpe) and 2x slippage. Small-sample flag (<100 trades in the pair window) applies to every sleeve; the 2013-2026 Sharpe is the primary evidence. With 14 sleeves the expected number beating a p95 null by chance is 0.7; any sleeve count must be read against that (a Bonferroni-style note is included in the report).

## CYCLE 3 (registered 2026-10-15T22:12Z, before any cycle-3 run)
Data: purchased NQ/ES/RTY 1-minute bars 2013-2026 (data/autopilot/ohlcv1m) give 10 years of UNTOUCHED pre-2023 history (2013-01..2023-07); the live pair and the 5-min family were developed/selected on 2023-02..2026 only, so 2013-2022 is a true out-of-sample holdout for them.
- C3-H1 INTRADAY MOMENTUM (Gao-Han-Li-Zhou style) on NQ/ES/RTY. Mechanism: first-30-minute return (and prior-day close to 09:30 gap) predicts the last-30-minute return via dealer/hedger rebalancing; structural, documented, long evidence. Prior 10%. Rule frozen a priori: sign of r1 = ret(prev-close(16:00 ET)->10:00 ET) [variant: 09:30->10:00], enter at 15:30 ET in that direction, exit 15:55 CT (16:55 ET); variants adding sign of r12 = ret(15:00->15:30) agreement; threshold none or |r1|>0.5*ADR-pct. 3 instruments x 2 r1 definitions x {with/without r12 agreement} = 12 cells, no parameter fitting. Choice and sanity on 2013-2022; pair window (clean/H1/H2) read once; null: random days same hold (>=500). 1 tick/side + commission (ES 0.5).
- C3-H2 PAIR LONG-HISTORY HOLDOUT (diagnostic, not a candidate). Run the live ST/HM (frozen parameters, EMA200, ET clock, 60/60 bracket, session windows) on NQ 1-minute bars 2013-01..2023-07 with the 1-minute engine (state fidelity vs the 1-second engine on the overlap 2023-02..2023-12 and apply the same live rules via b11_nt8/d1_core style); report weekly Sharpe/MAR/DD by year, regime dependence, and whether the pair has an edge pre-2023. No tuning.
- C3-H3 5-MIN NQ FAMILY ON THE LONG HISTORY. V5 P1 frozen specs (SPECS_5MIN.md), 5-book ensemble (the clean-chosen representatives) on 2013-2022 1-minute bars (5-min bars built from 1-min), costs 1 tick/side + commission; correlation with the H2 pair on the same history (all weeks and the pair's worst-decile weeks), additive uplift at 10-50% share per year, own null (>=200), bootstrap, plateau, 2x slippage. Pre-stated reading: the family is a FOUND-quality candidate only if the additive Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 over 2013-2022 as a whole AND in at least 6 of 10 calendar years AND worst-decile correlation <= +0.30.

## CYCLE 4 (registered 2026-10-15T22:20Z, before any cycle-4 run)
- C4-K1 LET-WINNERS-RUN (conditional target extension). Mechanism: C2-G1 showed the pair's edge is in the runners (trades touching +40 finish at +60 more often than at -60); D3 lever: +10% avg win = +106% net. Extend the target only after the +40 touch, with stop kept (or moved to {entry, entry+20}) so the added risk is only the open profit. Prior 8% (E5/E6 static longer targets failed, but those extended every trade). Grid on the 1-second paths using the exact re-simulator of C2-G1 (code/census/night_ins/ap_c2_g1_core.py): trigger touch +{35,40,45}; new target {70,80,100}; stop after trigger {original -60, entry, entry+20}; applied to the same 59% of rows C2-G1 could manage (netting-closed rows keep live exits; state the limitation). Clean-only choice; H1/H2 read once. Null: apply the same extension logic to path-destroyed (time-shifted) paths and to random trigger rows (>=100). Gates adapted for a change to the pair itself: weekly Sharpe vs live up >=0.10 on each span, worst week not worse, own null p95, plateau (±25%), 2x slippage. Baseline Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None); also vs B.Cfg().

## CYCLE 5 (registered 2026-10-15T22:35Z, before any cycle-5 run). Context: C3-H2 showed the live pair and the 5-min family have no edge on NQ 2013-2022; edges are period-specific. New principle for cycle 5: DEVELOP on the long untouched history 2013-2022 and READ clean/H1/H2 untouched afterwards (selection span strictly precedes clean, which is stricter than 'clean only'). A FOUND candidate must therefore work across 2013-2022 years AND in the pair window.
- C5-M1 LONG-HISTORY-SELECTED INTRADAY FAMILIES on NQ and ES (1-min bars 2013-2026, 5-min/15-min bars built from them). Mechanism: none a priori beyond trend/mean-reversion families that persist across regimes; selection criterion enforces regime robustness. Prior 8%. Grid ~1,500 cells: rule {EMA cross, MACD zero-cross, Supertrend, Donchian intraday, linreg-slope sign, RSI(2-14) MR, VWAP-distance MR, opening-range} x timeframe {5,15} x exit {opposite, bracket 40/60/90, time stop 60/120/240} x session {all, RTH, Globex} x filter {EMA200 off/on}. Selection on 2013-2022 ONLY by: positive weekly Sharpe in >= 7 of 10 calendar years AND overall Sharpe rank; keep top 10. Then read clean, H1, H2 once, and the additive uplift vs the pair (B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None)). Null: the same selection run on timing-destroyed books (>=50 replicates), top-10 stats vs replicate distribution (false-survivor test); own random-entry null p95; bootstrap; plateau; 2x slippage.
- C5-M2 RTY ML CELL OOS: the C1-F7 RTY H240/8-leaves/tau 0.55 cell (and its 16-leaf sibling) re-run walk-forward on RTY 2017-2022 (and 2013-2022 if files complete) with the frozen recipe; the only question is whether Sharpe > 0 and beats its surrogate null out of sample; if yes, re-score as sleeve on the pair window. Prior 5%.

### C5-M1 grid (appended 2026-10-15T22:50Z by the C5-M1 worker, BEFORE the first full simulation; only 8 smoke-test cells had been run to check the code, results not inspected for selection)
Instruments NQ and ES, 1-minute bars 2013-01-02..2026-10-13 (purchased Databento continuous .v.0; difference back-adjustment at the 55 instrument_id changes: gap = first open of new contract - last close of old, earlier bars shifted; intraday trades never span a roll because every trade is flat by 16:55 ET). Bars: 5 and 15 min built from 1-min on the ET clock, trading-day label = ET+6h (18:00 start). Cells per instrument = 1,464 (2,928 total):
- Rules (21): EMA cross (9,21),(13,40),(20,60); MACD zero-cross (8,17),(12,26),(19,39); Supertrend ATR/mult (10,2),(14,3),(21,4) on close; Donchian intraday 20/50/100 bars; linreg-slope sign 20/50/80 bars; RSI MR (n=2, 10/90),(5, 20/80),(14, 30/70) fade; VWAP-distance MR |z|>2 and >3 (z = (close - session VWAP)/ATR14 of the bar series, fade on cross); opening-range breakout (first 30 min of RTH, first close beyond the range after 10:00 ET, one trade/day).
- Timeframe {5,15}; exit {opposite signal (MR/ORB: opposite event or end of day), bracket stop=target 60 and 90 NQ points (ES points x 0.3), time stop 60/120/240 min}; entry session {all, RTH, Globex; ORB RTH only}; EMA200 filter {off,on}. All trades flat at the 16:55 ET bar open. Entry at next bar open. Costs: NQ 1 tick/side, ES 0.5 tick/side, + $4.36/contract RT, 8 contracts, stop-first fills; targets fill at the target price without slippage (stops with slippage), as in V5 P2.
- Eligibility: >= 300 trades in 2013-2022. SELECTION (2013-2022 only): positive weekly Sharpe in >= 7 of 10 calendar years (2013..2022), then rank by overall 2013-2022 weekly Sharpe; keep top 10 PER INSTRUMENT (20 total). Then read clean 2023-08..2025-07, H1, H2 ONCE and report ALL 20. Sleeve scaled to the pair's clean weekly volatility using the sleeve's 2013-2022 weekly volatility (no use of H1/H2).
- False-survivor test: repeat the whole selection (same eligibility, same year rule, same ranking) on timing-destroyed replicates (same event counts per cell, random bar ends, alternating sides) — as many replicates as compute allows, >= 50 per instrument on a fixed random 1/3 subgrid if the full grid is too slow (then the real selection is also repeated on that subgrid for comparability). Statistic: number of cells passing the 7-of-10 rule, top-10 mean 2013-2022 Sharpe, top-10 mean clean/H1/H2 additive uplift at 20% share.
- Gates for FOUND: AUTOPILOT gates 1-7 with the amended gate 4 (own random-entry null p95 on standalone Sharpe; bootstrap CI of additive uplift > 0), plateau (+-25% on parameters, tf 5<->15 not a plateau variable), 2x slippage, worst-decile correlation <= +0.30.

## CYCLE 6 (registered 2026-10-15T22:55Z, before any cycle-6 run)
- C6-N1 EX-ANTE REGIME SWITCH FOR THE PAIR. Context: the pair earns 2024-2026 only (C3-H2). If a state variable known BEFORE each week separates pair-profitable from pair-unprofitable weeks over 2013-2026, a size switch (0/50/100%) is a candidate. Prior 10%. State variables (frozen list, all computed from data up to the Friday close before the week): {20/60-day variance ratio (VR) of NQ daily returns, 20-day realised vol and its 60-day percentile, 20-day mean ADR percentile, trailing 4/13-week pair Sharpe (included as a benchmark), 20-day mean |overnight gap|, 20-day volume percentile}. Rule: size 100% when state in the favourable tercile/half, else 0% or 50%. FIT on 2013-2022 ONLY (pair series from the 1-min engine; accept its 90% entry fidelity, state it); READ clean, H1, H2 untouched on the live corrected series. Null: shuffled state labels by year-block (>=500) and random same-exposure switches; bootstrap; plateau (cut points ±1 decile); 2x slippage via live rows. FOUND gates adapted (pair itself): weekly Sharpe vs live up >=0.10 on clean, H1 and H2 separately with exposure change reported, worst week not worse, own null p95, plateau, 2x slippage.
- C6-N2 repeat of C5-M2 (RTY ML cell OOS on 2013-2022 bars, now complete) and C3-H1 RTY cells (rerun ap_c3_h1_run.py now that 14 RTY files exist); executed by the same agent.

(C6-N2 amendment: RTY holdout is 2017-2022 because RTY.v.0 is unavailable before 2017.)

- C7-P1 LONG-HISTORY-SELECTED INTRADAY FAMILIES ON NON-EQUITY FUTURES {ZN, ZB, 6E, 6J, GC, CL}. Mechanism: sleeves on rates/FX/commodity intraday are uncorrelated with the NQ pair by construction; the C5-M1 protocol (develop on 2013-2022 with >= 7 of 10 positive years, read clean/H1/H2 untouched) removes the regime-luck problem found in C3-H2. Grid: C5-M1 rule families x {5,15}-min x exits {opposite, bracket 40/60/90 scaled to each instrument's 20-day range so R is equal, time stops 60/120/240} x sessions {all, 08:00-16:55 ET}; EMA200 filter off/on; ~1,000 cells per instrument (exact grid appended to this file before the first full run). ### C7-P1 exact grid (appended 2026-10-15T23:08Z by the C7-P1 worker, BEFORE the first full run)
Instruments: ZN, ZB, 6E, 6J, GC, CL. 1-min bars data/autopilot/ohlcv1m 2013-2026, per-instrument_id difference back-adjustment, ET DST clock, label day = ET+6h (18:00 ET start), flatten at the open of the 16:55 ET bar, entries 18:00-16:40 ET ('all') or 08:00-16:40 ET ('day'). CL: events on 2020-04-17..04-23 invalidated (negative-price episode), flagged.
Cells per instrument = 2 timeframes {5,15} x 20 rules x 7 exits x 2 sessions x 2 filters = 1,120.
Rules (20): EMA cross (9,21),(13,40),(20,60); MACD zero-cross (8,17),(12,26),(19,39); Supertrend (ATR,mult) (10,2),(14,3),(21,4); Donchian 20/50/100 bars; linreg slope sign 20/50/80; RSI (period,level) (2,10),(5,20),(14,30) mean-reversion; VWAP-distance z (ATR-normalised) 2 and 3 mean-reversion. (Opening-range rule of the equity grid dropped: equity-specific.)
Exits (7): opposite signal; bracket stop=target = k x B with k in {2/3, 1, 3/2} (i.e. 40/60/90 relative to a 60 base) and B = 0.193 x prior-20-label-day mean (high-low) in the instrument's price units (T3 S2 convention so R is equal across instruments); time stop 60/120/240 min; all force-flat at the 16:55 ET bar open.
Filter: EMA200 (on the signal-timeframe close) off/on.
Sessions (2): 'all' (entries 18:00-16:40 ET), 'day' (08:00-16:40 ET).
Costs: slippage ticks/side ZN 0.5, ZB 0.5, 6E 0.5, 6J 0.5, GC 1, CL 1; commission $4.36/contract/round trip (assumption, as T3 S2). Contract constants (tick, $/point): ZN 1/64, 1000; ZB 1/32, 1000; 6E 0.00005, 125000; 6J 0.0000005, 12500000 ; GC 0.10, 100; CL 0.01, 1000.
Sleeve scaling: daily P&L (1 contract-unit) scaled so its 2013-2022 weekly stdev equals the pair's clean weekly stdev x share (C5-M1 convention); pair-window uplift at 20% is the headline, 10/30/50% reported.
Selection (2013-2022 ONLY): >= 300 trades in 2013-2022, >= 7 of 10 calendar years with positive weekly Sharpe, rank by 2013-2022 weekly Sharpe, top 10 per instrument (60 total). False-survivor test: 50 timing-destroyed replicate grids per instrument on a fixed random third of the cells (same count of events, random bar ends, alternating sides), full selection pipeline, top-10 mean Sharpe vs real. Pair-window read (clean/H1/H2) once on all 60: additive uplift vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) (clean process), worst-decile correlation, own random-entry null (100 draws) p95 on pair-window standalone Sharpe, bootstrap 90% CI, plateau (±25% parameters, +-25% bracket), 2x slippage. FOUND gate: as AUTOPILOT.md (uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week within the share; own null p95; bootstrap lower bound > 0; plateau >= 75%; 2x slippage; worst-decile corr <= +0.30).

## CYCLE 8 (registered 2026-10-15T23:20Z, before any cycle-8 run)
- C8-Q1 ERA DIAGNOSTIC (not a candidate): why does the pair earn only in 2024-2026? Year-by-year (2013-2026) NQ intraday statistics from 1-min bars: variance ratios at 5/15/30/60-min horizons, autocorrelation of 1/5/15-min returns, VWAP-distance persistence, share of trend vs chop days (define a priori: |close-open|/range > 0.5 trend), average flip-to-flip run length of the ST/HM signals, realised vol and ADR, volume. Relate to the pair's yearly Sharpe (C3-H2 + live). Output: which statistic tracks the pair's edge, whether the 2023-26 level is within the historical range or outside it (percentile of 2023-26 against 2013-22). Purpose: tell the user what the edge depends on and whether it looks like a new regime (e.g. 0DTE-era) vs a draw from the old distribution. No gate.
- C8-Q2 TIME-OF-DAY DRIFT SLEEVES on NQ/ES: fixed 30-minute buckets (ET) long/short by sign of the 2013-2022 mean bucket return, plus buckets conditioned on prior-bucket sign; selection on 2013-2022 with >=7 of 10 years consistent sign; read pair window untouched; random-bucket null; prior 5%.

### C8-Q2 exact cell list (appended 2026-10-15 before the first full run; fork of cycle 8)
- Session: CME trading day, 18:00 ET (prev. calendar day) to 17:00 ET; trading-day label = ET date of (time + 6h). 45 full 30-minute buckets b=0..44 starting 18:00, 18:30, ..., 16:00 ET (last ends 16:30). Entry at the open of the first 1-minute bar at/after the bucket start (within 3 min), exit at the same rule at the next bucket start; both prices must have the same instrument_id (roll-safe). One contract; costs per round trip NQ 2x1 tick x 0.25 + $4.36 commission; ES 2x0.5 tick x 0.25 + $4.36 (PV NQ 20, ES 50). Gross and net both reported.
- Variant U (unconditional): cell (instrument, b), direction = sign of the mean bucket return over 2013-2022 (fixed once, on 2013-2022 only): 45 cells per instrument.
- Variant C (conditioned on the prior bucket): cell (instrument, b), b=1..44, direction = sign(prior-bucket return) x s, s = sign of the 2013-2022 mean of r_b x sign(prior r) (+1 momentum, -1 reversal): 44 cells per instrument. Total 178 cells.
- Selection (2013-2022 ONLY): cell qualifies if >= 7 of the 10 calendar years have gross mean P&L with the chosen direction's sign; keep the top 10 per instrument by 2013-2022 NET weekly Sharpe (fewer if fewer qualify). Pair window clean/H1/H2 read once for the selected cells only.
- Nulls: (a) random-bucket null: random bucket (and random direction) same window, >= 500 draws, p95 of net weekly Sharpe; (b) false-survivor test: >= 200 timing-destroyed replicates (per-day random circular rotation of the bucket sequence) re-running the whole selection; statistic = mean 2013-2022 net Sharpe of the replicate's top 10 and number of qualifying cells.
- Plateau: adjacent buckets b-1, b+1 (own fitted direction) keep >= 75% of the cell's all-in additive uplift; 2x slippage; bootstrap/shift null/worst-decile correlation via t3_score.score_one/corr_block; baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), robustness vs B.Cfg().

## CYCLE 9 (registered 2026-10-15T23:20Z, before any cycle-9 run)
- C9-R1 CALENDAR AVOID-FILTERS ON THE PAIR. Mechanism: the pair's stop-outs concentrate where microstructure is abnormal — scheduled-release spikes, quarterly roll weeks (volume migration, spread widening), thin holiday sessions, month-end/index-rebalance days. Avoid-filters are conceptually sign-stable (they remove abnormal-liquidity trades) so they can be developed on the pair's 2013-2022 1-minute-engine trades (a losing sample, but filter direction should still help) and read untouched on 2023-2026. Prior 8%. Filters (frozen a priori, each independent, binary skip of NEW entries): E1 release windows [-5,+30] and [-5,+60] min around FOMC 14:00 / NFP and CPI 08:30 (dates: use outputs/autopilot/c2/g3 verified lists for 2023-2026; 2013-2022 FOMC from the published calendar, NFP rule; CPI pre-2023 unavailable -> E1 on FOMC+NFP only for the long history); E2 quarterly roll week (Thursday..Wednesday around the 2nd Friday-before-expiry: define as 5 trading days before the third-Friday expiry of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec); E3 holiday sessions (CME early-close days and the day after Thanksgiving, Dec 24-26, Dec 31, Jan 2); E4 last trading day of month; E5 Friday after 14:00 ET. Development: effect on the pair's 2013-2022 trades (net points of removed trades, sign, and per-year consistency >= 6 of 10 years for removed-trade mean < overall mean); then clean/H1/H2 via b11_nt8 skipmask on the corrected rows with the live rules vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process. Null: matched random removal at the same removal rate (>=500) at p95; bootstrap; plateau (window widths); 2x slippage. FOUND gates adapted for a change to the pair (weekly Sharpe up >=0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately).

## CYCLE 10 (registered 2026-10-16T00:10Z, before any cycle-10 run)
- C10-S1 HIGHER-TIMEFRAME INTRADAY/MULTI-HOUR TREND AND MR on NQ, ES, ZN, 6E, 6J, GC, CL. Mechanism: C1-F1/C7-P1/C8-Q2 died to cost (slippage + commission as % of the bracket); at 30/60/240-minute bars the same costs are a much smaller share of the move and multi-hour time-series momentum is the horizon where documented trend persistence sits between the 5-min noise and the daily trend (which showed no edge in the pair window). Prior 8%. Grid ~600 cells per instrument: rules {EMA cross, MACD zero-cross, Supertrend, Donchian, linreg-slope, RSI MR, VWAP-z MR} x bars {30,60,240}-min x exits {opposite signal, ATR trail 2-3x, bracket 1.5-3 ATR(14) of the bar, time stop 4-24 bars} x sessions {all, day session} x EMA(200 bars) filter off/on; overnight holds allowed (positions carry across the 17:00-18:00 break only for 240-min bars; 30/60-min flatten at 16:55 ET). Same protocol as C5-M1/C7-P1: select top 10 per instrument on 2013-2022 only (>=7 of 10 calendar years positive weekly Sharpe), false-survivor test with >=50 timing-destroyed replicates, then read clean/H1/H2 untouched once; additive uplift vs the pair (B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) clean process), worst-decile correlation, own null p95, bootstrap, plateau, 2x slippage. RTY excluded (history from 2017 only).

### C10-S1 exact grid (appended 2026-10-16 before the first full run)
600 cells per instrument = 3 bar sizes {30,60,240} min x 10 rules x 5 exits x 2 sessions {all (18:00-16:40 ET entries), day (08:00-16:40)} x EMA(200 bars of that size) filter {off,on}. Rules: EMA(9,21) cross, EMA(20,60) cross, MACD(12,26) zero-cross, Supertrend(10,2), Supertrend(14,3), Donchian(20), Donchian(50), linreg-slope(20) sign, RSI(14) MR at 30/70, VWAP-z MR (z=3 vs ATR14 of the bar). Exits: opposite signal; ATR(14) trail 2.5x; stop=target 1.5 x ATR(14) of the bar; stop=target 3 x ATR(14) of the bar; time stop 8 bars. 30/60-min positions flatten at the 16:55 ET bar open; 240-min positions may carry across the 17:00-18:00 ET break into the next trading day (not across weekends) and flatten at that day's 16:55 bar open; gaps are realised at the next open. Instruments NQ, ES, ZN, 6E, 6J, GC, CL (RTY excluded). Costs: NQ 1 tick/side, ES/ZN/6E/6J 0.5, GC/CL 1, + $4.36 per contract round trip (assumption), 1 contract then volatility-matched to the pair's clean weekly sigma (scale applies to commission as well). Selection: positive weekly Sharpe in >=7 of 10 calendar years 2013-2022 AND >=300 trades in 2013-2022; top 10 per instrument by 2013-2022 weekly Sharpe. False-survivor test: >=50 timing-destroyed replicates per instrument over the FULL grid (random bar-end entries, same count, alternating sides). Then clean/H1/H2 read once.

## B9 (registered 2026-10-16, before any B9 run)
- B9 CROSS-INSTRUMENT TREND CONFIRMATION AS SIZING on the live pair. Mechanism: when NQ's own 5-min trend is echoed by ES and RTY (broad index move, not NQ-specific noise) the ST/HM trend entries have follow-through; when ES and RTY disagree (rotation/chop) trend entries are worse. Used as a size multiplier on NEW entries, never a filter (no entry is skipped). Prior 6%.
- Data: databento 1-min series_{NQ,ES,RTY}.npz (all start 2023-02-01, so there is no history before the pair window; the definitions are fixed a priori and nothing is fitted, selection among cells is on clean 2023-08..2025-07 only). 5-min bars = last 1-min close at/before each UTC multiple-of-5 minute (asof for missing bars). Signal at an entry = last COMPLETED 5-min bar at or before the entry bar open (no lookahead).
- Trend direction definitions per instrument (3): T1 sign(EMA9-EMA21 of 5-min closes); T2 sign(MACD(12,26) line of 5-min closes); T3 sign(close - close 12 bars earlier) (60-min return). Zero -> 0 (neutral, counts as disagreement).
- Schemes (2): A (headline, literal): ES and RTY disagree with each other (signs differ or either 0) -> low multiplier; NQ, ES, RTY all same non-zero sign -> high multiplier; else (ES=RTY but NQ differs) -> 1.0. B (side-aware): both ES and RTY sign == trade side -> high; both opposite to trade side -> low; mixed -> 1.0.
- Multipliers (2): (low,high) = (0.5,1.5) headline, (0.75,1.25).
- Grid = 3 x 2 x 2 = 12 cells. Headline cell = T1/A/(0.5,1.5). Applied via Cfg.sizefn on the corrected live rows (b11_nt8): engine natively scales P&L, per-book day/loss rules, day lock (cap 8R / give-back) and week floor 8R by the size factor, so no approximation. Baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a CLEAN process (no b10_core import; asserted); robustness vs B.Cfg(). Fill model: unchanged live rows, 1 tick/side + $34.88 commission, scaled with the factor; 2x slippage test.
- Selection: the one cell with the highest clean weekly-Sharpe uplift among the 12 is the selected cell (the headline is reported regardless). H1/H2 read once, for the selected cell and the headline, after selection. UNTOUCHED never read.
- Gates for a change to the pair (all required): weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week not worse (all spans); matched random-size null: the same multiplier multiset permuted at random across entries (per book), >= 500 draws, real clean/H1/H2 uplift above null p95; bootstrap 90% CI (5000 joint week resamples) of the uplift lower bound > 0; plateau: >= 75% of the +-25% parameter neighbours (EMA 7/16 and 11/26, MACD 9/20 and 15/33, return lookback 9 and 15 bars, multipliers (0.375,1.625)/(0.625,1.375) as +-25% of the deviation) keep >= 75% of the uplift; survives 2x slippage (uplift vs 2x-slippage baseline >= +0.10 on clean/H1/H2 is not required; positive on all three and clean >= +0.10 is). Also report mean multiplier (risk change), constant-risk framing (multipliers renormalised to mean 1 over the span), insurance metric set. Walk-forward: no parameters are fitted, so the entire window is out of sample for the definitions; rolling-26-week Sharpe share >= baseline is reported.

## B8 (registered 2026-10-16, before any B8 run) CONDITIONAL HEDGE: 5-MINUTE NQ ENSEMBLE SWITCHED ON ONLY AFTER A LOSING PAIR STRETCH
- Mechanism: the 5-minute NQ ensemble has negative weekly corr with the pair (C3-H3 -0.19 all weeks; V5 P1), but no standalone edge on 2013-2022 (Sharpe +0.07, uplift +0.001 at 20%). If the pair's bad stretches cluster (autocorrelated weekly P&L) the hedge is only needed after one, so carrying it only then keeps the hedge benefit and drops the cost drag in good periods. Prior 6% (C1-F2 found trailing-statistic tilts add nothing to the static blend; the pair's weekly P&L has weak autocorrelation).
- Difference from C1-F2: F2 tilted a ALWAYS-ON 20% blend by +-25/50% using trailing inverse-vol/corr/Sharpe statistics of both streams. B8 is a binary 0/s switch on one statistic only (sign of trailing pair P&L), so exposure to the sleeve is 0 for most good weeks; it tests the conditional-hedge claim itself, not an allocation tilt.
- Rule (walk-forward): for week t (Mon open), L_t = sum of the pair's weekly P&L over the N completed weeks t-N..t-1 (live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) on the live window; the 1-minute-engine pair on 2013-2022); on_t = 1 if L_t < 0 else 0; sleeve weight s on in week t, 0 otherwise. No data from week t is used. Weeks without N completed prior weeks: off.
- Grid (24 cells): N in {4,8,13} x share s in {10,20,30,50}% of pair clean weekly sigma (sleeve scaled as V5/C3-H3: clean weekly std = s x pair clean std) x framing {additive: pair + on*S ; constant-risk: (1-s*on)*pair + s*on*S}. Pre-stated primary: N=8 (as assigned); N=4 and 13 are variants. Selection of N on CLEAN only by (additive + constant-risk) clean Sharpe uplift at s=20%; H1 and H2 read once afterwards. Sleeve for the live window = the V5 P1 five cluster representatives rebuilt at 1 tick/side (identical to C1-F2); for 2013-2022 = C3-H3 primary five-book ensemble on 1-min bars (h3_stage1 E1) against the 1-min-engine pair (h2_pair_long), same scale constants.
- Comparators: pair alone; STATIC blend at the same share; static blend scaled to the same average exposure (share x on-fraction). The switch's incremental value = switch minus static-at-equal-exposure and minus static.
- Gates (FOUND, hedge claim): (1) additive Sharpe uplift vs pair >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately at 10/20/30/50% (headline 20%), worst week not worse than the share; (2) CIRCULAR-SHIFT NULL IS GATING: shifting the on/off vector (same duty cycle and persistence), real all-in additive uplift >= p95 of shifts; and worst-decile (pair's worst 10% weeks) corr of the switched contribution with the pair < 0; (3) bootstrap 90% CI (5,000 week resamples) of the all-in additive uplift > 0, and reported vs the static blend; sleeve standalone random-entry null from C3-H3/V5 own nulls is cited; (4) plateau: N x0.75/x1.25 (6, 10) and share +-25%: >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of the uplift on clean/H1/H2; 2x slippage (sleeve P&L rebuilt at 2 ticks/side, size fixed); (5) 2013-2022 long history reported: switch uplift, per year, on-fraction, shift null, vs static (no gate; the pre-2023 reading is binding for the 'edge' claim only by comparison with C3-H3's +0.001 at 20%). Insurance metric set (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg weeks) on clean/H1/H2/all, both framings, INSURANCE/SURVIVES via the rep_common rule.
- Null/trail variants not in the grid: none. WT version only if outputs/autopilot/REPORT_WT.md exists and shows negative correlation; otherwise skipped and stated. Fill model: 1 tick/side + commission (sleeve: market entry, 60/60 bracket, 5R/-2R rule; pair: corrected-engine live rows). Executability: needs a new 5-minute NQ multi-book strategy plus a Monday-open sizing step reading the pair's trailing realised weekly P&L (NT8 account performance / a small script); NinjaTrader Brokerage via Rithmic.

## CYCLE 11 (registered 2026-10-16, before any P1B / P2 run; worker P1B+P2)
### P1B BRACKET-SCALED RE-RUN OF THE 5-MIN ENSEMBLE AND THE ES DIP-BUY LEAD ON 2013-2026 (diagnostic of faithfulness, not a candidate)
Mechanism: the fixed 60-pt stop/target is ~0.3% of price in 2013 and ~0.3% only if scaled; the same three scalings as P1 are applied: fixed(60, reference), a = 60 x raw price/20000, b = clean-median ratio x ADR20, c = clean-median ratio x ATR120 (1-min supertrend ATR), rounded to 0.25. Cost modes: real (1 tick/side-equivalent 0.5 pt RT x 20 + $2.49/side/contract on q = 12000/(bracket x 20) contracts), scaled (cost x bracket/60), gross (0). Engine: ap_c3_h3_core book engine with per-signal-bar bracket (stop = target); the 5 primary members of C3-H3 (none-twins of the delta books), per-book 5R/-2R daily rule with R = $12,000. Each member is divided by its clean weekly std (re-estimated per scaling under real costs), averaged; pair-uplift computed on 2013-2022 for shares 10/20/30/50% against the same-scaling pair (outputs/autopilot/p1/run_<s>_real|scaled|gross.pkl), via the C3-H3 convention. Fidelity check: fixed/real must reproduce ap_c3_h3_run member series. Per-year table 2013-2026: net R and $, weekly Sharpe, maxDD R, trades/day, mean pts per trade in bracket units.
ES dip-buy (T4 B lead dip_rsi5_days5_stop3: RSI(2)<5 and close>SMA200 on 16:00-ET-boundary daily bars, exit after 5 days, stop 3 x ATR20(daily) intrabar): price-level dependence audit: RSI/SMA/ATR-stop are level-free; what depends on level is position size (1 contract = 5x more notional in 2026 than 2013) and the cost share (fixed $ tick+commission vs ATR-scaled R). Scalings: fixed = 1 ES contract; a = constant notional (contracts = clean-median raw price / raw price at entry); b = constant dollar risk (contracts = 9600/(3 x ATR20 x 50)). Costs: real (1 tick/side + $4.36 RT per contract traded), scaled (cost x ATR/clean-median ATR), gross. Siblings reported for context: stop2 and rsi10_days5_stop3. No selection, no H1/H2 gating.
Reading rule (binding): a scaling is called faithful if its 2013-2022 per-year picture is not dominated by cost-in-R drift (gross vs scaled-cost vs real agree in sign) AND trade size in R is stable across years.

### P2 REGIME STAND-DOWN (walk-forward size switch) on the pair, 2013-2026
Mechanism: the pair earns in some regimes only; a trailing own-P&L health stat may detect bad regimes with lag, but the cost is false stand-downs during the 2024-26 profitable era. Prior 7% for a real net benefit on the faithful path; sizing switch to be implemented in quant-dash monitor + a size switch (not DualStrat logic).
Paths: run_a_real (scaled bracket a, real costs; faithful) and run_fixed_real (live fixed 60). Unit: ISO-week sum of daily P&L by label day (Mon-Fri week). Decision at week t end uses weeks <= t of the pair's own UNGATED (shadow) weekly P&L; applies to week t+1.
Grid A (36 rules): window W in {13,26,52} weeks, threshold th in {0,0.5,1.0} (annualised Sharpe = mean/std(ddof1) x sqrt(52) of the trailing W weeks), stand-down size s in {0,0.5}, re-entry after trailing Sharpe > th for N in {4,8} consecutive weeks (N counts only after the stand-down started; first eligible decision needs W weeks of history). Stand down when trailing Sharpe < th.
Grid B (HM early warning, D3 S3 6a statistic = trailing-8-week and 13-week Sharpe of the HM book's weekly P&L, HM rows of the same pair run with live rules): alarm if stat < expanding q-quantile of the stat's own past values (q in {0.05, 0.10}, min 52 weeks history; pure walk-forward) OR fixed D3 thresholds (sharpe8 < -2.65 / -3.74, sharpe13 < -1.54 / -2.17, taken from S3_early_warning.csv; flagged: those numbers were estimated on 2023-08..2025-12, so the fixed variant is not clean pre-2023 and is reported only as a reference), stat in {sharpe8, sharpe13}, s in {0,0.5}, re-entry N in {4,8} weeks stat above threshold. Stand-down applies to the whole pair (both books).
Selection (per path, parameters may use ONLY 2013-2022 + clean 2023-08..2025-07): rank the 36+ rules by weekly Sharpe of the gated path on the concatenated 2013-2022 + clean weeks, subject to non-negative net change on clean (false-stand-down cost on clean must not exceed 25% of clean always-on net); top rule and the whole-grid summary reported. H1 (2025-08-01..2026-02-06) and H2 (2026-02-07..2026-10-13) are read once for the selected rule only. Scores: Sharpe, net, maxDD, fraction of weeks stood down, worst calendar year, worst week; for 2013-2026 as one path and for 2023-2026 only, versus always-on. False-stand-down cost in 2024-26: net lost, number/share of stood-down weeks in which the always-on pair earned > 0, and net of those weeks.
Nulls: (1) timing-destroyed: 2000 random stand-down week sets with the same number of stood-down weeks (iid) and 2000 circular shifts of the actual stand-down mask; statistic = gated Sharpe on 2013-2026 and on 2023-2026; p-value = share of nulls with Sharpe >= real. (2) stationary block bootstrap (mean block 8 weeks, 2000 reps) of paired weekly (gated - always-on) P&L for 90% CI of the Sharpe difference and of the net difference. Gates (informational; this is a monitor/size switch, not a new sleeve): useful only if gated Sharpe beats always-on on BOTH 2013-2022 and 2023-2026 by >= +0.10, the iid-null p <= 0.05, and the bootstrap 90% CI of the Sharpe difference on 2023-2026 does not include a loss > 0.10 of net.

## B12 (registered 2026-10-16, before any B12 run) - MULTI-HOUR TIME-EXIT HOLDS OF THE PAIR'S OWN ST/HM SIGNALS, NO BRACKET, AS A SEPARATE BOOK
- Mechanism: the live pair trades the ST(120,2.0)+EMA200 and HM(20,55 on close-VWAP)+EMA200 flips with a 60-pt stop=target bracket; most flips resolve inside the bracket in minutes (cost-heavy, short-horizon, stopped out in chop). The same entry events, held for a fixed 2/4/6 h with no stop or target, trade the multi-hour continuation of the flip rather than the first 60 pts; its P&L is a different exit paradigm on the same information, so correlation with the pair is expected positive but below 1 (it earns when trends persist, loses in chop without the bracket's stop). Costs are a much smaller share of a multi-hour move. Prior 6% (C10-S1 found multi-hour NQ/ES rules had 2013-22 structure that did not add in 2023-26; here the signal is the pair's own, whose edge lives in 2024-26 only).
- Engine: 1-minute NQ long series (outputs/autopilot/c3/h23/series_NQ_long.npz, back-adjusted), d1_f4_core/C3-H2 style: signals C.signals (ST and HM, identical filters), entries at next-bar open, same BLOCKED entry windows (no entries 15:00-18:00 ET other than >=18:00, none 09:00-09:58 ET), forced flat at the 16:55 ET bar open (no overnight holds). Exit = open of the first bar >= entry+H, or the forced flat, whichever is first. No stop, no target, no day/week locks (locks are bracket-risk tools; risk is judged by the insurance metric set). 1-sec engine NOT used (not cheap: 3.5 years of tick data not on box); fidelity caveat: signals are 1-minute-bar signals; with no intrabar stop the 1-minute fill model is much less sensitive than for the bracketed pair; a signal-match check vs ROWS_corrected_engine.parquet entries (+-1 min) is reported.
- Fill/cost: 8 NQ lots per trade (the pair's size), slippage 1 tick/side (0.5 pt RT x $20 x 8) + commission $34.88 per 8-lot round trip; 2x slippage test = 2 ticks/side, commission unchanged. Sleeve P&L is then scaled by a single constant so its CLEAN weekly sd equals the pair's clean weekly sd (scale from clean only); raw 8-lot dollars also reported.
- EXACT GRID (18 cells): books {ST, HM, ST+HM (two independent books, one open trade each)} x hold H in {2h, 4h, 6h} x re-entry {R0: ignore signals while the book is in a trade; R1: an opposite-side signal closes the trade at the next open and enters the opposite side (same-side signals ignored)}.
- Selection (CLEAN 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 ONLY): rank the 18 cells by additive weekly-Sharpe uplift at 20% share vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) (clean process, ROWS_corrected_engine.parquet), require clean standalone Sharpe > 0; carry the TOP 3 forward. H1 (2025-08-01..2026-02-06) and H2 (2026-02-07..2026-10-13) are read once for those 3 after selection; UNTOUCHED never.
- Gates for FOUND (AUTOPILOT.md, unmoved): additive uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately at 10/20/30/50% (headline 20%), worst week not worse than the share; own timing-destroyed null = random entry bars (same count of entries per book in the pair window, random side, same hold and re-entry rule, 200 draws) p95 on pair-window standalone Sharpe; bootstrap 90% CI lower bound of the all-in additive uplift > 0 (3000 draws); circular-shift null reported (gate only for hedge claims); plateau: hold x0.75 and x1.25 of the selected cell (both neighbours must keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift) ; survives 2x slippage (uplift stays > 0 and >= 75% of... reported; gate = all-in uplift >= +0.10 positive on clean/H1/H2 not required, gate is all-in uplift still > 0 and 2x standalone Sharpe > 0); worst-decile-week correlation with the pair <= +0.30; NT8-codable spec. Constant-risk framing (1-s)P+sS reported with INSURANCE verdict via t3_score.verdict. small-sample flag if < 100 trades in the pair window, with 2013-2026 standalone Sharpe beside it.
- Null for the family: with 18 cells and top-3 carried, expected chance passes ~ 3 x (p95-null rate 5% x P(uplift gates | noise) ~ 0.1) ~ 1-3%; reported.

## B14 (= C2-G2 relaunch) META-LABELING OF THE PAIR'S ENTRIES (registered 2026-10-16, before any B14 run; previous C2-G2 outputs treated as unvalidated and regenerated from scratch under ap_b14_*)
- Mechanism: a gradient-boosted classifier on features known at the entry bar (not single variables) predicts whether a signal entry of the live ST/HM books wins; skip or half-size the lowest-probability entries. Prior 6% (previous worker's unvalidated AUC ~0.52; single-variable filters in D1/E2/C9 all died; expect NO).
- Data: ROWS_corrected_engine.parquet (all raw signal entries incl. ones the live rules later block), label y = 1 if gross trade points > 0. Features (frozen, 36): book_hm, side, own_dist, own_age, oth_age, adr20, dist_rel, ema_sd, ema_sd_rel, st_agree, hm_agree (nt8sim FEAT at the signal bar), tod, rth, globex_late, dow, rv30, rv120, atr14, rng5, rng30, lv30, vrel, drange, rv_ratio, ret5_s, ret30_s, ret120_s, vwapd_s, pos240_s, dpos_s, rng30_rel (1-min bars <= signal bar; VWAP/day range/position by ET label day; side-signed), last5_sign, last3_book_sign, win10, day_pts_so_far, prior_day_sign (outcomes of raw trades that EXITED strictly before this entry; shadow-book assumption: skipped trades' hypothetical outcomes remain observable). Causality test: truncation of the bar series at 30/60/85% must reproduce every feature on earlier bars; plus label audit (training rows only with exit before the month start).
- Model: LightGBM, fixed a priori (300 trees, lr 0.03, 15 leaves, min_child 60, subsample 0.8, colsample 0.7, L2 5), no tuning. Walk-forward expanding window, retrained monthly; first training span = live rows 2023-02..2023-07 (train rows must exit before the test month starts); first scored month 2023-08. 2013-2022 1-min-engine pair trades (h2_pair_long.pkl) are NOT used as training data (different engine, 90% entry match, features not reproducible there without the same nt8sim state: not clearly causal/consistent) - decision registered.
- Cells (6): skip bottom q% of predicted p, q in {10,20,30}; or half-size those entries (mode skip/half). Threshold = q-th percentile of the walk-forward p over the clean span, frozen then applied unchanged to H1/H2 (rows before 2023-08 never masked). Mask applied via b11_nt8 (skipmask for skip; sizefn 0.5 for half) vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process (no b10_core import), 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots.
- Selection: max clean Sharpe uplift among the 6 cells. H1/H2 read once for the selected cell only (plus the cell's plateau neighbours). Robustness vs B.Cfg().
- Gates (pair change): weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately AND worst week not worse on each; label-shuffle null: >= 200 replicates, labels permuted within calendar month, FULL pipeline (walk-forward, 6 cells, select max clean uplift), real clean uplift above the null p95 of the max-cell clean uplift; matched random-skip null (>=300 draws, same removed count per book (and half-size for half mode), selected cell) clean uplift p95; bootstrap 5th percentile of all-in uplift > 0; plateau: neighbours q x {0.75,1.25} x n_estimators {225,300,375} (5 neighbours, center excluded) - >= 75% keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift; 2x slippage uplift (vs 2x-slippage baseline) >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; implementability note (model must be exported as a static scoring table/ONNX or precomputed daily file for NT8: reported, not a gate failure by itself). No gate moves; no re-run after seeing results.
- B12 clarification (appended before first run; makes the 2x-slippage gate exact): survives 2x slippage = all-in additive uplift at 20% share under 2 ticks/side is >= +0.10. Stricter reading, no gate moved.

## CYCLE 11 - C11-DIP and C11-HM (registered 2026-10-16T14:20Z by the C11 worker, BEFORE any C11 run or selection computation)
### C11-DIP ES/YM(+NQ/RTY) RSI(2) dip-buy, ONE pre-selected cell
- Mechanism: short-horizon equity-index mean reversion (RSI(2) < threshold while close > SMA200: buy the dip in an uptrend, exit after 3/5 bars or on RSI recovery, ATR stop). Daily-horizon income independent of the pair's intraday trend flips. Prior 6% of a FOUND (30 of 64 T4-B cells had a random-date p <= 0.05 but they are one idea; pair-window n is 28-47 trades; 2013-2026 Sharpe is 0.4-0.7).
- Candidate set: the 64 T4-B-registered B1 cells (instrument {NQ, ES, RTY, YM} x RSI(2) < {5,10} x exit {RSI>65, RSI>80, 3 bars, 5 bars} x stop {2,3} x ATR20 of the daily bar), rebuilt from the 1-minute series 2013-2026 (data/autopilot/ohlcv1m; 16:00 ET to 16:00 ET bars, trading-day label as day_key, difference back-adjustment at instrument_id changes, RTY from 2017). Entry/exit at the bar close (MOC fiction as in T4 B), stop fills at stop (or open if gapped) minus 1 tick, cost 1 tick/side + $4.36 per contract round trip (T4 B conventions); 2x slippage = 2 ticks/side.
- SELECTION RULE (frozen now, uses the clean span 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 only): among cells with >= 10 closed trades whose exit date is inside the clean span, pick the cell with the highest weekly Sharpe (annualised, sqrt 52) of its 1-contract net daily P&L over the clean span; ties -> more clean trades. Exactly ONE cell is scored afterwards. DISCLOSURE: the T4-B and AMEND reports already displayed full-window (incl. H1/H2) numbers for these cells, so H1/H2 are not virgin for this family; this is stated in the report, and the gate verdict is not softened because of it.
- Scoring of the single cell: sleeve scaled so its 2023-08..2025-07 weekly sigma equals the pair's (T3 convention; 100% share = pair-equivalent risk), additive at 10/20/30/50% (headline 20%) vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process; constant-risk framing (pair x (1-s) + sleeve x s; 16-contract-equivalent total) INSURANCE verdict via t3_score.verdict. Gates exactly as AUTOPILOT.md FOUND 3-7 at 20%: uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week within the share; own random-date null (>= 500 draws, same signal count and exits) p95 on standalone pair-window Sharpe; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; circular-shift null reported; plateau = parameter neighbours at +-25% (RSI threshold x0.75/x1.25, SMA length 150/250, hold bars 0.75x/1.25x rounded, stop ATR 0.75x/1.25x, RSI exit level +-25%): >= 75% keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift at 20%; 2x slippage keeps uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst-decile corr <= +0.30; small-sample flag if < 100 trades in the pair window (it will be), 2013-2026 per-year table (trades, net, weekly Sharpe) reported next to it. Also a sensitivity (not a gate): entry at the next day's open instead of the signal close.
### C11-HM HM-health trigger as a SIZE switch on HM only
- Mechanism/context: D3 S3 found HM's late-summer-2026 drawdown (-$238k raw in 5 weeks) was preceded by fast health statistics crossing red lines 2-4 weeks in. Question: does a causal trailing-health trigger that cuts HM size (not ST) improve the pair's weekly Sharpe? Prior 4% (n = 1 event in the history; D3 itself says the fast statistics alarm 1-3 times a year). DISCLOSURE: the D3 S3 statistics list was built looking at the H2 collapse, so H2 is not virgin for the statistic family; H2 is nonetheless read once and a pass there is not independent evidence.
- Statistic (weekly, computed at each Sunday week end from the UNRULED, full-size HM raw rows of the corrected engine (f=1, no per-book rules or locks, so it is independent of the switch itself), trades assigned to the week of their exit label): S in {sharpe8 = 8-week rolling Sharpe of weekly HM net (annualised), sharpe13 = 13-week, apl50 = mean gross points of the last 50 closed HM trades}.
- Threshold rule (walk-forward, expanding): trigger fires for week w+1 if S(w) < the q-quantile of all S values observed up to and including week w (minimum 20 observations, else no trigger), q in {10%, 20%}. No hysteresis: re-evaluated each week. Entries (HM only) in the following week's trading days are sized at m x full, m in {0.5, 0}; ST untouched; open positions are not altered. Implemented as B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None, sizefn=...) so all live rules (per-book 5R/-2R, day lock, week floor -8R) act on the resized HM rows.
- Grid (exactly 12 cells): S (3) x q (2) x m (2). SELECTION on the clean span only: highest clean weekly Sharpe of the pair among cells with >= 4 triggered weeks in the clean span; exactly one cell goes on. H1 and H2 read once for that cell.
- Baseline: B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process (weekly Sharpe 1.46/2.83/2.30). Gates (pair change): weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1 and H2 separately; worst week not worse; MaxDD/CVaR5 reported (rep_common insurance metric set; SURVIVES/INSURANCE verdicts as the D1 harness); matched random-switch null: >= 500 draws that shuffle the order of the selected cell's on/off run lengths over the same eligible weeks (same exposure and episode count), full B.run each, p95 of all-in Sharpe uplift must be below the real uplift; bootstrap 90% CI (weekly, paired) of all-in uplift > 0; plateau: S window 8/13 -> 6/10/16 as +-25%, q x0.75/x1.25, m 0.375/0.625 neighbours (>= 75% keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift); 2x slippage (SLIP_PT 1.0 on both baseline and variant) keeps uplift >= +0.10 on each span; worst-week-decile test n/a (the switch is not a hedge claim); NT8-codable spec (HM entry quantity as a function of a weekly-updated, externally computed flag; needs a weekly script that computes the statistic from the HM trade list) and margin (reduces, never raises, contracts).

## CAPACITY BACKLOG B2/B3/B5/B6 (registered 2026-10-16T14:06Z by the B2356 worker, BEFORE any B-item run; only loader smoke tests were executed)
Common: daily items use the 26-outright daily universe (data/t4, t4_a_core.build, back-adjusted continuous .v.0/.v.1, UTC-day bars); intraday items use the 1-min series npz (back-adjusted, ET clock, label day = ET+6h). Fill model: daily - stop-entry orders fill at the trigger level (gap: at the open), market entries at the bar open, time exits at the bar close, stop exits at the stop price (gap-through at the open), stop tested first on the entry bar (conservative); 1 tick/side + per-product commission (T4 A table / $4.36 RT intraday), 2x slippage = 2 ticks/side; one extra round trip when a roll falls inside a trade; one open position per instrument per cell. Size = $7,000 per ATR20 per trade (t4 convention); sleeve then scaled to the pair's clean weekly vol using the sleeve's OWN 2013-2022 weekly vol (no use of clean/H1/H2 in the scale). Development/selection on 2013-2022 ONLY (strictly earlier than clean; RTY legs 2017-2022); clean 2023-08..2025-07, H1, H2 read ONCE for the selected cells (plus every cell of B5, which has no selection). Selection rule (B2, B3, B6): >= 300 trades (B3: >= 100 position changes) in 2013-2022, >= 7 of 10 calendar years with positive weekly Sharpe (B3 pairs with RTY: >= 4 of 6), rank by 2013-2022 weekly Sharpe, top 3 per family. Gates for FOUND: AUTOPILOT gates 1-7 with the amended gate 4 (own random-entry null, 200 draws, same count of trades per calendar year per instrument, same hold/stop, random side; p95 on standalone pair-window Sharpe, also reported on 2013-2022; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in additive uplift > 0), plateau (+-25% on each parameter), 2x slippage, worst-decile corr <= +0.30, small-sample flag (< 100 trades in pair window) with 2013-2026 Sharpe beside it. Baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) from a clean process; B.Cfg() robustness line. Multiplicity reported: cells per family x 5% = expected chance passes of the own null.

- B2 VOLATILITY-COMPRESSION BREAKOUTS. Mechanism: volatility clusters and compression precedes expansion; a stop-entry beyond the compressed bar's extreme catches the first expansion with a defined risk (Crabel NR7 / inside-day lore, no peer-reviewed edge beyond vol clustering). Prior 6% (daily trend after costs was ~0 in the pair window, C1-F1/C10-S1 died to cost). 
  B2-D daily, 26 markets, grid 36 cells = setup {NR7: range_t < min(range_{t-6..t-1}); ID: inside day h_t<=h_{t-1} & l_t>=l_{t-1}; P10: range_t <= 10th percentile of range_{t-19..t} (rank among 20 <= 2)} x hold {1,2,3,5} days (entry day counts as day 1, exit at close of day t+hold) x ATR stop {1,2,3} x ATR20(t) from entry. Entry: next day buy-stop at high_t / sell-stop at low_t (if both touched, the side whose level is closer to the open is taken; stop-first handling as above). Plateau neighbours: NR7 length 5/9 (ID, P10: P10 -> 7.5%/12.5% = rank <=1.5/2.5 rounded as percentile on 20), hold and stop x0.75/x1.25 (hold rounded, min 1, neighbours that round to the base are skipped).
  B2-M 30-minute bars, NQ ES RTY GC CL ZN (6 markets pooled equal risk): bars from the 1-min series (30-min buckets from 18:00 ET), setup bar start 08:00-15:00 ET (hme in [480,900)), setup {NR7 on bar range vs previous 6 bars, inside bar, bar range <= 10th percentile of the trailing 920 bars (~20 sessions)} x hold {1,2,3,5} label days (exit at the OPEN of the last 30-min bar of label day s+hold-1, 16:30 ET bar) x ATR stop {0.5,1,1.5} x daily ATR20 (label-day range mean, prior days). Entry: stop order valid for the next 30-min bar only (long above setup high, short below setup low); same-bar both-touch rule as above; skip if the next bar belongs to the next label day. 36 cells. Costs 1 tick/side + $4.36 RT per contract.
- B3 TREND ON EQUITY-INDEX RATIOS. Mechanism: relative-strength persistence between NQ/ES/RTY (growth vs broad vs small cap) is documented as a style/size momentum; spreads were tested only as mean reversion (C1-F4). Prior 8%. Ratio index x_t = cumsum of (pct return leg A - pct return leg B) (pct return = back-adjusted close diff / prior raw close; roll bars use the adjusted diff). Pairs NQ/ES, NQ/RTY, ES/RTY. Long spread = long A / short B, legs sized to equal dollar ATR20 risk ($7,000 per leg per ATR20, refreshed weekly on prior-week data), costs and slippage on both legs (+ both legs' roll round trip when held through a roll). Signals on x at bar close, position acts at the next bar open. Daily bars (label day): EMA cross (10,40),(20,100),(50,200); Donchian N/M 20/10, 55/20 (enter on close beyond N-bar extreme, exit on M-bar opposite extreme); TSM sign(x_t - x_{t-L}) L=20,60,120 -> 8 rules. 30-min bars: EMA (16,64),(48,192),(96,384); Donchian 48/24, 144/48; TSM L=48,144,288 -> 8 rules. 3 pairs x 2 timeframes x 8 rules = 48 cells. Plateau: every length x0.75/x1.25.
- B5 COMMODITY AND RATES SEASONALITY (NO selection; all 8 sleeves + equal-risk combination reported; rules fixed from the literature/lore BEFORE looking at any return; they differ from C2-G4 R1-R7): NG_A long Sep 1-Nov 30 (pre-winter build; Suenaga-Smith-Williams forward-curve seasonality); NG_B short Mar 1-Jun 30 (spring shoulder/injection season); CL_A short Sep 15-Nov 15 (refinery-maintenance demand trough); GC_A long Jan 1-Feb 28 (Lucey-Tully / Baur calendar effects, Chinese New Year demand); GC_B long Aug 1-Sep 30 (Indian wedding/festival restocking); GC_C short Apr 1-Jun 30 (summer lull); ZN_A long around quarter-ends: from the open of the 2nd-to-last trading day of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec to the close of the 2nd trading day of the next month (index-extension / rebalancing demand, Etula et al.); ZN_B long the first 3 trading days of every month. Window entry at the open of the first trading day on/after the start date, exit at the close of the last trading day on/before the end date, no stop. Prior 4% per sleeve. Scored 2013-2026 (standalone, by year), then the pair window. Own null: random-date windows with the same length and count per calendar year, same side, 500 draws. Plateau: window start/end shifted by +-25% of the window length (>= 1 day).
- B6 SHORT-TERM REVERSAL (commodities and rates) AND CONTINUATION AFTER LARGE MOVES. Mechanism: reversal after liquidity-driven moves in commodities/rates (inventory/price-pressure, Nagel-type), continuation after very large moves (information/under-reaction). Prior 5%. Signal at close t, entry at the next open, exit at the close of day t+hold (hold 1 = same-day close of the next day), no stop, one position at a time per instrument. 
  B6-RV: fade the n-day return z = (c_t - c_{t-n})/(ATR20_t x sqrt(n)): |z| >= zth; n {1,2,3,5} x zth {1.0,2.0} x hold {1,2,3,5} x group {COM = GC SI HG PL CL NG RB HO ZC ZS ZW LE HE, RATES = ZN ZB ZF ZT, BOTH} = 96 cells. 
  B6-CT: follow the 1-day move |ret_1|/ATR20 >= m: m {1.5,2,2.5,3} x hold {1,2,3,5} x universe {ALL26, COM+RATES} = 32 cells. Plateau: n, zth, m, hold x0.75/x1.25 (rounded, distinct).

## PRIORITY 0 - WT RE-SCORE (C-WT), registered 2026-10-16 by the WT worker BEFORE any WT grid run or any WT sleeve/trio scoring (only: signal-equality check vs the B3 dispatch F2, pair reproduction check, and one smoke run of the live spec for trade counts were executed)
- Spec confirmed from code (b3_dispatch F2, b3_refs WT_MODAL, b10_build spec_B, b10_trackB LIVE): WT = Wilder ATR ratchet trail flip. atr = RMA(TR, n) on the signal-timeframe bars; trend long while close stays above stop; stop = max(stop, close - m*atr) (ratchets up only), flips short when close < stop, then stop = close + m*atr (mirror). Entry on the flip bar close -> next 1-min bar open; EMA(e) filter on the signal-timeframe close (buy: close>EMA, sell: close<EMA); bracket 60 pt stop / 60 pt target; one open trade per book; windows and flatten as ST/HM (signal-bar start 18:00-15:00 ET except 09:00-09:58, flat at the 16:55 bar open). WT-LIVE = ATR 20 / mult 2.0 / EMA 100 / 1-min (b10_trackB LIVE dict B_WT_20_2.0_100); WT-B10 = B_WT_20_1.75_50. Both fixed here a priori, not selected.
- Mechanism: same trend-flip family as ST (Supertrend ATR120 x2) but with a short ATR (fast, tight trail) and the ratchet instead of the band recompute; flip timing differs from ST/HM, so some independence of entries is possible, but the payoff is the same convex trend-follow with bracket. Prior of a FOUND 5% (B3-era rejection was against the retired HM; ST and WT likely correlated; fast flips are cost heavy: 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots is ~0.5+ pt/trade).
- Engines: NT8-faithful 1-second path = nt8sim order-level account model (netting, orphan protective orders) extended to 3 books (ap_wt_core.acct3, tie priority ST>HM>WT) + generic b11 account (ap_wt_acct), both VERIFIED to reproduce ROWS_corrected_engine and B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) to the cent before use. 2013-2022 history: 1-minute engine (p1_core style, 1-min bar fills, stop-first) with the stated fidelity caveat (no second data before 2023-02).
- (a) Standalone, history: WT-LIVE and WT-B10 at 8 NQ-equivalent R ($9,600), scalings a/b/c of the P1 pair run (a = 60 x raw price/20000, b = clean-median ratio to ADR20, c = clean-median ratio to ATR(120,1-min)) and fixed 60, each at real / cost-scaled / gross costs; per-year table 2013-2026. No gate; descriptive.
- (b) Trio: ST/HM/WT contracts {8/8/4, 8/8/8, 6/6/4, 6/6/6} vs pair 8/8; additive (pair at 8/8 unchanged -> trio sizes literal) and constant-risk (all sizes x 16/(sum of contracts), 16-contract-equivalent total) framings; account rules in dollars (R=9,600 fixed, as live); insurance metric set; correlation of WT-book daily P&L and trio with the pair all weeks and worst-decile weeks. Descriptive plus the sleeve gates at 10/20/30/50% shares for WT standalone (t3_score convention: WT sleeve scaled to pair clean weekly vol; own account rules).
- (c) Grid (exactly 432 cells): ATR period {10,14,20,30} x mult {2,3,4} x EMA {off,100,200} x timeframe {1,3,5,10} min x exit {X1 = 60/60 bracket on 1-s path; X2 = trail-only (exit at the open after the opposite raw flip, no stop/target, flat 16:55); X3 = X2 + 120-bar (minute) time stop}. ATR and EMA are on the signal-timeframe bars. 8 NQ contracts, 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots, live account rules (per-book 5R/-2R day, combined day lock cap 8R/arm 2R/keep 33%, week floor -8R; WT as a single book).
  SELECTION (clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 only; trades outside clean are not scored in this stage): eligible = >= 300 clean trades and clean standalone weekly Sharpe > 0; rank by clean ADDITIVE Sharpe uplift of pair + 20% sleeve (sleeve scaled to the pair's clean weekly vol) vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None); top 10 cells advance (multiplicity 432 cells, expected chance passes of a 5% null = 21.6; stated). H1 and H2 are read ONCE, for the 10 advanced cells only.
  GATES per advanced cell (AUTOPILOT.md FOUND 3-7 as amended): additive uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately at each of 10/20/30/50% (headline 20%), worst week not worse than the share; own timing-destroyed null (random entry minute inside the allowed windows, random side, same trade count; X1: same bracket; X2/X3: hold drawn from the cell's own hold-time distribution, no stop; 200 draws) p95 on STANDALONE pair-window (2023-08..2026-10) Sharpe; bootstrap 90% CI (3000 draws, weekly) of the all-in additive uplift > 0; circular-shift null reported; plateau = +-25% neighbours (ATR x0.75/x1.25 rounded, mult x0.75/x1.25, EMA 75/125 or 150/250, timeframe round(x0.75/x1.25) when distinct, X1 bracket 45/75 pts, X3 time stop 90/150) >= 75% keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift at 20%; 2x slippage (SLIP_PT 1.0 both baseline and variant) uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst-decile-week correlation with the pair <= +0.30; NT8-codable spec and margin; small-sample flag (< 100 trades in the pair window); 2013-2026 standalone Sharpe (1-min engine, X1 only, fixed 60 pt bracket scaled by 60*raw/20000, real costs) reported next to every advanced cell; constant-risk INSURANCE verdict via t3_score.verdict. FOUND needs all; LEAD = passes uplift gates on all spans at some share.
- (d) Trade Health: WT-LIVE and WT-B10 through the harness engine st_run9 (ticks, loss-side TH Cell parameters of the B10 WT spec: ml=10 pts, required 2, 30 s windows, mask (mom, stall, eff, vol) as b3_common CELLA/params) with TH off vs on, NT8 windows (18:00-15:00 except 09:00-09:58, flat 16:55), 8 lots, 1 tick/side + commission; also ST Cell A (ml 15, req 3, 30 s) on WT for reference. Reported: net, Sharpe, maxDD, trades, TH exits per span clean/H1/H2. Verdict on 'TH collapses WT' = TH-on weekly Sharpe or net lower than TH-off by > 30% on clean and H1/H2 (and the mechanism: share of TH exits and mean pts of TH exits).
