## CYCLE 12: B-FAMILIES (registered 2026-10-16T14:30Z, before any B-family replicate, pair-window read or scoring; only grid-cell enumeration and one selection-span-only smoke run of B4/ES (2013-2022) were executed)
Common protocol for B1, B4, B7, B10, B11 (template C10-S1 / C7-P1). Instruments ES, RTY (2017-07+), GC, CL, ZN; B1 also BTC and ETH (CME 1-min files data/t4, available from 2022-01 only). 1-min bars, difference back-adjusted at rolls; CL 2020-04-17..23 dropped. Signal at tf-bar close, entry at the next 1-min open, one position per cell, stop-first within a 1-min row, stop gaps at the open, flatten at the 16:55 ET open (B10: window end). Fill model: 1 tick adverse per side (ZN 1/64) on entries, stops and time/flatten exits (targets as limits, no slippage) + $4.36 per contract round trip scaled by quantity; 2x slippage = 2 ticks/side. B1: equal R = $9,600 per full stop (qty = R / (stop distance x $/pt), fractional contracts as d1_f4_core); B4/B7/B10/B11: 1 contract then scaled to the pair's clean weekly sigma (selection-span weekly sigma used for the factor, as C10-S1).
Selection (ONLY the selection span; pair window never read until after selection): ES/GC/CL/ZN: 2013-2022, positive weekly Sharpe in >=7 of 10 calendar years AND >=300 trades; RTY: 2018-2022, >=4 of 5 years AND >=150 trades; BTC/ETH: 2022-01..2023-07 in three blocks (2022H1, 2022H2, 2023-01..07), >=2 of 3 AND >=100 trades (low power, flagged). Top 10 per instrument by selection-span weekly Sharpe (B10: top 5 per window, Asia and London separately). If fewer than the quota pass, up to 3 further cells (best selection Sharpe among those failing the rule) are read as DIAGNOSTIC and cannot be FOUND. Then clean 2023-08..2025-07, H1, H2 read once.
False-survivor test: 200 timing-destroyed replicates of the WHOLE grid per instrument (same trade count, random valid entry rows, alternating sides, same exits/windows), re-running the selection rule; statistics = number of cells passing, mean selection Sharpe of the top 10. Own random-entry null for each selected cell: 100 replicates, pair-window standalone Sharpe p95. Bootstrap 3,000 draws 90% CI of the additive uplift; circular-shift null reported. Plateau: +-25% neighbours (listed in code) keep >=75% of all-in uplift for >=75% of neighbours. 2x slippage: all-in uplift at 20% must stay >= +0.10 for the plateau/slippage gate. Worst-decile weekly correlation <= +0.30. Small-sample flag < 100 trades in the pair window (2013-2026 or full-history Sharpe reported). Pair baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process. Gates unchanged. Insurance metric set + constant-risk (30% share) framing reported.
- B1 (prior 3%): live ST (ATR-rma, close-source supertrend flip + EMA filter), HM (HMA(a) x HMA(b) of close-minus-session-VWAP, slope and EMA filter), WT (Wilder-ATR trailing-stop flip, anchor close, reset on flip, EMA filter; WT_MODAL of b3_refs) on 5/10/15/30-min bars, ATR-trail exits, on other instruments. Mechanism: the NQ edge is a structure effect (large intraday swings relative to cost) that may exist on other liquid contracts when brackets are ADR-scaled. Exact grid per instrument = 4 tf {5,10,15,30} x 9 signal parameter sets x 5 exits = 180 cells. Parameter sets: ST (atr, mult, ema) in {(max(4,round(120/tf)),2.0,max(20,round(200/tf))), (14,2.0,200), (14,3.0,200)}; HM (a,b,ema) in {(max(3,round(20/tf)),max(6,round(55/tf)),max(20,round(200/tf))), (20,55,200), (9,21,200)} (VWAP reset 04:00 UTC as d1_f4_core); WT (atr,mult,ema) in {(20,2.0,100),(14,2.0,100),(20,3.0,100)}. Exits (initial stop = trail distance D, ratcheting from the favourable extreme, no target, flat at 16:55): ADR trail D = k x ADR20, k in {0.10,0.15,0.20}; ATR trail D = m x ATR(14) of the tf bar, m in {2,3}. Entries 18:00-09:00 and 09:59-15:00 ET. Neighbours: atr/a +-25%, mult/b +-25%, exit value +-25%.
- B4 (prior 3%): intraday mean reversion fading closes outside session-VWAP +- k sigma bands (sigma = volume-weighted std of typical price about the session VWAP). Mechanism: bands mark stretched price after liquidity-driven moves; the reversion to VWAP is the classic execution-benchmark pull. Grid per instrument = 192 cells: tf {5,10,15,30} x k {1.5,2.0,2.5} (cross from inside to outside on the bar close) x anchor {globex 18:00 ET, rth open (ES/RTY 09:30, GC/ZN 08:20, CL 09:00)} x stop s in {1.0,2.0} sigma from entry x target {VWAP level at signal, halfway} x time stop {60,180} min. Entry window: globex 19:00-15:30 (>=60 min after anchor), rth anchor+60min..15:30. Neighbours k,s,time +-25%.
- B7 (prior 2%): time-exit-only rule books, no stop. Mechanism: tests signal timing separately from stop/target mechanics (stops convert noise into losses; a pure holding-period read isolates drift after the signal). Grid per instrument = 288 cells: tf {5,10,15} x signal {ST(14,2) flip, EMA 9/21 cross, Donchian-20 close break, RSI(14) 30/70 cross-in (oversold=long), VWAP-z 2.0 cross (below band=long), MACD 12/26 zero cross} x direction {with, against} x hold {60,120,180,240} min x session {all 18:00-15:30, day 08:00-15:30}; only holds that complete before the 16:55 flatten are taken. Neighbours hold +-25% and signal numbers +-25%.
- B10 (prior 3%): overnight (Asia entries 19:00-01:30 ET flat 02:00; London entries 02:30-07:30 flat 08:25) specialists with brackets in units of the window's own 20-day mean range SR. Grid per instrument = 144 cells: window {asia,lon} x tf {5,15,30} x strategy {ST(14,2), EMA 9/21, Donchian 20, window opening range (first 30 min of the window) breakout, VWAP-z 2.0 fade (globex VWAP), RSI(14,30) fade} x exit {bracket stop=target 0.25 SR, bracket 0.5 SR, trail 0.25 SR, trail 0.5 SR}. Top 5 per window per instrument. Neighbours exit +-25%, strategy numbers +-25%.
- B11 (prior 3%): opening-range (first 15/30/60 min of the instrument RTH) and prior-trading-day high/low breakouts and fades. Grid per instrument = 192 cells: level {OR15, OR30, OR60, PDH/PDL} x tf {5,15} x mode {with, fade} x exit {b1 stop a x ADR20 & target 1R, b2 target 2R, tr trail a x ADR20, hd stop only to 16:55} x a in {0.10,0.20,0.30}; first close beyond a level per day, entries until 15:00 ET (RTH open = ES/RTY 09:30, GC/ZN 08:20, CL 09:00). Neighbours a +-25%, OR minutes +-25%.

## B19 BOOK-TILT (Q-5), registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any B19 tilt run (executed before this entry: only reading code/reports; no tilt simulation)
- Mechanism: ST (supertrend) and HM books have alternating edge across spans (S5: book rho -1 across spans; D1 F3: ST Sharpe H1 -0.11 vs HM 2.61). If book edge persists at 8-13 week horizon, tilting contracts toward the recently better book at constant total risk (ST+HM = 16 contracts-equivalent) should lift the pair's weekly Sharpe; if it mean-reverts, the reverse tilt. Prior of FOUND 4% (D1 F3 static HM-heavy gain was regime, within random-tilt null in clean/H1; 24 cells).
- Engine: b11_nt8 (ROWS_corrected_engine, live rules at dollar levels, 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots), main Cfg = B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) in a CLEAN process (b10_core never imported), tilt via Cfg.sizefn (per-row book scale n_book/8, constant n_ST+n_HM=16). Second Cfg = B.Cfg() (reported for selected cells). Baseline = same Cfg at 8/8.
- Signal (causal): shadow per-book weekly P&L from the 8/8 run of the same Cfg (weeks by exit ISO week). For week w use only weeks < w; first L weeks of data = 8/8. d_k = P&L_HM,k - P&L_ST,k (pnl metric) or the trailing Sharpe difference (mean/std over the L weeks, HM minus ST). z = metric difference sign (binary) or t-stat of weekly d (proportional).
- Grid (exactly 24 cells): L {8,13} x metric {pnl, sharpe} x direction {mom (tilt to the recently better book), rev (tilt to the worse)} x mode {B2: ST = 8 -/+ 2, B4: ST = 8 -/+ 4 (12/4..4/12), P4: t = round(4*clip(tstat/2,-1,1)) contracts, tstat = mean(d)/sd(d)*sqrt(L) for the pnl metric; for the sharpe metric tstat = (Sharpe_HM-Sharpe_ST)*sqrt(L/52)... i.e. same form using weekly Sharpe difference per sqrt(2/L)*... scaled: tstat_s = (SR_HM - SR_ST)/sqrt(2/L) with SR weekly (non-annualised)}. Ties (metric diff == 0) = 8/8.
- Selection (CLEAN 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 only): eligible = clean weekly-Sharpe uplift vs 8/8 baseline >= +0.10; rank by clean uplift; top 3 advance (multiplicity 24 cells; chance passes at a 5% null ~1.2, stated). If none eligible: verdict NO and H1/H2 are not read for the grid. H1 (2025-08-01..2026-02-06) and H2 (2026-02-07..2026-10-13) read ONCE for the advanced cells only. Static references (D1 F3 4:12 HM-heavy, 3:13, 5:11, 8:8) are reported descriptively, not selected.
- GATES per advanced cell (a cell must pass all): (1) weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately vs baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) (also reported vs B.Cfg() pair); (2) worst week not worse than baseline on all-in AND on each of clean/H1/H2 (strict, constant risk so no share allowance); (3) matched random-tilt null: the cell's own weekly tilt sequence randomly permuted across weeks (same tilt-size distribution and direction mix), 200 full re-simulations; real weekly Sharpe above null p95 on clean, H1, H2 and all-in; (4) bootstrap (3000 week resamples, paired) 90% CI of the all-in weekly Sharpe uplift lower bound > 0; circular-shift of the tilt sequence reported; (5) plateau: four neighbours (L x0.75, x1.25 rounded; tilt magnitude x0.75, x1.25 with fractional contract scale allowed in the test only) keep >= 75% of the cell's all-in Sharpe uplift for >= 75% (3 of 4) of neighbours; (6) 2x slippage (SLIP_PT 1.0 pt, baseline also at 2x): uplift still >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; (7) insurance metric set (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg weeks) clean/H1/H2/all-in; rep_common verdict; worst-decile-week correlation with the baseline reported (<= +0.30 not applicable to a re-weighting, reported); NT8 spec = DualStrat weekly contract-quantity input per book set at the Sunday-evening session start from the previous weeks' realised per-book P&L (integer contracts; margin unchanged at 16 contracts total). Zero FOUND acceptable. No gate moves, no re-run after seeing results.

## B15 (Q-1, LOCKRE) and B16 (Q-2, WKGRAD): pair-rule changes (registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any B15/B16 run; only code reading and an engine-reproduction check of the baseline were executed)
Common. Both modify the live pair's account rules inside the b11_nt8 engine (ROWS_corrected_engine.parquet, pair 8/8, 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots, minute mark-to-market of the combined day/week equity, as B.lock_stage). Baseline = B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) computed in a CLEAN process (b11_nt8 imported first; b10_core never imported), reproduced to weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30 before use; also reported vs B.Cfg() (modification applied on top of the full live config incl. per-book -6R week). Spans: clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31, H1 2025-08-01..2026-02-06, H2 2026-02-07..2026-10-13. Selection: stage 1 prints CLEAN ONLY; the single best clean cell (by clean weekly Sharpe uplift vs baseline, must be > 0) is carried; stage 2 reads H1/H2 once for that cell (and for the plateau/slip variants of it). Entries that the live per-book rules skip stay skipped (pass-1 factors unchanged); only the combined stages change.
GATES (never moved; all required for FOUND): (1) weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1 and H2 separately vs the baseline; (2) worst week (all-in) not worse than the baseline's; (3) matched random-switch null: uplift of the real rule on all-in weekly Sharpe > p95 of 200 draws in which the trigger is placed at random (see each family) at the SAME frequency; (4) bootstrap 90% CI (3,000 weekly resamples, paired, all-in) of the weekly-Sharpe uplift > 0; (5) plateau: >= 75% of +-25% neighbours keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift of the selected cell (computed on all-in because neighbours are not selection); (6) 2x slippage (SLIP_PT 1.0 in baseline and variant, whole chain re-run): uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; (7) insurance metric set reported (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg weeks) clean/H1/H2/all, incl. the rep_common INSURANCE/SURVIVES tests as a constant-risk read (same contracts, so constant-risk = additive); worst-decile-week correlation is not a meaningful gate for a rule change on the pair itself (reported as corr(variant-minus-baseline, baseline) over the baseline worst-decile weeks); NT8-codable spec + margin (margin unchanged: sizes never exceed the live 8/8). Small-sample flag: number of trigger events (< 30 events flagged); 2013-2026 Sharpe is not available for the pair engine (live rows start 2023) and is stated as N/A. FOUND-PENDING until replicated on a window selection never touched (none exists for the pair rows; so the best possible verdict is LEAD/FOUND-PENDING on H1+H2 read once).
- B15 (Q-1, prior 8%). Mechanism: the combined day lock (8R cap or 67% give-back from a >= 2R peak) flattens both books for the rest of the day; D3 S2 found the lock's cost concentrated on trend/high-range days where the rest of the day was positive (rest_plain +$55-94k on the worst days). Continuing at reduced size keeps a fraction of the lock's protection and recovers part of the trend day. Rule: when the lock fires at minute tl, open positions are closed at the open of tl+1 exactly as live (unchanged); every entry after tl on that day is taken at fraction q of its pass-1 size; no second day lock after the first (the week floor -8R, applied on the resulting equity, stays). GRID (2 cells): q in {0.25, 0.50}; q=0 is the live baseline. Applies to both lock kinds (cap and give-back). Null (matched random-switch): the real day-lock times are destroyed: per draw a random set of trading-day groups with the same count (same fraction of live days as the real locks) is locked at a uniformly random minute of the day's active grid; uplift = [random locks + continuation q] minus [random locks + flat]; 200 draws; the real uplift must exceed p95. Plateau neighbours: q*0.75, q*1.25 (2 neighbours; both must keep >= 75%).
- B16 (Q-2, prior 6%). Mechanism: the -8R week floor flattens the book for the rest of the week and cost clean/H2 recoveries; a graded step reduces size earlier and keeps recovery optionality. Rule: in each week (iso week of exit date, as the week lock) once the minute-marked combined week equity (after the day-lock stage) first reaches <= -X R (R=$9,600), every ENTRY after that minute in the week is taken at fraction k of its size (positions already open are left alone); the -8R floor is then applied unchanged on the resulting (reduced-size) equity. GRID (6 cells): X in {3,4,5} x k in {0.50, 0.25}. Null (matched random-switch): per draw choose a random set of weeks with the same count as real triggered weeks, trigger at a uniformly random minute of that week's active grid, size k afterwards; uplift distribution vs baseline; 200 draws; real uplift must exceed p95. Plateau neighbours: X*0.75, X*1.25 and k*0.75, k*1.25 (4 neighbours; X 4->3/5, k .5->.375/.625).
Multiplicity: 2 + 6 = 8 cells; chance passes of the clean selection are not corrected (stated).

## B17 (registered 2026-10-16, before any B17 run) - TRAILING-REALISED-VOL SIZING OF THE PAIR (Q-3)
- Mechanism: the pair's weekly income is a fat-tailed sum of convex trend trades; if P&L volatility clusters (lock-day regimes, high-range trend weeks), shrinking size after volatile stretches and growing it after quiet ones lowers weekly sigma more than mean and lifts weekly Sharpe / smooths income. No signal change. Prior 10% (weekly P&L is not positively autocorrelated in level, B8; vol clustering is the only handle).
- Engine: the live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) (ROWS_corrected_engine via d3_common, clean process; reproduce clean/H1/H2 weekly Sharpe 1.46/2.83/2.30 before use). A size multiplier m_w is applied to every entry whose entry trading-label day falls in week w, through B.Cfg.sizefn, so all dollar rules (per-book 5R/-2R, day cap 8R/arm/keep, week floor -8R) stay in fixed dollars as live. Slippage 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 for baseline and variant.
- Rule: sigma_w = stdev of the unit-size baseline daily pair P&L over the last 5K trading days strictly before the first trading-label day of week w (known at the Monday open; live equivalent = realised daily P&L divided by the size in force); first weeks with < 15 days of history: m=1. raw_w = clip(T / sigma_w, lo, hi), T = median sigma_w over clean weeks; m_w = raw_w / mean(raw over clean weeks) so the average size over the clean span is exactly 1 (no leverage change).
- Grid (exactly 4 cells): K in {4, 8} weeks (20, 40 trading days) x (lo,hi) in {(0.6,1.4), (0.75,1.25)}.
- Selection: clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 only, rank by clean weekly Sharpe uplift vs baseline; the single top cell is carried (multiplicity 4); H1 and H2 then read ONCE for that cell. If its clean uplift is <= 0 the family is NO and H1/H2 are still read once for the carried cell for the report.
- Gates (uplift replaces the additive sleeve framing, since sizing has no sleeve): weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) (also reported vs B.Cfg()); all-in worst week not worse than baseline; matched random-size null: 100 draws permuting the realised weekly multiplier sequence across weeks (same multiset, renormalised to clean mean 1), real uplift must exceed the p95 of the null uplift on clean and on all-in; paired weekly bootstrap (3000 draws, all-in weeks) 90% CI of the Sharpe uplift > 0; plateau = one-at-a-time neighbours (K x0.75/x1.25 -> 15/25 or 30/50 days; band half-widths x0.75/x1.25) with >= 75% keeping >= 75% of the selected cell's all-in uplift; survives 2x slippage (uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 with SLIP_PT 1.0 both sides); insurance metric set (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg weeks) clean/H1/H2/all, mean size is neutral so constant-risk framing = the same series (INSURANCE verdict via ins_metrics.flags: cost >= -5% and CVaR5 or worst week >= 25% or maxDD >= 20% better); NT8-codable (a size multiplier per week computed from the strategy's own daily P&L; contracts = round(8 x m), rounding stated; margin max 8 x 1.4 = 11.2 -> 12 lots). FOUND needs all; zero is acceptable. Mean multiplier distribution and rounding to whole lots reported.

## B18 (registered 2026-10-16, before any B18 run) - SIZE AFTER THE PRIOR-DAY OUTCOME (Q-4)
- Mechanism: the combined day lock (cap 8R / give-back) and per-book day stops make day outcomes structurally dependent (a capped day exhausts the trend, a loss day leaves flat risk); a tilt on yesterday's state could exploit daily autocorrelation (momentum or reversal). Prior 5%.
- Engine and fill model as B17. m_d applied to every entry whose entry trading-label day is d. Prior trading-label day state from the baseline unit-size run: CAP = the combined day lock kind == 'cap' (d3_common.run_detail dinfo), LOSS = day net P&L < 0 (and not CAP); a day can be CAP or LOSS or neither (neither -> 1.0). Days without a prior day: 1.0. Raw multipliers: a after CAP, b after LOSS; m_d = raw_d / mean(raw over clean days) so the clean average size is 1.
- Grid (exactly 8 cells): (a,b) in {0.75,1.0,1.25}^2 minus (1,1). Direction (momentum vs reversal) is thus selected on clean only. Selection: top cell by clean weekly Sharpe uplift, carried alone (multiplicity 8); H1/H2 read once afterwards. 
- Gates, nulls, bootstrap, 2x slippage, insurance, executability exactly as B17, with: null = 100 permutations of the realised daily multiplier vector across days (renormalised to clean mean 1); plateau neighbours = each active multiplier m moved to 1+(m-1)x0.75 and 1+(m-1)x1.25 one at a time (a, then b). NT8-codable: multiplier from the previous trading day's own P&L / lock flag; contracts = round(8 x m).

## BATCH I (B15 + I1..I10), registered 2026-10-16 by worker I BEFORE any family run (only loader smoke tests, the 1-minute cache build ap_i_load.py, and the pair reproduction check B.run timing were executed; no returns of any candidate were computed)
### I-COMMON (applies to every family below unless overridden)
- Pair: B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) from b11_nt8 in a clean process (t3_score imported first); baseline weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30. Uplift vs B.Cfg() reported for any lead. Spans clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31, H1 2025-08-01..2026-02-06, H2 2026-02-07..2026-10-13 (H1/H2 read once for one selected cell per family).
- SLEEVE families (I1,I2,I3,I4,I5,I8,I9): DEVELOPMENT/SELECTION SPAN = 2013-01-01..2022-12-31 (strictly precedes the clean span; stricter than clean-only selection). Per cell, unit-risk trade P&L (after costs) -> weekly series (Mon-Sun weeks of the exit trading day, zero-filled) -> dev weekly Sharpe (sqrt 52). ELIGIBLE cell: >= 100 dev trades (>= 60 for I3) AND net > 0 in >= 6 of the 10 calendar years 2013..2022. SELECTED = eligible cell with the highest dev weekly Sharpe (ties: more trades). If no cell is eligible the family is NO and the pair window is not read. Exactly ONE cell is scored in the pair window. RTY joins baskets only from 2017-07.
- Sleeve P&L convention: basket normalisation = 1 ADR-dollar per trade (contracts q = 1/(ADR20_pts x pv), ADR20 = mean prior-20-trading-day 1-minute high-low range; I9 uses ATR20 of the daily bar); costs 1 tick/side x 2 + $4.36 per contract round trip, scaled by q; 2x slippage = 2 ticks/side. Scoring: sleeve scaled by ONE constant so its clean-span weekly sd equals the pair's (T3 convention; 100% = pair-equivalent risk); additive P + s*S at s = 10/20/30/50% (headline 20%); constant-risk (1-s)P + sS with t3_score.verdict (INSURANCE). Day labels: trading day = 18:00 ET to 18:00 ET (daily-bar families I9: 16:00 ET to 16:00 ET as C11).
- GATES at 20% (AUTOPILOT.md 3-7 exactly): uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week not worse than the share (t3_score ww_chg <= 100*s); gate-null N0 = random-entry null (500 draws; random entry times from the family's eligible set ignoring signal thresholds, SAME count K as the real pair-window trades, direction from the rule's own direction function at that time, same exit) standalone all-in pair-window weekly Sharpe p95 < real; bootstrap 90% CI (3,000 weekly resamples, paired) of all-in additive uplift lower bound > 0; circular-shift null reported; plateau: numeric parameters of the selected cell scaled x0.75 and x1.25 one at a time (thresholds rounded to 2 decimals, integers to the nearest integer >= 1, neighbours that round to the same value skipped, categorical parameters fixed): >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of the selected cell's all-in 20% uplift; 2x slippage keeps uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst-decile-week correlation with the pair <= +0.30; NT8 spec + margin in the report; 'small-sample' flag < 100 trades in the pair window with the 2013-2026 per-year table beside it. Selection-aware null (REPORT ONLY): 40 replicates of the whole grid selection on dev with random-entry events (same counts) -> rank of the real selected dev Sharpe. Insurance metric set (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg weeks) clean/H1/H2/all for pair, 20% and 30% additive and constant-risk.
- PAIR-SIDE families (B15, I6, I7, I10): variants of B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) re-simulated with B.run (18 s per run). Selection on CLEAN only: highest clean weekly Sharpe of the pair among the grid cells that change >= 5% of clean entries (or lock days for B15); ONE cell carried; H1/H2 read once. Gates (pair change): weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week (all-in) not worse than baseline; matched family null (below, 300 draws), p95 of all-in Sharpe uplift < real; bootstrap 90% CI (paired weekly, 5,000) of all-in uplift > 0; plateau: numeric params x0.75/x1.25 one at a time, >= 75% keep >= 75% of all-in uplift; 2x slippage (SLIP_PT 1.0 in baseline and variant) keeps uplift >= +0.10 each span; NT8 spec + margin; verdict SURVIVES/INSURANCE by the D1-harness formula (rep_common.verdict_fn: H2 net >= +5% and H2 DD <= -10%, H1 net >= 90%, clean net >= 95% ...; INSURANCE = cost >= -5% and CVaR5/worst week +25% or DD +20%).
- Fill models: stated per family. NinjaTrader Brokerage via Rithmic for all CME products (IBKR not needed).
### B15 (= Q-1 B15-LOCKRE) POST-LOCK REDUCED-SIZE CONTINUATION. Mechanism: D3 S2: the combined day lock costs money on trend days (H1 -$422k) because plain continued profitably after the lock minute; keeping a fraction of risk on after the lock retains optionality. Prior 8%.
- Implementation: B.lock_stage re-implemented (ap_i_pair.lock_stage2) with an `after` option: when the day lock fires at minute tl, positions open at tl are flattened as live (pts at next open), and entries with EI > tl keep size factor m (instead of 0) for eligible books; no second lock the same day; week floor stage unchanged afterwards. Reproduction test: after=None must equal B.run exactly (max |diff| = 0) - run BEFORE the grid.
- Grid (8 cells): m in {0.25, 0.5} x books {both, HM only} x lock kind restored {all locks, give-back locks only (cap locks flat as live)}.
- Null (matched, 300 draws): keep the REAL number of locked days but replace each lock minute by a uniformly random minute inside that day's active grid; compute uplift of (cell vs flat-after-lock) under the same random lock minutes; the real uplift must exceed the p95 of this random-timing uplift distribution. Plateau: m x0.75/x1.25 (0.1875/0.3125 or 0.375/0.625).
### I1 SUNDAY WEEKEND-GAP BASKET. Mechanism: Sunday-open liquidity is thin; gap vs Friday close overshoots (fade) or is informative (follow); stream is uncorrelated with the pair's weekdays. Prior 5%.
- Instruments (12): ES, NQ, YM, RTY, 6E, 6J, 6B, 6A, 6C, GC, CL, ZN (1-minute cache ap_i_load). Week-open bar = first bar after a break >= 30 h whose ET weekday is Sunday; G = open(week-open bar) - close(last bar before); gz = |G| / ADR20 (prior 20 trading days, adjusted pts). Trade if gz >= thr; ENTRY at the open of the SECOND bar of the week (not the first print); direction fade = -sign(G), follow = +sign(G); EXIT at the open of the first bar >= entry + H minutes; no stop.
- Grid (18 cells): thr {0.10, 0.20, 0.40} x mode {fade, follow} x H {60, 240, 720} min. Null N0: for each instrument random weekends (any gap size >0) with the same per-instrument count, rule direction.
- Executability: Sunday 18:00 ET entry; NT8 strategy on 12 instruments with a calendar trigger; micros (MES, MNQ, M6E...) for sizing; margin ~ sum of initial margins of open legs.
### I2 RTH OPENING GAP SLEEVE. Mechanism: overnight gap vs prior RTH close fills or extends; conditional on size/ADR. Prior 5%.
- Instruments ES, NQ, YM, RTY. G = open(09:30 ET bar) - close(15:59 ET bar of the previous RTH session); gz = |G|/ADR20; trade if gz >= thr; ENTRY at the open of the 09:31 bar; fade = -sign(G), follow = +sign(G); EXIT at the open of the first bar >= 09:31 + H minutes, forced no later than the 15:55 ET bar open.
- Grid (18 cells): thr {0.10, 0.25, 0.50} x mode {fade, follow} x H {30, 120, 360} min. N0: random RTH days (any gap), same per-instrument count, rule direction.
### I3 FOMC / NFP DRIFT (ES+NQ, each 50% of unit risk). Mechanism: documented pre-announcement drift; post-release continuation/reversal of the first 5 minutes. Prior 3%; small-sample certain in the pair window (about 25 FOMC and 36 NFP events).
- Event dates: outputs of ap_c2_g3_events (FOMC_V + FOMC_M, NFP_V verified 2023-26, nfp_rule_dates for 2013-22 UNVERIFIED rule, caveat stated).
- Pre-FOMC cells (4): long from entry E in {D-1 14:00 ET, D 00:00 ET, D 02:00 ET, D 09:30 ET} to exit at the open of the 13:55 ET bar of FOMC day D (D-1 = previous weekday).
- Post-event cells (12): event {FOMC 14:00 ET, NFP 08:30 ET} x mode {follow, fade} x H {30, 60, close}: r = close of the bar opened at T+4 min minus close of the bar opened at T-1 min (skip r=0); ENTRY at the open of the bar at T+5 min; follow=sign(r), fade=-sign(r); EXIT at the open of the first bar >= entry+H (H=close: first bar >= 16:00 ET). Eligibility threshold for dev: >= 60 dev trades. N0: random trading days at the same clock time (same count), rule direction (for pre cells: random days, long).
### I4 VOLUME-SURGE 5-MIN CONTINUATION/REVERSAL (ES, NQ, YM, RTY basket). Mechanism: abnormal volume with a large bar = information (continue) or liquidity shock (revert). Prior 4%.
- 5-min bars aggregated from 1-minute (epoch/5-minute buckets, ET slot-of-day). rvol = bar volume / median volume of the same slot over the previous 20 trading days (>= 10 obs); r = (close-open) / mean(high-low) of the prior 78 5-min bars (>= 50 obs); signal at bar close if rvol >= R and |r| >= K; ENTRY at the next 5-min bar's open, allowed only if that bar starts in [03:00, 15:30) ET; direction follow = sign(r), fade = -sign(r); EXIT at the open of the 5-min bar H bars later (forced no later than the 16:55 ET open); one position per instrument at a time (signals during an open trade skipped).
- Grid (36 cells): R {2, 3, 5} x K {1, 2} x mode {follow, fade} x H {3, 6, 12} bars. N0: random eligible 5-min bars (same count per instrument), rule direction = mode x sign(r) of that bar.
### I5 ES-NQ 1-MINUTE LEAD-LAG CATCH-UP. Mechanism: relative-return dislocation between ES and NQ closes within minutes. Prior 2%.
- Inner-join ES and NQ 1-minute closes. L(t) = ret_ES(m) - ret_NQ(m) with ret = c_t/c_{t-m} - 1 (adjusted closes), z = L / std(L over the prior 390 bars). Signal if |z| >= thr and bar start in [09:35, 15:45) ET; target instrument T: NQ -> trade NQ in sign(L) direction; ES -> trade ES in -sign(L) direction; ENTRY at the next bar open; EXIT at the open h minutes later; one position per target.
- Grid (54 cells): m {1, 3, 5} x thr {2, 3, 4} x h {2, 5, 10} x T {NQ, ES}. N0: random RTH 1-minute bars (same count), direction by sign(L) rule at that bar.
### I6 WALK-FORWARD HOUR/DOW SIZE TILT OF THE PAIR. Prior 2%.
- At the start of each calendar month recompute, from UNRULED raw rows (f=1, net_of(PTS0, 1.0)) that EXITED before the month start (expanding window), the mean net per trade of each bucket; bucket keys: hour = entry hour ET (0-23), dow = weekday of lab_in, hourbook = hour x book; buckets with < 30 rows are ineligible; if < 3 eligible buckets all sizes 1. The worst q of eligible buckets (ceil(q*n_eligible), min 1) get multiplier m for entries that month. Implemented as Cfg.sizefn.
- Grid (12 cells): key {hour, dow, hourbook} x q {0.20, 0.33} x m {0.5, 0}. Null: each month cut the SAME NUMBER of eligible buckets chosen at random (300 draws). Plateau: q x0.75/x1.25 (0.15/0.25, 0.25/0.41), m x0.75/x1.25 (0.375/0.625; m=0 -> 0.25 only).
### I7 WALK-FORWARD SIDE / BOOK x SIDE SIZE TILT. Prior 3%.
- At each month start, per bucket the mean unruled net per trade of rows exited in the trailing W weeks before the month (W in {13, 26}); bucket with n >= 30 and a NEGATIVE mean gets multiplier m for that month's entries; keys side (2) or book x side (4).
- Grid (8 cells): key {side, bookside} x W {13, 26} x m {0.5, 0}. Null: each month cut the same number of buckets at random among the eligible (300 draws). Plateau: W x0.75/x1.25 (10/16, 20/33 weeks), m 0.375/0.625.
### I8 POST-EXIT RE-ENTRY BOOK (NQ). Mechanism: after a 60-pt stop-out or 60-pt target hit price continues or reverses; lever table (win rate / avg loss). Prior 4%.
- Trigger events: exits of the pair's ST and HM signal books reproduced on the 1-minute NQ long series with the fixed 60/60 bracket (p1_core.trades(a, None, fixed=60), independent books, no locks, 2013-2026; stop = pts <= -59.5, target = pts >= 59.5). RE-ENTRY at the open of the bar after the exit bar, only if that bar is not in the pair's blocked entry window, same trading day, flat by 16:55 ET; one re-entry open at a time (events while open skipped); 8 NQ lots then scaled.
- Cells (16): case {A: after STOP, reverse (enter opposite to the stopped trade); B: after STOP, same side; C: after TARGET, same side (continue); D: after TARGET, opposite side (fade)} x exit {bracket 30/30, bracket 60/60, time 30 min, time 60 min}. N0: re-entries at random trade exits of ANY kind (same count), side by the case rule relative to the original trade's side.
### I9 IBS / CONSECUTIVE-DOWN-DAYS REVERSAL (ES, NQ, YM, RTY daily, C11 16:00-ET daily bars). Prior 4%.
- Signal at the close of day t with C_t > SMA200: IBS = (C-L)/(H-L) < theta, theta {0.10, 0.20} ('ibs'); or N consecutive lower closes N {3, 4} ('down'). ENTRY at the close of t (MOC fiction as T4 B; next-open sensitivity reported); stop at C_t - 3 x ATR20 (fill at stop or the open if gapped, minus 1 tick); EXIT at the first close above the prior day's high ('hi'), or after 2 days ('d2'), or after 5 days ('d5').
- Grid (12 cells): signal {ibs0.10, ibs0.20, down3, down4} x exit {hi, d2, d5}. N0: random days (same count per instrument), long, same exit.
### I10 PRIOR-WEEK-CONDITIONED PAIR SIZE. Prior 3%.
- Week signal = net of the previous ISO week (by lab_out) of the baseline live run (causal). Entries of week w sized (1+a) if prev week net < 0, (1-b) if prev week net > 0, 1 for the first week. Cells (5): (a,b) in {(0.25,0),(0.5,0),(0,0.25),(0,0.5),(0.25,0.25)}. Null: random permutation of the realised week-multiplier sequence across weeks (300 draws). Plateau: a,b x0.75/x1.25. Worst-week gate literal (not worse than baseline).
### Report-only statistics across batch I: false-discovery count (families run x chance-pass probability from own null), reported in MORNING.md by the lead.

- Y9: each day z_n = (c_t - c_{t-n})/(ATR20 x sqrt n) across the 26 markets; long the 4 lowest and short the 4 highest (reversal) or the opposite (momentum); n {1,5}; hold {1,3,5} = 12 cells.

## WT2 follow-up (registered 2026-10-16 by the WT worker on the coordinator's instruction, BEFORE any WT2 run; no gate moves, no new tuning)
Cell under test is fixed: C* = ATR 14 / mult 3.0 / EMA off / 10-min signal bars / X1 60-60 bracket (NQ, 8 lots, 1-s engine).
1. Plateau, two definitions. D1 one-at-a-time: ATR 10.5->11 and 17.5->18 (rounded: 10/11 tie -> 11, 18), mult 2.25/3.75, bracket 45/75, tf 7/13 (one parameter moved, others at C*): 8 neighbours. D2 = D1 plus all 2-parameter corners (6 parameter pairs x 4 sign combos = 24 corners; 32 cells total). Metric: all-in additive Sharpe uplift at 20% share (sleeve scaled to pair clean vol, sleeve with its own rules); keep = uplift >= 75% of C*'s. Report fraction under D1 and D2 (gate threshold 75% for each; D1 is the AUTOPILOT definition).
2. Separate-account sleeve with its own rules: sleeve = WT alone in its own account with per-book 5R/-2R, combined day lock (cap 8R, arm 2R, keep 33%) and week floor, all defined in the sleeve's R (R = bracket x contracts x $20) so they scale with the share (floor = 8 R_s). Variants: floor 8R_s (live-style), 4R_s (tight). Shares 10/20/30/50%; sleeve contracts = share-scaled (scale to pair clean weekly vol). Report additive uplift on clean/H1/H2, worst week of pair+sleeve vs the pair's (gate: worse by no more than the share) and the sleeve's own worst week in dollars and as a share of the pair's worst week.
3. Era characterisation of C* by year 2013-2026 (1-min engine, fixed 60 and scaled a): trades/day, net R, Sharpe, median price, median ATR(14) of 10-min bars in points and % of price, bracket / ATR ratio, win rate, share of time-stop/force exits. Replication on unseen data (pre-registered): same spec on ES and RTY (second instruments, 1-min series data/databento, 2023-02..2026-10, 1-min engine of d1_f4_core with bracket = k x ADR20 where k = 60 / median ADR20(NQ, clean) = 0.2109, equal R $9,600, 1 tick/side + $4.36/contract), cells ATR {10,14,18} x tf {8,10,12} x mult 3.0 x EMA off x X1 (9 cells per instrument, no selection: all reported), sleeve scaled to the pair clean vol, additive uplift at 20% on clean/H1/H2 and all-in. Also the NQ cell in the same 1-min engine with ADR bracket as comparator. Success criterion for replication: median uplift of the 9 cells > 0 on all three spans for BOTH ES and RTY. Note: this is not virgin data for the period (same regime) but is untouched by the selection; 2013-2022 for ES via data/autopilot/ohlcv1m is NOT used here.
4. Selection-aware accounting: 60 timing-destroyed replicates of the whole 432-cell grid over the full pair window (random entries, same trade count and hold distribution per cell); per replicate count cells with uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1 and H2 at 20% (G3 uplift gate) and the number among them with clean-uplift-rank in the top 10; expected false passes per family = mean; compare with the real family (number of real cells passing G3 uplift).
5. B1-style WT on other instruments: REPORT_B1.md already covers WT_MODAL signals with ATR-trail exits on ES/RTY/GC/CL/ZN (LEAD count there is from ST/HM, WT cells NO/diag); not repeated.
- T1b AMENDMENT (2026-10-16T15:12Z): T1 dev screen (2013-22 only; pair window untouched) showed the fade cells at thr 0.25 with Sharpe 0.49-0.59 and 7-8 of 10 positive years but only 164-170 trades (6 markets, ~17 confirmed weekend gaps a year), below the 300-trade floor. The floor is lowered to 150 trades for T1 ONLY (sample-size floor for a calendar-sparse family, as for V2/V8/T5), same >= 7/10 years and top-3 rules, reported as 'T1b' and flagged small-sample (< 100 trades in the pair window possible). The pair window has not been read for any T1 cell. The 18 T1 cells count toward false discovery either way.

## B2356 BATCH S1-S10 (registered 2026-10-16T15:15Z before any S run). Protocol/engine/fill/gates/selection as Y/Z/W/V/T (dev+selection 2013-22: >= 300 trades [S7, S8, S9: >= 60, calendar-sparse; flagged small-sample], >= 7/10 years, top 3; clean/H1/H2 read once; own random-entry null; bootstrap; plateau; 2x slippage). 30-min families path-resolved, markets = the 16 with 30-min series, size $7,000 per ATR20 (label-day ATR through the prior day), exit at the open of a named bar.
- S1: class open bar o_slot (EQ 570, RATES/MET/FX 480, EN 540 min since midnight ET); z0 = (close - open)/ATR20 of that bar; |z0| >= 0.15; follow or fade; entry next bar open; exit after 4 bars or at the last-bar open; classes EQ(NQ ES RTY YM) RATES(ZN ZB) MET(GC SI HG) EN(CL NG RB HO) FX(6E 6J 6B); 5 x 2 x 2 = 20 cells.
- S2: Asia range = high/low of 18:00-03:00 ET bars; first touch of the range by a 03:00-09:00 ET bar opens a trade at the level (both in a bar -> side nearer the bar open); stop = opposite extreme or 1 ATR20; exit at the open of the 08:00 bar, 12:00 bar or the last bar; markets 6E 6J 6B GC SI CL ES NQ ZN pooled; 3 x 2 = 6 cells.
- S3: skew of the last W {20,60} daily close changes; skew <= -thr long, >= +thr short, thr {0.75,1.25}; entry next open; hold {3,5}; universe {ALL26,SCR}: 16 cells.
- S4: carry_t = ln(F_v0/F_v1) annualised by contract spacing (C1-F3 loader, valid-day rule); signal = carry_t - carry_(t-L), L {10,20,40}; cross-sectional rank among valid markets, long top 4 / short bottom 4; hold {5,10}: 6 cells; adjusted-price P&L, equal ATR20 risk per position.
- S5: close > max(high of previous N) with volume_t < v x mean volume of previous 20 days -> short; close < min(low) and volume < v x mean -> long; N {20,60}, v {0.8,1.0}; hold {3,5}; universe {ALL26,SCR}: 16 cells.
- S6: ES Friday-label-day z = (close of last bar - close of Thursday's last bar)/ATR20; |z| >= thr {0.5,1.0}; response ZN ZB GC 6J goes the opposite way of the ES move (long after ES falls); entry at the open of Friday's last bar, exit at the open of the second bar of the next label day; 2 x 4 = 8 cells.
- S7: last label day of each month; entry at the 14:00 or 15:00 ET bar open, exit at the last-bar open; m = (close of the bar before entry - close of the previous month's last label day)/(ATR20 x sqrt 21); |m| >= thr {0.5,1.0}; fade or follow m; scope all month-ends or quarter-ends only; NQ ES RTY pooled; 2 x 2 x 2 x 2 = 16 cells.
- S8: opex = third Friday of each month; windows: opex week (entry open of Monday, exit close of Friday), opex Friday only, Monday after (open->close); long ES NQ RTY YM: 12 cells.
- S9: roll date = first day of a new front contract in the volume-front series (instrument_id change); window = the 5 trading days before the roll day (entry open of day -5, exit close of day -1); ES NQ RTY YM ZN; long or short: 10 cells.
- S10: Monday candle z = (c-o)/ATR20 (Monday UTC bar); |z| >= 0.5; Tuesday fade or follow, entry Tuesday open, exit Tuesday close; classes EQ RATES MET EN FX: 10 cells.
- WT2 addition (registered before any WT2 run, same date): (A) WT-10 = C* scored as a SEPARATE ACCOUNT exactly as run: own per-book 5R/-2R day rule at WT's R (R = 60 pt x contracts x $20), own day lock (cap 8R / arm 2R / give-back 67%), own week floor -8R in its own R, no netting; sizes {2,3,4,6} NQ (rules and costs scale linearly with contracts, so size n = n/8 of the 8-lot sleeve run) added to the pair at 8/8 and at 6/6 (pair 6/6 = B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) with ST/HM bookscale 0.75). Report two-account total: net, weekly Sharpe, maxDD, worst week, CVaR5/10, Sortino, MAR, %neg weeks on clean/H1/H2/all, worst-decile correlation of the sleeve with the pair, margin (per-contract margin is not on the box: reported as added contracts and gross exposure; any $ margin figure is an assumption). (B) Entry timing: harness = next 1-min open after the 10-min bar closes; NT8 on a 10-min chart with on-bar-close logic fills the market order at the first tick after the bar close = the same instant (open of the next 1-min bar; the 10-min bar close price). Variant run: entry at the signal bar's last 1-min CLOSE instead of the next open (pts adjusted by side*(open_next - close_signal), bracket levels not shifted; approximation) to measure the sensitivity. (C) Exact +-25% plateau: D1/D2 above, sleeve rules ON (already the default in G.sleeve). (D) ES/RTY replication as item 3. (E) 2013-2022 per-year table at scaling a for C* beside the pair (p1 run_a_real) and the correlation of yearly and weekly results (does WT's bad years = pair's bad years).

## B2356 BATCH R1-R10 (registered 2026-10-16T15:22Z before any R run). Protocol/engine/fill/gates/selection as Y/Z/W/V/T/S (dev+selection 2013-22: >= 300 trades, >= 7/10 years, top 3; pair window read once; random-entry null; bootstrap; plateau; 2x slippage). R9/R10 additionally report 2013-2026 Sharpe. 30-min families: 16 markets (NQ ES RTY YM ZN ZB GC SI HG CL NG RB HO 6E 6J 6B), bar ATR14 sizing ($7,000 per ATR), signal at bar close, entry next bar open, exit at close of bar t+hold.
- R1: kurtosis (excess, pandas) of the last W {20,60} daily close changes >= k {3,6}: fade the last day's move (long after a down day), entry next open, hold {1,3}; universe {ALL26,SCR}: 16 cells.
- R2: S2 geometry (Asia range 18:00-03:00 ET, first touch 03:00-09:00 ET) with exit at the open of the 08:00 bar of label day k+1 or k+2 (if absent: last bar of that day); stop = opposite extreme, 1 ATR20 or 2 ATR20; markets 6E 6J 6B GC SI CL ES NQ ZN: 2 x 3 = 6 cells.
- R3: bar high > max high of previous N {20,40} bars and close < that max -> short (mirror long); hold {2,4,8}: 6 cells. R4: skew of last W {40,120} bar changes beyond +-thr {0.75,1.25} contrarian; hold {2,4,8}: 12 cells. R5: N {3,4} consecutive same-direction bar closes, fade or follow, hold {2,4,8}: 12 cells. R6: ATR5/ATR20(bars) <= r {0.6,0.8}, direction sign(c_t - c_{t-L}), L {20,60}, hold {2,4,8}: 12 cells. R7: outside bar (high>prev high and low<prev low), close location >= 0.7 / <= 0.3, follow or fade, hold {2,4,8}: 6 cells. R8: bar open above prior bar high with bullish bar -> +1 (mirror), follow or fade, hold {2,4,8}: 6 cells.
- R9: z_L = (c_t - c_{t-L})/(ATR20 x sqrt L) at the last trading day of each month, L {10,15,20}; TS = sign(z_L) per market; XS = long top / short bottom 5 (ALL26) or 3 (macro12 = ES NQ RTY ZN ZB GC CL 6E 6J 6B 6A HG); entry next open, hold 19 days; 3 x 2 x 2 = 12 cells. R10 = the same with the opposite sign (reversal): 12 cells.

## B2356 BATCH Q1-Q10 (registered 2026-10-16T15:28Z before any Q run). Protocol/engine/fill/gates/selection as before (dev+selection 2013-22: >= 300 trades, >= 7/10 years, top 3; pair window read once; random-entry null; bootstrap; plateau; 2x slippage); panel families also report all cells' 2013-2026 Sharpe. Signal at the last trading day of the month (hold 19 days) or week (hold 4), entry next open, ATR20-equal-risk legs. Value v (long the highest): mom z=(c_t-c_{t-L})/(ATR20 sqrt L); hi52 = -(max high of 252 days - c_t)/ATR20; skew = -skew(L daily changes); lowvol = -ATR20/raw close; volcmp = -ATR5/ATR60. mode 'as' or 'rev' (reversed sign). Universes: ALL26 (k=5), MACRO12 = ES NQ RTY ZN ZB GC CL 6E 6J 6B 6A HG (k=3), classes EQ4 (k=1), RATES4 (k=1), FX5 (k=2), COM13 (k=3).
- Q1: ONE book of 5 components (listed above), weights 1/(2013-22 weekly vol), null = every component replaced by its own random-entry replicate (105 draws), gate on pair-window Sharpe vs p95 AND p(1-0.05/5); additive/bootstrap/wd corr/drop-one plateau/2x slippage as U2.
- Q2: z of each class = mean z_L of members (L {20,60}); long all members of the top class, short all of the bottom class; mode as/rev; monthly: 4 cells.
- Q3: mom, L {63,126,252}, TS (sign) or XS, ALL26 or MACRO12, monthly: 12 cells.
- Q4: mom, L {10,20}, XS, weekly (hold 4), mode {as,rev}, ALL26 or MACRO12: 8 cells.
- Q5: mom XS monthly MACRO12, L {15,20,25,30} x k {2,3,4}: 12 cells.
- Q6: volcmp, monthly, mode {as,rev}, ALL26/MACRO12: 4 cells.
- Q7: hi52, mode {as,rev}, rebalance {monthly, weekly}, ALL26/MACRO12: 8 cells.
- Q8: skew L {60,120}, mode {as,rev}, ALL26/MACRO12, monthly: 8 cells.
- Q9: lowvol, mode {as,rev}, ALL26/MACRO12, monthly, ATR window 20: 4 cells.
- Q10: mom XS monthly within class (EQ4, RATES4, FX5, COM13), L {10,20,40}: 12 cells.

## B2356 BATCH N1-N7 (registered 2026-10-16T15:38Z before any N run; GATE AMENDMENT 2: SEL=clean). Selection on CLEAN only (eligible = Sharpe > 0 in both clean halves and >= 40 pair-window trades; top 3 by clean weekly Sharpe), pair window read once, standard pipeline, ERA flag. Fill model/gates as before; 30-min families path-resolved with exits at named bar opens; sizes $7,000 per ATR20.
- N1: ES NQ RTY YM; z = (close-open)/ATR20 of the bar starting 15:30 ET; |z| >= thr {0, 0.15, 0.3}; follow or fade; entry at the open of the 16:00 bar, exit at the open of the 16:30 bar: 6 cells.
- N2: events FOMC (14:00 bar), NFP and CPI (08:30 bar) (dates: ap_c2_g3_events lists; FOMC 2013-22 and NFP 2013-22 per the C9-R1 convention, CPI 2023+ only); z of the event bar; follow or fade; entry next bar open; exit at the last-bar open; ES NQ ZN GC 6E pooled; |z| >= 0.1; 3 x 2 = 6 cells.
- N3: VX F1/F2 = the two nearest-month VX contracts alive on the date (VX/Xy-style symbols in data/databento/B10/vx_1d.dbn.zst, 2018-11+); slope = F2/F1 - 1; signal day slope < 0 or < 0.03; long ES, NQ or RTY at the next open; hold 1 or 5: 2 x 3 x 2 = 12 cells.
- N4: driver 6J, 6A or HG: sign of the L {5,10}-day change times s {+1,-1}; response = long/short ES and NQ (equal risk) at the next open, hold 5: 3 x 2 x 2 = 12 cells.
- N5: x = L {10,20}-day pct change of HG minus that of GC; rising x -> long ES and short ZN, falling -> reverse; hold 5; response ES or ZN or both: L x {ES, ZN, both} = 6 cells.
- N6: overnight range = 18:00-09:30 ET bars' high/low; p = (close of the 09:30 bar - low)/(high - low); p >= 1-q or <= q with q {0.15, 0.25}: follow or fade; entry open of the 10:00 bar; exit after 4 bars or at the last-bar open; ES NQ RTY YM: 2 x 2 x 2 = 8 cells.
- N7: Friday close position p in the ISO week's high-low range; p >= 1-q or <= q (q {0.1, 0.2}) -> follow or fade for the next 5 days (entry Monday open); universe {ALL26, SCR}: 8 cells.

## CYCLE 12 RE-REGISTRATION UNDER GATE AMENDMENT 2 (ERA), 2026-10-16T16:20Z, before any clean-only run. SUPERSEDES the selection rule of CYCLE 12 B-FAMILIES and of FU1-FU5 above (grids, fill model, costs, gates other than selection UNCHANGED).
Families screened out on a 2013-22 dev screen and therefore RE-RUN with clean-only selection: B1 (ES, RTY, GC, CL, ZN, BTC, ETH), B4 (ES, RTY, GC, CL, ZN), B7 (ES, RTY, GC, CL, ZN; ZN legacy run unfinished), B10 (ES, RTY, GC, CL, ZN), B11 (ES, RTY, GC, CL, ZN; never ran under the old rule). The legacy 2013-22-selected results (REPORT_B1, REPORT_B4 first versions, B7/B10 partial) are kept as `*_legacy2013` and are superseded.
- Selection ONLY on clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31: a cell qualifies if it has >= 100 trades in clean and positive weekly Sharpe in BOTH clean halves (A 2023-08..2024-07, B 2024-08..2025-07); top 10 per instrument (B10: 5 per window) by clean weekly Sharpe; if fewer qualify, up to 3 best failing cells are read as DIAGNOSTIC (cannot be FOUND). H1 (2025-08-01..2026-02-06) and H2 (2026-02-07..2026-10-13) read once afterwards. UNTOUCHED never. The sleeve scale factor (vol-matched families) uses the selected cell's clean weekly sigma.
- False-survivor test: 200 (FU: 100) timing-destroyed replicates of the whole grid re-running the clean-only rule; statistics = number of qualifying cells and mean clean Sharpe of the top 10 (p(count), p(top)). Because the clean span is also the pair-uplift span, the clean uplift is in-sample; the gating evidence is H1 and H2 (read once), the replicate test, the bootstrap, and the out-of-selection standalone Sharpe (H1+H2 combined) against its own random-entry null p95 (reported as OOS null). The all-in standalone null of the previous protocol is still computed and gated as before.
- ERA FLAG for every scored cell: pre-2023 (before 2023-08; RTY from 2017-07, BTC/ETH from 2022-01) weekly Sharpe and per-trade decomposition: gross per trade (no slippage, no commission), net at the fixed cost model, net at a price-scaled cost model (per-trade cost multiplied by raw entry price / median raw price in clean). Flag = 'positive pre-2023' (net fixed Sharpe > 0), 'gross-positive, cost-negative pre-2023', or 'negative pre-2023'. Reported only, not gating.
- Replication for FOUND (>= 2 of 4 must hold with the same sign and >= 50% of the uplift): (a) live weeks - none exist for these sleeves (N/A); (b) H0 2023-02..07 standalone sleeve Sharpe same sign (the pair has no H0 rows in the scoring axis, so only standalone is available; counts as replication only if the standalone Sharpe is > 0 and the cell trades >= 30 times); (c) cross-instrument: the identical cell run on ES and RTY over 2023-08..2026-10 (vol-matched, additive 20% vs the pair): uplift positive on both and >= 50% of the cell's own all-in uplift (skipped if the cell's own instrument is ES or RTY: then the other of the two); (d) alternating months: the cell's all-in additive uplift (20%) computed on odd calendar months only and on even calendar months only (2023-08..2026-10 days, months by day label) must both be positive and each >= 50% of the full uplift. Evaluated for leads only (cells passing gate 3 on H1 and H2).

### C11-DIP ERA re-issue replication tests (registered 2026-10-16 under GATE AMENDMENT 2, BEFORE running them; single registered cell ES dip_rsi5_days5_stop3, no re-selection)
Uplift = change in pair weekly Sharpe from adding the sleeve at 20% share, sleeve scaled by the same factor as the main run (ES: 30.3 contract-equivalents at 100%; other instruments scaled to the pair's clean weekly sigma by the same rule). Reference = the all-in 2023-08..2026-10 ES uplift (+0.37). A test HOLDS if the uplift has the same sign (positive) and is >= 50% of the reference (>= +0.18).
- RA newest live weeks: the last 8 weeks of data (2026-08-17..2026-10-13) - HOLDS only if the sleeve has >= 3 trades there; otherwise 'not testable'.
- RB H0 2023-02-09..2023-07-31: same rule on the same bars, uplift vs the pair in H0 (pair rows exist from 2023-02-10).
- RC cross-instrument 2023-08..2026-10: the same registered rule (RSI(2)<5, SMA200, 5 bars, 3xATR20) on NQ, RTY, YM, each scaled to the pair's clean sigma; HOLDS if >= 2 of 3 hold individually.
- RD alternating-month split: the single cell is read separately on odd and even calendar months of 2023-08..2026-10 (sleeve and pair daily P&L masked to the month parity, weekly Sharpe on the masked series); with one pre-registered cell there is nothing to select, so each half is a read of the same cell; HOLDS if both halves are positive and each >= 50% of the reference.
- ERA FLAG: 2013-2022 reported with gross per trade, net at fixed cost (1 tick/side + $4.36 per contract), and net at price-scaled cost (costs multiplied by ES entry price / mean ES close of 2023-08..2026-10); flag by the sign of those: 'positive pre-2023', 'gross-positive, cost-negative pre-2023', 'negative pre-2023'.
- FOUND needs >= 2 of RA-RD to hold.

## BASKET (COVERAGE ADDITION A) - cash basket (QQQ + top-10 NDX) lead-lag, breadth divergence, basket-flow conditioning. Registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any BASKET analysis run (only the purchase and file-format inspection were done).
- Data (logged in SPEND.json family BASKET): XNAS.ITCH ohlcv-1m 2022-01-01..2026-10-14 for AAPL MSFT NVDA AMZN META GOOGL AVGO TSLA COST NFLX QQQ PEP ADBE CSCO CMCSA AMD TMUS (+FB for the pre-rename months), DBEQ.BASIC and EQUS.MINI for QQQ+the ten from 2023-03-28 as a consolidated-volume cross-check. ITCH = Nasdaq-venue prints only (understates volume; prices fine). Cash window 09:30-16:00 ET only (bar label = open minute; 390 bars/day). A missing equity minute = return 0.
- Top-10 rule: top ten NDX members by market cap at the PRIOR year-end close, fixed for the calendar year (no lookahead), Alphabet counted once (GOOGL); caps are approximate from public year-end prices x shares (state in report). 2022: AAPL MSFT GOOGL AMZN TSLA META NVDA AVGO ADBE NFLX; 2023: AAPL MSFT GOOGL AMZN TSLA NVDA META PEP AVGO COST; 2024: AAPL MSFT GOOGL AMZN NVDA META TSLA AVGO COST ADBE; 2025: AAPL NVDA MSFT AMZN GOOGL META TSLA AVGO COST NFLX; 2026: NVDA AAPL MSFT GOOGL AMZN AVGO META TSLA NFLX COST. Weights: year-start approximate market caps renormalised within the ten, fixed all year. Baskets: CAP10 (headline), QQQ (own 1-min return), EQ10 (equal weight).
- NQ: raw 1-minute NQ (data/autopilot/ohlcv1m, continuous .v.0, difference-adjusted for P&L as everywhere in the I-series); costs 1 tick/side + $4.36/contract round trip; 2x slippage test. Signal known at the close of bar m (time m+1); entry at the NEXT NQ bar open, exit at the open H minutes later; one position at a time; entries 09:35-15:50 ET.
- Basket leg = signal only (no equity trading). Era: modern only (data starts 2022); ERA FLAG uses 2022 as the "pre-2023" span (reported, not gating).

### BASKET-1 LEAD-LAG (sleeve). Mechanism: Nasdaq-100 index arbitrage - cash constituents price information first in the first minutes; NQ catches up. Prior 4% (NQ is the price-discovery leader; any cash lead is eaten by 1 tick + commission).
- Diagnostic (reported, not gating): per year and basket, correlation and OLS slope of NQ return over t+1..t+h (h = 1,2,3,5 min) on (a) basket 1-min return at t, (b) basket-minus-NQ cumulative return over the last k min; and the same with the roles reversed (NQ at t -> basket t+1..t+h) to show who leads. Gross points per trade before cost.
- Sleeve: dev_k(m) = sum over the last k bars of (basket return - NQ return); z = dev_k / (sqrt(k) * mean daily sd of the 1-minute (basket - NQ) return over the prior 20 trading days, fixed through the day); enter in the sign(dev) direction (NQ catches up) when |z| >= thr. Grid (144 cells): basket {CAP10, QQQ, EQ10} x k {1,2,3,5} x thr {1.5, 2, 3} x H {1,2,3,5 min}. Selection: clean 2023-08..2025-07 only, eligible = weekly Sharpe > 0 in BOTH clean halves and >= 100 clean trades; best clean weekly Sharpe carried. H1/H2 read once. Null: random-entry (same count as pair-window trades, random RTH bars in the entry window with direction = sign(dev) at that bar, same H), 500 draws; also a selection-aware null (30 replicates of the full grid selection). Bootstrap 90% CI of all-in additive uplift (3000), plateau (+-25% of numeric parameters, rounded; k/H adjacent), 2x slippage, worst-decile corr <= +0.30, circular-shift reported.
- Both framings (additive 10/20/30/50%, constant-risk), insurance metric set, scale = pair clean weekly sd (T3 sleeve_full pipeline).

### BASKET-2 BREADTH DIVERGENCE FADE (sleeve). Mechanism: a NQ 30-min extreme not confirmed by the constituents is driven by NQ-only (futures/hedging) flow and mean-reverts. Prior 4%.
- Event: NQ close at bar m is a new 30-min high (above the max of the previous 30 closes; low symmetric); confirm_i = constituent i also closes at a new 30-min high at m (STRICT) or within 0.05% of its 30-min rolling high (NEAR). Divergence = confirmers among the ten < n. Fade: short on a divergent high, long on a divergent low, next-open entry, exit H min later. Entry window 10:00-15:30 ET. Grid (16 cells): n {3,4} x confirm {STRICT, NEAR} x H {10,20,30,60}. No stops (time exit). Same selection rule, MINC 60 clean trades (events are rare), null = random bars with direction = fade of sign(NQ 30-min change) at that bar, same H and count; same gates and framings as BASKET-1.

### BASKET-3 SIZE TILT OF THE PAIR'S FLIPS BY BREADTH (pair change; Cfg.sizefn on ROWS_corrected_engine rows, b11_nt8 in a clean process). Mechanism: a flip into a push that cash breadth does not confirm is weaker; flips with confirming breadth are stronger. Prior 3%. Only pair rows whose entry minute lies in the cash session (09:30-15:59 ET) have a state; all other rows multiplier 1.
- State at the entry bar (last completed minute before et_in): AT_EXT = NQ at a 30-min extreme in the direction of the flip (long flip & new 30-min high within the last j min; j = 1 (NOW) or 5 (5M)); DIV = confirmers < thr (thr {3,4}, STRICT confirmation). Modes: CUT0.5 (DIV&AT_EXT -> x0.5), CUT0 (-> x0), WF0.5 (monthly walk-forward: apply x0.5 to the DIV&AT_EXT bucket only if the mean unruled net per trade of such rows that EXITED before the month start, expanding, with >= 20 rows, is negative), BOOST (AT_EXT and confirmers >= 8 -> x1.25). Grid 2 x 2 x 4 = 16 cells. Eligible: >= 2% of clean entries change. Selection = highest clean weekly-Sharpe uplift; gates for a pair change as ap_i_pair (uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, matched null = the same multiplier multiset permuted among the cash-session rows (500 draws) p95, bootstrap, plateau thr/j/m +-25% adjacent, 2x slippage). Constant-risk framing: multipliers renormalised to mean 1 over the clean span, reported for the selected cell.

### BASKET-4 BASKET FLOW PERSISTENCE AS PAIR CONDITIONER (pair change; join with DRIVERS section 5). Mechanism: persistent one-sided cash flow in the heavyweights continues (trend flips work) or dries out (flips fail). Prior 3%.
- Basket flow s_m = sum_i sign(close_i,m - close_i,m-1) x ITCH dollar volume_i,m / sum_i dollar volume_i,m (CAP10 constituents; signed-bar-volume proxy: ohlcv has no aggressor side; ITCH volume is Nasdaq-only). F_W(m) = mean of s over the last W min (W in {15,30,60}); alignment A = flip side x F_W. Threshold theta_q = q-th percentile of |F_W| over the prior 60 trading days at the same W (causal), q in {67, 80}. Modes: CUT_AGAINST (A <= -theta -> x0.5), BOOST_WITH (A >= theta -> x1.25), BOTH. Grid 3 x 2 x 3 = 18 cells; same gates as BASKET-3 (null: multiset permutation within cash-session rows). DRIVERS join: if per-minute NQ/ES flow aggregates exist under outputs/autopilot/drivers/ at run time, the identical cells with NQ own-flow persistence are run as a REPORT-ONLY comparison and the basket-flow vs NQ-flow correlation is reported; otherwise stated as not available.
- Executability (all four): needs a cash-equity real-time feed inside the NT8 chart/strategy (multi-instrument via AddDataSeries). NinjaTrader Brokerage via Rithmic supplies CME futures only, so the basket leg needs a second data provider (e.g. Kinetick/IQFeed/Nasdaq Basic via NT8, extra monthly fee, or a sidecar process writing a signal file) - stated honestly in each report.

## DRIVERS-5 / COVERAGE ADDITION B: NQ FLOW-PERSISTENCE STATE AS A SIZE SWITCH / TILT OF THE PAIR'S FLIPS. Registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any pair-by-state table or switch run (only the market-wide sections 1-3 on per-minute NQ/ES signed volume had been computed; those do not involve pair rows). Prior 4%.
- Mechanism: persistent one-sided aggressor flow (metaorder footprint) is what a trend/flip entry feeds on; flips entered into flow that is persistent and aligned with the flip continue, flips against persistent flow or into one-sided exhaustion fail. If true, sizing by the entry-minute flow state raises the pair's weekly Sharpe. Depends on current microstructure (era flag reported).
- Data: per-minute NQ signed volume (front outright, aggressor side B = buy) s_m = buy - sell contracts, vol_m, from outputs/autopilot/drivers (Databento trades). State at a pair row uses minutes [em-W, em-1] only, em = epoch minute of et_in (causal, last completed minute before entry). Rows: outputs/h2/ROWS_corrected_engine.parquet in b11_nt8 (Cfg.sizefn), baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a CLEAN process (weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30), also vs B.Cfg(). Fill model/slippage as b11: 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots; 2x slippage SLIP_PT 1.0.
- Features (8): PERS_W = |sum s|/sum vol, W in {15,30,60}; ALIGN_W = side*sum s/sum vol, W in {15,30,60}; ALIGNRUN = side*sign(s_{em-1})*min(L,20) with L = length of the same-sign minute run ending at em-1 (zero-s minute breaks the run); AC = lag-1 autocorrelation of s over the 120 minutes before em (>=60 valid). Undefined (sum vol = 0) -> multiplier 1.
- Cells (exactly 256): scope {ALL (thresholds from all past rows), BLOCK (thresholds from past rows in the same ET block: 18:00-03:59, 04:00-09:29, 09:30-10:59, 11:00-13:59, 14:00-16:59)} x [switch: feature (8) x q {0.33, 0.5, 0.67} x reduce-when {HIGH (feature >= expanding q-quantile of past rows' feature), LOW (<)} x level {0.5, 0.0} = 96 ; tilt: feature (8) x amplitude a {0.25, 0.5} x sign {+,-} = 32 ] ; tilt multiplier = clip(1 + sign*a*(2*pct-1), 0.25, 1.75), pct = expanding percentile rank of the row's feature among past rows (mean about 1; renormalised by the clean-span mean multiplier in the constant-risk read). Thresholds/pct use ONLY rows with et_in strictly earlier than the row's (expanding, features not outcomes); warm-up 300 rows -> multiplier 1.
- Selection (CLEAN 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 only): cell eligible if switch changes >= 10% of clean entries (tilt: all) and clean weekly-Sharpe uplift vs baseline >= +0.10; rank by clean uplift; top 3 advance (multiplicity 256 cells: chance passes at a 5% rate ~13, stated). If none: verdict NO and H1/H2 are not read. Pair-by-state descriptive tables are produced FIRST for 2013-2022 (1-min engine, run_fixed_real.pkl, ERA FLAG report) and for the live rows clean span only; the 2025-08+ descriptive split is produced only after selection is written to disk. H1 (2025-08-01..2026-02-06) and H2 (2026-02-07..2026-10-13) read ONCE for advanced cells.
- GATES per advanced cell: (1) weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; (2) worst week (all-in and each of clean/H1/H2) not worse than baseline, (constant-risk renormalised read also reported); (3) own null = random masks flagging the same fraction of rows per calendar year as the real rule (same multiplier values applied to random rows; for tilt: the multiplier multiset permuted among rows), 200 re-simulations, real all-in uplift > p95; (4) paired bootstrap 3,000 weeks, 90% CI of all-in weekly-Sharpe uplift lower bound > 0; (5) plateau: neighbours q x0.75, x1.25 (tilt: a x0.75, x1.25) and level x0.75, x1.25 of the reduction (1-level) keep >= 75% of the cell's all-in uplift for >= 3 of 4; (6) 2x slippage whole chain: uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; (7) insurance metric set clean/H1/H2/all-in + rep_common read; (8) NT8 spec (rolling signed-volume from tick data via the Rithmic feed, bid/ask-classified; sizefn as per-entry quantity multiplier, integer contracts, margin unchanged as sizes never exceed 8/8). ERA FLAG: effect of the selected rule in the 1-min engine rows 2013-22 reported (gross, fixed-cost, price-scaled-cost per trade of flagged vs unflagged), not gating. Replication under Gate Amendment 2: alternating-month split (select odd months of clean+H1+H2? no: select on odd months of the clean span, read the even months of clean+H1+H2, then reverse) and cross-instrument (ES flow state on the ES-converted rows is not available: stated N/A); FOUND-PENDING at best on H1+H2 read once. Zero FOUND acceptable; no gate moves; no re-run after results.
- DRIVERS-4 gamma extension (GEX sign, distance to zero gamma, 0DTE share from OPTIONS data) will be registered as a separate entry when REPORT_OPTIONS data are ready, before use.

## ASSEMBLY (BOOK LIBRARY AND PORTFOLIO ASSEMBLY, user rule 2026-10-16). Registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE the first assembly run (code/census/night_ins/ap_assembly.py; only library construction and the exact ST/HM mix grid, which uses no candidate data, had been run).
- Question: is there a portfolio of 2-4 library books (ST and HM ordinary members, droppable/resizable) with a higher CLEAN weekly Sharpe than the live pair that also holds on H1 and H2? Mechanism prior: low (3%) that a 2-4-book mix beats the live pair on all three spans with worst week no worse, because the library is the same 2023-2026 window the pair lives in; reported with its own null so the chance expectation sits beside the real number.
- Library pool (pre-filter, clean-only information): status active and not tagged WITHDRAWN; full coverage of clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 (>= 98% of days in span); near-duplicates removed (daily |corr| >= 0.95 on the full overlap: keep the higher clean Sharpe); pool = ST, HM, WT-10 + every book with verdict LEAD/FOUND*/SURVIVOR/INSURANCE/PENDING (all 'leads') + the top 150 other books by clean standalone weekly Sharpe. Books lacking H1 or H2 (MBO seven) may enter and are scored on the spans they have (flag; cannot be PORTFOLIO-LEAD).
- Portfolio: k = 2,3,4 books; integer contracts >= 1 per member, total T = 16 (primary), 12 and 20 (reported); unit = one NQ-contract-equivalent = stored series / 8 (standard size 8 contracts, R = $9,600 per 60-pt stop).
- Accounts: max 2. NQ_INTRADAY books net against each other and against ST/HM in one NT8 account: ST and HM may share an account with each other (live), any other NQ_INTRADAY book must sit in a different account from ST/HM and from any other NQ_INTRADAY book; so <= 2 'NQ groups' ({ST,HM} counts as one). OTHER_INSTR books go in the account of the largest NQ group (account 1 if none). Each account has its own day lock (cap +8R, 2R/67% give-back, -2R) and week floor (-8R) at R_acct = 9600 x (block contracts / 16).
  Account series: the ST/HM block is the EXACT corrected-engine series for that (wST, wHM) mix at its own R (grid, b11_nt8 bookscale, B.R scaled); every other member is its stored series x contracts/8 summed and passed through a DAY-LEVEL APPROXIMATION of the account rules (day P&L capped at +8R_acct; week cumulative floored at -8R_acct, remaining days of that week zeroed; give-back and the -2R day stop need intraday paths and are not applied). Approximation stated in every report; exact B11-style re-simulation is only available for ST/HM mixes.
- Selection: stage 1 on CLEAN ONLY, linear sum of stored series (unlocked for non-pair books; standalone ST/HM), objective = clean weekly Sharpe (ISO weeks, ddof=1, sqrt(52)); exhaustive over all feasible subsets and allocations (mean/covariance form w'Cw), plateau requirement: every +-1-contract transfer between two members (including to zero) keeps >= 75% of the Sharpe. Stage 2: top 2,000 stage-1 plateau-passing portfolios re-scored with the exact pair block + account approximation, plateau re-checked, rank by stage-2 clean Sharpe; top 10 distinct book sets (and weekly-series corr < 0.98 to each higher-ranked one) reported. H1 (2025-08-01..2026-02-06) and H2 (2026-02-07..2026-10-13) read once, for the top 10 and the marked rows only; UNTOUCHED never.
- Metrics: weekly Sharpe, worst week, max DD, CVaR5 (weekly), %neg weeks, worst-decile-week behaviour (mean P&L and corr with the pair in the live pair's worst-decile weeks), margin proxy = contracts x ($1,500 day-margin per NQ-equivalent for intraday books, $21,000 for books held overnight/multi-day; unverified proxy).
- Own null: the same stage-1 search (same pre-filter rule re-applied to the destroyed library, same plateau rule, T = 16) over 100 replicates in which EVERY library book is timing-destroyed by an independent random circular day-shift inside its own span (preserves the marginal, serial structure and mean; destroys cross-book alignment and alignment with the calendar). Reported: distribution of the best-of-search plateau-passing clean Sharpe, its mean/p50/p95, and the real value's rank. Second reported null (N2): independent weekly random sign flips of each de-meaned book (edge destroyed) - reported, not a gate.
- Flag: PORTFOLIO-LEAD = clean, H1 and H2 weekly Sharpe all higher than the live pair 8/8 (1.46/2.83/2.30 pair-engine; same T) AND all-in worst week no worse than the pair's; a PORTFOLIO-LEAD goes through the FOUND gates (additive framing vs the pair is not defined for a replacement portfolio: gates 2-7 are run on the portfolio vs the pair as the 'candidate' replacing it, with its own null from this search, bootstrap of the Sharpe difference, plateau, 2x slippage on the members' stored costs where available, worst-decile corr, spec) before any FOUND label.
- Marked rows always shown: LIVE PAIR 8/8 (and 6/6 at T=12, 10/10 at T=20); LIVE PAIR + WT-10 in a separate account (T=16: ST6 HM6 + WT-10 x4; T=20: 8/8 + WT-10 x4; T=12: 5/5 + WT-10 x2).
- Re-run schedule: every 12 h and on every new LEAD/FOUND in CANDIDATES.md; the pool, null and report are rebuilt each time from the current INDEX.csv; each run is a new family of the false-discovery accounting (1 family; chance-pass = its own null).

### CYCLE 12 GAP-MAP REPLACEMENT OF FU1-FU5 (2026-10-16T16:50Z, before any run of it)
FU1-FU5 as written (6E, 6J, 6B, 6A, 6C, ZB, SI, HG, NG and ETH-only families) are CANCELLED: those instruments are not in GAPMAP. Replaced by the GAPMAP cells claimed above (D06/D11, H2-H4, M1-M3): the same pre-registered B1 grid on YM, and the B4 (192), B7 (288), B10 (144), B11 (192) grids on YM and BTC, with the clean-only selection and every gate of the CYCLE 12 re-registration (200 replicates). They are appended to the b/*_co directories and reported inside REPORT_B1/B4/B7/B10/B11 (instrument lists extended; BTC has B1 already). YM specs: tick 1.0, $5/pt, RTH open 09:30 ET.

## GAPMAP BATCH G1-G5 (registered 2026-10-16T15:52Z before any G run; claimed in GAPMAP_UPDATES.md as B2356). SEL=clean (selection on clean only: Sharpe > 0 in both clean halves and >= 40 pair-window trades; top 3 per family; 2013-22 reported as ERA flag only; pair window read once; own random-entry null; bootstrap; plateau +-25%; 2x slippage; wd corr; shift null for hedge claims). Daily bars; next-open entries, exit at the close of day t+hold; BTC = CME BTC futures 5 BTC (tick 5 pts, $25/side + $4.36 RT; label-day bars from the 1-min series, 2017+), VX = nearest-month VX daily (CFE; IBKR; roll days zeroed; tick 0.05 = $50/side + $2 RT; standalone cells only).
- G1: instruments NQ ES RTY YM GC CL ZN BTC VX; state = ATR20 percentile over 252 days (VX: price-level percentile) <= 0.33 ('lo') or >= 0.67 ('hi'); position long or short while in state at the close of day t; hold {1,5}: 9 x 2 x 2 x 2 = 72 cells.
- G2: BTC target: trigger in {ES, NQ, GC, 6J}, L {5,10} day sign x s {+1,-1}, hold 5: 16 cells; VX target: trigger ES L {5,10} sign x s, hold {1,5}: 8 cells; VX own momentum/reversal L {5,10,20} x s, hold 5: 6 cells: 30 cells.
- G3: events FOMC, NFP, CPI (ap_c2_g3_events); window = week of the event (entry Monday open of the ISO week, exit Friday close; sig at the previous Friday) or the event day only (entry open, exit close); instruments GC CL ZN BTC; long/short: 4 x 3 x 2 x 2 = 48 cells (CPI 2023+ only).
- G4: BTC: weekday w (Mon-Fri entry day open->close) x long/short = 10; Friday close -> Monday open weekend leg is not tradable (no weekend session) so skipped; month turn: last 2 / first 3 trading days long or short = 4; 14 cells.
- G5: BTC weekly: signal at the last trading day of the week: TS momentum L {4,8,13,26} weeks (sign of the L-week return) follow/fade; weekly Donchian N {4,13} breakout follow/fade; hold 5: 12 cells.

## OPT0DTE - intraday options hedging flow, 5 cells (registered 2026-10-16T16:05Z, BEFORE any flow/state was joined to NQ returns or pair rows; only data-quality checks of the reducer were run)
Data (bought under the user's <= $2,500 rule, SPEND.json family OPT0DTE): GLBX NQ.OPT + ES.OPT trades (true aggressor side), OPRA NDX.OPT + NDXP.OPT + SPX.OPT trades (NO aggressor side: customer sign inferred by the tick rule per contract and day; its accuracy is measured on GLBX where the true side exists and reported), SPXW.OPT ohlcv-1m (1-min bars: volume/premium/IV only; sign proxy = bar-close change, flagged weak), 2022-01-01..2026-10-14, plus definitions. 2022 is warm-up/threshold history only (the pair has no rows before 2023-02-10). Reducer: code/census/night_ins/ap_opt0_reduce.py -> data/autopilot/opt0dte/min/*.parquet.
CONVENTIONS. Customer sign: aggressor buy (side B, or uptick) = customer buys = dealer short the option. Delta-hedge flow (fut-equivalents, NQ/ES contracts) = cust_sign x delta x size x multiplier/futures multiplier; positive = dealers BUY futures. Delta/gamma: Black-76 on the put-call-parity forward (per date x expiry median of K + C - P - front future, same-minute pairs), IV solved per trade, T to the contract's exact expiry (PM roots SPXW/NDXP 16:00 ET, AM roots 09:30 ET; GLBX exact timestamp). Underlying for greeks = continuous front future (NQ for NQ.OPT/NDX/NDXP, ES for ES.OPT/SPX/SPXW) at the PRIOR completed minute. DTE bucket by ET trade date (GLBX: date after 18:00 ET) vs expiry date: 0DTE = 0, 1, 2-5, far. Families: NDXF = NQ.OPT + NDX + NDXP (NQ-relevant), SPXF = ES.OPT + SPX + SPXW (ES-relevant; SPXW sign is the weak bar proxy). All signals use minutes <= t-1; entry at the OPEN of minute t; ET clock; 0DTE bucket unless stated; RTH-only for OPRA families (09:30-16:15 ET), NQ.OPT/ES.OPT also overnight.
Fill model / cost (all cells): 1 tick/side slippage (0.5 pt per NQ round trip) + $4.36/contract commission round trip (NT8 $34.88 per 8 lots); 2x slippage in the slippage test. Pair baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) clean process (weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30). Selection on CLEAN 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 only; H1/H2 read once; UNTOUCHED never; 2013-2022 not a gate (and no options data exists before 2022: ERA FLAG = 'not testable pre-2022: the structure did not exist (0DTE daily expiries from 2022)'; the 2022-01..2023-01 span is reported standalone for cell 1). Replication under GATE AMENDMENT 2 (need >= 2 of): H0 2023-02-10..2023-07-31 (same sign, >= 50% of uplift), cross-instrument (cell 1 only: the same frozen rule on the other family's own future, ES with SPXF; for cells 2-5 the pair is NQ-only so N/A), alternating-month split (select odd months/read even and reverse, 2023-02..2026-10), newest live weeks (N/A until rows exist).
CELL 1 (standalone sleeve + additive/constant-risk vs pair): sleeve = forward NQ drift (ES for SPXF) from 5-60 min after cost. Signals x: S1 = trailing-w sum of 0DTE delta-hedge flow (dfl); S2 = dealer-gamma-implied hedge flow = (dealer 0DTE net gamma inventory since the open, G = -sum cust_sign x gamma$ (gfl)) times the trailing-w return of the future (sign: dealer long gamma sells rallies, i.e. flow = -G x ret x scale; z-scored); S3 = premium-weighted call-put signed imbalance (sprc - sprp)/(prc + prp) trailing w. w in {5, 30}; threshold |z| >= {1.0, 2.0} where z = x / std of x over the prior 20 trading days at the same w (causal); direction follow or fade; hold h in {5, 15, 30, 60} min (time exit at the open of minute t+1+h, no stop); family NDXF or SPXF (traded on NQ / ES); signals only 09:35-15:30 ET; one position at a time (signals ignored while in a trade); size 1 NQ-equivalent unit scaled afterwards to the pair's clean weekly sigma (vol-matched, as T3/S1 sleeves). 3 x 2 x 2 x 2 x 4 x 2 = 192 cells. Selection: >= 100 trades in clean, weekly Sharpe > 0 in BOTH clean halves, top 3 by clean weekly Sharpe per family; diagnostic read of the 3 best failing cells otherwise.
CELL 2 (pair conditioning / size tilt by flow): for each pair flip (ROWS_corrected_engine, entry minute t): aligned flow a = side x x_{t-1} with x in {S1 dfl, S3 imbalance, premium-flow imbalance PF = (sprc + sprp)/(prc + prp)} summed over trailing w in {15, 60} min, NDXF family; terciles from EXPANDING causal history (min 60 prior flips; 2022 flow history not paired so initial thresholds from the first 60 flips); orientation o in {penalise aligned (top tercile gets g), penalise opposed (bottom tercile gets g)}; action g in {0 skip, 0.5 half, 1.5 boost the OTHER tercile}; mix m in {10, 20, 30, 50}%: size f_i = 1 + m x (g_i - 1) so the switch moves m of pair risk (additive-equivalent framing); flips without a state (OPRA families outside 09:30-16:15) get f = 1. 3 x 2 x 2 x 3 = 36 cells (m is a gate level, not a cell). Descriptive by ET hour (per-hour mean pts of tercile groups) reported.
CELL 3 (0DTE volume share profile vs per-trade P&L): share_t = 0DTE volume / total volume over trailing w in {30, 60} min, converted to z against the mean/std of the same ET clock window over the prior 20 trading days (time-of-day profile removed); family NDXF or SPXF; terciles expanding-causal; orientation (penalise high-share or low-share), action g in {0, 0.5}; same mix m. 2 x 2 x 2 x 2 x ... = 16 cells.
CELL 4 (gamma-flip proximity, pair size switch): dealer inventory proxy (no OI is bought for the OPRA families; the OPTIONS worker's statistics are not used): inv(K,exp,cp) = - sum of cust-signed volume over ALL prior sessions and the current one (contracts with DTE <= 5 only), 30-minute updates. Zero-gamma level Z: the spot S* in S0 x [0.97, 1.03] (grid 0.05%) where total dealer dollar gamma sum inv x gamma(S,K,T,flat IV = the family's trailing ATM IV) x mult changes sign closest to S0; recomputed at 09:30 from prior-session inventory and re-updated at every completed 30-min slot with that slot's signed volume. prox = (S - Z)/S. State types: SIGN (prox > 0: above flip, positive dealer gamma), NEAR-0.25 and NEAR-0.5 (|prox| < 0.25% or 0.5%); orientation (penalise the state or its complement), action g in {0, 0.5, 1.5 on the other side}; family NDXF/SPXF; 3 x 2 x 3 x 2 = 36 cells, mix m as above. Flips with undefined Z: f = 1.
CELL 5 (options-implied move vs realised, bracket-sizing information): IVA = trailing 15-min volume-weighted ATM IV (|ln K/F| < 0.3%) of the 0DTE bucket (1DTE if no 0DTE trades), implied remaining-day sd in points IM = IVA x S x sqrt(T_rem) (T_rem to 16:00 ET), and states q1 = IM/ADR20 (prior-20-day mean daily range) and q2 = realised sd of 1-min returns over the trailing 30 min / implied 1-min sd (IVA x S x sqrt(1/525960)), both causal; terciles expanding; orientation/action/mix/family as cell 2 (action g in {0, 0.5, 1.5}); 2 x 2 x 3 x 2 x 2 = 48 cells. Bracket-sizing information (descriptive, not a gate): rank correlation of q1/q2 with each flip's |pts| and with the target-hit flag.
