GATES (per BRIEF_COMMON, unchanged): cell 1 sleeve: additive uplift >= +0.10 weekly Sharpe on clean, H1, H2 separately at 10/20/30/50% shares, worst week not worse than the share, own timing-destroyed null (random-entry, same frequency and hold, 200 reps) p95 on standalone Sharpe, bootstrap 90% CI of the additive uplift > 0, circular-shift null reported, plateau (w, h, thr at exactly +-25% neighbours where the definition is continuous: w, h; z-threshold 1.0/2.0 -> 0.75/1.25 and 1.5/2.5) >= 75% neighbours keep >= 75% of uplift, survives 2x slippage, worst-decile-week corr with the pair <= +0.30, NT8 spec + margin. Cells 2-5 (tilt): ΔSharpe(pair with tilt) - Sharpe(pair) >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 at m = 10/20/30/50%; worst week not worse than the share; own null = state labels permuted within (ET hour x year) blocks of the flips (timing destroyed), 200 permutations, the WHOLE family grid re-run with the clean-only selection rule, statistic = the selected cell's H1+H2 ΔSharpe at m = 30%, real must exceed null p95; bootstrap 90% CI (weekly blocks) of the all-in ΔSharpe > 0; plateau at +-25% of w, quantile cut (33% -> 25%/42%), proximity band; 2x slippage; worst-decile-week corr of the increment (tilted - plain daily P&L) with the pair <= +0.30; NT8 spec. Screening of tilt cells uses a linear re-weighting of the engine's baseline per-trade net (f x net, locks of the baseline unchanged); every finalist (and every cell reported as SURVIVOR) is re-run through b11 B.run(sizefn) exactly. Insurance metric set reported for finalists. FDR accounting: 192 + 36 + 16 + 36 + 48 = 328 cells; expected chance passes at each gate listed from the 200 nulls.
Executability: NQ.OPT/ES.OPT trade feed exists on CME via Rithmic, NDX/NDXP/SPX/SPXW flow requires an OPRA real-time feed which NinjaTrader Brokerage/Rithmic does not provide (an external feed or Databento live is needed): any FOUND built on OPRA flow is 'research-only unless a feed is added'. Margin: sleeve units NQ intraday margin per contract; tilt cells add none.

## G10 (registered 2026-10-16 by worker G10, BEFORE any G10 run; only calendar construction and pair reproduction smoke test done)
### G10-COMMON
- Pair-side size/halt overlays on B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) (clean process, d3_common first; reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30 before the grid). Multipliers applied per entry via B.Cfg.sizefn (G10-3: skipmask), dollar rules stay as live. Fill: 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots; 2x slippage SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant. NOT renormalised to mean size 1 (the overlay IS the proposal; net change reported; Sharpe is scale-free). Template code ap_b17_core/run/rep.
- Selection: clean 2023-08..2025-07 weekly Sharpe uplift vs baseline; ONE top cell carried; H1/H2 read once. Gates (uplift framing): >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2 separately; all-in worst week not worse; matched null (100 draws, family-specific below) uplift p95 < real on clean and all-in; paired weekly bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau one-at-a-time x0.75/x1.25 of every numeric parameter (m -> 1+(m-1)*f; windows' minute bounds *f; k *f), >=75% keep >=75% of all-in uplift; 2x slip >= +0.10 each span; insurance set clean/H1/H2/all; worst-decile corr n/a for overlays; NT8 spec. Era flag: overlay re-run on 2023-02..07 (H0) NOT possible (pair rows start 2023-08), so replication = newest weeks + alternating-month split + report-only; stated honestly. Zero FOUND acceptable.
- Event calendars (ET release time): FOMC 14:00 (FOMC_V verified), NFP 08:30 and CPI 08:30 (NFP_V/CPI_V verified, from ap_c2_g3_events), ECB 14:15 Europe/Berlin (= 08:15 ET; calendar FROM MEMORY, unverified, 2023-2026 meeting dates). Event day = entry label day (LIN) equals the event date.
### G10-1 EVENT-DAY SIZE TILT (D10 x M8, H1-H4). Mechanism: on scheduled-release days the order flow is information-driven and the pair's 5-minute trend/flip logic faces gap-risk through the print; cutting size on those days trims variance (and maybe mean) of weekly P&L. Prior 5% (C9-R1 avoid-filters and C2-G3 already NO; this is the partial-size version).
- Grid (6 cells): event set {FOMC; FOMC+NFP+CPI; FOMC+NFP+CPI+ECB} x m {0.5, 0.75} applied to all entries whose label day is an event day. Null: random non-event... same count of random trading days (drawn from all days in the pair span), same m, 100 draws. Plateau m->0.625/0.375-> (1+(m-1)f).
### G10-2 EVENT WINDOW TILT / POST-EVENT RE-ENGAGEMENT (D10 x M8, H2-H3). Mechanism: after the print is digested (+60..+240 min) trends from the release can persist (size up); around the print (-60..+60 min) fills are poor and flips are noise (size down). Prior 5%.
- Grid (8 cells): events {FOMC+NFP+CPI; +ECB} x window {W1 = entry within [T-60,T+60) min; W2 = [T+60,T+240]} x m {0.5, 1.5}. (Only entries inside the window on event days scaled.) Null: same window relative to pseudo-release times on random days with the same count and the same clock time of each event type, 100 draws. Plateau: window bounds x0.75/x1.25 (about T), m->1+(m-1)f.
### G10-3 CROSS-ASSET RISK-OFF HALT (D07 x M7, H1-H4). Mechanism: bond/yen bid (ZN up, 6J up) is a risk-off tell; when it prints beyond k sigma in 60 min, equity trend legs whipsaw -> halt NEW pair entries for the rest of that label day (open positions managed as live). Prior 3%; C1-F5 (context filters, 54 cells) NO, this is the sticky-halt version only.
- Signal: r60(t) = log change of the back-adjusted 1-min close over the prior 60 min (bar closed >=2 min before entry), sigma = sqrt(60) x std of 1-min returns over the previous 60 trading days; trigger when r60/sigma >= +k (ZN up; 6J up); sticky for the rest of the label day (18:00 ET to 18:00 ET). Grid (9 cells): asset {ZN; 6J; either} x k {1.5, 2.0, 2.5}. Null: assign the real trigger minute-of-day of each halted day to random other label days (same halted-day count), 100 draws. Plateau k x0.75/x1.25.
- Closing: UPD lines for D10 H1-H4 M8 (G10-1, G10-2) and D07 H1-H4 M7 (G10-3). M5 'exit-all at release' NOT run (needs engine change; left UNTESTED).

## B8WT (registered 2026-10-16 before any B8WT run) CONDITIONAL WT-10 SLEEVE: ON ONLY AFTER A LOSING PAIR STRETCH, SEPARATE ACCOUNT
- Mechanism/prior: B8 showed the pair's weekly P&L is not positively autocorrelated (lag-1 -0.15), so a trailing-loss state carries no information about the next week; WT-10 (ATR14/m3/no EMA/10-min/60-60, own rules, own account, library/WT10.parquet) correlates ~0.00-0.05 with pair weeks and its static uplift (+0.37/+0.42/+0.31 at 20%) comes from being on in all weeks. Prior that the conditional beats static: 3% (switching removes exposure in ~85% of weeks); prior it passes all gates: 1%.
- Grid: N in {4,8,13} completed weeks (rule: on in ISO week t iff the pair's realised P&L (library PAIR, B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None)) summed over weeks t-N..t-1 is < 0; known at Monday open; first N weeks off, history from 2023-02-10) x share {10,20,30,50}% (sleeve weekly sigma = share x pair clean weekly sigma, WT own day lock / week floor / 5R-2R rules at its own R, no netting with the pair) x framing {additive, constant-risk}. 24 cells. Static WT sleeve and equal-exposure static (share x on-fraction) reported beside.
- Selection of N on CLEAN only (sum of clean additive uplift vs pair at 20%, additive + cr); H1/H2 read once afterwards.
- Gates (unchanged): uplift >= +0.10 on clean/H1/H2 at every share, worst week not worse than share, own null = matched random-switch (same number of ON weeks per span, 2000 random draws, p95 on uplift) PLUS circular-shift null (reported; gating for hedge claim) and WT-10's own random-entry null cited from REPORT_WT; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift vs pair > 0 AND vs static (conditional must beat static to be worth the complexity); plateau (N x0.75/x1.25 and share +-25%, 8 neighbours, >=75% keep >=75%), 2x slippage (WT rebuilt at 2 ticks/side), wd corr <= +0.30, NT8 spec. ERA flag: 2013-2022 with the WT2 scaled-bracket history vs the 1-min-engine pair (reported); replication per GATE AMENDMENT 2 (H0 2023-02..07, odd/even month split) reported if clean/H1/H2 gates pass.
- Null for the verdict: the conditional is NO if it does not beat the static WT sleeve (bootstrap vs static CI > 0) irrespective of the vs-pair uplift.

## G0 batch (D00 pair-internal state, modes M7 risk rule / M8 sizing), registered 2026-10-16 by worker G0 BEFORE any G0 run (only reading of b11_nt8/d3_common/ap_b17_* and an inventory of Exit names / trade counts were executed; no variant returns computed)
Ledger check: B15 (post-lock reduced continuation), B16 (week de-risk graded at -XR week equity), B17 (trailing vol sizing), B18 (prior-day cap/loss tilt), B19 (ST/HM tilt), C2-G1 (exit mgmt), C4-K1 (target extension) are NOT repeated. New: intraday consecutive-stop/entry-count halts (G0a), multi-day equity-curve drawdown size step-down (G0b), per-side asymmetric daily loss limits (G0c). Anti-martingale after win-streak weeks was considered and dropped (B18 prior-day and B19 pnl tilt are its neighbours; both NO).
Engine: B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) re-simulated with a pass1 copy that has a state hook (identical to B.pass1 when the hook is None; checked to reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30). All state is computed from trades already CLOSED (TOUT<=entry time) among accepted trades at their realised net, so no look-ahead. Dollar rules (5R/-2R per book, day cap, week floor) unchanged. Selection: highest clean weekly Sharpe among grid cells that change >= 5% of clean entries; ONE cell carried; H1/H2 read once. Gates (pair change, all of): weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week (all-in) not worse; matched null (100 draws) p95 < real all-in uplift; paired weekly bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau (numeric params x0.75/x1.25 one at a time, >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift); 2x slippage (SLIP_PT 1.0 both sides) uplift >= +0.10 each span; NT8-codable + margin; insurance metrics and constant-risk framing reported (INSURANCE if cost >= -5% and CVaR5/worst week +25% or maxDD +20%). Era: pair has no pre-2023 trades (engine rows 2023-08..), so ERA flag n/a; 2013-22 replication impossible on this engine (noted).
- G0a (M7, H1-H3 intraday entry halts; prior 5%). Mechanism: runs of stops within a day signal a regime in which the signals are wrong (chop/whipsaw); the per-book -2R day loss only fires after two full stops on ONE book, while the combined cap fires on profit. A halt after n consecutive stops (Exit name *_Stop) of the same day, or after m entries in a day, would cut trade count in whipsaw days. Rule: an entry is skipped (size 0) if, among accepted trades closed before it on the same day label, the last n closed (by exit time) in the scope were all stop exits. GRID (9 cells): scope in {book, pair} x n in {2,3,4} (6 cells; halt for rest of the day), plus max entries per book per day in {5,8,12} (3 cells). Null: per draw, for every real (day,book) (or day) in which the rule fired, choose a uniformly random entry index in that group and skip all later entries of that group (same fired-group count); 100 draws. Plateau: n x0.75/x1.25 rounded (cells {n-1,n+1} where integer) and m x0.75/x1.25.
- G0b (M8, H3-H4 multi-day equity-curve drawdown size step-down; prior 5%). Mechanism: B16 stepped size down on WEEK equity only (X in 3-5R inside the week; NO, lost Sharpe). The pair's own equity curve over a rolling 10-20 trading-day window is a slower state: trade at k size while realised equity is >= X R below its trailing-W-day peak (realised accepted net, closed trades only), resume at full size when back within X/2 R of the peak (hysteresis). GRID (8 cells): W in {10,20} days x X in {6,10}R x k in {0.5,0.25}. Not mean-neutral (de-risking); constant-risk normalisation (scale to equal clean mean size) reported. Null: per draw, circularly shift the realised per-trade multiplier sequence by a random offset (keeps frequency and durations of step-down); 100 draws. Plateau: W x0.75/x1.25, X x0.75/x1.25, k x0.75/x1.25 (6 neighbours).
- G0c (M7, H1-H3 per-side asymmetric daily loss limit; prior 4%). Mechanism: the live per-book -2R day limit is book-wise and side-blind; when NQ trends against the pair, losses accrue on ONE SIDE across both books. Rule: skip an entry on side s if realised net of closed accepted trades on side s on that day (both books) <= -L R. GRID (8 cells): both sides L in {1,1.5,2,3}R; long-only limit L in {1.5,2}R; short-only limit L in {1.5,2}R. Null: per draw, for every real fired (day,side), random entry index in that day-side group after which that side is skipped; 100 draws. Plateau: L x0.75/x1.25.
Report ID prefix G0a/G0b/G0c; REPORT_G0a.md etc.

## G6 (D06 clock/calendar x pair management; worker G6; registered 2026-10-16 before first run)
Engine for all three: live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), clean process via d3_common, 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT, spans clean 2023-08..2025-07 / H1 / H2. Baseline 1.46/2.83/2.30. Selection on clean weekly Sharpe uplift only; H1/H2 read once for the carried cell. Gates unchanged (uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week not worse; own null p95; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau >= 75% of +-25% neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; wd corr n/a for overlays; NT8 spec). Replication (Amend. 2): H0 (2023-02-10..2023-07-31 pair weeks) and alternating-month split inside 2023-08..2026-10 (select on odd-month weeks, read on even and reverse; hold = both reads positive and >= 50% of the selection-half uplift); ES/RTY replication not available (pair engine NQ only), stated. ERA flag: pair rows start 2023-02, so 'n/a pre-2023 (no pair history)'.
- G6a (M4/M8, H3-H4; prior 6%). Session-phase entry filter/size on the pair's flips. Mechanism: the pair's edge (trend-continuation of 1-min flips) should depend on liquidity regime by ET clock phase (open drive, lunch lull, late-day, Globex halves). Phase by entry ET: OPEN 09:30-10:30, LUNCH 11:30-13:30, LATE 14:00-17:00, GLX_A 18:00-02:00, GLX_B 02:00-09:30. GRID (15 cells): phase x m in {0 (skip), 0.5, 1.5} applied to that phase's entries, multiplier normalised to mean 1 over clean trades (risk-neutral; skip cells inflate others). Null: 100 permutations of the realised per-trade multipliers across trades (same multiset). Plateau: m deviation from 1 x0.75/x1.25 and phase window edges +-25% of width (4 neighbours).
- G6b (M8/M4/M7, H5-H6; prior 4%). Day-of-week / OPEX-week size tilt on the pair, entry label day. Mechanism: Monday gap/weekend-risk, Friday position-squaring and OPEX-week pinning change trend persistence. D3 S5 found DOW effects unstable across spans: this family is registered as an expected NO unless the alternating-month read holds. Targets: MON, FRI, OPEXW (week containing 3rd Friday), TRIPLEW (quarterly OPEX week), MEND (last 2 trading days of the month); m in {0.5,1.5}; 10 cells; normalised to mean 1 on clean. Null: 100 permutations of day-level multipliers across days. Plateau: m deviation x0.75/x1.25. Alternating-month replication required to be reported for every cell, and the carried cell must hold it for LEAD.
- G6c (M5/M7, H3-H4; prior 4%). Pair time-flatten before RTH close/settlement. Mechanism: positions held through the 15:45-16:00 close auction and into the 16:00-17:00 thin hour carry gap/imbalance risk the bracket does not control; flat at the cutoff trades a little trend for less end-of-day variance. Rule: any pair position open at cutoff T (entry label day's 15:xx) is closed at the cutoff bar open (exit slippage as the model); new entries after T until 17:00 unchanged. GRID (5 cells): all days T in {15:00, 15:30, 15:45}; Fridays only T in {15:00, 15:45}. Locks/day/week rules re-run on the truncated rows. Null: for the same number of truncated trades, truncation at a uniformly random minute inside each trade's life; 100 draws. Plateau: T +-25% of time to 17:00 (T-30 / T+30 min equivalents rounded to 15 min).
Report IDs G6a, G6b, G6c.

## G11 - price-pattern conditioning/management of the live pair (3 families; registered 2026-10-16 ~16:10Z BEFORE any feature was joined to pair rows or any resim run; only code-path checks of the 60/60 resim reproduction were allowed)
Common: engine = live pair ROWS_corrected_engine via b11_nt8 B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a CLEAN process (clean/H1/H2 weekly Sharpe 1.46/2.83/2.30 reproduced first). Fill model 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots (2x slippage: SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant). Spans clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 (selection), H1 2025-08-01..2026-02-06, H2 2026-02-07..2026-10-13 read ONCE for the carried cell. UNTOUCHED never. All features causal: only 1-min bars with index < entry bar (entry = open of bar ei). Bars on disk (V.A) cover 2023-02..2026-10, so the first trading days lack history (feature undefined -> multiplier 1). ET clock, trading-day labels as b11 (18:00 ET boundary); "RTH" = 09:30-16:00 ET. Selection: the cell with the largest clean weekly-Sharpe uplift (exact B.run) is carried whatever its sign; H1/H2 read for it only. Gates (pair-modification form, as B14/B17): (1) uplift >= +0.10 weekly Sharpe on clean, H1, H2 separately; (2) worst week not worse (clean/H1/H2/all); (3) own timing-destroyed null: 200 replicates of the WHOLE grid with the clean-only selection rule, statistic = selected cell's H1+H2 mean uplift (linear re-weighting of the baseline per-trade net; locks of the baseline unchanged), real must exceed null p95; (4) bootstrap 90% CI (3000 weekly draws) of the all-in uplift > 0; (5) plateau: exact +-25% neighbours (>= 75% keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift); (6) 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; (7) executable NT8 spec (all rules are Monday-to-Friday bar rules the strategy computes itself; sizing in whole lots reported). Worst-decile correlation: not applicable to a pair modification (no hedge claim). Insurance metric set reported (clean/H1/H2/all-in) and constant-risk verdict. Replication (GATE AMEND. 2, need >= 2 of): alternating-month split inside 2023-02..2026-10 (rule re-selected on odd months and read on even, then reverse), H0 2023-02-10..2023-07-31 same sign and >= 50% of uplift, newest live weeks (none yet). ERA FLAG: features need 1-min bars before 2023 for NQ; the pair has no rows before 2023-02, so the 2013-22 re-test is N/A for pair modifications (stated). FDR accounting: 16 + 15 + 32 = 63 cells over three families.

### G11-A  entry-location conditioning of the pair's flips (skip/half; cells D11-H1-M4, D11-H5-M4, D00-H1-M4 NQ)
Mechanism: ST/HM are trend/breakout books; a flip entered after the market has already run to/through the prior-day extreme, far from VWAP, or at the far end of the overnight range pays for an extended move (reversal risk) or, opposite, entering with little room before a prior-day level caps the target; conversely entries from the value area have room. Prior that a single-feature skip/half rule raises weekly Sharpe >= +0.10 on all three spans: 5% (B14 GBM on 36 features incl. VWAP/range position had OOS AUC 0.52; D2 E2 signal-strength tilts 0/146; B18 outcome tilts 0/8).
Features (s = trade side +1/-1, px = open of the entry bar, PD = previous trading-day RTH high/low): A1 pdpos = s*(px-(PDH+PDL)/2)/(PDH-PDL); A2 pdroom = s*(PDextreme_ahead - px)/60 (PDH if long, PDL if short; negative = already beyond); A3 onpos = s*(px - ONmid)/max(ONrange,20) with ON = Globex bars from 18:00 label start to 09:29 (partial range before 09:30 using bars so far); A4 vwapd = s*(px - VWAP)/ADR20 with VWAP = cumulative typical price x volume from the 18:00 label start and ADR20 = mean full-day range of the 20 prior label days. Tail edges = clean-span 30th / 70th percentiles of the feature over pair entries, frozen for H1/H2. Cells: feature (4) x penalised tail {high >p70, low <p30} (2) x action {skip, half-size} (2) = 16. Plateau: tail share 30% -> 22.5% / 37.5% (edges re-cut on clean). Null permutes the feature value among entries within blocks of (year x book x ET hour). Pair-state cells D00-H1-M4: the same cells are the 1-min pair-state conditioning that D00-H1-M4 asked for only to the extent the pair's own side/entry state defines s; no other pair-state rule is tested here (stated).
### G11-B  prior-day range-state size tilt and risk rule (cells D11-H5-M8, D11-H5-M7 NQ)
Mechanism: after a compressed day (NR7, inside day, low range/ADR20) range expands; the trend/breakout pair should earn more per entry. Prior: 5% (B17 vol-target sizing NO; D2 E1 vol targeting 0/2; the low-range -> larger size direction is the vol-target direction). States on the PREVIOUS label day's RTH range (known by 16:00): NR7 = smallest RTH range of the last 7 days, ID = RTH high < previous high and low > previous low, LOW = range/ADR20(RTH, 20 prior) below the clean 33rd percentile (frozen). M8 cells: multiplier a in {0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5} on entries of state days (else 1), renormalised so the clean mean per-trade multiplier = 1 (risk-neutral); 3 x 4 = 12. M7 cells (risk rule): on state days the combined day cap 8R is halved to 4R (arm/give-back unchanged), 3 cells. Total 15. Plateau: |a-1| x0.75 / x1.25, LOW cut 33 -> 25/42%. Null permutes the state label among trading days within (year x month). Whole-lot rounding (round(8m)/8) reported.
### G11-C  stop placement relative to the recent swing, 1-second re-simulation (cells D11-H4-M5, D00-H1-M5 NQ; H0 M5 cells closed by D2 E5)
Mechanism: a fixed 60-pt stop ignores where the market's recent swing sits; a stop just beyond the lookback extreme avoids being the obvious liquidity pocket (fewer premature stop-outs) and sizes risk to structure. Prior: 5% (C4-K1 raising stops harmful; D2 E6 bracket ensemble 0 cells; E5 target models 0/6). Re-simulation: every one of the 13,089 rows is replayed from its actual fill on the 1-second bars (d2_e5 machinery: stop/target tested per second, stop first when both touch) with stop distance sd = clamp(distance from the entry price to the lookback extreme of the last L 1-min bars (low for longs, high for shorts) + 2 pt buffer, lo, hi); target distance 60 (fixed) or = sd (1R:1R); rows closed externally (netting/time block) keep the external exit unless the new bracket hits first. Control: sd=60, td=60 must reproduce the baseline rows (gate before any cell). Cells: L {15, 30, 60, 120} x clamp {[30,90], [45,75]} x target {60, =sd} x sizing {fixed lots, risk-scaled lots 60/sd} = 32. Risk-scaled cells use per-row weights w = 60/sd through the account model (fractional lots, whole-lot reported). Plateau: L x0.75/x1.25 and clamp edges x0.75/x1.25. Null (stop distances permuted across rows within (book x year-month) blocks, same marginal distribution, linear net swap with baseline locks, 100 replicates of the whole grid with the clean-only selection rule) plus the exact selected-cell run. Fill note: stop exits pay 1 tick/side as live; the resim does not model stop-slippage beyond that.

## G8 - GAPMAP D08 (volatility regime) / D03 (daily options proxy), modes M4/M5/M7/M8, NQ (registered 2026-10-16T17:10Z, BEFORE any G8 run; only data-shape checks and one baseline timing run were done)
Not re-skins: C1-F6 (VIX/VX/NQ 20d RV level -> size) and C6-N1 (weekly regime switch) are NO and used daily LEVEL observables at weekly or entry-size granularity. G8 uses (A) INTRADAY realised-vol ratio at the entry minute, (B) overnight-range and opening-gap state of the day, (C) a vol-scaled DAY-LOCK rule (M7), (D) a daily NQ.OPT activity proxy for the 0DTE/dealer-gamma regime. Common: engine = B11 corrected NT8-faithful engine (b11_nt8, clean process, B11_ROWS corrected), baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) (weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30); fill = 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT, 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0; ET clock. Bar data = harness 1-min series 2023-02-08..2026-10-12 (pair window only; no earlier bars, so the 2013-22 ERA FLAG is 'not testable: pair rows and the harness series start 2023-02' - reported as such). Every feature uses only bars with index < entry bar (closes of the prior minute or older), day features only sessions strictly before the day. Selection on CLEAN 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 only (clean uplift in weekly Sharpe of the best cell); H1/H2 read once on the selected cell; UNTOUCHED never; thresholds = percentiles of the feature over CLEAN entries (absolute thresholds thereafter).
Gates (as for C1-F6/B15: a pair change is not a new book, so gate 3 is the weekly-Sharpe uplift of the pair with the rule fully applied; the 10/20/30/50% mixes f=1+m(g-1) are REPORTED): 3 uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately and worst week (all-in) not worse; 4 matched random-switch null (flags permuted, same count: A within year x ET-hour blocks of entries, B/C/D across days) 200 replicates, real all-in uplift > p95, and weekly-block bootstrap 90% CI of the all-in uplift > 0; 5 plateau (selected cell's parameters at x0.75 and x1.25: threshold percentile, multiplier distance |m-1|, lock levels; >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift) and 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; 6 corr of the increment with the pair in worst-decile weeks reported (n/a gate: no hedge claim); 7 NT8 spec. Insurance set (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg wk) clean/H1/H2/all via ins_metrics.score; uplift also vs B.Cfg(). Replication (GATE AMENDMENT 2) only if all gates pass: alternating-month split 2023-26 (select odd/read even and reverse), H0 window 2023-02..07, newest weeks.
G8-A (D08, H2/H3, M4 tilt + M8 sizing; 24 cells): at each pair entry, rvr = (sum of squared 1-min log returns over the last 60 bars) / (mean of the same statistic at the same ET minute-of-day over the prior 20 sessions; >= 10 valid, else flag off). Flag = rvr above (direction HI) or below (LO) the clean-entry percentile P in {33, 50, 67, 80}; entry size multiplier m in {0, 0.5, 1.5} on flagged entries (0 = skip). 4 x 2 x 3 = 24. Prior that any cell passes all gates: 4%.
G8-B (D08, H3, M4 tilt/M8; 48 cells): per ET date, ONR = overnight range (high-low from 18:00 prior to 09:29) / mean ONR of the prior 20 sessions; GAP = |open 09:30 - close 15:59 of the previous RTH day| / mean RTH range of the prior 20 RTH sessions. Applies to entries with ET time in [09:30, 16:00) that date (others m = 1). Flag by feature in {ONR, GAP}, percentile P in {33, 50, 67, 80} of clean entries, direction HI/LO, m in {0, 0.5, 1.5}: 2 x 4 x 2 x 3 = 48. Prior 4%.
G8-C (D08, H3-H4, M7 risk rule; 16 cells): flagged days = prior-20-RTH-session mean daily range above (HI) clean percentile P in {67, 80} (or below the 33/20th percentile for the LO control); on flagged days the combined day lock uses a TIGHTER set S (live set: cap +8R, give-back arm 2R / keep 33%, no loss floor): S1 cap 6R; S2 loss floor -4R; S3 cap 6R + floor -4R; S4 arm 1.5R keep 50%. HI x {67,80} x S1-S4 = 8 cells; LO control x {33,20} x S1-S4 = 8 cells (control is a placebo: a vol-dependence claim needs HI to beat LO). Prior 3%.
G8-D (D03, H5, M4 tilt, NQ; 36 cells): daily NQ.OPT (CME, all expiries bought as ohlcv-1d, p1/optdaily.pkl, 2013-2026) activity of the PRIOR session: OV = total option contracts / mean of prior 20 sessions; PC = put volume share. This is a CRUDE proxy of dealer-gamma/0DTE activity (no OI or expiry split is on disk: the statistics/definition downloads are incomplete; the intraday NDX/NDXP/NQ.OPT gamma cells are the OPT0DTE family's, not repeated here). Entries on date D use the session ending before D's 18:00 ET label start. Flag by feature in {OV, PC}, P in {33, 50, 67}, direction HI/LO, m in {0, 0.5, 1.5}: 2 x 3 x 2 x 3 = 36. Prior 3%.
FDR accounting: 124 cells over 4 families; matched null replicates give the per-family chance-pass rate; the same-cell null is NOT best-of-grid aware, so a selection-aware second null (grid re-run on 30 flag permutations, selected cell's clean uplift distribution) is REPORTED for each family.
Executability: all four are entry-size / lock-parameter rules on the existing strategies computed from NQ 1-min bars (A,B,C) or CME option volume (D, needs a data feed for daily option volume); NT8 can scale quantity in OnOrder per flag; margin unchanged or lower (m <= 1.5 raises peak exposure to 1.5x only for flagged entries: report peak contracts).
- G10-3 AMENDMENT (before any P&L was computed; smoke test showed halt rates of 15-57% of entries at k in {1.5,2,2.5} because the sticky any-minute-of-day trigger fires on fat-tailed 6J/ZN 60-min moves): grid k replaced by {3.0, 4.0, 5.0} (halt rates 2.6%-22% of entries). Plateau k x0.75/x1.25 unchanged.

## Q5 GRID ENSEMBLE BOOK (registered 2026-10-16T16:00Z before it was run; B2356 worker; family = macro12/commodity monthly cross-sectional momentum: Q5, Q10, R9, Q3 count as ONE family). Book = equal-risk (1/clean weekly vol) average of ALL 12 pre-registered Q5 cells (macro12 XS 'as' momentum, monthly rebalance, 19-day hold, L {15,20,25,30} x breadth k {2,3,4}); NO selection among the 12. Gates: standalone pair-window Sharpe vs the HIGHER of (i) the p95 of the ensemble of independent random-entry replicates (105 draws per cell) and (ii) the p95 of the single-cell random-entry Sharpe distribution (conservative, since the 12 real cells are highly correlated), and vs the Bonferroni percentile 1-0.05/4 (4 sub-families Q5/Q10/R9/Q3 considered); additive/const framings, bootstrap, 2x slippage, wd corr; hedge-claim circular-shift null if wd corr < 0 (reported with rank; gate for the hedge claim); plateau on the ensemble weights = the 7 leave-one-group-out books (drop each L value, drop each k value): >= 75% of them keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift; ERA flag with cost decomposition; modern-era replication per GATE AMENDMENT 2: (a) H0 2023-02..07 uplift, (b) alternating months 2023-26 (ensemble uplift on odd months and on even months, each > 0 and >= 50% of the all-in uplift), (c) disjoint market halves of macro12 (A = ES NQ ZN GC 6E HG, B = RTY ZB CL 6J 6B 6A; k=2 each; ensemble over the 4 L values) uplift all-in > 0 and >= 50% of the full ensemble's; (d) live weeks pending. At least two must hold (live weeks excluded until they exist) for FOUND-PENDING. Books saved via lib_save.save_book.

## BATCH J (J1..J10), registered 2026-10-16 by worker I BEFORE any J run. Source: outputs/GAPMAP.csv drivers D09 (COT), D10 (index events, mega-cap earnings), D08 (VIX), D07 (NKD); cells claimed in GAPMAP_UPDATES.md. Data (free unless stated): CFTC COT TFF/disaggregated futures-only 2013-2026 (data/gap/cot), CBOE VIX/VIX3M/VVIX/VIX9D/VIX6M/SKEW daily (data/gap/vix), SEC EDGAR 8-K item 2.02 filings of 10 mega-caps (data/gap/edgar), NKD 1-minute bars 2013-2026-10-15 (Databento, $12.00, logged in SPEND.json as J10_NKD_1m BEFORE the purchase). No return of any candidate was computed before this registration (only downloads and file-structure checks).
### J-COMMON
- GATE AMENDMENT 2 applies: SELECTION ON CLEAN ONLY (2023-08-01..2025-07-31): sleeves: eligible cell = >= MINC clean trades (stated per family), selected = highest clean weekly Sharpe (ties: more trades), ONE cell carried, H1/H2 read once; pair-side: highest clean pair weekly Sharpe among cells that change >= MINCH of clean entries, one cell carried. All other gates as I-COMMON (additive uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2 at 20%, worst week within share, own random-date/entry null p95 on standalone Sharpe (500 draws; pair-side matched null 300 draws), bootstrap 90% CI > 0, plateau +-25% (>= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75%), 2x slippage, worst-decile corr <= +0.30, NT8 spec). ERA FLAG + cost decomposition (gross / fixed / ADR-scaled cost) reported, not gating. Replication for FOUND only (at least two of: newest live weeks, H0 2023-02..07, ES/RTY 2023-26, alternating-month split); not run unless every gate passes.
- Sleeve P&L: daily families mark to market to the close of the last 1-minute bar at or before 16:16 ET of each trading label (px; no VIX/COT value is used before it is published; COT effective date = report as-of Tuesday + 7 days; VIX/VVIX/VIX3M close used for entries priced at the 16:16 bar or later), unit risk = 1 ADR-dollar per trade (ADR20 from 1-minute ranges), cost 1 tick/side + $4.36 per contract RT (2x for the slippage test), scaled to the pair's clean weekly sd at 100%; framings additive 10/20/30/50% and constant-risk. Every scored book is saved with lib_save.save_book (volmatch size basis for sleeves).
### J1 COT TFF POSITIONING SLEEVE, equity indices (NQ, ES, RTY from 2017-08, YM). Mechanism: leveraged-fund / asset-manager net positioning extremes mark crowding (contrarian) or trend (follow); weekly, uncorrelated with the pair's intraday flips. Prior 4%. MINC = 40.
- Weekly trade per instrument: entry at px of the first label >= as-of + 7 days (Tuesday), exit 5 trading days later; position side from the TFF net/OI of class c at the as-of date.
- Grid (30 cells): ext signal: class {lev, am, dealer} x mode {contra, follow} x lookback L {52, 156} weeks x band {0.8, 0.9} (pct rank within the last L weeks >= band -> short if contra / long if follow; <= 1-band -> opposite; else flat) = 24; chg signal: class x mode (sign of the 4-week change in net/OI; always in market) = 6. Null N0: random weeks, same count per instrument, side = rule direction evaluated on the pct-rank sign ((pr-0.5) for ext, change sign for chg). Plateau: L x0.75/x1.25 (39/65 -> 52 grid values 39, 65), band tail (1-band) x0.75/x1.25.
### J2 COT PAIR SIZE TILT (pair-side). Mechanism: pair edge differs with equity-index crowding. Prior 3%. MINCH = 0.05.
- Weekly flag from the equity-index basket (mean of NQ and ES) pct rank (L = 156 weeks) of class c net/OI, effective Tuesday-after; entries with lab_in in [effective, effective+6 days] get multiplier m when the flag is on. Grid (24): class {lev, am} x tail {high (pr >= band), low (pr <= 1-band), either} x band {0.8, 0.9} x m {0.5, 0}. Null: permute the weekly flags across weeks (300 draws). Plateau: band tail x0.75/x1.25, m x0.75/x1.25 (m=0 -> 0.25).
### J3 COT SLEEVE, other assets (GC, CL via disaggregated managed money / producer-merchant; ZN, 6E, 6J via TFF leveraged / dealer). Prior 4%. MINC = 40.
- Same construction as J1 with classes {spec, hedge}: ext 16 cells (class 2 x mode 2 x L 2 x band 2) + chg 4 cells (class 2 x mode 2) = 20 cells. Null and plateau as J1.
### J4 INDEX-EVENT CALENDAR SLEEVE (NQ, ES, RTY). Mechanism: index-rebalance and expiry flows (quarterly S&P/Nasdaq-100 rebalance and quad witching on the third Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec, monthly opex third Friday, Russell reconstitution last Friday of June, month-end). Prior 3%; small-sample certain. MINC = 20.
- Event day D = last trading label <= the event date. Trade: entry px at D+a, exit px at D+b (trading days), side long or short. Grid (32): event {quad, opex_nonquad, russell, monthend} x window (a,b) {(-3,-1), (-1,0), (0,1), (0,3)} x side {long, short}. N0: random days D (any day), same count, same window and side. Plateau: window offsets cannot be scaled by +-25% -> plateau n/a for J4 and treated FAIL (stated).
### J5 INDEX-EVENT PAIR SIZE TILT (pair-side; C9-R1 did not test opex/quad/Russell/month-end). Prior 3%. MINCH = 0.01.
- Entries whose lab_in is an event label get multiplier m. Grid (12): event set {quad, quad+opex+russell, monthend, all of the three groups} x m {0.5, 0, 1.5}. Null: same number of random labels (300 draws). Plateau: m x0.75/x1.25.
### J6 MEGA-CAP EARNINGS-MORNING GAP SLEEVE (NQ; ES/RTY only for replication). Mechanism: after-close earnings of the ten largest NDX members move NQ overnight; the open continues or reverts. Prior 4%. MINC = 20.
- Earnings dates: EDGAR 8-K with item 2.02 for AAPL, MSFT, NVDA, AMZN, GOOGL, META, TSLA, AVGO (2018+), COST, NFLX, acceptance time after 16:00 ET -> reaction label = next trading label. RTH gap G = open(09:30) - close(15:59 prev) as in I2; entry 09:31 open; side follow = sign(G) or fade = -sign(G); exit at the open of the first bar >= 09:31 + H minutes (no later than 15:55). Grid (18): gz threshold {0, 0.10, 0.25} (|G|/ADR20) x mode {follow, fade} x H {60, 150, 384}. N0: random RTH days (any), same count, rule direction. Plateau: threshold x0.75/x1.25 (0 skipped), H x0.75/x1.25.
### J7 MEGA-CAP EARNINGS PAIR SIZE TILT (pair-side). Prior 3%. MINCH = 0.03.
- Entries whose lab_in is a reaction label (the evening of the release and the next session) get multiplier m. Grid (6): set {all ten, top five by weight (NVDA AAPL MSFT AMZN GOOGL)} x m {0.5, 0, 1.5}. Null: same number of random labels. Plateau: m x0.75/x1.25.
### J8 VIX / TERM-STRUCTURE / VVIX PAIR SWITCH (pair-side; C1-F6 tested only VIX percentile x1.5). Prior 4%. MINCH = 0.05.
- State at the 16:16 ET close of label d applies to entries with lab_in = next label. Variables v: vixpct (VIX close pct rank over 252 days), term (VIX/VIX3M), vvix (VVIX pct rank over 252 days), vchg (5-day VIX change in pts), v9 (VIX9D/VIX). Flag on when v is in its trailing-756-day top (high) or bottom (low) 1-q tail: Grid (40): v {5} x tail {high, low} x q {0.8, 0.9} x m {0.5, 0}. Null: permute daily flags across labels (300 draws). Plateau: tail width (1-q) x0.75/x1.25, m x0.75/x1.25.
### J9 VIX-SPIKE / TERM-INVERSION INDEX DIP SLEEVE (ES, NQ, RTY, YM basket). Prior 4%; likely correlated with I9. MINC = 40.
- Signal at the 16:16 px of label d: spike = VIX/mean(VIX of the prior 10 labels) >= s in {1.15, 1.30}, or inv = VIX/VIX3M >= r in {1.00, 1.05}; with or without px > SMA200; long entry at px, exit after N in {2, 5} trading days. Grid (16): signal {spike1.15, spike1.30, inv1.00, inv1.05} x sma {on, off} x N {2, 5}. N0: random days, long, same N. Plateau: s/r tail x0.75/x1.25 around the neutral (1.00), N x0.75/x1.25.
### J10 NKD OVERNIGHT LEAD FOR NQ GLOBEX. Mechanism: Nikkei futures during Tokyo hours lead or are echoed by NQ during its quiet overnight session. Prior 4%. MINC = 60.
- Window: UTC 00:00 to 06:15 (Tokyo cash session); signal s = NKD log return (raw) or NKD minus NQ log return over the same window (resid); z = s / std(s over the prior 60 observations); trade NQ if |z| >= thr; entry at the open of the 06:20 UTC bar; side follow = sign(s), fade = -sign(s); exit at +90 min, +240 min, or the first bar >= 09:25 ET. Grid (36): signal {raw, resid} x thr {0, 0.5, 1.0} x mode {follow, fade} x exit {90, 240, 0925}. N0: random weekdays (any z), same count, rule direction. Plateau: thr x0.75/x1.25 (0 skipped), exit minutes x0.75/x1.25 (0925 skipped).

## OPTIONS (registered 2026-10-16 by the OPTIONS worker BEFORE any P&L was scored against any options variable; feature series built and QC'd on price/OI data only)
Data bought (OPTIONS cap $450): GLBX ES.OPT definition+statistics+ohlcv-1d 2019-01..2026-10-14; GLBX NQ.OPT statistics (+definition from the P4 item); GLBX NQ weekly/EOM families Q1A-Q3D, QN1-QN4, QNE definition+statistics 2023-01..2026-10-14 (parent NQ.OPT holds the monthly/quarterly cycle only; weeklies are separate assets); OPRA NDXP.OPT and NDX.OPT ohlcv-1d 2019-01..2026-10-14; OPRA NDX.OPT statistics (OI). Logged in SPEND.json (ids OPT_*).
Variables (each computed at the settlement of trade date t from records with ts_event <= t 21:45 UTC; OI = latest record published by then = OI of t-1 close, conservative; the value of date t sizes the NEXT trading label only; b11_nt8 label = session starting 18:00 ET):
 V1 gex_sign = (Gcall-Gput)/(Gcall+Gput), V2 gex_lvl = GEX$/median(past 252 d of Gcall+Gput), V3 zgd = (F - zero-gamma level)/F (grid +-10%, capped at +-0.10), V4 share0 = (NDXP+NDX contracts expiring on the trade date)/(all NDXP+NDX contracts traded), V5 skew = (IV(25d put)-IV(25d call))/ATM IV on the expiry nearest 30 DTE in [14,50], V6 term = ATM IV near (DTE 3-14, nearest 7) / ATM IV far (DTE 21-60, nearest 35).
 GEX convention: dealers LONG calls / SHORT puts; G = Black-76 gamma x F^2 x 0.01 x multiplier ($20 NQ) x OI; IV inverted from settlement of the OTM side per strike, r = 0, F from put-call parity per (expiry, underlying); options with settle < 1 tick, DTE < 1 or > 120 dropped. Two option sets: A = all NQ expiries (quarterly + monthly + weekly/EOM; exists 2023-01..), Q = quarterly/monthly NQ.OPT only (2019..). V4 is OPRA-based (one set). Variables = {V1..V3,V5,V6} x {A,Q} + V4 = 11.
Transform (walk-forward): u_t = percentile rank of v_t among v_{<=t} (expanding, min 120 obs, else size 1.0). r = u (dir +1) or 1-u (dir -1); direction is a grid dimension.
 SWITCH grid (22/var): (q0,q50) in {(0,.2),(0,.3),(0,.4),(0,.5),(.1,.2),(.1,.3),(.1,.4),(.1,.5),(.2,.3),(.2,.4),(.2,.5)} x dir {+,-}: size 0 if r<q0; 0.5 if r<q50; else 1.
 TILT grid (8/var): m = clip(1+k(2r-1),0,2), k in {.25,.5,.75,1.0} x dir {+,-} (mean ~1 by construction; realised mean reported).
Application: per-day scaling of the corrected-engine pair daily P&L (B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), clean process) by the size of that label; survivors are re-run on the exact day-scaled engine. Cost scaled with size (linear).
Selection: per (variable, kind) the cell with the highest clean (2023-08-01..2025-07-31) weekly Sharpe uplift vs always-on among cells with a changed-size share of days in [10%,60%] (switch) / any (tilt) = 22 selections; H1 and H2 read once for those. Variable-set A has 120-obs warm-up from 2023-01, so its clean window is live from ~2023-07.
Gates (all must hold): uplift >= +0.10 weekly Sharpe on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst weekly P&L (in $) not worse than always-on in clean, H1, H2; own null = 2,000 random same-composition day-assignments within clean (p95 of clean uplift); bootstrap (weeks, 4,000) 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau (q0,q50) / k scaled x0.75, x1.25 (>= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of clean uplift); exact-engine 2x slippage; NT8/execution spec; REPLICATION (>= 2 of: alternating-month split within 2023-08..2026-10 select-odd/read-even and reverse both same sign and >= 50% uplift; H0 2023-02..07 using the Q set; ES-options analogue of the variable with the same cell, 2023-08+; newest live weeks - none exist beyond H2 end yet). wd-corr: not applicable to a size switch (reported as the correlation of the P&L increment with the base in worst-decile weeks).
FALSE-DISCOVERY: whole-procedure null = 300 replicates where each variable's rank series is replaced by a block-permuted (4-week blocks) copy and the 22 selections + H1/H2 gates re-run; the chance-pass rate per selection is reported with expected false passes = 22 x rate. Prior probability of a genuine size-timing effect from options positioning on a trend pair: 5%.
Part 2 (ERA diagnostics, no gates): weekly values (Friday-of-prior-week value and week mean) of the 11 variables vs the pair's GROSS weekly P&L (outputs/autopilot/p1/run_fixed_gross.pkl, 2019-2026): rolling 26-week Pearson/Spearman correlation (mean, % positive, range, by year), and separation of 2024-26 from 2019-23: AUC (Mann-Whitney), Cohen d, permutation p with circular shifts of the era label (>= 26 weeks offset) and iid; yearly mean vs yearly weekly Sharpe Spearman (n=8). 11 variables x 3 stats, no multiplicity correction (reported against the C8-Q1 benchmark: best |rho| 0.45, perm p 0.11).
- AMENDMENT to ASSEMBLY pool rule (2026-10-16 16:25Z, before the first non-smoke run; smoke runs only timed the code): 'leads' = books whose verdict string STARTS with LEAD, FOUND, PROVISIONAL, SURVIVOR or INSURANCE (not 'NO/LEAD', not WITHDRAWN), capped at the 3 best by clean standalone Sharpe per library family (family-level LEAD labels on screen grids otherwise admit hundreds of unselected cells). Books tagged pair-variant / class PAIR_ACCT other than ST/HM (substitutes for the pair) are not pool members. k=4 search: exhaustive on a sub-pool (top 70 by clean Sharpe + ST, HM, WT-10), plus beam extension of the best 300 triples by any 4th book (k=2,3 are exhaustive on the full pool); same procedure inside every null replicate (nbeam 100 there).
- ERRATUM (2026-10-16, DRIVERS-5; filed before any valid stage-1 result is read): the first stage-1 grid run (256 cells, clean Sharpe only, all other registered rules unchanged) used windows ending at minute em-1, but the engine's entry bar is minute em-1 (et_in is the bar-close stamp; ei = searchsorted(minute, em-1)), so the window included the entry bar itself (look-ahead; a LOW-ALIGNRUN switch showed a spurious +1.28 clean uplift). That output is discarded (kept as SW_stage1_clean_LEAKED_discarded.csv, never used for selection). Corrected rule: state windows end at the minute BEFORE the entry bar, i.e. [bar-W, bar-1] with bar = em-1 (run length ends at bar-1). The grid, thresholds, gates and selection rule are unchanged; the grid has therefore been evaluated twice (the first time on leaked features), disclosed in the multiplicity count.
OPTIONS AMENDMENT (2026-10-16T17:00Z, before any P&L scored; QC of the ES quarterly series showed the weekly-free chains have no 3-14 DTE expiry): V5 skew uses the expiry nearest 30 DTE in [14,50], else in [10,100]; V6 term for set A = ATM IV (DTE 3-14, nearest 7) / ATM IV (DTE 21-60, nearest 35); for set Q (quarterly chain only) term = ATM IV of the first expiry with DTE >= 3 / ATM IV of the first expiry >= 40 days later (quarter-to-quarter slope). No other change. Weekly NQ families (Q1A-Q3D, QN1-4, QNE) form set A together with NQ.OPT.
OPTIONS AMENDMENT 2 (same time, still before any P&L): DTE cap 200 days instead of 120 (needed so a second quarterly exists for set-Q term slope); GEX/IV use 1 <= DTE <= 200.

## BATCH K (K1..K10) + J4X, registered 2026-10-16 by worker I BEFORE any K run (only data-layer smoke tests of loaders). Source: GAPMAP UNTESTED on-disk cells not claimed by others; no data bought. All J-COMMON rules apply (Gate Amendment 2, clean-only selection, ERA flag, both framings, 500-draw own null for sleeves / 300 matched for pair-side, plateau +-25%, 2x slippage, wd corr, bootstrap; pool of 6 workers; every scored book saved with lib_save.save_book). MINC = minimum clean trades for eligibility.
### K1 CROSS-ASSET 60-MIN LEAD SLEEVE (targets NQ, ES, RTY, YM basket; leaders ZN, 6J, GC, CL, 6E). Mechanism: macro assets move first and equity indices adjust over the next hour (follow) or over-adjust (inverse). Prior 3%. MINC 100.
- x = (close of leader at the last bar <= t minus its close w minutes earlier) / ADR20(leader); signal bar every minute in 09:35-14:55 ET (leader bar must be within 3 min of t); entry at the next target bar open, exit 60 minutes later (cap 16:00 ET), one position per target; side = sign(x) (follow) or -sign(x) (inverse). Grid (60): leader {5} x w {30, 60, 120} x thr {0.15, 0.30} (|x| in ADR units) x mode {follow, inverse} x ... = 5*3*2*2 = 60. N0: random 1-minute bars in the same window, rule direction. Plateau: w x0.75/x1.25, thr x0.75/x1.25.
### K2 WALK-FORWARD INTRADAY SEASONALITY SLEEVE. Mechanism: persistent time-of-day drift per instrument, estimated only from the prior L days, traded when the t-stat is large. Prior 3%. MINC 100.
- Buckets of B minutes starting every B minutes between 03:00 and 15:30 ET; r(d,k) = open(start) to open(end) in price; m = mean of the prior L same-bucket returns, t = m/(sd/sqrt(L)); trade sign(m) for the bucket when |t| >= tmin. Grid (16): L {40, 80} x B {30, 60} x tmin {1.0, 2.0} x universe {idx: NQ ES RTY YM; oth: GC CL ZN 6E 6J}. N0: random buckets (same count per instrument), side = sign(m). Plateau: L x0.75/x1.25, B x0.75/x1.25 (rounded to multiples of 5), tmin x0.75/x1.25.
### K3 MACRO-RELEASE POST-MOVE DRIFT ON NON-NQ INSTRUMENTS (GC, ZN, 6E, CL, RTY, YM basket). Mechanism: the first 5 minutes after FOMC/NFP/CPI continue or revert. Prior 3%. MINC 20.
- Same construction as I3 post cells (r = close(T+4) - close(T-1), entry T+5 open, follow/fade) with events {FOMC 14:00, NFP 08:30, CPI 08:30 (CPI dates 2023-26 only, verified)} x mode {follow, fade} x H {30, 60} = 12 cells; each instrument one unit of ADR risk per event. N0: random days at the same clock time. Plateau: H x0.75/x1.25.
### K4 DAILY WALK-FORWARD GBM ON ES/NQ/RTY/YM WITH THE J-DRIVERS. Mechanism: nonlinear combination of price, VIX-state, COT-crowding and calendar features predicts the next 1-3 day direction. Prior 3%. MINC 100.
- Pooled panel of 4 instruments at the 16:16 label px. Features (all known at the label): log returns 1/3/5/10/20 d divided by ADR-based vol, distance to SMA50/SMA200 in ADR units, ADR20/ADR60, VIX pct (252d), VIX/VIX3M, VVIX pct, 5-day VIX change, COT lev and am net/OI pct rank (156w, effective as-of+7d) of the instrument (equity index contracts), day-of-week, opex-week flag, month-end flag, instrument id. Target: sign of the px change over N days. Walk-forward: expanding train on rows whose target is fully realised before the retrain date, retrain every calendar quarter from 2016-01 (>= 3 years of data), LightGBM; no tuning on any span other than the fixed grid.
- Grid (16): model {d2: depth 2, 150 trees, lr 0.05; d4: depth 4, 300 trees, lr 0.03} x N {1, 3} x delta {0.04, 0.08} x mode {longshort, longonly}: trade when |p-0.5| >= delta (long-only: only p >= 0.5+delta), hold N days. N0: random days (same count), side = sign(p-0.5). Plateau: delta x0.75/x1.25 and N x0.75/x1.25 (N=1 skipped).
### K5 TREASURY CURVE SPREAD SLEEVE (ZT, ZF, ZN, ZB). Mechanism: curve spreads mean-revert intraday-to-weekly or trend with policy repricing; stream uncorrelated with equity-index intraday trend. Prior 3%. MINC 40.
- Spread S = px_A/ADRref_A - px_B/ADRref_B in ADR units (ADRref = median ADR20 of the clean span per leg, so each leg carries equal ADR risk). Spreads {ZN-ZT, ZB-ZN, ZB-ZF}; signals at the 16:16 px: mr_k = z-score of S vs its prior 20-day mean/sd; trade against when |z| >= k (k in {1.5, 2.0}); trend = sign of the 20-day change of S, always in market; hold N days {5, 10}. Costs: 1 tick/side on each leg + $4.36 per contract per leg (leg costs expressed in ADR units). Grid (18): spread 3 x signal {mr1.5, mr2.0, trend} x N {5, 10}. N0: random days, side by the sign of the rule's signal. Plateau: k x0.75/x1.25, N x0.75/x1.25.
### K6 EIA WEEKLY ENERGY RELEASES (CL, HO, RB Wednesday 10:30 ET; NG Thursday 10:30 ET). Mechanism: inventory surprise moves energy futures; the first 5 minutes continue or revert. Prior 4%. MINC 30.
- Event day = every Wednesday (NG: Thursday); an event is CONFIRMED only if the volume of the 10:30 bar >= 2.5x the mean volume of the 30 bars before it (holiday-shifted releases fail the test and are skipped; no calendar lookup). r = close(10:34 bar) - close(10:29 bar); entry 10:35 open; follow/fade; exit +30, +60 min or 14:25 ET. Grid (24): instrument {CL, HO, RB, NG} x mode x H {30, 60, close}. N0: random same-weekday days, rule direction. Plateau: H x0.75/x1.25 (close skipped).
### K7 ISM RELEASE DRIFT (NQ, ES, ZN, 6E basket). Mechanism: 10:00 ET ISM releases move rates, FX and equities. Rule-based dates: ISM manufacturing = first trading label of the month, ISM services = third trading label (documented approximation; unverified calendar). Prior 3%. MINC 20.
- r = close(10:04 bar) - close(09:59 bar), entry 10:05 open, follow/fade, exit +30, +60 or 16:00. Grid (12): event {ism_mfg, ism_svc} x mode x H {30, 60, close}. N0: random days at 10:00. Plateau: H x0.75/x1.25.
### K8 DAILY WALK-FORWARD GBM ON GC, CL, ZN, 6E, 6J. Same as K4 (same features except the COT classes spec/hedge of the instrument's own contract; VIX features kept). Prior 3%. MINC 100. Grid (16) as K4.
### K9 VARIANCE-RISK-PREMIUM SPREAD SLEEVE (ES, NQ, RTY, YM). Mechanism: when implied vol (VIX) exceeds trailing realised vol of the S&P by a wide margin, index returns are higher (premium harvest); when the spread is negative, risk is under-priced. Prior 4%. MINC 40.
- spread = VIX - 100*sqrt(252)*std(ES daily log returns over 20 labels) at the 16:16 px of the label (VIX 16:15 close); pr = percentile rank over the prior 252 labels; long when pr >= band, short when pr <= 1-band (mode longshort) or flat (longonly); hold N days. Grid (8): band {0.8, 0.9} x mode {longshort, longonly} x N {2, 5}. N0: random days, long (and sign(pr-0.5) for longshort). Plateau: tail (1-band) x0.75/x1.25, N x0.75/x1.25.
### K10 ROUND-NUMBER LEVEL-AHEAD CONDITIONING OF THE PAIR (pair-side). Mechanism: NQ round numbers act as support/resistance; entries with a round level just ahead in the trade direction have worse follow-through. Prior 3%. MINCH 0.05.
- For each pair row: dist = distance from the entry price to the next multiple of s ahead in the trade direction (long: up; short: down). Rows with dist <= d get multiplier m. Grid (18): s {100, 250, 500} points x d {5, 10, 20} points x m {0.5, 0}. Null: permute the flag across rows (300 draws). Plateau: d x0.75/x1.25, m x0.75/x1.25 (m=0 -> 0.25 only).
### J4X J4 FOLLOW-UP (REPORT ONLY, no gate): quad-witching/rebalance Friday close -> next close long (and the other three windows, long and short) on ES, RTY, YM, NQ separately, 2013-22 vs 2023-26 and per year, with gross / fixed / ADR-scaled cost decomposition and event counts; also the monthly-opex and month-end analogues. Purpose: ERA flag and cross-instrument replication.

## H6 (D06 clock/calendar at weekly-monthly horizons x pair sizing/risk; worker H6; registered 2026-10-16 before first run)
Ledger check: G6b (MON/FRI/OPEX-week/triple-witching-week/last-2-days size tilt, NO), J5 (opex/quad/Russell/month-end tilt, NO), G4 (BTC calendar, NO) are NOT repeated. New observables: week-of-month and turn-of-month window (H6a), month-of-quarter and quarter-end window (H6b), holiday-week/pre-post-holiday and post-roll-week risk reduction (H6c). A month-of-year effect (12 levels) is NOT tested: the clean span has 2 observations per calendar month, the grouping into month-of-quarter and quarter-end is the only defensible resolution, and a month-of-year effect cannot use alternating months (each month would appear in only one parity); H6b therefore uses alternating CALENDAR QUARTERS (quarter-index parity; select on odd quarters, read on even, then reverse), and alternating years is infeasible (3 years). H6a and H6c use the standard alternating-month split.
Common: engine = live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) re-run in a clean process via B.Cfg.sizefn with a per-trade multiplier by entry label day (ap_g6_core machinery); baseline must reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30. Fill: 1 tick/side + $34.88/8-lot RT, 2x for slippage. Gates unchanged (clean/H1/H2 uplift >= +0.10 each, worst week, own null = 100 permutations of day-level multipliers across days (same multiset), bootstrap, plateau of m deviation x0.75/x1.25 and window edge +-25% where applicable, 2x slip, H0 + alternating split replication). Selection = max clean uplift over the family grid; H1/H2 read once for the carried cell. ES/RTY replication unavailable (pair is NQ-only). ERA: n/a pre-2023.
- H6a (M4/M8, H5-H6; prior 4%). Week-of-month and turn-of-month size tilt. Mechanism: month-start/month-end flow (payroll, pension and fund rebalancing, CTA monthly resets) changes trend persistence in the first days of the month; week-of-month proxies the monthly flow cycle. GRID (10 cells): WOM1 (entry day-of-month 1-7), WOM2 (8-14), WOM3 (15-21), WOM4 (22-end) x m in {0.5,1.5}; TOM (last trading day of the month plus the first 3 trading days of the next) x m in {0.5,1.5}. Multipliers mean-normalised on clean (risk-neutral). Plateau: m deviation x0.75/x1.25.
- H6b (M4/M8, H6; prior 3%). Quarter-phase size tilt. Mechanism: the earnings/guidance cycle (month 1 of a quarter, the heavy-earnings month, carries single-stock-driven index moves) and quarter-end rebalancing (last 10 trading days of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec: pension and balanced-fund rebalancing, window dressing) alter trend persistence. GRID (8 cells): MQ1/MQ2/MQ3 (month of calendar quarter by entry label) x m in {0.5,1.5}; QEND (last 10 trading days of a calendar quarter) x m in {0.5,1.5}. Mean-normalised on clean. Alternating-quarter replication as above.
- H6c (M7, H3/H5; prior 4%). Holiday-week and post-roll-week risk rule (reduce only, NOT mean-normalised, so it is a pure risk cut). Mechanism: holiday-shortened weeks have thin liquidity and gap/early-close risk; the week starting at the quarterly roll (Thursday 8 days before expiry, i.e. second Thursday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec) has contract-migration noise and thinner front-month depth. Distinct from R9 E3 (event-day avoid filters) because it is calendar-structural and acts as a size cut applied through the live rules. Holidays: CME equity full-closure dates 2023-2026 (listed in the code; Good Friday included, Jan 9 2025 excluded). GRID (6 cells): HOLW (entry day in an ISO week containing a closure) , HOLADJ (entry day is the last trading day before or first after a closure) , ROLLW (the 5 trading days beginning at the roll Thursday) x m in {0, 0.5}. Null: day-level multiplier permutations. Plateau: m deviation (x0.75/x1.25 of the cut).
Report IDs H6a, H6b, H6c. Expected: NO on all three (priors above); the usual failure is a clean gain that reverses on H1/H2.

## H11 batch 2 (D11 price patterns x M7/M8, NQ pair-side; worker H11, 2026-10-16). Written before any state was joined to pair rows. Baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) clean process; engine, fills, gates, selection (clean 2023-08..2025-07 only, H1/H2 once), plateau, 2x slip, bootstrap, alt-month, H0, as REPORT_G11_B/G0a. Overlap notes: G0a tested consecutive-stop halts (any duration) and failed to a random-halt null; G11-A/B tested location and prior-day range states; H11 tests different observables (intraday path efficiency/run state, stop SPEED, weekly state).
### H11-A TREND-VS-CHOP SIZE TILT (M8, H1/H2). Mechanism: the pair is a stop-and-reverse breakout/trend system; entries made when the last 30-60 min path was efficient (directional) get follow-through, entries inside choppy tape get whipsawed. Prior 5%. MINCH 0.05.
- Features at the entry bar (bars strictly before it): ER30 = |c[-1]-c[-31]|/sum|1-min changes| over 30 bars; ER60 same over 60; SER60 = side*(c[-1]-c[-61])/sum|changes| (aligned signed efficiency, in [-1,1]); RUN = signed count (cap 12) of consecutive same-direction closed 5-min steps ending at the last bar, positive when aligned with the trade side, negative when opposed. Tail edges from clean entries (30th/70th percentile). Multiplier renormalised to mean 1 over clean entries (risk-neutral, as G11-B).
- Grid (8): feature {ER30, ER60, SER60, RUN} x rule {CUT: bottom-30% tail x0.5 ; ADD: top-30% tail x1.5}. Null N0: feature permuted within year x book x entry hour (200 replicates of the WHOLE grid, linear screen, clean-only selection rule), statistic mean(H1,H2) uplift. Plateau: tail share 22.5%/37.5%. Alt-month: select on odd months, read even and reverse.
### H11-B WHIPSAW DETECTOR DAY HALT (M7, H1-H3). Mechanism: when entries stop out within minutes, the tape is whipsawing (spread of reversals), and later same-day entries have lower edge; a fast-stop count detects it better than a plain stop count. Prior 3% (G0a plain consecutive stops failed; the new element is stop speed). MINCH 0.05 of clean raw entries touched.
- State before entry t: n = closed (exit <= t) pair trades of the same label day whose exit name contains Stop and whose life <= X minutes. Rule: n >= N -> skip (HALT) or size x0.5 (HALF) for the remaining entries of the day.
- Grid (12): N {2,3} x X {10,20,40} min x action {HALT, HALF}. Null N0: matched random-halt (same fired days, random entry index within the day as the break, same action), 100 replicates, statistic clean/H1/H2 uplift. Plateau: N+-1, X x0.75/x1.25.
### H11-C WEEKLY RANGE-COMPRESSION / WEEKLY-EFFICIENCY SIZE TILT (M8, H6). Mechanism: after a compressed week (range below its 8-week mean) the next week expands and the breakout pair is rewarded; after weeks of efficient weekly trend (ER over 8 weekly closes high) trend entries persist. Prior 3% (G11-B daily NR7 contra; ~100 clean weeks = low power, stated). MINCH 0.05.
- Week = label-day ISO week. At Monday open: WR = range(w-1)/mean(range(w-9..w-2)) (full-session high-low by label day max/min within the week); WER = |wc(w-1)-wc(w-9)|/sum|wc changes| over 8 weekly closes (last bar of the week). Tail edges from clean entries (33rd/67th percentile of entries). Multiplier renormalised to mean 1 on clean.
- Grid (8): feature {WR, WER} x tail {LOW, HIGH} x a {0.5, 1.5}. Null N0: week state labels permuted among weeks within the same year (200 replicates of the whole grid, linear screen). Plateau: a scaled 0.75/1.25 of (a-1), tail share 25%/42%. Alt-month: as above.

## BATCH H8 (H8-A, H8-B, H8-C), registered 2026-10-16T17:55Z by worker H8 BEFORE any H8 run (only loader/smoke checks). Source: GAPMAP UNTESTED D08 H5/H6 M7 NQ and D12 H5/H6 M4/M8 NQ. Closed neighbours not repeated: G8-A/B/C/D (intraday rv ratio, overnight range/gap, vol-scaled DAY lock, option volume), J8 (VIX/term/VVIX/VIX9D label-level SIZE switch), B14 (entry-level meta-labelling), G0b (equity-DD size). Engine: b11_nt8 clean process, baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30; 1 tick/side + commission, 2x slippage test; ET clock; pool of 5. Gates unchanged (uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2 separately, worst week not worse, matched random-flag null p95 (200 draws, same number of flagged weeks), bootstrap 90% CI > 0, plateau +-25% (>=75% neighbours keep >=75% of all-in uplift), 2x slippage, wd corr reported, NT8 spec). Selection on clean (2023-08..2025-07) weekly-Sharpe uplift only; H1/H2 read once. REPLICATION (pre-registered, only if the clean/H1/H2 gates are met): alternating-month split in 2023-02..2026-10 (cell chosen on odd calendar months' weeks, read on even and reverse) + H0 2023-02..07; ES/RTY not applicable (pair rows NQ). Selection-aware null: 12 flag-permuted replicates of the whole grid (best clean uplift). Weeks = ISO week of label-day exit (WKO). State for a week uses only label days strictly before the week's first label day. Whole sample incl. pre-2023 not scored (no pair rows).
### H8-A REALISED-VOL WEEK FLOOR (M7, D08 H5/H6). Mechanism: the live week floor is a fixed -8R; when realised vol is high the same R is hit by smaller relative moves (floor too tight, cuts winners' recovery) or conversely high-vol weeks carry the fat-tail loss weeks and want a tighter floor; scaling the floor with the vol state is a risk rule that costs nothing in normal weeks. Prior 3%.
- State = percentile rank (over the prior 252 label days) of RV20 (std of daily NQ close-to-close points-returns over 20 label days) at the last label day before the week. Grid (24): dir/P {HI: rank>P in 50,67,80; LO: rank<P in 20,33,50} x floor on flagged weeks F in {4,6,10,12} R (unflagged weeks keep 8R). Null: permute flags over weeks (same count), rule floors unchanged. Plateau: P x0.75/x1.25, F x0.75/x1.25.
### H8-B WEEK-LEVEL WALK-FORWARD CLASSIFIER SIZE (M8 + M4, D12 H5/H6). Mechanism: a regularised linear model on 10 daily state features known at the week start predicts the sign of the pair's next-week P&L; weeks predicted bad get half size. B14 was entry-level GBM meta-labelling; this is week-level and linear. Prior 2%.
- Features (week start, causal): NQ 5d and 20d return / 20d daily vol; RV20 percentile(252); RV5/RV20; VIX pct(252); VIX/VIX3M; VVIX pct(252); prior-week pair P&L / trailing-52w std; mean of prior 4 weeks pair P&L / std; distance of close to SMA50 in daily-vol units. Training target: weekly sum of the p1 pair daily series (outputs/autopilot/p1/run_fixed_gross.pkl, gross fixed-slip pair replica 2013-2026, ERA caveat: used only as label history, never scored), training = all weeks ended before the test week, refit every week, min 156 weeks, features standardised on the training window. Grid (16): model {ridge a=100, ridge a=1000, logit L2 C=0.05, logit C=0.5} x window {expanding, 260w} x threshold {neutral: yhat<0 / p<0.5; conservative: yhat<-0.25 sd_train / p<0.45}; flagged weeks size m=0.5. Null: permute flags over weeks (same count). Plateau: alpha(C) x0.75/x1.25, window x0.75/x1.25 (195/325w), m 0.375/0.625.
### H8-C VX-TERM WEEK FLOOR (M7, D08 H5/H6). As H8-A with state = percentile rank (prior 252 label days) of VIX/VIX3M at the last label day before the week (term slope; high = flat/backwardated). Mechanism: inverted VX term = stress regime where weekly tails of a trend pair are fatter, so a tighter floor insures; steep contango = calm, loosen. Prior 3%. Same 24-cell grid, null, plateau as H8-A.

## H7 - D07 cross-asset conditioning/management of the live pair, NQ (3 families; registered 2026-10-16 ~18:10Z BEFORE any feature was joined to pair rows or any resim run; only data loading/alignment checks and baseline reproduction allowed)
Not re-skins: B9 (ES/RTY 5-min TREND agreement as size, 12 cells, NO) used trend-state agreement; C1-F5 (ZN/6J/BTC context FILTERS at entry, 54 cells, NO) and G10-3 (ZN/6J 60-min sigma HALT, 9 cells, NO) used macro-asset moves as skip/halt. H7 uses (A) the NQ-minus-ES/RTY RELATIVE-STRENGTH spread (an equity-internal divergence, not a trend state) as entry size tilt, (B) the same spread as a MID-TRADE EXIT trigger (M5, never tested for D07), (C) a DAILY composite risk-on/off state from ZN/6J/GC and a copper/oil growth proxy as a SIZE tilt (not halt/filter).
Common: engine = live pair via b11_nt8 B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), clean process (d3_common first; ap_b17_core/ap_g6_core machinery), reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30 before any cell. Fill 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant. Overlays NOT renormalised (net change; Sharpe scale-free). Features causal: 1-min closes of bars with open time < entry minute (ES/RTY/ZN/6J/GC/CL/HG from outputs/autopilot/i/m1_*.npz back-adjusted, forward-filled to the harness minute grid, staleness > 5 min -> feature off, multiplier 1). Spans: clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 selection; H1 2025-08-01..2026-02-06 and H2 2026-02-07..2026-10-13 read ONCE for the carried cell. UNTOUCHED never. Pair rows start 2023-08, so H0 (2023-02..07) and pre-2023 are not available: ERA FLAG 'not testable' (stated). Selection: the cell with the largest clean weekly-Sharpe uplift is carried whatever its sign. Gates as G8/G10 (pair-modification form): uplift >= +0.10 on clean/H1/H2, worst week (all-in) not worse, matched random-switch null p95 (100 reps) + weekly bootstrap 90% CI > 0, plateau (+-25% of threshold/k/multiplier distance, >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of uplift), 2x slippage >= +0.10 each, wd corr n/a (no hedge claim), NT8 spec. REPLICATION (reported for every carried cell, needed for FOUND): alternating-month split pre-registered here (select cell on ODD label-months of 2023-08..2026-10, read EVEN months, then reverse; weekly Sharpe from the daily series of days in those months); H0 n/a; cross-instrument = ref instrument dimension (ES vs RTY) inside A/B (carried cell's twin on the other ref must have same sign) ; newest live weeks as they arrive.
### H7-A  NQ-vs-ES/RTY relative-strength divergence as entry size tilt (D07 x M4/M8, H2-H4; prior any cell passes all gates 4%)
Mechanism: index futures lead/lag on minutes-to-hours; when NQ has run in the trade direction much more than ES (or RTY) over the last L minutes the move is NQ-idiosyncratic (mega-cap flow) and more reversal-prone, when ES/RTY confirm (NQ spread not stretched) the trend is broad and persists. Feature RS = s * (ln NQ_c[ei-1]/NQ_c[ei-1-L] - ln REF_c[ei-1]/REF_c[ei-1-L]) / (sigma_spread_1m * sqrt(L)), sigma = std of the 1-min NQ-REF log-return spread over the previous 1300 bars; s = trade side. Cells: REF in {ES, RTY} x L in {30, 60} x action in {A1: RS above clean-entry 70th pct -> 0.5 (NQ over-extended vs REF), A2: RS above p70 -> skip, A3: RS below clean p30 (NQ lagging REF in trade direction = REF-led move) -> 1.5} = 12 cells. Edges frozen from clean entries. Plateau: percentile 70/30 -> 62.5/77.5 (and 37.5/22.5), multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25. Null: RS values permuted among entries within (year x book x ET hour) blocks, 100 reps.
### H7-B  exit when NQ/REF relative-strength confirmation breaks mid-trade (D07 x M5, H2-H4; prior 3%)
Mechanism: H7-A's divergence measured since entry: if NQ has moved in the trade's favour by much more than ES/RTY since entry the profit is NQ-specific and fades; exiting banks it. (Control direction included: REF ahead of NQ since entry.) Rule: from minute ei+5 to xi-1 evaluate at each bar close: Dv = s * (ln NQ_c[j]/NQ_c[ei-1] - ln REF_c[j]/REF_c[ei-1]) / (sigma_spread_1m*sqrt(j-ei+1)); direction NQAHEAD: first j with Dv >= k; REFAHEAD: first j with Dv <= -k; exit at open of bar j+1 (B.o, trade stays live in engine otherwise; same patch machinery as G6c flat_apply: XI, PTS0, time-out/label rewrites, locks re-run). Cells: REF {ES, RTY} x dir {NQAHEAD, REFAHEAD} x k {1.5, 2.0, 3.0} = 12. Plateau k x0.75/x1.25. Null: for the same exited trades, exit at a uniformly random bar inside the trade, 100 reps.
### H7-C  daily cross-asset risk-on/off composite and growth proxy as size tilt (D07 x M8, H5; prior 4%)
Mechanism: macro risk appetite measured by safe-haven (ZN, 6J, GC) and growth (HG, CL) trailing returns sets whether equity trends persist (risk-on) or whipsaw (risk-off); a size tilt (not halt) is the continuous version of G10-3/C1-F5. State known at the 17:00 ET close of the previous label day: R_L = L-day return (L in {5, 20} sessions of 17:00 ET closes) of each asset / (trailing 60-day daily sigma * sqrt(L)). SAFE = -(R_ZN + R_6J + R_GC)/3 (high = risk-on), GROW = (R_HG + R_CL)/2, NET = (SAFE + GROW)/2. Terciles from clean label days, frozen. Schemes: DOWN (bottom tercile -> 0.5), UP (top tercile -> 1.5), TWO (bottom 0.75, top 1.25). Cells: feature {SAFE, GROW, NET} x L {5, 20} x scheme {DOWN, UP, TWO} = 18. Plateau: tercile 33 -> 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25. Null: state labels permuted among label days, 100 reps.
Report IDs H7_A, H7_B, H7_C. Executability: NT8 multi-instrument strategy (ES/RTY/macro feeds in the same workspace) scaling quantity or issuing a market exit; margin unchanged (multipliers <= 1.5 raise peak contracts only on flagged entries).

## H10 (registered 2026-10-16 by worker H10, BEFORE any H10 P&L run; only event-row counting done: rows open at release FOMC 33 / NFP 50 / CPI 41 of 13,089 pair rows, 2023-08..2026-10)
### H10-COMMON
- Verified calendars only (ap_c2_g3_events FOMC_V 14:00 ET, NFP_V and CPI_V 08:30 ET, 2023-08-01..2026-10-13). NO ECB. Baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) clean process, reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30 before grid. Fill: 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots; 2x slip = SLIP_PT 1.0 on both. Selection clean only, ONE top cell carried, H1/H2 read once. Gates as BRIEF_COMMON (uplift >=+0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, matched-null p95, bootstrap CI>0, plateau x0.75/x1.25 of every numeric parameter >=75% keep >=75% uplift, 2x slip). Replication: pair rows start 2023-08 (no H0/pre-2023); alternating-month split pre-registered (select on odd months, read even, then reverse) + newest weeks (H2). Era flag: n/a (pair rows 2023+). Power note: events touch ~3% of pair days, so a +0.10 weekly-Sharpe uplift needs a large per-event effect; low prior is accepted.
### H10-A  EXIT-ALL / FLATTEN AT RELEASE (D10 x M5, H1-H4)
- Mechanism: positions held through a scheduled print carry gap risk that the pair's 60-pt bracket does not control; flattening just before/at the print converts jump variance into a known small cost. Prior 4% (positive-mean trend legs through prints may be the pair's fat tail winners).
- Implementation: minute/1-second replay of pair rows (ap_g11_c_core.rs): a row open at flatten bar fi (entry bar <= fi < original exit bar) is exited at close of bar fi (market, 1 tick slippage as for any exit), 60/60 bracket checked on 1-s bars until then; other rows unchanged; later pair signals unchanged. Re-run through the live account (lock stages) with modified rows.
- Grid (8 cells): events {FOMC; FOMC+NFP+CPI} x flatten offset from print {-5 min, 0 min} x scope {all open; only positions in unrealised loss at fi}. Null: same count of pseudo-events on random non-event... any pair days at the same clock time, same offset/scope, 100 draws (linear delta-P&L approximation for null only, as G11-C). Plateau: offset x0.75/x1.25 (-5 -> -3.75/-6.25 min rounded; 0 stays 0 so use +-1 min for the 0 offset).
### H10-B  EVENT-DAY TIGHTER DAY LOCK (D10 x M7, H3-H4)
- Mechanism: on FOMC/NFP/CPI days the realised daily range is fat-tailed; a tighter per-book daily loss/win limit and combined cap on those days (and only those) trims the bad-day tail. Prior 4% (G8-C vol-scaled tighter lock failed on H1; G10-1 tilt NO).
- Implementation: per-book day_loss/day_win in pass1 and combined cap in lock_stage varied on event label days only (live 5R/-2R, combined cap 8R, give-back arm 2R keep 33%).
- Grid (6 cells, FNC days only): (day_loss,day_win) in {(1.0,5),(1.5,5),(2,3),(1.0,3),(1.5,3)} and combined cap 5R with live per-book. Null: same count of random label days, same parameters, 100 draws. Plateau: tightened parameter x0.75/x1.25.
### H10-C  EVENT HARD-SKIP AND POST-RELEASE SIDE SWITCH (D10 x M4, H1-H4)
- Mechanism: (i) skip = the m=0 extreme of G10 tilts; (ii) switch: after a print the first 5-minute reaction (close T+4 vs close T-1) sets the information direction; pair entries opposing it in [T+5, T+W) are suppressed (continuation) or aligned entries suppressed (fade of overshoot). Prior 3%.
- Grid (6 cells): S1 skip all FNC-day entries; S2 skip entries in [T-60,T+60); A1 oppose-skip W=60; A2 oppose-skip W=240; F1 aligned-skip W=60; F2 aligned-skip W=240 (reaction = sign of NQ close(T+4)-close(T-1), zero-reaction -> no skip). Null: pseudo-releases on random pair days, same clock times and count, 100 draws. Plateau: windows x0.75/x1.25.
- Closing: UPD lines for D10 M5 (A), M7 (B), M4 (C) at H1-H4.

## H0 batch (D00 pair-internal state; modes M4/M5/M6, horizons H0-H5; worker H0; registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any H0 variant return was computed; only code reading and hook-identity checks)
Ledger check: not repeated - G0a/b/c (consecutive-stop/entry-count halts, equity-DD size, per-side loss limit), B15 (post-lock continuation), B16/B17/B18/B19 (week/vol/prior-day/ST-HM tilts), C2-G1 (breakeven/trail/scale/time stop exits), D2 E5 (limit/chase/cancel entries up to 300 s, 0-10 s delay, target models), G11 (location, range-state, swing stop), B11 T2 2a (single cell sec_same 0.75 in the H2-only era, no clean selection; 2c netcap). New: graded stacking multipliers (H0a), time-clustered post-stop-out re-entry multipliers (H0b), a second give-back tier on the combined day lock (H0c), minutes-scale confirmation delay of entries on the 1-second engine (H0d).
Common: engine = live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), clean process (d3_common / ap_g0_core pass1 with hook), baseline 1.46/2.83/2.30 reproduced first (H0d: G11-C 1-second control must reproduce the live rows). Fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant. State uses only accepted trades already closed (TOUT <= entry time) or open at the entry time. Spans clean 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 (selection), H1/H2 read once for the single carried cell. Selection: highest clean weekly Sharpe uplift among cells that change >= 5% of clean entries (else all cells, flagged), ONE cell carried. Gates (all): uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week (all-in) not worse; own null p95 < real all-in uplift (100 draws); paired weekly bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau (numeric parameters deviation-scaled x0.75/x1.25 one at a time, multipliers as 1+(m-1)x; m=0 -> 0.25; >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift); 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; NT8 spec + margin; insurance metrics and constant-risk read. Replication reported (Amend. 2): H0 window 2023-02-10..2023-07-31 where pair rows exist, alternating-month split (select on odd-month weeks, read on even, and reverse; cells' uplifts recomputed on masked days). ES/RTY replication not available (pair engine NQ only). ERA flag: n/a (pair rows start 2023-02). Boost cells (m > 1) raise risk and are marked; they are not normalised.
- H0a STACKING (M4/M8, H1-H3; prior 5%). Mechanism: when ST and HM are open in the same direction the account carries 2R of correlated risk on one NQ move; the second entrant at full size doubles variance without doubling edge (cut), or alternatively concurrence is a conviction signal (boost). Rule: at an entry, if the OTHER book has an accepted open position (TOUT > t): same direction -> multiply by a, opposite direction -> multiply by b. GRID (11): a {0, 0.5, 1.5} x b {1, 0.5, 0, 1.5} minus (1,1)... precisely a in {1,0,0.5,1.5} x b in {1,0.5,0,1.5} minus (1,1). Null: circular shift (100 random offsets) of the realised per-entry multiplier vector (same frequency/size distribution). Plateau: a, b deviation x0.75/x1.25.
- H0b RE-ENTRY CLUSTER AFTER A STOP-OUT (M4/M7, H1-H2; prior 4%). Mechanism: a signal that fires within N minutes of a stop-out is either a whipsaw re-trigger (chop; cut) or a fresh impulse after liquidity was swept (fine/boost); G0a tested only 'n consecutive stops -> halt rest of day', not a time window around the last stop. Rule: at an entry, if any accepted trade of either book exited by a stop within the previous N minutes (exit time in [t-N, t]) and its side equals (same) / differs from (opp) the entry side, multiply by m. GRID (18): side {same, opp} x N {5, 15, 45} min x m {0, 0.5, 1.5}. Null: circular shift of the multiplier vector (100). Plateau: N x0.75/x1.25 (rounded), m deviation x0.75/x1.25.
- H0c SECOND GIVE-BACK TIER ON THE COMBINED DAY LOCK (M5, H1-H4; prior 6%). Mechanism: the live day lock gives back 67% of a peak >= 2R before flattening; for large peaks (>= 3-5R) that donates more than the entire typical remaining edge, so a tighter tier after a high peak banks more of the best days (fires between arm and the 8R cap). Rule (day lock only, week/month unchanged): additional trigger when day peak equity >= A R and equity <= peak x k2 (k2 = fraction kept). GRID (9): A {3, 4, 5} R x k2 {0.5, 0.67, 0.8}. Lock effect as live (flatten, no more entries that day). Null: 100 draws adding, on each real day-group, an extra lock at a uniformly random grid minute with probability = share of days where the selected tier fired earlier than the live trigger (random extra locks, same rate). Plateau: A x0.75/x1.25, k2 deviation from the live 0.33 x0.75/x1.25.
- H0d ENTRY CONFIRMATION DELAY ON THE 1-SECOND ENGINE (M6, H0-H2; prior 2%). Mechanism: many 1-minute flips are immediately rejected; waiting d minutes and entering only if price has moved >= c pts in the trade direction since the signal fill removes false breaks at the price of a worse fill (D2 E5 tested limits/chases/cancels to 300 s but not move-confirmation). Rule: the row's entry is re-done at the open of minute ei+d (stop/target 60/60 pts from the NEW fill, exactly as a live bracket), skipped if the signal row had already exited (own bracket / external exit) before ei+d or if s*(open[ei+d]-E0) < c; entry timestamps shifted by d minutes in the account (state/locks see the delayed trade). Rows closed externally keep the external exit price. GRID (12): d {1, 2, 3} x c {none, 0, 5, 10} pts. Control: d=0 must reproduce the live rows. Null: same delay d, same number of skipped rows chosen uniformly at random (100 draws, exact account). Plateau: d x0.75/x1.25 (rounded to minutes: 3 -> 2,4; 2 -> 2 (1.5 rounds to 2),2.5->2; so d in {1,2,3} neighbours d-1,d+1), c x0.75/x1.25.
Report IDs H0a, H0b, H0c, H0d.
### K4P SUPPLEMENTARY (registered 2026-10-16 AFTER the K4 result was seen; disclosed as post-hoc in the report; report-only, it cannot upgrade K4 by itself). K4 failed ONLY the plateau gate (50% of 2 neighbours: delta 0.06 +0.333, delta 0.10 +0.127 vs selected +0.203; N=1 cannot be scaled). Reason for the supplement: for a GBM the plateau gate as written ignores model hyperparameters.
