- Extended plateau (all-in 20% uplift; gate rule unchanged: >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of the selected uplift): the K4 selected cell (d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly) with one change at a time: delta x0.75/x1.25 (0.06, 0.10), n_estimators x0.75/x1.25 (112, 188), learning rate x0.75/x1.25 (0.0375, 0.0625), and three subsample seeds (random_state 1, 2, 3). 9 neighbours.
- Replication battery (report only): (a) per-instrument sleeves (ES, NQ, RTY, YM separately, same rule) 2023-08..2026-10: standalone weekly Sharpe and 20%-share uplift sign; (b) H0 window 2023-02-10..2023-07-31: additive 20% uplift vs the pair rows; (c) alternating-month split of 2023-08..2026-10: additive 20% uplift on odd-numbered months and on even-numbered months separately, using the SAME selected cell (the cell was selected on clean; this is a stability check, not an independent selection); (d) walk-forward OOS 2016-2022 years of the same cell (ERA, already in REPORT_K4.md).

## BATCH L (K4v2 first, then L2..L10), registered 2026-10-16 by worker I. K4v2 is registered BEFORE any K4v2 run; its design uses the K4 report and K4P as the only prior information (disclosed: the K4 cell was selected on the clean span from 16 cells, and K4P showed H1/H2).
### K4v2 - CONFIRMATION TEST OF THE DAILY WALK-FORWARD GBM LONG-ONLY EQUITY-INDEX MODEL
- Pre-declared hypothesis cell (fixed now, NOT re-selected): model d2 (LightGBM max_depth 2, num_leaves 4, n_estimators 150, learning_rate 0.05, subsample 0.8, colsample 0.8, min_child 50, random_state 0), target = sign of the 1-day px change (px = last 1-minute close at or before 16:16 ET), features exactly as K4 (ap_k_fams.features, universe idx), walk-forward retrained each quarter from 2016-01 on rows whose target was realised before the retrain date, trade long ES, NQ, RTY, YM at the 16:16 px when p >= 0.5 + delta, delta = 0.08, exit at the next label's 16:16 px, one ADR-dollar of risk per trade, costs 1 tick/side + $4.36 per contract RT. Code frozen: ap_k_fams.K4 / ml_preds (unchanged).
- Full grid for the selection-corrected null: 32 cells = model {d2, d4} x N {1, 3} x delta {0.04, 0.06, 0.08, 0.10} x mode {longshort, longonly}. Clean-span (2023-08..2025-07) weekly-Sharpe selection over this grid is REPORTED (is the pre-declared cell the clean winner?) and a selection-aware null is computed: 100 random-entry replicates (random days, same count per instrument per cell, long sign by the cell rule's direction function = sign(p-0.5)) of the whole 32-cell clean selection; the real best clean Sharpe is ranked against the replicate bests (report; a rank < 0.95 is stated as a caveat).
- HYPERPARAMETER PLATEAU (gate 5, defined up front): exact +-25% one-at-a-time neighbours of the pre-declared cell: delta 0.06 and 0.10; n_estimators 112 and 188; learning_rate 0.0375 and 0.0625; plus 3 other random_state seeds (1, 2, 3) = 9 neighbours; metric = all-in 20% additive uplift; PASS = >= 75% of neighbours (>= 7 of 9) keep >= 75% of the pre-declared cell's uplift. (N and depth cannot be scaled +-25% to a different integer and are fixed.)
- Gates on the pre-declared cell (headline 20%): uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week within the share; bootstrap 90% CI of the all-in uplift > 0; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 on each span; worst-decile corr <= +0.30; plus THREE nulls on standalone all-in weekly Sharpe, all required at p95: (N0a) long-only random-entry null (1,000 draws: random days, same count per instrument, long, 1-day hold); (N0b) DRIFT-MATCHED null (1,000 draws: random days with the same count per instrument per calendar month and the same hold, long) which removes the bull-market drift exposure of a long-only sleeve; (N0c) beta test: regress the sleeve's daily P&L on the unconditional always-long basket P&L (same instruments, same unit risk, every label) over 2023-08..2026-10 and report the alpha weekly Sharpe of the residual and its bootstrap 90% CI (CI lower bound > 0 required).
- ERA: walk-forward out-of-sample years 2016-2022 reported per year with gross / fixed / ADR-scaled cost decomposition and the flag.
- REPLICATION (needs at least TWO of the following to hold with the same sign and >= 50% of the pre-declared cell's all-in 20% uplift): (R1) newest live weeks after data extension: NOT AVAILABLE (bars and pair rows end 2026-10-13, fewer than one new week) - stated and not counted; (R2) H0 2023-02-10..2023-07-31 additive 20% uplift; (R3) cross-instrument: a model trained ONLY on NQ and ES rows (no instrument id feature) applied to RTY and YM, additive 20% uplift of the RTY+YM sleeve (scaled by the pre-declared cell's factor f) over the pair, 2023-08..2026-10; (R4) alternating-month split: select the best cell of the 32-cell grid on ODD calendar months of 2023-08..2026-10 (standalone weekly Sharpe over odd-month days), read its additive 20% uplift on EVEN months; then reverse; both directions must show same-sign uplift >= 50% of the pre-declared uplift.
- Executability: NT8 daily signal file (instrument, side, quantity) published after the 16:15 ET refresh of VIX/COT-derived features; market entry at 16:16 ET, exit at the next 16:16 ET; margin and contract counts at 10/20/30% of pair risk reported (ES, NQ, RTY, YM; CME initial margins stated as assumptions).
- Library: pre-declared cell saved with lib_save.save_book (volmatch).
### L2..L10 are registered in the next block before their first run.
### L2..L10 (registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any L2..L10 run; K4v2 was already running when this block was written but L2..L10 use no K4v2 output). All J-COMMON rules (clean-only selection, ERA flag, both framings, 500-draw own null for sleeves / 300 matched for pair-side, bootstrap, plateau +-25% (gate as defined: >= 75% of exact neighbours keep >= 75%), 2x slippage, wd corr, library save; pool 6). Every long-only or beta-exposed sleeve ALSO reports the month-stratified drift-matched null and the beta-regression alpha CI (K4v2 definition).
- L2 K4-DIRECTION AGREEMENT TILT OF THE PAIR (pair-side). Mechanism: the daily GBM's next-day index direction p (K4 model d2, N=1, universe idx, basket mean of the four indices) identifies bullish/bearish days; the pair's long entries are more reliable on bullish days. Prior 5%. MINCH 0.05. Signal known at the 16:16 px of label d, applied to pair rows with lab_in = next label. Bull day: pbar >= 0.5+delta; bear day: pbar <= 0.5-delta. Rows whose side AGREES with the day get multiplier 1+a, DISAGREEING rows 1-b. Grid (8): delta {0.03, 0.06} x (a,b) {(0,0.5), (0,1.0), (0.5,0), (0.25,0.25)}. Null: permute the daily bull/bear/neutral labels across days (300). Plateau: delta, a, b x0.75/x1.25 (zeros skipped; b=1.0 -> 0.75 only).
- L3 CROSS-SECTIONAL MARKET-NEUTRAL SLEEVE FROM THE K4 PREDICTIONS (ES, NQ, RTY, YM). Each label long the index with the highest p and short the lowest p if (p_max - p_min) >= gap, hold N days, unit ADR risk per leg. Grid (8): model {d2, d4} x N {1, 3} x gap {0.04, 0.08}. MINC 100. N0: random days (same count), legs by the rule's argmax/argmin on that day. Prior 3%.
- L4 WEEKLY-HORIZON GBM (ES, NQ, RTY, YM): K4 construction with N in {5, 10}: model {d2, d4} x N {5, 10} x delta {0.04, 0.08} x mode {longshort, longonly} = 16 cells; non-overlapping per instrument. MINC 60. Prior 3%.
- L5 CONDITIONAL-BETA SLEEVE (long ES, NQ, RTY, YM while a VIX state is ON; spells entered at the first ON label and exited at the first OFF label, 16:16 px). States (5 cells): contango1.00 (VIX/VIX3M < 1.00), contango0.95 (< 0.95), vixmed (VIX < median of the prior 252 labels), both (contango1.00 and vixmed), falling (VIX below its value 5 labels earlier). MINC 20. N0: same number of spells with the same durations from random start labels (month-stratified variant reported). Prior 3%.
- L6 COMMODITY SPREAD SLEEVE (K5 construction): spreads GC-SI, HO-CL, RB-CL, ZS-ZC, ZW-ZC, LE-HE x signal {mr1.5, mr2.0, trend} x N {5, 10} = 36 cells; MINC 40. Prior 3%.
- L7 FX CROSS SPREAD SLEEVE (K5 construction): spreads 6E-6B, 6A-6C, 6B-6A, 6E-6C, 6J-6E x signal {mr1.5, mr2.0, trend} x N {5, 10} = 30 cells; MINC 40. Prior 3%.
- L8 INTRADAY GBM WITH CROSS-ASSET AND VIX FEATURES (NQ, ES). Sample every 30 minutes from 09:30 to 14:30 ET; features: own returns over 30/60/120 min and since the open in ADR units, leaders ZN, 6J, GC, CL, 6E 60-min returns in ADR units, minute-of-day sin/cos, prior-day return/ADR, VIX percentile (252d) and VIX/VIX3M at the prior label, ADR20/ADR60, instrument id; target = sign of the next 60-minute change; walk-forward quarterly from 2016-01 on rows whose target is realised before the retrain date; entry at the next 1-minute bar open, exit 60 minutes later (cap 16:00), one position per instrument. Grid (8): model {d2, d4} x delta {0.02, 0.04} x mode {longshort, longonly}. MINC 100. Prior 3%.
- L9 K4 SIGNAL DECOMPOSITION INTO OVERNIGHT AND RTH LEGS (ES, NQ, RTY, YM): K4 model d2, N=1 predictions; overnight leg = enter at the 16:17 bar open of the signal label, exit at the 09:30 ET open of the next label; RTH leg = enter at the 09:31 open of the next label, exit at the 16:16 px of that day. Grid (8): leg {overnight, rth} x delta {0.04, 0.08} x mode {longshort, longonly}. MINC 100. Purpose: where the K4 edge sits and whether it needs overnight holding. Prior 4%.
- L10 DAILY GBM ON TREASURIES (ZT, ZF, ZN, ZB): K4 construction, universe 'rates' (no COT features except ZN where available; VIX features kept); grid (16) as K4. MINC 100. Prior 3%.

## BATCH J11 (J11-A, J11-B, J11-C), registered 2026-10-16 by worker J11 BEFORE any J11 run (only loader/schema checks). Cells: GAPMAP D11 H6 M7 NQ (week-floor variant left UNTESTED by H11-C) and D08 H5,H6 M7 NQ (variant of H8-C). Closed neighbours not repeated as-is: H11-C (weekly range/ER SIZE tilt, all 8 cells negative on clean), H8-A (RV20 state floor), H8-C (VX-term floor, both directions, F 4/6/10/12), B16/B17/B18/B19 (week/prior-day tilts), G0b. Engine/gates/null/plateau exactly as H8 (ap_h8_core.py run_floor: flagged weeks get week floor F R instead of 8R; unflagged weeks unchanged at 8R; clean process, baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) 1.46/2.83/2.30; 1 tick/side + commission; 2x slippage; ET; pool of 5). Selection = best clean weekly-Sharpe uplift of the grid; H1/H2 read once for the selected cell. Null = 200 draws flag-permuted among valid weeks (same count). Plateau = P x0.75/x1.25 and F x0.75/x1.25 (4 neighbours, >=75% must keep >=75% of all-in uplift). Selection-aware null 12 grid permutations (report). Replication if clean/H1/H2 gate met: alternating-month split + H0 2023-02..07 (ES/RTY n/a). State for week g uses only completed weeks/label days before g's first label day; percentile ranks are against the PRIOR 104 weeks (min 52; causal), not clean-span quantiles.
DESIGN RULE for all J11 cells (the H8-C failure): TIGHTEN-ONLY. F in {3,4,5,6} < 8, never above the live 8R. H8-C's clean gain (+0.13, F12 loosening) came with a worst week of -116k vs -82k because loosening exposes the book to the very weeks it flagged; the insurance reading of the mechanism (stress/expanded state = fatter weekly tail = cut earlier) only makes sense as tighter. A tighten-only cell cannot make the worst week worse by construction except through the post-floor re-entry path, which is tested by the gate. Disclosure: H1/H2 were already read once for the parent H8-C (selected HI67 F12) and H11-C; parent clean top-10 was seen: the tighten-only parent cells HI P50/67/80 F6 had clean uplift -0.025/-0.038/-0.009 and F4 was not displayed. So J11-B is largely known to fail on clean; it is run because pre-registered and cheap, and any H1/H2 result of J11-B is non-virgin evidence. A J11 cell cannot reach FOUND without the ERA-amendment replication on untouched data (H0, alternating month; newest live weeks not available).
### J11-A WEEKLY PRICE-PATTERN STATE TIGHTEN-ONLY WEEK FLOOR (M7, D11 H6). Mechanism: volatility clusters at the weekly scale - after an expanded prior week (range >> trailing average), after a gap-heavy weekend open, or in a choppy (low weekly efficiency) regime, the trend pair's weekly loss tail is fatter (whipsaw), so a floor of F<8R on those weeks cuts the left tail at little cost to the winners that need >8R rarely. Prior 3% (H11-C rejected the same features as a size tilt, which lowers the prior; the floor acts only in the loss tail, a different channel). Grid (12): feature {WR = prior-week range / mean range of 8 weeks before it (HIGH tail); WER = efficiency ratio of the prior 8 weekly closes (LOW tail = chop); GAP = |Sunday-open minus Friday close| / mean weekly range of prior 8 weeks (HIGH tail)} x P {67, 80} (percentile vs prior 104 weeks; LOW tail uses 100-P) x F {4, 6}.
### J11-B H8-C VARIANT: VX-TERM STATE TIGHTEN-ONLY WEEK FLOOR (M7, D08 H5/H6). Same state as H8-C (VIX/VIX3M percentile rank vs prior 252 label days at the last label day before the week; HIGH = flat/inverted). Only tighten: grid (9) P {50, 67, 80} x F {3, 4, 6}. Why it addresses the failure: see DESIGN RULE. Prior 2%.
### J11-C INTERSECTION: VX-TERM HIGH x EXPANDED PRIOR WEEK TIGHTEN-ONLY FLOOR (M7). Mechanism: two independent stress readings (implied term inversion and realised prior-week range expansion) flagging together isolate genuine stress weeks with fewer false flags than either alone (flag count lower, power lower). Grid (4): combine {AND, OR} x F {4, 6}, both states at P=67 (term pct > 0.67 and WR pct > 0.67). Prior 2%. Plateau: P x0.75/x1.25 (applied to both), F x0.75/x1.25.
Closing: UPD D11 H6 M7 (A, C) and D08 H5,H6 M7 (B). Report IDs J11-A, J11-B, J11-C.

## G8-E - D03 daily NQ option OPEN-INTEREST state, M4/M8 (registered 2026-10-16T18:55Z before any run; supersedes the crude G8-D proxy now that statistics+definitions landed)
Data: GLBX NQ statistics stat_type 9 (open interest) per option instrument, joined to definitions (class C/P, expiration), 2023-2026 (data/autopilot/opts/pq; outputs/autopilot/opt/oi_daily.pkl via ap_g8_oi.py). Features per OI date: pcoi = put OI / total OI; oinear = OI with expiry <= 7 calendar days / total (weekly/0DTE-pinning share proxy); oirat = total OI / mean of prior 20. Publication lag: OI for ref date X appears ~10:00 ET the next trading day, so an entry on label day D uses the latest ref date <= the second trading date before D (lag 2). No spot-relative GEX (no raw underlying price joined; a Black-Scholes GEX needs IV and is a later cell). Grid: feature x P {33,50,67} x HI/LO x entry multiplier {0,0.5,1.5} = 54 cells, tilt on all pair entries; identical engine, selection, gates, nulls (day-block permutation), plateau, 2x slippage, selection-aware null as G8-A..D. Prior 3%. ERA: not testable pre-2023 (pair rows start 2023-02). Executability: needs daily CME NQ options OI (public CME file or Databento statistics) fed to NT8 at the open.

## J5W batch (variants of H11-B whipsaw day halt; worker J5, 2026-10-16). Written BEFORE any variant was run. (Prefix J5W because REPORT_J5.md already exists = index-event tilt.)
DISCLOSURE: the parent H11-B was run and its H1/H2 numbers were read by the parent worker (H1/H2 were "read once" for the parent) and then read by me for this design. Therefore variant results on H1/H2 are NON-VIRGIN evidence; H1/H2 gate numbers are reported but cannot support FOUND on their own. Selection is clean-only (2023-08..2025-07) within each variant. Replication needs untouched modern-era data: H0 (2023-02-10..07-31) and a pre-registered alternating-month split (newest live weeks are handled by the WATCH updater, not scored here; ES/RTY n/a because the pair is NQ). Both H0 and alt-month must hold (same sign, >= 50% of the selected clean uplift; alt-month in BOTH directions) for a replication pass, since only two of the four sources exist. Parent failure being addressed: (i) response surface sharp (plateau 0/4); (ii) a random-break null of the same fired days earned +0.23 clean vs real +0.20 (late-day truncation after bad mornings helps in the clean sample, G0a artefact); (iii) H0 +0.01; (iv) alt-month 1 of 2. Variants are NOT parameter hunting on H1/H2: each changes the ACTION (not the detector) to remove or test the truncation channel, triggers stay fixed at the parent's two positive clusters T1=(N2,X10) and T2=(N3,X40), and cells are fixed here.
Common: baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process (must reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30); pass1 state hook as ap_h11_b.py; state = same-label-day pair trades closed (exit <= entry time) whose exit name contains Stop and life <= X min ("fast stops"); fill 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lot RT, 2x slip = SLIP_PT 1.0 in both arms. Selection per variant: max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift among cells touching >= 2% of clean raw entries (lowered from the parent's 5% because book scope / expiry / size cuts touch fewer entries; stated now). Gates per variant (checklist as REPORT_H11_B): uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week not worse (clean, H1, H2); own matched random null (100 draws) with BOTH clean uplift and mean(H1,H2) uplift at rank >= 0.95 (stricter than the parent, which only gated on the latter); bootstrap 90% CI of all-in weekly-Sharpe uplift > 0; plateau = every neighbour in [N-1, N+1, X*0.75, X*1.25, plus the variant parameter +-25% (K, M+-1, cut +-1h)] with >= 75% of neighbours keeping >= 75% of all-in uplift; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 on clean and all-in sign positive; replication as above; worst-decile corr n/a (rule on the pair). NT8: all variants are day-counters over closed trades plus a clock; integer lots (4 of 8 for half). Insurance metrics + constant-risk read via ins_metrics. Closest-miss FOUND needs all gates; anything else is NO/WATCH.
### J5W-A BOOK-SCOPED HALT (M7, H1-H3). Mechanism: a whipsaw may be specific to one book's entry/stop geometry (ST vs HM); halting the book that got stopped keeps the other book's independent edge and halves the truncation. Prior 3%. Cells (4): A1 per-book count: the entering book alone has >= N fast stops (N2/X10, N2/X40) -> that book halted for the rest of the day; A2 contributors: pair count >= N fast stops (T1, T2) -> halt only books with >= 1 of those stops. Null: random break within the same fired (day, book) group (later rows of that book skipped). 
### J5W-B EXPIRING HALT (M7, H1-H3). Mechanism: parent's gain may be end-of-day truncation, not whipsaw detection; if whipsaws are bursts (clustered vol), a halt of K minutes after the latest fast stop (once count >= N) captures the burst without truncating the late day. Prior 4%. Cells (6): T1,T2 x K {60,120,180} min. Active if count >= N and entry time < exit time of the latest counted fast stop + K. Null: for each fired day a random entry row as window start, skip rows within K minutes after it (time-of-day neutral). 
### J5W-C SMOOTH SIZE REDUCTION (M8/M7, H1-H3). Mechanism: binary halt is lumpy and the parent's HALF variant (+0.17 N2X10) suggests the effect is monotone; 0.5x on the next M entries after the trigger, then full size again (no truncation). Prior 4%. Cells (4): T1,T2 x M {2,4}. Null: random start, same M entries halved.
### J5W-D LATE-DAY TRUNCATION CONTROL (M7, H1-H3). Mechanism test: halt rest of day only if the Nth fast stop exited before 12:00 ET of the label day (overnight session from 18:00 counts as before noon). If the parent gain survives with a morning-only trigger and beats a morning-restricted random break (break row drawn uniformly among the day's entries before 12:00 ET), the effect is detection; if it equals the null, it is truncation. Prior 2%. Cells (2): T1, T2. Plateau extra: cut 11:00/13:00 ET.

## J6 (D06 clock/microstructure-minute x pair management, H1/H2 = 1- to 15-minute; worker J6; registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any J6 run; before registering only the entry-minute COUNT histogram (no P&L by minute) and the code path were read)
Ledger check: G6a (session phase by ET hour band, NO), G6b/H6a-c (calendar, NO), G6c (time flatten at 15:xx, NO), H0d (1-3 min entry delay on the 1-s engine, NO), D2 E3 (phase-shifted SIGNAL bars :15/:30/:45 and 2-min bars; 0 of 2 gates; the E3 ":00+:15" blend = INSURANCE lead failed plateau/bootstrap) are not repeated. How J6 differs from E3: E3 moved the bar clock on which ST/HM form signals (a different strategy member, blended); J6 keeps the LIVE :00 signal clock untouched and conditions/manages the realised trades on the MINUTE-OF-HOUR of the entry or exit fill (tilt/skip entries, profit-take at spike minutes). No new signal member is created, so nothing is blended and the result is a pure overlay on the pair rows (rows start 2023-02; no pre-2023 history, ERA flag n/a).
Engine/spans/baseline as G6: B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a CLEAN process (d3_common first), baseline 1.46/2.83/2.30 asserted; fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT, 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 for candidate and baseline; selection max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift over the family's cells only; the carried cell's H1/H2 read once; UNTOUCHED never. Entry minute = ET minute of the entry fill (HRIN), minute-of-hour identical in UTC. Gates unchanged: uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week not worse; own null p95 (all-in uplift); bootstrap 90% CI all-in > 0 (3000 weekly draws); plateau >= 75% of 4 neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; wd corr n/a (overlay, no hedge claim); NT8 spec. Replication (Amend. 2): H0 (2023-02-10..07-31) uplift > 0 and alternating-month split (select odd -> read even, select even -> read odd; carried cell positive in both parities); ES/RTY and newest live weeks not available for a pair overlay. H1/H2 are not virgin for nothing here: first read of these families.
- J6a (M4/M8, H1-H2; prior 4%). Entry-minute phase of the 5- and 15-minute bar grid. Mechanism: the pair's 1-min flip entries that fill in the first minutes of a 5/15-min bar are chasing the bar-open burst from 5/15-min algorithmic bar-based order flow; the burst partially reverses, so entries filled there pay a worse effective price than entries mid-bar. Rule: entry whose ET minute-of-hour m satisfies the window gets multiplier mm (reduce-only, NOT mean-normalised, via B.Cfg.sizefn). GRID (8 cells): window in {5-min bar first 1 min (m%5==0), 5-min bar first 2 min (m%5<=1), 15-min bar first 1 min (m%15==0), 15-min bar first 3 min (m%15<=2)} x mm in {0, 0.5}. Null: 100 permutations of the realised per-trade multiplier vector across all trades (same multiset). Plateau neighbours: mm x0.75/x1.25 (mm=0 -> 0.25 / 0) and window width k x0.75/x1.25 rounded to whole minutes (k=1 stays 1).
- J6b (M7, H1-H2; prior 3%). Entry avoidance around the :00/:30 clock. Mechanism: scheduled releases, index-auction/fixing and option-expiry style flows cluster at :00 and :30 (10:00 ISM-type releases, 8:30, half-hour prints); spreads and adverse selection widen for minutes either side, so a trend-continuation flip filled there is more likely to be a spike that reverses. Rule: skip (mm=0) or halve (mm=0.5) entries whose distance to the nearest :00 or :30 is <= k minutes (before AND after). GRID (6 cells): {:00 and :30 with k=1, :00 and :30 with k=3, :00 only with k=3} x mm in {0, 0.5}. Overlaps J6a at minute 0 by construction; selection is separate per family. Null: 100 permutations of per-trade multipliers across trades. Plateau: mm x0.75/x1.25 and k x0.75/x1.25 rounded (k=3 -> 2,4; k=1 -> 1).
- J6c (M5, H1-H2; prior 3%). Profit-take into the bar-open spike. Mechanism: if the open position is in profit by >= X points at the close of the minute before a :00/:30 (or quarter-hour) minute, the spike at that minute is more likely to be liquidity to sell into than a continuation, so close at that minute's open. Rule: for each pair trade, the first bar k in (entry+1 .. exit] with minute-of-hour in the set S and side*(close[k-1]-entry_price) >= X exits at open[k] (slippage as engine; locks/day/week rules re-run on the changed trades via the xflat route of G6c). GRID (6 cells): S in {{0,30}, {0,15,30,45}} x X in {10,20,40} NQ points. Null: same number of fired exits, each moved to a uniformly random bar inside the same trade (100 draws). Plateau: X x0.75/x1.25 (S fixed). Prior low: C2-G1 exit management and C4-K1 target extension were NO, and trend books lose from truncating winners.
Expected: NO on all three; the usual failure is a clean gain that reverses on H1/H2. Report IDs J6a, J6b, J6c.

## J0 batch (D00 pair-internal; M5 exit management / M6, horizons H4-H5 multi-hour-daily; worker J0; registered 2026-10-16T18:50Z BEFORE any J0 variant return was computed; only data inspection of hold-time and boundary-crossing counts)
Ledger check (not repeated): C2-G1 = UNCONDITIONAL max-hold time stop {60,120,240} min plus breakeven/trail/scale-out (all NO; early exits cut runners); G6c = all/Friday time-flatten at 15:00-15:45 ET before RTH close (NO); H10-A = flatten at FOMC/NFP/CPI event times only (NO); H0c = give-back tier of the day lock. Observation (inspection only): the live pair is already flat at the 17:00 ET break (0 clean trades cross it), 10.8% of trades cross midnight, 557 clean trades cross 09:30 ET. So the open "time-exit" gaps are (A) PROGRESS-CONDITIONAL time stops and (B) flatten of Globex-session carries before the European/macro/RTH open, neither tested. All three families patch exit rows exactly like G6c (flat_apply: exit at open of the exit bar, 1 tick/side + $34.88/8-lot RT, locks/day/week rules re-run, baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) 1.46/2.83/2.30 in a clean process). Selection: max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift (cells touching <100 clean trades flagged small-sample); ONE cell carried; H1/H2 read once. Gates = standard (uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, own null p95, bootstrap CI>0, plateau, 2x slippage, insurance metrics) plus Amendment-2 replication (H0 2023-02..07 and alternating-month split select-odd/read-even and reverse; ES/RTY n/a, pair NQ-only; ERA flag n/a, pair starts 2023-02). Bar convention: 1-min bars, bar j has open o[j], high h[j], low l[j], close c[j]; trade enters at bar ei; progress measured on bars ei+1..k-1 (excludes the entry bar), exit at the open of bar k = ei+N; applies only if the live exit is at bar >= k.
- J0-A STAGNATION STOP (M5, H4; prior 4%). Mechanism: the 60/60 bracket leaves trades that never move in favour open for hours; such trades have no edge left (sign persistence is in the early impulse) but still carry the full -60 stop and the overnight/lock interactions, and tie up the one-position-per-book slot while new flips are netted/blocked. Unlike C2-G1's unconditional time stop, trades that did make progress are untouched, so runners are kept. Rule: at age N min, if MFE (favourable extreme since entry, pts) < X, exit at the open of bar ei+N. GRID (9): N {60,120,240} x X {10,20,30} pts. Null (100): same number of trades drawn uniformly among trades alive at age N (live exit at bar >= ei+N), exited at age N (random victims, same N), cell = selected. Plateau: N x0.75/x1.25 rounded to 15 min (45,75 / 90,150 / 180,300), X x0.75/x1.25 (rounded to 1 pt).
- J0-B GLOBEX-CARRY FLATTEN (M5/M6, H4-H5; prior 3%). Mechanism: positions opened in the Asian/Globex session and still open at the start of the European session, the 08:30 ET data window or the 09:30 RTH open are exposed to liquidity/regime breaks the 60-pt bracket does not control (clean: 557 trades cross 09:30, mean -0.75 pt/trade against +2.0 for the rest, descriptive only). G6c tested only the 15:00-15:45 RTH-close clock; H10-A only on event days. Rule: a trade open at clock T (first occurrence after entry) is closed at the open of the T bar. GRID (6): T {03:00, 08:25, 09:25} ET x scope {all trades, only trades of age >= 120 min at T}. Null (100): same trades truncated at a uniformly random minute inside their life (G6c null). Plateau: T +-15 min.
- J0-C STUCK-UNDERWATER TIME CHECK (M5, H4; prior 3%). Mechanism: a trade that is already -X pts under water after N min has spent its early impulse against the entry; the later outcome is dominated by the remaining stop distance, so exit early cuts average loss (D3 lever: -10% avg loss = +93% net) without capping winners (winners are above water at N). Differs from a tighter stop: one check at age N, not a continuous level. Rule: at age N, if close of bar ei+N-1 is <= entry - X pts (side-signed), exit at the open of bar ei+N. GRID (9): N {30,60,120} min x X {15,30,40} pts. Null (100): same number of random trades alive at age N, exited at age N. Plateau: N x0.75/x1.25 (rounded to 15 min... 30 -> 22/38 rounded to 20,40), X x0.75/x1.25.
Report IDs J0-A, J0-B, J0-C (REPORT_J0A.md etc).

## J7V + J9P (registered 2026-10-16 ~19:00Z by worker J7V/J9P BEFORE any J7V/J9P variant return was computed; only the jump-event COUNT table was produced (frequency of flagged entries per k, W; no P&L)). Report ids avoid the existing REPORT_J7.md / REPORT_J9.md (older worker I files): REPORT_J7V.md, REPORT_J9P_A.md, REPORT_J9P_B.md, REPORT_J9P_C.md.
Common: baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a CLEAN process (d3_common first), 1.46/2.83/2.30 asserted; fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and candidate; overlay NOT renormalised; selection = max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift over the family's cells; carried cell's H1/H2 read once; UNTOUCHED never; gates as in H7-C (uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, own-null p95 on clean and all-in, bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0, plateau >= 75% of +-25% neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift, 2x slippage >= +0.10 each span); alternating-month split (select on odd months and read on even, then reverse) over all cells; H0 (2023-02..07) where rows exist; ERA flag n/a (pair rows start 2023). Pool of 5.
### J7V  (variant of H7-C; D07 x M8, H5; prior 4%, below the parent's 4% because the parent was clean +0.07 and sign-unstable)
DISCLOSURE: the parent H7-C was run and its H1/H2 were read once for its selected cell (SAFE_L5_DOWN: +0.07/-0.02/+0.63). That is non-virgin evidence: the variant cannot be FOUND on its own NQ H1/H2; a FOUND needs the Amendment-2 replication (>= 2 of: newest live weeks, H0, ES/RTY 2023-26, alternating-month split) on data the selection did not touch. I have NOT looked at the H1/H2 numbers of the parent's other 17 cells.
Why this addresses the parent's failure. (i) The parent state was a raw L-day sum of three assets' returns (L=5, 20): label-to-label noisy, the tilt flipped between neighbouring days (its L5/L20 results disagreed in sign on H1/H2 and plateau was 0.25). The mechanism (risk-off = macro-driven whipsaw regime that persists weeks) is a slow state, so the state should be a smoothed, persistent one: composite z_t = -(r1_ZN + r1_6J + r1_GC)/3 with each r1 the 1-day change / trailing-60-day sigma (no L-day sums), smoothed by a W-session rolling mean (W 10/20/40, all closes of 17:00 ET strictly before the entry label day). (ii) The parent kept GROW/NET only to lose on clean; restricting to SAFE removes 2/3 of the cell count (multiplicity) and is a decision taken from the parent's CLEAN numbers only. (iii) The asymmetry: DOWN (bottom tercile x0.5), ASYM (bottom x0.5 and top x1.25), UPONLY (top x1.25). The parent's UP/TWO cells were strongly negative on clean (-0.1..-0.46), so ASYM and UPONLY are expected to lose; they are in the grid as the pre-stated asymmetry test and cost only multiplicity (9 cells).
Grid (9): W {10,20,40} x scheme {DOWN, ASYM, UPONLY}. Terciles frozen from clean entries' state values. Plateau neighbours (6): tercile share 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25, W x0.75/x1.25 (rounded 8/12, 15/25, 30/50). Null: state labels permuted among label days, 100 reps, same scheme.
Instrument cross-check (pre-registered, not used for selection): the SELECTED cell's day state (same z series, same frozen edges) applied as a per-entry-day size multiplier to ES and RTY ST+HM books built by d1_f4_core (ADR20-bracket k=0.75, equal R=$9,600, live account rules, 1 tick/side) over 2023-08..2026-10; holds if the weekly-Sharpe uplift is > 0 on BOTH ES and RTY and their mean >= 50% of the NQ all-in uplift. Also reported: the parent cell SAFE_L5_DOWN on ES/RTY (report-only). 
Executability: NT8 multi-instrument strategy reading ZN/6J/GC daily closes, writing a quantity multiplier (<= 1.25) at entry; margin unchanged for DOWN, +25% peak on flagged entries for ASYM.
### J9P-A  OWN-NQ MULTI-DAY RETURN ALIGNMENT AS ENTRY SIZE TILT (D11 x M4/M8, H5; prior 5%)
Mechanism: ST and HM are trend-continuation (breakout) systems; the day-level state of the instrument itself (is the side with or against the 3/10/30/60-session drift?) is untested (B9 used ES/RTY agreement of 5-minute trend; G11-A/B used prior-day position and range; H11-A used intraday efficiency). A with-trend entry rides a persistent drift, a counter-trend entry fights it. Feature a_L = SIDE x (D[t] - D[t-L]) / (sigma_D60 x sqrt L), D = NQ 17:00 ET closes (strictly before the entry label day), sigma_D60 = trailing-60-session sd of daily changes. Cells (12): L {3,10,30,60} x scheme {COUNTER_DOWN (bottom tercile x0.5), ALIGN_UP (top tercile x1.5), TWO (bottom x0.75 / top x1.25)}. Tercile edges frozen from clean entries. Plateau neighbours (6): share 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25, L x0.75/x1.25 (2/4, 8/12, 22/38, 45/75). Null: feature permuted among trades within (book, year, ET hour) blocks, 100 reps. Alternating-month as common. Executability: NT8 reads NQ daily closes in the strategy, multiplies quantity.
### J9P-B  UNSCHEDULED JUMP SHOCK HALT/HALF (D07/D10 x M7, H1-H2; prior 3%)
Mechanism: an abnormal 1-minute move relative to its own time-of-day norm (news, a large order, a tech headline) opens a window of reflexive volatility and wide spreads; a trend flip filled inside it is more likely to whipsaw. Differs from H10 (verified calendars only; 33-50 rows), H11-B (stop-outs of pair trades, not the tape), G8-A (60-minute variance ratio), G10-3 (ZN/6J 60-min sigma sticky halt). Jump bar: |ln close_t / close_{t-1}| / mean(|r| at the same ET minute over the previous 20 sessions, shift 1) >= k; bars after a gap > 1 min (session reopen) excluded. Entry flagged if a jump bar occurred in the W minutes ending at the entry's signal bar. Cells (18): k {8,12,16} x W {15,30,60} x action {SKIP (0), HALF (0.5)}; cells with < 3% of clean entries flagged are ineligible for selection (k16 W15 only). Plateau neighbours (4): k x0.75/x1.25, W x0.75/x1.25. Null: for the same number of flagged entries per label day, flags assigned to random entries of the same day (100 reps). Expected 2% / 3% worst case; counts of flagged entries per cell were the only look (table in the report).
### J9P-C  RUN-UP-INTO-ENTRY (CHASE) TILT (D11 x M4/M8, H2; prior 3%)
Mechanism: the pair buys breakouts; entries that follow a sharp 10-60 minute run in the trade direction are late (exhaustion/retrace), entries that follow a pullback are early (the lever table: 1 tick better fills = +0.30 Sharpe, so entry price quality matters). Feature u_k = SIDE x (c[EI-1] - c[EI-1-k]) / (sigma_1m x sqrt k) on 1-minute closes of the harness grid, k {10,30,60}, sigma_1m the trailing 1300-bar sd of 1-minute log returns. Cells (9): k x scheme {CHASE_DOWN (top tercile x0.5), PULL_UP (bottom tercile x1.5), TWO (top 0.75 / bottom 1.25)}. Differs from H11-A (path efficiency, not magnitude), G11-A (levels), H0d (delay). Plateau (6): share 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25, k x0.75/x1.25 (8/12, 22/38, 45/75). Null: as J9P-A.
Order of execution: J7V first (alone, pool 5), then J9P-A, J9P-B, J9P-C. Reports end 'DONE <ID>'.

## EVIDENCE-DRIVEN FAMILIES E1, E2, E3, E4, E7 (user directive 2026-10-16 ~19:00Z, AUTOPILOT.md section E1-E8), registered BEFORE any E-run by worker I. E5/E6 belong to the GAP worker, E8 to the lead. Common: Gate Amendment 2; clean-only selection; H1/H2 once; both framings where the object is a sleeve; ERA flag; own null; bootstrap; plateau +-25%; 2x slippage; replication by H0 (2023-02..07, where pair rows exist) and alternating months (cross-instrument/newest weeks stated when not applicable); save_book where a series exists; UPD lines; 6-worker pool.
### E4 WHIPSAW-HALT NEIGHBOURHOOD (pair-side, corrected B11 engine with the G0 hook framework; refines H11-B and G0a)
- Mechanism: bursts of consecutive stop-outs mark a chop regime; halting new entries for a short window avoids the next stop-out. Prior 4%.
- Rule: executed trades are the pair rows that passed all live rules; STOP = row exit name contains 'Stop'. An entry is halted when the last n CLOSED executed trades in the counted set are all stop-outs (a non-stop closing trade resets the streak) and the most recent of them closed less than W minutes ago ('sess' = until the end of the session instance in which that last stop closed; session instance = Globex-or-RTH segment of one trading label; RTH = 09:30-16:00 ET entry time, Globex otherwise). Counted set: scope 'book' = same book as the entry, 'pair' = both books; session mode 'globex' / 'rth' = only entries of that session are halted and only stops of that session count, 'both' = any.
- Grid (72): n {2, 3, 4} x W {30, 60, 120 min, sess} x scope {book, pair} x session {globex, rth, both}. Eligible: changes >= 5% of clean raw entries (if none, all cells). Selection: highest clean weekly Sharpe. Gates: pair-change gates (uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week not worse; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau = n-1, n+1, W x0.75, W x1.25 (sess: n+-1 only), >= 75% keep >= 75%; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; own null = random-halt null: each fired halt interval is moved to a random start inside its trading label with the same length and the same scope/session filter, 100 draws, all-in uplift real > p95; replication = H0 and alternating-month split (select cell on odd months of 2023-08..2026-10, read on even, and reverse; same sign and >= 50% of the selected cell's all-in uplift). Cross-instrument and newest-weeks replications do not apply (pair-internal rule, no new week).
### E3 PERS TILT (pair-side). Mechanism: DRIVERS found that flow persistence (|net signed volume|/volume over W minutes before the entry bar) separates good and bad flips; re-cast as a continuous size tilt at normal contract counts. Prior 5%.
- Feature: PERS_W for W {15, 30, 60, 120} minutes from the NQ per-minute signed volume frame (ap_drv_feat base; PERS120 added; window ends one minute before the entry bar). Walk-forward percentile p of the feature among earlier rows (expanding, same warm-up rule as ap_drv_sw, scope ALL or BLOCK = by ET block). Tilt factor f = clip(1 + sg*a*(2p-1), 0.5, 1.5) with sg = -1 (size DOWN where persistence is high; the DRIVERS direction) or +1 (control), shrinkage a {0.25, 0.5}; f = 1 when the percentile is undefined. Mean factor on clean is reported (target ~1; contract counts are normal, range 0.5-1.5).
- Grid (32): W {15, 30, 60, 120} x a {0.25, 0.5} x scope {ALL, BLOCK} x sg {-1, +1}. Eligible: all cells (every row tilted); selection = highest clean weekly Sharpe. Gates as E4 (null: circular shift of the factor vector by a random offset, 100 draws; plateau: a x0.75/x1.25 and adjacent windows (15<->30<->60<->120), >= 75% of 4 neighbours keep >= 75%). Replication H0 + alternating month as E4.
### E7 K4v2 SYMMETRIC (sleeves; pre-declared cells, no selection) - uses the frozen K4 predictions (ap_k_fams.ml_preds idx, d2, N=1)
- E7a long-short: d2, N=1, delta 0.08, mode longshort (unhedged). E7b beta-neutral long-only: for NQ, RTY, YM only (ES is the hedge instrument), long X when p >= 0.58 AND short ES contracts = beta x q_X where beta = rolling 60-label regression slope of the daily dollar P&L of one X contract on one ES contract's (prior 60 labels, known at the label), exit after 1 day; E7c beta-neutral long-short: same with the shorts mirrored (short X, long ES) when p <= 0.42. Each leg priced at the 16:16 px, 1 tick/side + $4.36 per contract on both legs, one ADR-dollar of X risk. Gates on the full set (clean uplift, H1, H2, worst week, bootstrap, own random-date null with month-stratified drift-matched variant, plateau by delta +-25%, hyperparameter seeds as in K4v2, 2x slippage, wd corr) + beta test. Verdict question: does the edge survive symmetric / beta-neutral.
### E1 DENOISED SIGNALS (pair replacement, 1-minute NQ engine d1_f4/p1_core with the live rules; compare to the SAME engine run with the live NQ signals)
- Signal sources: base (live: NQ bars); (a) ES 1-min bars (ST/HM signals computed on ES, trades executed on NQ with the 60/60 bracket); (b) composite: per bar, average of the vol-normalised ES and NQ OHLC relatives to the previous close (sigma = trailing 390-minute std of 1-minute close returns), rebuilt into a price path in NQ-point units, volume = NQ volume; (c) residual: NQ bar relatives minus beta_t x ES bar relatives (beta_t = trailing 20-day regression slope of NQ on ES 1-min returns, known before the bar), cumulated. Signals: the live ST (supertrend 120/2.0 close + EMA200) and HM (HMA20/55 of close-VWAP + EMA200) computed on the alternative OHLCV; entries at the next NQ bar open; exits, brackets (60 pts), windows, day lock 8R/2R/67%, week floor 8R exactly as p1_core.account. Window 2023-02..2026-10 only (engine rows).
- Reports per source: trades/day, average points per trade, win rate, whipsaw count (stop-outs within 10 minutes of entry), net, weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2. Gates: uplift vs the engine baseline >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week not worse; bootstrap CI > 0; null = ES (and the ES leg of the composite/residual) circularly shifted by a random whole number of trading days (100 draws), all-in uplift real > p95; plateau on the signal parameters (ST period/mult x0.75/x1.25, HM fast/slow x0.75/x1.25 = 8 neighbours) >= 75% keep >= 75%; 2x slippage; replication H0 window where the engine has it (2023-02..07) + alternating months. Pre-declared 3 variants; no selection (all three reported; FOUND-class verdict per variant).
### E2 ES-KEYED EXITS (pair, same engine). Mechanism: ES is the price-discovery leader; an early exit of the NQ trade when ES disagrees. Cells (10), applied to every pair trade on top of the 60/60 bracket (exit at the next NQ bar open after the trigger bar): (i) ES adverse excursion since entry >= x ES-ADR20, x {0.10, 0.15, 0.20}; (ii) ES flips its own supertrend (120/2.0) against the trade; (iii) ES fails to confirm: within N {5, 10, 20} minutes ES has not moved >= c ES-ADR20 in the trade direction, c {0.02, 0.05} - exit at N. Gates as E1 (null: ES circularly shifted by random days; plateau on x, N, c +-25%). Selection clean-only among the 10 cells.
