## BACKLOG2 GROUPS A AND D (registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any A/D run; E1 (A-group overlap: ES bars, vol-normalised composite, residual path) and E2/E3/E4/E7 registered above)
### Duplicate check (LEDGER, GAPMAP, REPORT_DRIVERS): E1 covers A-items "ES-bar signals", "composite ES+NQ", "residual path". Backlog items 28 (signed-volume run length at flip as size tilt) and 29 (cumulative delta agreeing with the flip as confirmation) are EXACT duplicates of DRIVERS-5 cells (features ALIGNRUN and ALIGN15/30/60, tilts and switches, 256 cells, REPORT_DRIVERS.md) and are skipped. Item 4 waits for the L2 files.
### GROUP A: SIGNAL SOURCE VARIANTS in the E1 engine (1-minute NQ p1_core account, live rules, 60/60 bracket, 2023-02..2026-10; uplift vs the SAME engine run with the live NQ signals). One pre-declared cell each unless stated; gates and nulls as E1, except that variants that do not involve ES use a RANDOM-ENTRY null (each book's signal events moved to random minutes with the same count and side mix, 60 draws) and plateau neighbours of the variant's own numeric parameters +-25%.
- A1: ST on 2-minute NQ bars: ST(60, 2.0) + EMA(100) on 2-min OHLC; signal flagged at the last 1-minute bar of the 2-min bar; HM unchanged (1-min). Stops/targets on the 1-minute path (as the engine).
- A2: ST with the ATR taken from ES bars (Wilder 120 on ES, converted to NQ points by multiplying by NQ close / ES close) on NQ price; HM unchanged.
- A3: HM with the VWAP weighted by ES volume (VWAP = cumulative(NQ close x ES volume)/cumulative(ES volume) per session); ST unchanged.
- A5: composite ES+NQ price path (E1 construction b) re-sampled to volume-clock bars: composite volume per minute = 0.5 x (NQ volume / its 20-day median + ES volume / its 20-day median); a bar closes whenever cumulative composite volume crosses the next integer; ST(120,2.0)+EMA200 and HM(20,55) on these bars; signal flagged at the last 1-minute bar of the volume bar.
- A6: Heikin-Ashi OHLC of the NQ 1-min bars as ST input (ST(120,2.0), EMA200 on HA close); HM unchanged.
- A7: Kalman local-level filter on the NQ close (process variance = qr x trailing 7,800-minute return variance, observation variance = trailing 390-minute return variance, both shifted one bar) with qr in {0.05, 0.2} (2 cells, clean-only selection); HM computed on x = filtered close - VWAP, ST unchanged.
- A8: NQ flip confirmation: an NQ flip of book b at bar i is executed only when ES (and for majority also RTY) shows a same-direction flip of the same book within the window; entry at max(NQ flip, confirming flip) + 1 bar; no confirmation within k bars after the NQ flip = signal dropped. Cells (6): k {1, 2, 3} x confirmer {ES, majority NQ+ES+RTY (any 2 of 3 same-direction flips within k bars)}; clean-only selection. RTY from the adjusted 1-minute cache (2017+).
- A9: HM fast/slow on x = NQ close x (ES close - ES VWAP)/ES close (ES deviation from its own VWAP, scaled to NQ); ST unchanged.
- A10: ST computed with the close replaced by its 3-bar causal median (EMA200 on the median close); HM unchanged.
### GROUP D: FLOW PERSISTENCE (pair-side, corrected B11 engine, per-minute NQ/ES signed-volume frames 2023-02..2026-10; features use minutes strictly before the entry bar; walk-forward expanding percentile as ap_drv_sw; clean-only selection among the family's cells; E3 gate set, null = circular shift of the factor vector (100 draws), plateau = a x0.75/x1.25 and the adjacent feature variant, replication = H0 and alternating months)
- D30 volume at the flip: VOL5 and VOL30 (sum of volume over the last 5 / 30 minutes); percentile tilt f = clip(1 + sg a (2p-1), 0.5, 1.5); cells (16): feature {VOL5, VOL30} x scope {ALL, BLOCK} x a {0.25, 0.5} x sg {-1, +1}.
- D31 large-trade share (the frame has unsigned big-trade volume, not signed, so the item is run as SHARE not imbalance - deviation stated): BIG5 and BIG15 = sum(big)/sum(volume); same 16-cell tilt grid.
- D32 trade-size regime: AVGSZ = sum(volume)/sum(trades) over the trailing 1,440 minutes; percentile tilt, scope ALL, a {0.25, 0.5} x sg {-1,+1} (4 cells).
- D33 ES signed volume confirms NQ flips: ALIGNES_W = side x ES imbalance over W {15, 30, 60} minutes; tilt grid 3 x 2 x 2 x 2 = 24 cells.
- D34 overnight flow carry: ON = (sum of NQ signed volume from the 18:00 ET session start to the entry bar)/(sum of volume); for entries between 09:30 and the end of the window (first RTH hour {10:30, 11:30}) f = 1 + sg a if side x ON > 0 else 1 - sg a... (agreeing flips scaled by 1+sg a, disagreeing by 1-sg a); other entries 1; cells 2 windows x a {0.25, 0.5} x sg {-1,+1} = 8.
- D35 quiet-flow filter: ABS5 = |sum of signed volume over the last 5 minutes|; skip (x0) or halve (x0.5) when below its expanding q-quantile; q {0.1, 0.2, 0.33} x action {x0, x0.5} x scope {ALL, BLOCK} = 12 cells.
- D36 VPIN approximation: VPIN30 = mean over the last 30 minutes of |signed volume|/volume per traded minute; tilt grid scope {ALL, BLOCK} x a x sg (8) + switches (HIGH tail q {0.67, 0.8} x action {x0.5, x0}: 4) = 12 cells.
- D37 trade-arrival intensity: N5 and N15 (trade counts over the last 5 / 15 minutes); same 16-cell tilt grid as D30.

## K19/K20/K21/K23 (BACKLOG2 group C, D00 pair-internal intraday; worker K; registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any run)
Ledger check: G0a (hard halts after n consecutive stops / max entries per book per day: clean +0.32, H1 -1.40, H2 -1.84), H0a-c (stacking, post-stop-out re-entry window, 2nd combined give-back tier), G0b/B16-B18 (pair-state sizing). Not duplicates: K19 is a SOFT (graded) size decline by the day's accepted-trade count (G0a halted, did not scale; counts both books combined); K20 is a PER-BOOK give-back lock beside the combined lock (H0c added a second tier to the COMBINED lock only; the live per-book rules are only +5R / -2R day and -6R week, no per-book give-back); K21 is a minimum spacing between ENTRIES of one book regardless of stop-outs (H0b conditioned on a prior STOP-OUT within N min, either book, with multipliers; here the condition is merely the previous entry of the same book, entries skipped, so different information and a different mechanism); K23 is a day-start / realised-day-state size ramp (H0b conditioned on stop-outs, G0b on multi-day DD, B17 prior day; none on the day's own opening state).
Common (as H0 batch): engine = live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process (ap_g0_core pass1 hook; baseline 1.46/2.83/2.30 reproduced first, asserted). Fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant. State uses only accepted trades closed (TOUT<=t) or open at the entry time; "day" = entry local day (LIN). Selection: highest clean weekly Sharpe uplift among cells that change >= 5% of clean entries (else all, flagged); ONE cell carried; H1/H2 read once. Gates: uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week not worse; null p95 (100 draws) < real all-in uplift; paired weekly bootstrap 90% CI all-in > 0; plateau (parameters deviation-scaled x0.75/x1.25 one at a time, integers rounded half-up, multipliers as 1+(m-1)x; >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift); 2x slippage >= +0.10 each span; NT8 spec + margin; insurance metrics. Replication reported: H0 window 2023-02-10..07-31, alternating-month split; ES/RTY n/a (pair engine NQ only); ERA n/a (pair rows from 2023-02). Pair-rule, so additive framing = pair change; constant-risk = INSURANCE read via ins_metrics. No stand-alone book, no library save. Grids exactly the backlog's values; where the backlog gives none (K23) the three cells below are fixed now.
- K19 (prior 5%). Mechanism: later trades of a busy day are re-entries inside chop; the day's n-th accepted trade (both books combined) is scaled by m once n > K. GRID (4): K {4,6} x m {0.5,0.75}. Null: circular shift (100) of the realised per-entry multiplier vector. Plateau: K x0.75/x1.25 (4->3,5; 6->5,8 [4.5 rounds half-up to 5, 7.5 to 8]), m deviation.
- K20 (prior 4%). Mechanism: the live system locks only combined P&L and per-book -2R/+5R; a book that peaked >= 1.5R and gives back 67% is in a failing regime for that book while the other keeps trading. Rule: per (exit-day, book) group, equity path of that book's accepted trades (mark-to-market, as the combined lock): trigger when book peak >= 1.5R and equity <= peak x 0.33 -> flatten that book at the next open and block its remaining entries that day; applied BEFORE the combined lock stage (combined lock then runs on the modified rows, unchanged). GRID (1): arm 1.5R / keep 0.33. Null: 100 draws adding a random per-(day,book) lock at a uniform minute with probability = share of groups where the rule fired. Plateau: arm x0.75/x1.25 (1.125, 1.875), keep deviation from 0.33 x0.75/x1.25. Single cell so selection is trivial; the <5% change check is reported.
- K21 (prior 4%). Mechanism: consecutive same-book entries within minutes are re-triggers of the same noisy signal. Rule: skip an entry if the same book's previous ACCEPTED entry was < N minutes earlier (entry-to-entry). GRID (3): N {10,20,30} min. Null: circular shift (100) of the multiplier vector. Plateau: N x0.75/x1.25 (rounded).
- K23 (prior 5%). Mechanism: anti-martingale: start each day small and size up only after the day has paid (opposite of the live system's flat sizing, which relies on day locks to cut losers). GRID (3, fixed here): (a) first accepted entry of the day x0.5, rest full; (b) all entries x0.5 until a closed winning trade exists that day (either book), then full; (c) all entries x0.5 until the day's realised P&L >= +0.5R, then full. Null: circular shift (100). Plateau: m 0.5 -> 0.625/0.375; (c) threshold 0.5R x0.75/x1.25 additionally.
IDs K19, K20, K21, K23.

## BACKLOG2 GROUP C, worker K (registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any K22-K27 run). Items 22, 24, 25, 26, 27 of BACKLOG2.md; item 23 and 19-21 belong to another worker.
Common: live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) rows (ROWS_corrected_engine), clean process, pass1 state hook (ap_g0_core style) in code/census/night_ins/ap_k22_core.py; state uses only trades CLOSED before the entry. Fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT, 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 in baseline and variant. Selection clean-only (cells that change >=5% of clean raw entries, else all); H1/H2 read once; one carried cell. Gates: uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; worst week not worse per span; own timing-destroyed null (100 draws, matched count/duration of the rule's actions placed at random) all-in uplift real > p95; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0 (3000 weekly draws); plateau (+-25% neighbours of the carried cell's numeric parameters, >=75% keep >=75% of all-in uplift); 2x slippage >= +0.10 each span; NT8-codable. Replication (Amend. 2; need 2 of 4): H0 2023-02-10..07-31 uplift >= +0.10; alternating-month split (carried cell uplift >= +0.10 in BOTH odd and even months, and for multi-cell families the cell selected on one parity keeps >= +0.10 on the other); newest live weeks (not separable from H2, n/a); ES/RTY (n/a, pair is NQ). ERA: pair rows start 2023-02 so 2013-22 is n/a. Disclosure: the pair's H1/H2 are read in earlier families (G0a, H11-B, J0 etc.) but these cells were not selected on them. Only pair-change framing exists (rules on the pair, not separate books); constant-risk read via insurance metrics; no library save.
### K22 SYNCHRONISED-WHIPSAW LOCK (BACKLOG2 #22). Mechanism: when ST and HM are both stopped out within a few minutes of each other the market reversed through both books' stops (a shock/whipsaw regime in which the next flips are also noise); a short timed pause avoids the next stop. Prior 4%. Rule: halt ALL new entries (both books) for H minutes after the later of two Stop exits from different books that closed within W=5 min of each other (both exit names contain 'Stop'). Grid (2): H in {30, 60}. Plateau: H x0.75/x1.25 and W x0.75/x1.25 (3.75, 6.25 min). Null: each fired event re-placed at a random entry time of the same label day with the same H. Difference from tested: G0a halts the rest of the day after n consecutive stops of any speed/book; H11-B halts the rest of the day after N stops within X min OF ENTRY; E4 (running elsewhere) varies consecutive-stop count x halt length. K22 triggers on CROSS-BOOK SYNCHRONICITY of the exits and is timed (30/60 min), not day-long: distinct trigger, distinct horizon; not a duplicate, but H11-B shares the late-day truncation artefact so the random-placement null is the key gate.
### K24 HOURLY RISK BUDGET (#24). Mechanism: losses clustered inside one clock hour mark a local chop; capping per-book hourly loss at 1R prevents the book from re-spending the same risk. Prior 3%. Rule: skip a book's entry while the sum of net P&L of that book's trades CLOSED inside the current ET clock hour (hour start..entry) is <= -1.0R (R = $9,600), blocking until the hour ends. Grid (1): L=1.0R. Plateau: L x0.75/x1.25. Null: each fired (book, hour) block re-placed at a random start among that book's entries of the same label day with the same duration. Differs from the per-book live rules (5R/-2R per DAY) and G0a/H0b (consecutive stops / stop-time clusters): P&L-denominated and hour-anchored.
### K25 RE-ENTRY ONLY AFTER PRICE CLEARS THE STOP-OUT LEVEL (#25). Mechanism: after a stop-out the market is on the wrong side of the book's thesis; re-entering the same direction while price is still near/behind the stop level re-buys the same failed idea; require recovery of X pts beyond the stop-out level. Prior 4%. Rule: if the book's most recent closed trade (same label day) was a Stop exit in the same direction as the new entry at stop price L, then skip the entry unless side x (entry price - L) >= X points. Grid (2): X in {10, 20}. Plateau: X x0.75/x1.25. Null: the same number of skips drawn at random among same-book same-direction entries of the same label days. Differs from H0b (time-window multiplier after a stop; no price test) and J0 (exit side).
### K26 OPEN-RISK GATE (#26). Mechanism: the live pair already caps per-book and per-day risk but not simultaneous exposure of the two books; blocking a second entry while combined open risk is high limits the stacked exposure. Prior 3%. Open risk is built from the rows: each open accepted trade j contributes f_j x (60 + u_j)/60 R with u_j = side x (open of the entry bar of the new row - entry open of j) clipped to [-60, +60] points (remaining distance to the 60-pt stop; f_j = 1 live), and the proposed entry adds its own 1R. Block when the total >= X R. Grid (2): X in {1.5, 2}. (Reading note: with nominal 1R per trade and at most 2 concurrent positions in the live rows the total is 1 or 2 R and the thresholds degenerate to 'no concurrency'; the distance-to-stop reading is the only one that gives the two thresholds different meanings: X=1.5 admits the second entry only when the other position is more than 30 pt under water, X=2 when it is under water at all.) Plateau: X x0.75/x1.25. Null: the same number of skips drawn at random among entries that have another position open. Differs from H0a (same-direction stacking multiplier by direction) by using the mark-to-market distance to stop and from netcap/riskcap in B11 development (nominal).
### K27 LAST 2 RTH HOURS HALF SIZE (#27). Mechanism: late-session flow is dominated by closing-auction/MOC mechanics, not the trend flow ST/HM ride. Prior 4%. Rule: entries with ET time in [14:00, 16:00) trade 4 lots (m=0.5), others unchanged and NOT re-normalised. Grid (1). Plateau: m dev x0.75/x1.25 (0.625, 0.375) and window width 90/150 min (start 14:30/13:30, end 16:00). Null: m=0.5 applied to a random set of entries with the same count (permuted multipliers across rows). Difference from G6a: G6a's LATE phase was 14:00-17:00 and every multiplier was mean-normalised to 1 (risk-neutral); K27 is the plain risk cut over 14:00-16:00. Near-duplicate: G6a LATE_m0.5 clean -0.01; run as one cell to settle the un-normalised reading, expectation NO.

## K11 / K13 / K14 / K16 - BACKLOG2 group B exits keyed to external state (workerK, 2026-10-16/17, registered BEFORE first run)
Common: live pair rows (outputs/h2/ROWS_corrected_engine.parquet, 13,089 rows 2023-02-10..2026-10-13) replayed from the fill on 1-second bars (as G11-C/H10-A: 60/60 bracket checked on 1-s bars until the exit bar, then exit at that 1-min bar close; other rows and later signals unchanged; modified rows re-run through the live account B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), clean process, 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots, 2x = SLIP_PT 1.0). A trigger is evaluated at the CLOSE of 1-min bar f >= ei+1 (data through f only) and the exit fills at that close. Baseline weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30. Selection = best CLEAN weekly-Sharpe uplift over the grid; H1/H2 read once for the selected cell. Pair-change framing only (these are exit rules of the pair rows, no separate book, constant-risk n/a). ES/RTY cross-check n/a. Replication = >=2 of {H0 2023-02-10..07-31 uplift same sign and >=50% of clean uplift, alt-month split both directions same sign, newest live weeks (none after 10-13)}. ERA: pair rows start 2023-02, pre-2023 n/a. Gates: uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week not worse; own null p95; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau (>=75% of exact +-25% neighbours keep >=75% of all-in uplift); 2x slip uplift >= +0.10 each span; worst-decile corr n/a (exit rule of the pair, no hedge claim; weekly corr with pair reported); NT8 spec. NULL (timing-destroyed, best-of-grid, 100 draws): the EXTERNAL state series (ES returns for K11/K16; signed volume and its thresholds for K14; close-minus-VWAP deviation for K13) is circularly shifted by a random whole number of days in [7,400] days (either direction, time-of-day preserved), the identical rule is re-run over the full grid, best clean uplift per draw kept; linear delta-P&L on the baseline account (as H10-A). Priors: each 6-8% FOUND (earlier exit families ~0/40; truncating winners reverses on H1/H2). Disclosure: no H1/H2 look at these observables before registration; ES-keyed exits (E2) not yet run by anyone as of claim.
- K11 (item 11, D07 x M5): residual = NQ 1-min point change - beta_{t-1} x ES 1-min point change, beta = rolling 390-bar OLS (lag 1), sigma = rolling 390-bar std of the residual at entry bar (lag 1). Z_f = side x (cum residual from entry close to f) / (sigma x sqrt(f-ei)). Exit at the first f >= ei+5 with Z_f <= -k. GRID 4 cells: k {1,2} x scope {all rows; only when the trade is also in unrealised loss at f}. Plateau: k x0.75/x1.25.
- K13 (item 13, D00/D01 x M5): session VWAP of NQ (typical price (h+l+c)/3 x volume), anchor Globex (18:00 ET) or RTH (09:30-16:00 ET only; undefined outside). dev = close - VWAP. A trade is ARMED once side x dev > 0 at some bar >= ei (incl. entry bar); exit at the first later bar with side x dev <= -b ('reclaim against'). GRID 8 cells: anchor {Globex, RTH} x b {0, 10 pts} x scope {ST rows only; ST+HM}. Plateau: b 10 -> 7.5/12.5; b 0 -> -2.5/+2.5 pts.
- K14 (item 14, D02 x M5): NQ signed volume per minute (vb - vs, outright contracts summed, drivers parquet 2023-02-02..2026-10-13). RUN against the trade over the last m bars (all after entry, i.e. f-ei >= m): net signed volume against the trade >= thr and >= 70% of the m bars signed against. thr = mean over the previous 10 sessions of the 85th percentile of |m-bar net signed volume| in the same ET hour (causal). Exit at the first such f. GRID 6 cells: m {5,10,15} x scope {all; only when in unrealised loss}. Plateau: m x0.75/x1.25 and percentile 75/95.
- K16 (item 16, D07 x M5): the pair rows have fixed 60/60 brackets, no trail. Add a stop that ratchets: MFE (points, bar highs/lows after entry) >= D arms it; at the close of bar f if ES CONFIRMS (ES cumulative close change since entry, in the trade's favour, sets a new running best within the last N bars and is > 0) the stop moves to E0 + side x min(MFE - D, close - 1 pt offset), only ever tighter; target 60 unchanged. GRID 6 cells: D {20,30,45} pts x N {1,5} bars. Reported beside it (not selected): the same ratchet with no ES condition (3 cells), so the ES-confirmation increment is separable. Plateau: D x0.75/x1.25, N x0.75/x1.25 (N=1 -> 2 only).

## BACKLOG2 B12, B17, B18, M96, M99, M100 (K-worker; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any K run). Families K12 K17 K18 K96 K99 K100. Item 98 closed UNTESTED-needs-L2-queue (97 likewise).
Common: live pair rows (13,089, ROWS_corrected_engine) replayed from their actual fill on the 1-second bars (G11-C/H10-A machinery, bracket stop-before-target in the same second, rows closed externally keep their exit if their own bracket is not hit first); account B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process (control reproduces 1.46/2.83/2.30); 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots, 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0; ET clock; selection clean 2023-08..2025-07 only, H1/H2 read once. Gates: uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, own null p95 (clean AND all-in rank), bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0, plateau (>= 75% of +-25% neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift), 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span, alternating-month split (select odd/read even and reverse), H0 (2023-02..07) reported where the family has it, ERA flag (pair rows start 2023-02: pre-2023 n/a), NT8 spec. Disclosures: nothing in these six families was read on H1/H2 before; G11-C found the 60/60 bracket a sharp optimum (Prior low for K12/K18/K99).
### K12 session brackets (D00, M5). Mechanism: Globex and RTH have different range per unit time, so one 60/60 bracket may not be the optimum in both; D2 E6 / G11-C found single-bracket changes lose, this asks whether per-session widths differ. Prior 3%. Grid (8 cells, 60/60 control excluded): Globex width g in {45,60,75} x RTH width r in {60,75,90}, stop = target = width, fixed lots, session by entry time (RTH = 09:30-16:00 ET, Globex otherwise). Selection: highest clean weekly Sharpe. Null: session-label permutation (labels permuted across rows within book x year-month, whole 8-cell grid, 100 draws, linear net swap against baseline locks, statistic best-of-grid uplift). Plateau: each width x0.75 / x1.25 separately (4 neighbours). Constant-risk framing: risk-scaled lots 60/width for the selected cell.
### K17 flatten before CPI/NFP/FOMC (D10, M5, exits only). Mechanism: scheduled prints gap through the 60-pt stop; flattening earlier than the print avoids the jump. H10-A tested offsets 0/-5 min with loss/all scope (NO); this tests the backlog offsets {5, 15, 30} min with scope all open rows, set FOMC (14:00) + NFP + CPI (08:30) verified calendars 2023-08..2026-10; exits only (later pair signals unchanged, no entry block, as H10-A). The 5-min cell repeats H10-A FNC -5 all (disclosed, same numbers expected). Prior 3% (low power: ~125 rows touched). Grid: offset {-5,-15,-30} (3 cells). Plateau: offset x0.75 / x1.25 (rounded). Null: pseudo-events at the same clock times on random pair days (100 draws, linear net swap).
### K18 target scaled by the last-30-min realised vol, stop fixed 60 (D08, M5). Mechanism: when recent realised vol is high the 60-pt target is reached sooner and a wider target captures more; when vol is low a closer target is hit more often. VOLSCALE (H2) scaled stop AND target on prior-day vol; target-only on last-30-min vol is new. Prior 4%. rv30 = RMS of the 1-min close changes over the 30 bars before the entry bar; ref = median of the 30-min-sampled rv30 over the trailing 920 samples (about 20 trading days), shifted one sample; r = rv30/ref; target = 60 x clip(r^(sg*p), lo, hi). Grid (8): p {0.5, 1.0} x clip {[0.75,1.33], [0.5,2.0]} x sg {+1 (wider when vol high), -1 (control)}. Selection: highest clean weekly Sharpe. Null: r permuted across rows within book x year-month (whole grid, 100 draws, linear net swap, best-of). Plateau: p x0.75/x1.25 and clip half-width x0.75/x1.25 (4 neighbours).
### K96 stop-entry orders at flip level +1 tick instead of market at the signal-bar close (D00, M6, H0). Mechanism: a resting stop-entry order enters at the trigger price instead of the next bar open (earlier, at a better price when the bar confirms the flip) but also fires on touches the close does not confirm (failed breaks). Prior 4%. Engine: the corrected order-level account (nt8sim.acct: per-book busy, FIFO netting, protective-order persistence, orphan fills) re-run with entries at (bar i, second k) instead of (bar i+1, open). Trigger level for the signal bar i is computed from the state at the close of bar i-1: ST long: level = max(dn[i-1], ema[i-1]) while trend[i-1] = -1 (short mirrored with up/ema); HM long: smallest close c' with a_i(c') > b_i(c'), b_i(c') > b_{i-1}, c' > ema[i-1] (a, b affine in c' via the HMA end-weights; VWAP of the bar taken at its actual value), while a[i-1] <= b[i-1]. Buy-stop at level + 1 tick (sell-stop at level - 1 tick); fill at the first 1-second bar whose high (low) reaches it, price max(level+tick, previous second close) for buys (min for sells); same BLOCKED/entry-window rules as live on the signal bar; stop/target 60/60 from the fill second; account slippage 1 tick/side as every row. Cells (3): (A) UPPER BOUND, diagnostic only, not a candidate: live pair rows (confirmed flips only) with the entry moved to the first touch second; (B) one-shot: only the first touch per regime run (trend run for ST, a>b state run for HM) per book; (C) re-arm: the order is re-placed every bar while the precondition holds and the book is flat. Candidates B and C; selection clean weekly Sharpe; the control is the same simulator in market mode (must reproduce the live 13,089 rows). Gates as above; null: the same trigger bars filled by a market order at a uniformly random second of the bar (100 draws). Plateau: offset +0 / +2 ticks (2 neighbours, flip level +- rule) . If A fails the clean gate, B and C are not run (upper bound already NO).
### K99 target re-quoted to the nearest session-VWAP band (D11, M5). Mechanism: VWAP +- k sigma bands are where intraday reversion/stalls cluster; a target on a band may fill more often than at +60. Prior 3%. VWAP = the harness daily VWAP (HM's), sigma = volume-weighted std of close around it; at each minute from entry the target is the band level vw + j*s*sigma (j integer) nearest to entry + side*60, using bar i-1 values, distance clamped to [20,100] pts; stop fixed 60. Grid (4): step s {0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0} sigma. Selection: clean weekly Sharpe. Null: the band offsets (level - close, in pts) circularly shifted by a random whole number of trading days (100 draws, selected cell). Plateau: s x0.75/x1.25 and clamp [15,75] / [25,125].
### K100 low-volume-day filter (D08/D06, M4/M8). Mechanism: thin first-30-min volume marks a low-participation day on which the trend books whipsaw. Prior 3%. First-30 volume = NQ volume 09:30-10:00 ET of the trading label; q = 20th percentile of the trailing 60 labels' values (at least 20 prior); entries from 10:00 ET to the end of the label are skipped on days below q (earlier entries unaffected). One pre-declared cell (q=20, skip); plateau q 15 / 25 and trailing window 45 / 75 (4 neighbours); null: the same number of skipped days drawn at random among days with post-10:00 entries (100 draws); alternating-month: both halves reported (no selection). 
### K18b (variant of K18, registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE it was run; K18 as registered ran first and is NO)
Reason: in K18 the ratio r = rv30/ref has median 1.55 at the pair's entries (ref is the all-hours median, entries are in active hours), so every sg=+1 cell moved the MEAN target to 69-87 pts and every sg=-1 cell to 50-54 pts, and the cost of leaving the 60-pt optimum (D2 E6/G11-C) swamps any vol-scaling information. K18b removes that mean shift: r' = r / (median of r over the previous 500 pair rows in time order; 1.0 until 100 rows exist), so the target is 60 when vol is typical of recent entries. Everything else identical (same 8 cells, same selection, null, plateau, gates). Disclosure: K18 printed all cells on all spans before selection (as H10-A), so K18b on H1/H2 is non-virgin evidence for the cell family; K18b cannot be FOUND without the ERA-amendment replication on data not seen (H0 / alternating-month) and cannot override the K18 verdict, it only decides whether the idea is a lead.

## BACKLOG2 GROUP K (items 82, 83, 86, 88, 89; 87 folded into 82; 85 folded into 83; 84 separate). Registered 2026-10-17T~00:30Z. DISCLOSURE: the run script ap_k_run.py was launched a few minutes before this entry was written; at the time of writing only the AUC of the first two models (price features: y 0.523/0.505/0.519, whipsaw w 0.80) had been printed - no cell Sharpe or uplift had been looked at.
- Data: pair rows (ROWS_corrected_engine, 13,089 trades 2023-02..2026-10) with the C2-G2 price features + NEW features: NQ/ES per-minute signed volume (buy minus sell aggressor) imbalance over 5/15/30/60 min, large-trade share 15 min, trade intensity vs prior 5 sessions, same-sign run length, NQ-ES imbalance gap, side-signed versions (outputs/autopilot/drivers/min_*_2023.parquet), and daily NQ gamma/GEX features from the OPTIONS worker (outputs/autopilot/opt/feat_NQ.parquet; GEX normalised, zero-gamma distance, term, skew, ATM vol) lagged one trading day. L2 (mbp-10) features are NOT included (NQ mbp-10 under A5 control, not on this worker's disk path); item 85 (adverse-excursion exits) is represented by the whipsaw target (stopped out within 20 min); item 87 (pooled ES/NQ/RTY features) is reduced to ES+NQ flow (no RTY trades).
- K82/K83: walk-forward LightGBM (monthly refit on all rows whose exit precedes the month start, 2023-08 onwards; C2-G2 parameters). Feature sets {price, price+flow+gamma, flow+gamma}; targets y = trade wins, w = whipsaw (stop within 20 min). Cells: skip the {5,10,15}% worst-scored entries or half-size the {10,20}% (worst = lowest P(win) or highest P(whipsaw); cut-offs from the clean predictions) = 30 cells (3 sets x 2 targets x 5). Gates: pair weekly Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1 and H2 each (vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) = 1.46/2.83/2.30), worst week not worse, random-removal null (100 draws, same rate, p95 of the uplift) for the three cells with the best minimum-span uplift, bootstrap, plateau (rates +-25%: neighbours q), 2x slippage.
- K88 anomaly halt: IsolationForest on the flow+price features of past rows (monthly refit), skip entries scored in the top 5/10% most anomalous (walk-forward) - run after the above, same gates.
- K86 regime switch: 3-state Gaussian HMM on daily NQ flow/volatility features fit on all days up to the prior month (2013 onward), state of the previous day -> half-size on states whose pair P&L per trade in the training window (2023-02 on) is negative with >= 100 trades; walk-forward; same gates.
- K89 temporal transfer for every ML cell: train 2024-25 test 2026 and train 2026 test 2024-25 (single fit, LightGBM, rank cut-offs inside the test span at 10/20%); the cell passes only if the test-span weekly Sharpe change is >= +0.10 in BOTH directions.
- K84 sequence/direction model (standalone sleeve on NQ and ES, 15/30/60-minute time exits) is registered separately before it is run.
### K96b (variant of K96, registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE the variant's gate run; K96 as registered ran first: cells A upper bound +1.69/+2.19/+2.71, B one-shot -0.20/+1.28/+1.34, C re-arm -0.36/+1.95/+0.75 => NO, clean gate fails)
Why a variant: a DIAGNOSTIC run after K96 (not selection, not gated) split the stop entries by book on the same account: ST stop + HM market = -0.05/-0.23/-0.10 (clean/H1/H2); HM stop + ST market = +0.36/+0.59/+0.25 (non-causal VWAP) and +0.72/+0.66/+0.25 (causal VWAP, the bar's VWAP held at its previous value as an order placed at the bar start would). Mechanism: the HM cross level sits within a few points of price, so a touch is usually confirmed by the close and the stop fill buys the cross price instead of the close; the ST band is 2 ATR(120) away, so a touch is more often a failed break (whipsaw) and the entry-price gain does not repay it. Also the first K96 HM look-ahead (bar VWAP at its actual value) is removed. Spec: ST rows exactly as live (market at the next open); HM stop-entry one-shot (first touch per a<=b / a>b regime run) at the causal flip level +1 tick; everything else as K96. Single pre-declared cell. Plateau: +0 and +2 ticks, and re-arm instead of one-shot (3 neighbours). Null: the same HM trigger bars filled by a market order at a uniformly random second at or after the first touch (100 draws; fixes the K96 null, which could fill before the touch). Gates as K96 (clean/H1/H2 uplift, worst week, bootstrap, plateau, 2x slippage, alternating-month split, H0 2023-02..07). DISCLOSURE: the diagnostic showed all spans' all-in/clean/H1/H2 uplifts for this variant (non-virgin on clean, H1, H2), H0 and the alternating-month halves were NOT printed; so the variant cannot be FOUND by clean/H1/H2 and needs H0 + alternating-month (two replications) plus a live read; it can at most be a LEAD / FOUND-PENDING.

### K84 (registered 2026-10-17, before any K84 cell was evaluated; the OOS AUC of the direction models is printed by the training script and is not a pair-window P&L)
Standalone D12 sleeve on NQ and ES: LightGBM walk-forward (quarterly refit, expanding window from 2013, OOS predictions 2018-01 onward) classifying the sign of the forward 15/30/60-minute return (entry at the open after a 15-minute bar close, return in bar-ATR units) from 15-minute bar features (returns over 1/2/4/8 bars in ATR, 8-bar range position/width, ATR/ADR, time of day, day of week) with feature sets {price, price+flow} where flow = own and cross (NQ<->ES) signed-volume imbalance over 15/30/60/120 min, large-trade share, trade intensity. Cells per instrument: set {price, price+flow} x horizon {15,30,60} min x threshold delta {0.02,0.04,0.06} (long if p > 0.5+delta, short if p < 0.5-delta) = 18 cells; exit = time exit after the horizon, no stop, one position at a time, entries 18:00-15:00 ET and full holds before 16:55, 1 contract then vol-matched. Selection and all gates by the CYCLE 12 clean-only re-registration (ap_b_pipe2: >= 100 trades and positive weekly Sharpe in both clean halves, 200 timing-destroyed replicates of random entry timing with the SAME predictions' trade counts, H1/H2 once, bootstrap, plateau = delta/horizon neighbours, 2x slippage, ERA flag with 2018-2022 as the pre-2023 span). K89: the ML cell is also evaluated with the model trained on 2018-2022 only (no refit after 2022; frozen) over 2023-2026.

## PA4 / PA5 / PA6 / PA7 (worker PA, ideas 4-7 of 'Batch 3 ideas' in RESEARCH_LOG.md; registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any PA run; only feature coverage counts were looked at, no P&L)
Common: pipeline copied from J9P (ap_j79_run.py -> ap_pa1_run.py for the size tilts PA4/PA5; ap_kc/ap_k_core + ap_k_lib 1-second replay for the exits PA6/PA7). Baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), clean process, 1.46/2.83/2.30 asserted; fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and candidate; selection = max CLEAN weekly-Sharpe uplift over the family's cells; carried cell's H1/H2 read once; UNTOUCHED never; gates: uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, own null p95 (clean and all-in), bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0, plateau >= 75% of +-25% neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift, 2x slip >= +0.10 each span, alternating-month split (select on odd months, read on even, and reverse), H0 (2023-02..07) where the exit engine has rows. ERA flag n/a (pair rows start 2023). Pair-change framing only (tilts/exits of the live pair, no separate book). ES/RTY cross-check run only if the NQ gates pass (it would be needed for the Amendment-2 replication). Pool of 5. Disclosure: J9P (same author line) tilts all failed; J10 found no NKD lead standalone; no H1/H2 look at any PA observable before registration. Priors 2-3% each.
### PA4 NKD-STATE TILT OF GLOBEX ENTRIES (D07 x M4, H3; prior 2%)
Mechanism: Globex-hours NQ entries (18:00-03:00 ET) happen while Tokyo trades; NKD (CME USD Nikkei) is the local price-discovery leader for Asia risk; an NQ breakout agreeing with the contemporaneous NKD drift is supported by real Asian flow, a counter one is a thin-market drift. J10 tested NKD only as a standalone NQ signal. Feature n_L = SIDE x (ln NKD[EI-1] - ln NKD[EI-1-L]) / (sigma_NKD1m x sqrt L), 1-minute grid closes (forward fill <= 5 min, else NaN), sigma = trailing 1300-bar sd of NKD 1-min log returns (|r|>2% zeroed). Applies only to entries whose entry minute is 18:00-02:59 ET; all other entries and NaN features keep multiplier 1. Cells (9): L {60,180,360} min x scheme {COUNTER_DOWN (bottom tercile x0.5), ALIGN_UP (top tercile x1.5), TWO (bottom x0.75 / top x1.25)}; terciles frozen from clean Globex entries. Plateau (6): share 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25, L x0.75/x1.25 (45/75, 135/225, 270/450). Null: feature permuted among trades within (book, year, ET hour) blocks, 100 reps. NT8: second data series (NKD) in the strategy, quantity multiplier.
### PA5 NQ/ES REALISED-VOL RATIO TILT (D07/D08 x M8, H2; prior 3%)
Mechanism: H7-A used the NQ-minus-ES return spread; the ratio of realised volatility detects NQ-specific (megacap) stress where NQ moves are idiosyncratic and trend-continuation is different from index-wide moves. Feature q_W = ln( sqrt(sum r_NQ^2 over W bars) / sqrt(sum r_ES^2 over W bars) ) for the W 1-minute grid bars ending at EI-1 (|r|>2% zeroed), W {30,60,120}. Cells (9): W x scheme {HIGH_DOWN (top tercile x0.5), HIGH_UP (top tercile x1.5), TWO_V (top x0.75 / bottom x1.25)}; terciles frozen from clean entries. Plateau (6): share 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25, W x0.75/x1.25 (22/38, 45/75, 90/150). Null: block permutation as PA4. NT8: ES as second series, rolling sums.
### PA6 VOLUME-CLIMAX EXIT (D01 x M5, H1-H2; prior 3%)
Mechanism: a 1-minute bar printing volume far above its same-minute norm, in the direction of the trade, while the trade is already in profit, is exhaustion/absorption (the last aggressive buyers meet passive supply); H7-B was a price-spread exit, J6c clock-based, C2-G1 unconditional. Rule: at the CLOSE of 1-min bar f (ei+1 <= f < xi) exit at that close if vol[f] >= k x mean(vol at the same ET minute over the previous 20 sessions, shift 1) AND side x (close[f] - close[f-1]) > 0 AND side x (close[f] - E0) >= X points. Grid (6): k {3,5} x X {10,20,30}. Plateau (4): k x0.75/x1.25, X x0.75/x1.25. Null: the volume-ratio series circularly shifted by a random whole number of days in [7,400] (time of day preserved), identical rule over the whole grid, best-clean-uplift cell per draw, 100 draws (as K14). Replication: alt-month + H0. Rows: ROWS_corrected_engine on the 1-second replay (K14/K17 engine), kind-1 external-exit rows exit at the trigger close.
### PA7 EARLY ADVERSE-EXCURSION EXIT (D00 x M5, H1; prior 3%)
Mechanism: a fast adverse impulse right after a flip means informed flow against; leaving at once cuts the loss before the 60-pt stop (J0-C works at hours, J0-A on stagnation; nothing at 2-5 minutes). Rule: at the CLOSE of 1-min bar f with ei+1 <= f <= ei+M, exit at that close if side x (close[f] - E0) <= -X points. Grid (6): X {10,20,30} points x M {3,5} minutes. Plateau (4): X x0.75/x1.25, M x0.75/x1.25 (rounded 2/4 and 4/6). Null: the same number of rows as fired in the selected cell, chosen at random among rows of the same book, exited at a random bar ei+1..ei+M at that bar's close (100 draws, linear delta-P&L), report-only rank plus p95 as a gate. Cost heavy by construction (two fills); expected NO.

### PA-K96B / PA3 (fresh pre-registration of the K96b cure, registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any PA3 grid cell was evaluated; code ap_pa3_core.py / ap_pa3.py)
DISCLOSURE (non-virgin): K96b (HM stop-entry at the causal flip level +1 tick, one-shot, ST at market) already printed clean/H1/H2/all-in (+0.72/+0.66/+0.25/+0.62), H0 (+0.05) and both alternating-month halves (+1.33 / -0.03), plateau and 2x slippage for the 1-cell case. Every span and both replication windows (H0, alternating month) are therefore NON-VIRGIN for the cell (offset 1, lifetime = whole run), which is inside this grid. The only untouched evidence is live weeks after 2026-10-13 (none exist on disk at registration: ROWS_corrected_engine last exit 2026-10-12, 1-min bars end 2026-10-12 19:59 ET) and ES/RTY (not executable here: the pair-row account is NQ). So the best possible outcome of this study is FOUND-PENDING (live-week confirmation, >= 8 new weeks, watch protocol), never FOUND. Selection is clean-only; H1/H2 are read for the selected cell only and are disclosed as already seen.
Mechanism: the HM cross level sits a few points from price; entering on the touch buys the cross price instead of the confirmed close. Prior: low-moderate (the earlier lead failed bootstrap by 0.005, plateau 2/3, 2x-slip H2, and H0/odd-month were ~0). The question is whether a proper treatment of order lifetime removes the false-trigger tail that produced the plateau/alt-month weakness.
Spec: ST at market exactly as live (next open). HM entries only, as stop-market orders. Level for bar i from the state at the close of bar i-1 (causal, VWAP held at its previous value), as K96b: long smallest close c' with a_i(c')>b_i(c'), b_i(c')>b[i-1], c'>ema[i-1], precondition a[i-1]<=b[i-1] (short mirrored). Order placed at each bar start while the precondition holds, book flat is NOT required at placement (the account decides busy/netted, as live), re-quoted every bar to the current causal level, cancelled at expiry. One-shot per regime run (a<=b / a>b run, ids by state at i-1). BLOCKED bars as live. Fill = first 1-second bar reaching level + offset ticks, price max(level+offset, previous-second close) for buys (gap through the stop fills at the gap price, never better), mirrored for sells; 60/60 bracket from the fill second; account fees/slippage as every row; 2x = SLIP_PT 1.0. Orders never filled in their window are lost trades: no market fallback, no re-entry; fill rate, number of live HM market signals left without a stop fill, and their market-mode P&L are reported per cell.
GRID (15 cells, exactly): entry offset {0,1,2} ticks above/below the cross level  x  order lifetime {5,15,30,60 min from the first bar of the regime run, RUN = until the next signal i.e. until the run ends}. The K96b cell is (1,RUN).
SELECTION: max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift vs the live pair (control reproduces 13,103 live rows and 1.46/2.83/2.30 first; abort if not). Selected cell only is carried to H1/H2/all-in.
PLATEAU (discrete grid, replaces the +-25% rule): neighbours = the cells one step away along each axis (offset +-1 tick, lifetime +-1 step in 5/15/30/60/RUN), 2-4 of them. A neighbour keeps if its all-in uplift >= 75% of the selected cell's all-in uplift AND its clean uplift > 0; plateau passes if >= 75% of neighbours keep (3 of 4, 2 of 2, 3 of 3; a selected cell with only 2 neighbours needs both).
GATES: uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week (all-in) not worse; selection-corrected null (below) p95 on clean AND all-in; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau as above; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 on each span; alternating-month split with full re-selection over the 15 cells (select odd/read even and reverse, both same sign and >= 50% of the in-sample uplift); H0 (2023-02-10..07-31) uplift > 0 and >= 50% of the clean uplift. Report-only stress: stop-market slippage beyond the model, entry filled 1 and 2 ticks worse than modelled on every HM stop fill (trigger unchanged).
SELECTION-CORRECTED NULL: 100 replicates; each replicate re-draws, for every trigger event of every cell, a fill second uniformly at or after the first touch within the signal bar (draws shared across cells for the same bar and replicate, price = that second's close), re-runs the whole 15-cell account, applies the SAME selection (max clean uplift) and records that selected cell's uplift on clean/H1/H2/all. The real selected uplift must exceed the p95 of this selection-corrected distribution on clean and all-in.
REPLICATION for FOUND-PENDING (ERA amendment): >= 2 of {newest live weeks (none yet), H0, ES/RTY (n/a), alternating month}; since H0 and alternating month are non-virgin for this family the cap is FOUND-PENDING pending >= 8 new live weeks (watch script ap_pa3_watch.py, watermark label day 20261013, uses the saved selected cell).
Implementation spec for NT8 (order type, OCO, gaps, Rithmic stop-entry slippage) is part of REPORT_PA3.md.

## PA2 batch: PA8, PA9, PA10 (registered 2026-10-16 by worker PA2 BEFORE any PA8/PA9/PA10 variant return was computed; only the count of entries inside the weekly-open window (726 entries in 6 h, 308 clean) and the weekend-gap size distribution were looked at, no P&L). Source: RESEARCH_LOG.md 'Batch 3 ideas' 8, 9, 10 (worker J9P). Code: code/census/night_ins/ap_pa2_*.py (copied from ap_j79_*, ap_g11_c_*), outputs in outputs/autopilot/pa8|pa9|pa10/.
Common (as 'J7V + J9P'): baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a CLEAN process, 1.46/2.83/2.30 asserted; fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8-lot RT; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and candidate; overlay NOT renormalised; selection = max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift over the family's cells; carried cell's H1/H2 read once; UNTOUCHED never; gates: uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, own timing-destroyed null p95 on clean and all-in, bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0, plateau >= 75% of the +-25% neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift, 2x slippage >= +0.10 each span; alternating-month split over all cells; H0 (2023-02..07) where rows exist (pair rows start 2023-08, so usually n/a); ERA flag n/a. Pool of 5. Terciles/edges frozen from clean entries.
### PA8  SUNDAY-OPEN WEEKEND-GAP STATE TILT OF THE FIRST HOURS OF THE WEEK (D06/D11 x M4/M8, H2; prior 3%)
Mechanism: the first hours after the Sunday 18:00 ET reopen price the weekend news in thin liquidity; a gap in the trade's direction means the breakout is partly already paid (or continuation of weekend repricing), a gap against it means a fill-in fade. S5 shows the 18:00 hour is the largest and least stable bucket (clean 38% of net, H1 -24%). Not G8-B (RTH overnight gap), not J11-A (weekly floor), not I1/W6/Z3/T1 (standalone weekend-gap fade; here the gap conditions the EXISTING pair's entries). Feature g = SIDE x ln(first 1-min close after the Sunday reopen / last 1-min close before it) from the NQ 1-min tape (m1_NQ.npz; weekends where the contract id changes -> neutral 1.0). Entries between Sunday 18:00 ET and 18:00+H hours get a multiplier from the tercile of g (edges frozen from clean entries inside the window); all other entries 1.0. Cells (9): H {3,6,12} h x scheme {AGAINST_DOWN (bottom tercile x0.5), WITH_UP (top tercile x1.5), TWO (bottom 0.75 / top 1.25)}. Plateau neighbours (6): share 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25, H x0.75/x1.25. Null: the weekly gaps permuted across weekends (100 reps), SIDE alignment recomputed per trade. Expect 2-3%: only 308 clean entries are in the 6 h window, so the Sharpe uplift cannot be large and plateau is the likely failure. Executability: NT8 reads the Friday 17:00 close and the Sunday open and scales quantity.
### PA9  ST/HM BRACKET ASYMMETRY BY MULTI-DAY ALIGNMENT (D11 x M5/M6, H5; prior 2%)
Mechanism: with a persistent drift a breakout can afford a wider target and tighter stop; against it the reverse. G11-C proved 60/60 is a sharp optimum for swing-stops and fixed-width choices, so the prior is low; this family only asks whether the 60/60 symmetry is sharp in the alignment dimension. Feature a_L = SIDE x (D[t]-D[t-L])/(sigma_D60 sqrt L) on NQ 17:00 ET daily closes strictly before the entry label day (= J9P-A feature), L {10,30,60}. Rows replayed from the actual fill on 1-second bars with the G11-C machinery (ap_g11_c_core: stop tested before target within the same second; rows whose baseline exit was external, ~41%, keep their original exit; control sd=td=60 reproduces the live rows and 1.46/2.83/2.30). Brackets: top tercile (with trend) stop 50 / target 70, bottom tercile (against) stop 70 / target 50, middle 60/60; schemes BOTH, WITH_ONLY, AGAINST_ONLY; fixed lots; 9 cells (L x scheme). Plateau neighbours (6): share 25/42, bracket distance 10 -> 7.5/12.5, L x0.75/x1.25. Null: alignment values permuted among rows within (book, year, ET hour) blocks, replay, linear net swap as in G11-C (100 reps). DISCLOSURE: the parent idea J9P-A (same feature as a size tilt) was run and its H1/H2 read once for its carried cell (L60_ALIGN_UP +0.45/+0.11/-0.58): non-virgin evidence for the feature; a FOUND needs the Amendment-2 replication on untouched data. G11-C H1/H2 of the unselected 60/60-centred cells were also read. Executability: NT8 bracket set per entry from NQ daily closes.
### PA10  K4 WALK-FORWARD DAILY FORECAST AS DIRECTION-ALIGNMENT TILT OF THE PAIR (D12 x M4, H5; prior 3%)
Mechanism: the K4 pooled (ES/NQ/RTY/YM) walk-forward LightGBM p(up next day) is a long-biased LEAD sleeve; as a pair conditioner, entries whose side agrees with the model are sized up, against it down. Feature a = SIDE x (p - 0.5), p = K4 forecast of the last completed daily label strictly before the entry label day (walk-forward quarterly refits since 2016, purged; regenerated by ap_pa2_k4gen.py with the K4 code unchanged, NQ column). Cells (6): model {d2, d4} x scheme {COUNTER_DOWN (bottom tercile x0.5), ALIGN_UP (top x1.5), TWO (0.75/1.25)}. Plateau neighbours (6): share 25/42, multiplier distance x0.75/x1.25, and the two model variants {d2_s1 (seed 1), d2_t188 (188 trees)} if d2 is carried ({d2, d2_s1} if d4 is carried). Null: p permuted across label days (100 reps). DISCLOSURE: (a) the K4 model's own sleeve results used the pair spans in K4/K4v2 (H1/H2 read); (b) parent idea J9P-A H1/H2 read once. Both are non-virgin evidence; FOUND needs Amendment-2 replication (ES/RTY or newest weeks or H0).
Order: PA8, PA9, PA10. Reports REPORT_PA8.md, REPORT_PA9.md, REPORT_PA10.md, each ends 'DONE PA<k>'.

### L91 (registered 2026-10-17, before any run): pair-style signals on NKD in Asia hours
NKD 1-min bars are on disk (bought by worker I for J10: data/autopilot/ohlcv1m/NKD_*.dbn.zst, $12.00, SPEND.json) -> no new spend. Grid = the B1 grid (180 cells: ST/HM/WT sets x 5/10/15/30-min bars x ATR/ADR trail exits, equal R $9,600) restricted to entries 19:00-01:30 ET with flatten at 02:00 ET. NKD tick 5 index points ($25/tick, $5/pt) so 1 tick/side slippage = $25 per side per contract. Selection and gates: CYCLE 12 clean-only re-registration, 200 replicates. Prior 3% (thin market: many 1-minute rows are missing; the 5-minute bar construction uses the time keys).

### PX1 (registered 2026-10-17, BEFORE any PX1 run): PA5 NEAR-MISS CURE - NQ/ES 60-min realised-vol-ratio tilt, de-risking-controlled (D07/D08 x M8, H2; prior 6%)
DISCLOSURE (binding): the parent PA5 (REPORT_PA5.md) was already read on clean AND H1 AND H2: L60_TWO_V gave +0.55/+0.55/+0.06, plateau 1/6, 2x slip H2 -0.17, alt-month lopsided, mean multiplier 0.960. H1/H2 for the NQ pair are therefore NON-VIRGIN for this feature; no NQ result on H1/H2 or all-in can establish FOUND. Maximum attainable status for PX1 even if every gate passes = FOUND-PENDING, awaiting the newest live weeks (rows after 2026-10-13). H0 is not testable (pair rows start 2023-08). The mandatory ES/RTY leg below is the only data on which the rule is untouched by the parent, but it covers the same calendar window (correlated, not independent) so it too cannot lift the status above FOUND-PENDING.
Reason for the cure (not parameter hunting): the parent failure pattern is (i) H2 +0.06 and 2x-slip failure while the multipliers have mean 0.96 (a 4% de-risking that can mimic a Sharpe gain through the weekly-floor/day-cap interaction), (ii) lopsided alternating-month split, (iii) plateau 1/6 caused by the tercile/multiplier neighbours being strongly non-monotone. The cure tests whether the effect survives the removal of the size drift and whether it is more than a sizing artefact, using controls, not new feature engineering. Feature, terciles (frozen from clean entries) and TWO_V scheme (top tercile x0.75, bottom tercile x1.25, middle 1) are IDENTICAL to PA5; q_W = ln(sqrt(sum r_NQ^2)/sqrt(sum r_ES^2)) over the W 1-min grid bars ending at EI-1, |r|>2% zeroed.
GRID (6 cells, no selection among them): W {45, 60, 75} x norm {RAW (multipliers as PA5), MEAN1 (RAW multipliers divided by their mean over ALL pair trades 2023-02..2026-10; a constant scalar, diagnostic and ex-post for the scale only, so that the average size is exactly 1)}. PRE-DECLARED PRIMARY CELL for all gates = W60_MEAN1 (the de-risking-neutral version of the parent). W60_RAW is re-reported for continuity; the other four cells are reported, not carried. No other cells.
CONTROLS (reported with the verdict): (c1) FLAT 0.96x: constant size multiplier 0.960 on every trade (the parent's mean), baseline-vs-flat uplift reported per span; (c2) RANDOM-TERCILE-ASSIGNMENT NULL with identical multiplier distribution: 100 draws, the primary cell's multiplier vector randomly permuted across ALL trades (global permutation) - gate on p95 of clean and all-in uplift; (c3) block null as in PA5 (feature permuted within (book, year, ET hour) blocks, then MEAN1 scalar re-applied as for the primary): 100 draws, same gate; the stricter of c2/c3 p95 applies. Selection-correction not needed (no selection), stated.
PLATEAU (neighbours of W60_MEAN1, 6): tercile cut points +-25% (share 25% / 42% instead of 33.3%, edges re-frozen on clean), multiplier deviations from 1 x0.75 / x1.25 (0.8125/1.1875 and 0.6875/1.3125), lookback W 45 / 75; each neighbour is MEAN1-renormalised. PASS = at least 75% of the 6 keep >= 75% of the primary's all-in uplift (needs >= 5 of 6).
GATES (all on W60_MEAN1, baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) clean process asserted 1.46/2.83/2.30; fill 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and candidate): (g1) weekly-Sharpe uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; (g2) uplift minus flat-0.96x-control uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 (de-risking control); (g3) worst week not worse (all-in); (g4) null p95 (c2 and c3) clean and all-in; (g5) bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; (g6) plateau as above; (g7) 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; (g8) alternating-month split (select among the 6 cells on odd months, read on even, and reverse; plus the primary cell's held-out uplift) - gate: primary's held-out uplift > 0 in BOTH directions and >= 25% of its selection-half uplift; (g9) ES/RTY leg: sign positive and magnitude >= 50% of the NQ all-in uplift (mean of the two) on both books' all-in span, with no span-level contradiction larger than -0.10; (g10) insurance metrics (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg weeks; clean/H1/H2/all; report INS? flags); (g11) NT8-codable + margin.
MANDATORY ES/RTY LEG: the same rule applied to standalone ST+HM books on ES and RTY 1-min bars (d1_f4_core, ADR20 k=0.75, equal R $9,600, live account rules, 1 tick/side + commission; same as the J7V cross-check). Own-vs-reference vol ratio: NQ book NQ/ES (as above); ES book ES/NQ (reference NQ); RTY book RTY/ES. Per book: terciles frozen from that book's clean-window entries; W {45,60,75} x {RAW, MEAN1 over all that book's trades}, plus the flat 0.96x control; report weekly-Sharpe uplift on clean/H1/H2/all-in per cell with sign and magnitude; primary = W60_MEAN1 per book. These are proxies (not the live pair), no gate on H1/H2 of the proxies beyond g9.
Null on ES/RTY: not run (reported uplift only). Executability: NT8, ES (and RTY for the leg) as secondary series, rolling sums of squared 1-min returns, quantity multiplier (MEAN1 range about 0.78-1.30); no extra margin for multipliers <= 1; peak about +30% on bottom-tercile entries.
Outputs: outputs/autopilot/px1/, code/census/night_ins/ap_pc_*.py, REPORT_PX1.md ending 'DONE PX1'.

## PX20-PX27 (BACKLOG2 items 62, 63, 66, 69, 65, 72, 74, 70; worker PX; registered 2026-10-16 ~20:40Z BEFORE any PX run; only code paths and entry counts looked at, no P&L)
Duplicate check: A (signal denoising) is claimed/pre-registered by workerI (ap_a_run.py, not run yet) -> NOT taken. B/C/M by K-worker, D by workerI, E/L2-gated. Item 64 (VX intraday) and 71 (VVIX) need data not on disk; 73 (Garman-Klass next-hour forecast) is the same state as 70 with a different estimator -> folded into PX27 as non-separate; 75 duplicates H8-C/J11-B; 67 duplicates H7-A (RTY spread); 68 duplicates PA4 (NKD tilt); 77 duplicates J5/J4; 76/78/79 need calendars not on disk; 80/81 rule-based but tiny samples (not run). Items taken are untested pair-side/conditional cells with data on disk.
Common (as PA4/PA5): live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) via ROWS_corrected_engine in a CLEAN process (baseline must reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30); overlay multiplier per pair entry (0 = skip), not renormalised; features use only 1-minute bars closed before the entry bar (EI-1) or earlier days; terciles/thresholds frozen from clean entries (2023-08..2025-07); cells computed on clean first, best clean uplift carried (eligibility: touched share of clean entries 3-45%), H1/H2 read once for the carried cell; null = 100 block-permutations of the feature within (book,year,hour) blocks (same frequency), p95 on clean and all-in; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in weekly-Sharpe uplift > 0; plateau: neighbours of the carried cell at +-25% of its numeric parameters (threshold, window/lookback, tercile share, multiplier depth) keep >= 75% of all-in uplift for >= 75% of neighbours; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; worst week not worse; uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; alternating-month split (select on odd months read on even and reverse); H0/pre-2023 n/a (pair rows from 2023-08); ES/RTY cross-check only if NQ gates pass; insurance metrics; fill model 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots. Pool of 5. Code ap_pc_run.py/ap_pc_doc.py, outputs outputs/autopilot/px2*/ .
- PX20 (item 62, ZN 5-min shock): z5(t)=(ln ZN_t - ln ZN_{t-5})/(sigma_1m(t) sqrt5), sigma = trailing 1300-minute RMS of 1-minute returns (|r|>2% dropped); F_W = max |z5| over the last W minutes; flagged if F_W > 2.0. Mechanism: a rates shock transmits to NQ with a lag and whipsaws trend rules while repricing. Cells (4): W {30,60} x action {HALF x0.5, SKIP x0}. Neighbours: thr 1.5/2.5, W x0.75/x1.25.
- PX21 (item 63, 6E shock as DXY proxy): identical construction on 6E, thr 2.0. 4 cells.
- PX22 (item 66, CL shock days): identical on CL, thr 2.0. 4 cells.
- PX23 (item 69, gold+yen risk-off composite): c(t) = (z5_GC + z5_6J)/2 (both up = risk-off), F_W = max c over last W minutes, flagged if F_W > 1.5 (threshold 1.5 not 2.0 because averaging two z cuts the s.d. by about 1.4; fixed before running); 4 cells as PX20. Neighbours thr 1.125/1.875, W x0.75/x1.25.
- PX24 (item 65, BTC overnight trend, Globex bias): n_L = side x (ln BTC[EI-1] - ln BTC[EI-1-L])/(sigma_BTC1m sqrt L), L {60,180,360}, applied to Globex entries 18:00-02:59 ET only (others x1); schemes COUNTER_DOWN (bottom tercile x0.5), ALIGN_UP (top tercile x1.5), TWO (0.75/1.25); 9 cells, identical grid to PA4 (NKD). BTC 1-minute from data/t4 crypto_1m BTC (front contract, same builder as ap_b_series).
- PX25 (item 72, first-hour range / trend-day classifier): on each ET day, FH = NQ 1-minute closes 09:30-10:29; RNG = (max-min of FH closes)/ADR20 (mean of the previous 20 RTH days' 09:30-15:59 close range), EFF = |last-first|/(max-min). Applied to RTH entries 10:30-15:59 ET only (others x1). Cells (6): feature {RNG, EFF} x scheme {HIGH_DOWN (top tercile x0.5), HIGH_UP (top x1.5), TWO_V (top x0.75 / bottom x1.25)}. Neighbours: share 0.25/0.42, depth x0.75/x1.25.
- PX26 (item 74, overnight gap / ADR RTH regime): gap = ln(NQ 09:30 bar close / previous 15:59 close) with roll days (instrument id change) set to missing; ABS = |gap| / (ADR20/price), ALIGN = side x gap/(ADR20/price). RTH entries 09:30-15:59 ET only. Cells (6): ABS x {HIGH_DOWN, HIGH_UP, TWO_V}; ALIGN x {COUNTER_DOWN, ALIGN_UP, TWO}. Neighbours as PX25.
- PX27 (item 70 + 73 folded, last-30-min realised-vol state): q = ln( sqrt(sum r^2 over last 30 bars) / sqrt(30 x mean r^2 over the trailing 7,800 bars) ) at EI-1 (walk-forward by construction; terciles frozen from clean). Cells (3): schemes HIGH_DOWN, HIGH_UP, TWO_V. Neighbours: W 22/38, share, depth. (bracket variant not run: K18 already tested vol-scaled targets, NO.)
Null for every family: the family's own feature permuted within blocks, thresholded identically. Zero FOUND is acceptable; no cell/threshold added after seeing results.
- PX clarification (written before any run): the 3-45% touched-share eligibility applies to the skip/half (flag) families PX20-PX23 only; tercile-tilt families (PX24-PX27) touch about 2/3 of their applicable entries by construction, as in PA4/PA5, and carry no eligibility rule. Shock lookbacks use 5 grid bars and require the 5 bars to span exactly 5 minutes (else missing). Terciles are computed over the clean entries where the feature is defined.
- PX AMENDMENT 1 (2026-10-16 ~20:50Z, before any PX run; based only on flagged-share counts, no P&L): with overlapping 30/60-minute windows the rolling max of |z5| exceeds 2.0 on 47-60% (6E) and 54-66% (CL) of clean entries, so a 2-sigma 'shock' is routine for those assets and the eligibility rule (3-45%) would void the family. Threshold fixed at 3.0 for PX21 (6E) and PX22 (CL) (clean flagged share 23%/30% at W30); neighbours 2.25/3.75. PX20 (ZN, 2.0: 31% at W30) and PX23 (composite, 1.5) unchanged. All else as registered.

## GC FADE v2: ONE COMBINED GC REPORT (L93 ensemble + B7 GC cell), registered 2026-10-17 before any v2 run
Status of the evidence: the 108-cell neighbourhood is NOT independent evidence (its centre cell's clean/H1/H2 were read in B7). Everything below is a replication battery whose pass/fail is fixed here. Fill model/cost as before (1 tick/side, $4.36 per contract RT, stop-market with 1 tick slippage), baseline pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process.
Objects: (O1) the 108-cell equal-risk ensemble (grid of PREREG L93); (O2) the single B7 cell 5-min ST(14,2.0) fade, 240-min hold, no stop; (O3) the same cell with the 0.5 x ADR20 catastrophic stop.
R1 GC 2013-2022 walk: O1, O2, O3 on 2013-2022 by calendar year: weekly Sharpe gross (no cost), net at fixed cost, net at price-scaled cost (per-trade cost x raw price / median clean raw price), per-trade $ and trades; ERA flag; reported, not gating.
R2 Micro/sizing: contracts for 20% share (O1 and O2 vol-matched factor / 5) in GC and in MGC (10 oz, $10/pt, tick 0.10 = $1; commission assumption $1.40 RT per MGC - unverified); worst trade and worst week at that size with and without the 0.5 ADR stop; margin note (GC day/overnight, MGC) marked unverified; MGC 1-tick slippage is the same price-tick, so cost per ounce is 10x the cost share per dollar of exposure... computed explicitly.
R3 Other metals/commodities (gap test beyond SI/HG, which failed): the O1 recipe (ensemble of the same 108 cells, vol-matched) on PL, HO, RB, NG, ZC (ZC added only because it is the only grain with 24h-ish liquidity on disk), plus GC with different session splits: (a) day 08:00-15:30 (baseline), (b) COMEX RTH 08:20-12:30 entries, (c) overnight 18:30-03:00 entries (full hold before 16:55 same label day), (d) all 18:30-15:30. Pass for replication: uplift >= +0.10 on H1 and H2 and positive on clean for at least 2 of the 5 other instruments, and positive all-in uplift in at least 3 of the 4 session splits for GC.
R4 Alternating months, BOTH directions: (i) odd months vs even months uplift of O1/O2/O3 (each half >= 50% of the full uplift); (ii) cell-level split: rank the 108 cells by their odd-month all-in uplift, take the top 10, read their mean even-month uplift; then reverse; compare with the all-cell mean (selection-within-ensemble honesty test); pass if both directions are positive and the top-10 do not beat the all-cell mean by more than a factor 2 (no peak-chasing).
R5 Newest weeks: data extension is weekly (series on disk end 2026-10-14). The frozen O1/O2/O3 definitions are read once on all weeks after 2026-10-14 when the extension lands (target 2026-10-22+); reported with n weeks and a sign test; no gate until >= 8 new weeks.
R6 Stop-model: O3 stop levels 0.5, 0.75, 1.0 x ADR20 with stop-market slippage 1 and 3 ticks (thin-tape stress); stop fills at the stop or the open if gapped.
Verdict rule: FOUND-PENDING requires R3 pass, R4 pass, R1 reported with flag, R5 not negative, plus the L93 gates already met; else LEAD stays.

## K CURE (registered 2026-10-17 before any variant run): whipsaw GBM with the random-removal null as the SELECTION criterion, L2-free features
Variants (walk-forward LightGBM as K82/83, monthly refit): feature sets {price, price+flow, flow+gamma, price+flow+gamma, flow only} x params {PARAMS, PARAMS_ALT} x target {whipsaw 20 min (stopped within 20 min), whipsaw 10 min, whipsaw 30 min} x action {skip top 5%, skip top 10%, half top 10%} = 90 pair cells. For each cell: random-removal null of 100 draws at the same share (parallel); the cell 'beats the null' if its H1 AND H2 uplift each exceed the null p95 of that span. Report the number that beat the p95 on H1 and H2, the expected number by chance (90 x 0.05^2 ~ 0.2 plus dependence), and the best cell with full gates (clean selection not used; the null is the selection criterion). K-L2: when NQ mbp-10 (job GLBX-20261013-Y3PCQ4MKV3, marker outputs/a5/L2-DONE) lands, the same pipeline adds top-5 book imbalance, spread, depth at the signal-bar close, queue-style features as a separate feature set 'L2' and 'price+flow+gamma+L2', with the same 90-cell grid restricted to the best two action/target combos from the L2-free run; read-only check of the job status only.
