## BATCH R (R1..R15, prefix ap_r*_), registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any R run by worker I. R1-R5 = K4 deepening (the edge was shown to be index-direction timing); R6-R15 = fresh families from the D33/E4 closest misses and from LEDGER/DECISIONS. Rules as J-COMMON/E-block (Gate Amendment 2, clean-only selection, H1/H2 once, ERA flag for sleeves, own null, bootstrap, plateau +-25%, 2x slippage, wd corr, replication H0 + alternating months for pair-side, library save, UPD lines, pool 6). Frozen code: ap_k_fams (features, ml_preds, MODELS); K4 reference cell = d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly (K4v2 pre-declared).
### R1 EXPOSURE TIMING vs EQUAL-EXPOSURE BUY-AND-HOLD (analysis with gates, no selection)
- Compare the K4 reference sleeve with an always-long basket (ES, NQ, RTY, YM, same unit ADR risk) scaled per instrument by the K4 sleeve's own exposure fraction (days in market / eligible days, 2023-08..2026-10). Metrics: standalone weekly Sharpe of K4 vs scaled buy-and-hold; difference with paired-weekly bootstrap 90% CI; additive 20% uplift of each (both scaled to the pair's clean weekly sd) and their difference; timing-skill regression of K4 daily P&L on the scaled buy-and-hold P&L (alpha Sharpe, CI). PASS = Sharpe difference CI lower bound > 0 AND additive-uplift difference > 0 on clean, H1 and H2 separately.
### R2 FEATURE-BLOCK ABLATION of K4 (pre-declared cells, no selection)
- Blocks: PRICE (r1,r3,r5,r10,r20,sma50,sma200,adrr,iid), VIX (vixpct,term,vvixpct,vchg5), COT (cot_lev,cot_am), CAL (dow,opexwk,mend). Models (8), each with the K4 reference rule (d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly; same walk-forward): PRICE, PRICE+VIX, PRICE+COT, PRICE+CAL, FULL-VIX, FULL-COT, FULL-CAL, FULL. Report per model: trades, standalone Sharpe, additive 20% uplift clean/H1/H2, and which block removal drops the uplift most. Verdict by block: carries the signal if removing it lowers the all-in uplift by >= 50 percent and the block alone (with PRICE) restores >= 50 percent.
### R3 MODEL-CLASS INDEPENDENCE (non-tree models, same features, walk-forward)
- Models: logistic regression (standardised, median-imputed, L2 C=0.1) and ridge (alpha 100, target 0/1, p = clip prediction). Because scores are not calibrated like GBM probabilities, trade the top q of the model's scores relative to its own trailing 250 predictions per instrument-pooled panel: q {0.08, 0.16, 0.25}, longonly, N=1. Grid 6 cells; clean-only selection; K4v2 gates; compare with K4.
### R4 HOLDING PERIOD (K4 d2)
- Cells (5): fixed hold N=2 with delta {0.04, 0.06, 0.08} (model trained on the 2-day target); persistent hold with the N=1 model: enter when p >= 0.58, stay while p >= x, x {0.53, 0.50}. (N=5 is a duplicate of L4 and skipped.) Clean-only selection among the 5; K4v2 gates.
### R5 K4 AS A CONTINUOUS DAY-LEVEL PAIR-SIZE MODULATOR (pair-side; L2 tested threshold agreement tilts)
- pbar = mean K4 d2 p over the four indices at the 16:16 px of label d; z = clip((pbar-0.5)/0.1, -1, 1); applies to rows with lab_in = next label: factor = clip(1 + s_row a z sg, 0.5, 1.5) where s_row is the row's side (+1 long); a {0.25, 0.5}; books {both, HM only}; sg {+1 (long-biased), -1 control}. 8 cells. Gates as E3.
### R6 D33 CURE (post-hoc follow-up; disclosed: D33's H1/H2 were displayed) 
- ES-flow alignment tilt (ALIGNES_W = side x ES imbalance over W minutes; BLOCK percentile; sg=+1 as in D33) restricted by book: W {5, 10, 15} x book {ST, HM, both} x a {0.25, 0.5} = 18 cells. Gates as E3 (H1/H2 not virgin for the family).
### R7 LATE-DAY ENTRY CUTOFF (E4 truncation artefact tested directly; pair-side)
- Entries with ET hour >= h and < 16:00 are skipped (x0) or halved (x0.5): h {12, 13, 14} x action = 6 cells. Gates as E3.
### R8 TRAILING EFFICIENCY-RATIO REGIME TILT (S5: trend share is the stable P&L source but ex-post; this uses a causal proxy)
- ER_W = |c(e-1)-c(e-1-W)| / sum|dc| over W minutes before the entry bar from the NQ per-minute price frame, W {60, 120, 240}; percentile tilt f = clip(1 + sg a (2p-1), 0.5, 1.5), scope {ALL, BLOCK}, a {0.25, 0.5}, sg {-1, +1}: 24 cells. Gates as E3.
### R9 I9 TAIL CAP (closest miss: I9 failed only the worst-week gate)
- IBS(1) < 0.10, C > SMA200, buy at the close, exit after 2 days (C11 daily bars ES/NQ/YM/RTY, ADR-dollar sizing as I9); added: close-based stop at k x ATR20 below entry (exit at the first close below) k {1.0, 1.5, 2.0} and at most m new entries per day (the m lowest IBS among the signals) m {1, 2, 4}: 9 cells. Clean-only selection; I9 gates.
### R10 I9 x K4 CONSENSUS
- I9 signal (IBS < theta, C > SMA200, exit d2) taken only when the K4 d2 N=1 probability of that instrument is >= 0.5 + c: theta {0.10, 0.20} x c {0.0, 0.04} = 4 cells (+ I9 alone as control reported). Clean-only selection; I9 gates.
### R11 WALK-FORWARD BOOK TILT (Q-5 / B19-BOOKTILT logged by the lead, not run)
- At the start of each ISO week compare ST and HM over the trailing W weeks of raw unruled rows (net per trade, or sum): the better book x(1+s), the worse x(1-s); W {8, 13, 26} weeks x metric {per-trade, sum} x s {0.25, 0.5} = 12 cells. Null: permute better/worse assignment across weeks. Gates as E3.
### R12 K4 WITH EXTENDED FEATURES (single pre-declared cell vs K4)
- K4 features + SKEW percentile (252d), VIX9D/VIX, VIX6M/VIX3M, (VIX - 20d mean VIX)/20d std; same model d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly. Gates as K4v2 for the cell, plus paired comparison with K4 (uplift difference).
### R13 RANGE-USED REGIME TILT (pair-side)
- RU = (today's high-low so far)/ADR20 at the bar before entry (label session from 18:00 ET, from the 1-minute NQ cache); percentile tilt scope {ALL, BLOCK} x a {0.25, 0.5} x sg {-1, +1} (8) + switch high tail q {0.67, 0.8} x action {x0.5, x0} x scope ALL (4) = 12 cells. Gates as E3.
### R14 COT HEDGER ENSEMBLE (J3 near-miss cure)
- J3 construction (GC, CL, ZN, 6E, 6J; weekly, 5-day hold) with signal = mean percentile rank over L {52, 104, 156} weeks of the class net/OI; class {hedge, spec} x mode {contra, follow} x band {0.8, 0.9} = 8 cells; clean-only selection; J-gates.
### R15 NQ-ES RELATIVE STRENGTH TILT (pair-side, daily)
- rs_W = (NQ - ES px change over W labels)/ADR-normalised, W {5, 20}, known at the previous label's 16:16 px; expanding percentile p; mode abs: f = clip(1 + sg a (2p-1), .5, 1.5) on all rows; mode dir: f = clip(1 + side sg a z, 0.5, 1.5) with z = 2p-1: W 2 x mode 2 x a {0.25, 0.5} x sg {-1, +1} = 16 cells. Gates as E3.
### R12P CONFIRMATION OF R12 (registered 2026-10-16 AFTER the R12 result was seen; disclosed; the R12 cell was single and pre-declared, only the plateau gate failed with delta-only neighbours)
- Exactly the K4v2 protocol applied to the R12 pre-declared cell (extended features, d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly): hyperparameter plateau (delta 0.06/0.10, trees 112/188, lr 0.0375/0.0625, seeds 1-3; >= 7 of 9 keep >= 75% of the uplift), drift-matched month-stratified null (1,000 draws), long-only random-entry null (1,000), beta test, full 32-cell clean grid with the extended features and 100-replicate selection-aware null, replications R2 H0, R3 NQ+ES-trained model on RTY+YM, R4 alternating months select/read (two of the three needed, each >= 50% of the cell's all-in uplift), ERA, margin/NT8 text identical to K4v2. Output REPORT_R12P.md. Because the features were added after seeing K4 and the cell after R12, a FOUND would still need newest-weeks replication when data extends (E8 forward test).

## PX31 - MONTHLY CROSS-SECTIONAL MOMENTUM, FRESH PRE-REGISTRATION (cure of Q5/Q5ENS/Q10/R9; registered 2026-10-16 by worker ap_pd2 BEFORE any PX31 return was computed). Code ap_pd2_*.py, outputs/autopilot/pd2/.
- Parent lead and honesty about spans: R9 (20d XS, k=3/5), Q5 (L15-30, k2-4, MACRO12), Q5ENS (12-cell ensemble: all-in +0.18, H2 +0.02, 1 of 3 replications), Q10 (sector subsets EQ/RATES/FX/COM at L 10/20/40, k default), Q3 (63-252d). NO SPAN IS VIRGIN: clean, H1 and H2 were read for the parent family (Q5/Q5ENS) and for Q10 sector books at L10/20/40; 2013-22 was the original screen of R9/Q5. L=60 and the 4-lookback ensemble are new as a design; the 4 sector ENS books are new as ensembles. Consequence: a PX31 result cannot reach FOUND on span freshness; the best outcome is LEAD / FOUND-PENDING, and it needs >=2 replication legs that I define below. Newest live weeks: none beyond 2026-10-13 (pending).
- Mechanism: slow cross-sectional trend (time-series + cross-asset momentum, Moskowitz-Ooi-Pedersen / Asness-Moskowitz-Pedersen) harvested by ranking markets on ATR-normalised trailing return and holding long top-k / short bottom-k for one month; a lookback ensemble removes dependence on one lookback. Prior 4% (parent H2 uplift +0.02).
- Rule (frozen, same engine as Q5): z = (c_t - c_(t-L)) / (ATR20 x sqrt L) on back-adjusted daily bars; rebalance on the last trading day of each month, long top-k / short bottom-k, entry next open, exit close of day t+19 (hold 19), unit risk RISK=7000 per leg via ATR20, no stop, 1 tick/side + commission per T4 table (2x slippage = 2 ticks). Engine = ap_b2356_core.run_signal via ap_y_run.sig_Qpanel (unchanged).
- CELLS (12, all pre-registered, none selected; PRIMARY = C1, a fixed cell; C2-C12 are diagnostic/secondary and cannot rescue C1):
  C1 ENS4_M12: equal-weight mean of the P&L of L in {10,20,40,60}, universe MACRO12 (ES NQ RTY ZN ZB GC CL 6E 6J 6B 6A HG), k=3.   [PRIMARY]
  C2-C6 ENS4 sector books (same 4 lookbacks, k=1; 4-5 markets each): EQ (ES NQ RTY YM), RATES (ZN ZB ZF ZT), METALS (GC SI HG PL), ENERGY (CL NG RB HO), FX (6E 6J 6B 6A 6C).
  C7-C10 single lookbacks on MACRO12, k=3: L10, L20, L40, L60 (components of C1).
  C11 SECT5: equal-weight mean of the five sector ENS4 books (C2-C6).
  C12 ENS4_ALL26: the 4 lookbacks on the 26-outright universe, k=5.
- Gates for C1 (never softened): 7 FOUND gates exactly as AUTOPILOT.md: additive uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 each at 10/20/30/50%; worst week not worse than the share; own timing-destroyed null (random entry dates, same count per instrument per year, hold 19, per component, ensembled with the same weights) p95 on pair-window standalone Sharpe, AND the conservative variants (single-component null p95 and Bonferroni 98.75th pct as in Q5ENS) reported; bootstrap 90% CI of additive uplift > 0 at every share; plateau; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 on clean/H1/H2; worst-decile-week corr <= +0.30; NT8 spec. Constant-risk framing (INSURANCE/SURVIVES via rep_common/finalize) also reported. Scale: sleeve weekly sd = pair clean weekly sd (clean sd of the book).
- PLATEAU (defined now): the neighbours of C1 are the four books {lookbacks x0.75 = (8,15,30,45)}, {lookbacks x1.25 = (12,25,50,75)}, {k=2}, {k=4}; plateau passes if >= 3 of 4 keep >= 75% of C1's all-in additive uplift at 20%. Reported in addition (not gating): the four leave-one-lookback-out 3-lookback books.
- SELECTION-CORRECTED NULL (defined now): the whole selection pipeline "pick the cell of C1-C12 with the best clean weekly Sharpe, then read its pair-window Sharpe and its +20% additive uplift on clean/H1/H2" is re-run on 203 timing-destroyed replicates (independent random-entry draws per component, composed with the same weights); report (a) distribution of the best-of-12 pair-window Sharpe, (b) distribution of the selected cell's min(clean,H1,H2) uplift and the chance that it is >= +0.10, (c) the real values against p95. C1 must beat the p95 of (a) restricted to its own cell and the best-of-12 p95 is reported as the selection-corrected bar; failing the selection-corrected bar caps the verdict at LEAD.
- REPLICATION LEGS (>= 2 of 4 with the same sign and >= 50% of the C1 all-in 20% uplift; defined now): (i) newest live weeks (none yet -> not credited); (ii) H0 window 2023-02..07 (uplift of C1 at the clean scale); (iii) cross-instrument leg (ES/RTY not applicable to a macro book -> two disjoint market halves of MACRO12, A = ES NQ ZN GC 6E HG, B = RTY ZB CL 6J 6B 6A, ensemble of the 4 lookbacks at k=2, both halves must satisfy); (iv) alternating-month split 2023-26 (uplift on odd months and on even months both > 0 and >= 50%). Every leg uses spans that were already read for the parent family; credit is therefore recorded as 'non-virgin'.
- ERA FLAG + cost decomposition for C1 (and every cell): 2013-22 weekly Sharpe at zero cost / fixed cost / price-scaled cost (cost x median(price 2013-22 / price 2023-26) of the cell's markets), plus gross P&L per trade (USD at RISK=7000), net per trade at fixed and at price-scaled cost, and trades. Flag per AUTOPILOT.md amendment 2 (not gating).
- Also reported: long-history (2013-01..2026-10) weekly Sharpe and a per-year table (weekly Sharpe and USD at pair-scale) for C1, C11, C12; insurance metric set; both framings (additive 10/20/30/50%, constant-risk); alternative baseline B.Cfg(); library save (lib_save, volmatch) of all 12 cells as family PX31; UPD line.
- Fill model/executability: next-open entry, exit close t+19, 1 tick/side + $4.36-class commission; NT8 monthly rebalance script over micros/outrights via Rithmic; no netting with the NQ pair (different instruments except ES/NQ/RTY legs: separate sub-account recommended).

## PX30 WEEKEND-GAP FADE ENSEMBLE, FRESH PRE-REGISTRATION (BATCH 6 lead cure; registered 2026-10-17 before any PX30 run; code ap_pd1_*.py, out outputs/autopilot/pd1/)
Parent lead: weekend-gap fade family (REPORT_T1b, Z3, W6, RW6, V3). Parent failure: dev Sharpe 0.49-0.59 (T1b), pair-window uplift +0.07..+0.15 clean / H1 -0.16..-0.51 / H2 +0.11..+0.18, boot lo < 0, plateau, small sample (164 dev trades; ~17 gaps/yr over 6 markets). Prior for FOUND: 3% (H1 already read negative for every parent cell).
Mechanism: the Sunday reopen prices weekend news in thin liquidity; when the first N 30-min bars of Monday keep extending the gap, the move is an over-extension by the thin-book flow that partially reverts into the Monday session. Calendar structure, current-structure dependence: moderate (relies on Sunday-reopen liquidity).
RULE (frozen; engine = ap_t30_run.trades/arrs, 30-min bars, label-day ET): Monday (label day whose previous label day is >= 2 calendar days earlier) gap g = (open of first bar - close of last bar of previous label day)/ATR20(prior days); trade if |g| >= thr and the first N 30-min bars close beyond the open in the direction of g; FADE at the open of bar N+1; exit at the open of the last bar of the label day; no stop; one trade per instrument per Monday.
ENSEMBLE (pre-fixed, no optimisation): NQ ES RTY GC CL ZN, equal volatility weights = equal $7,000 risk per ATR20 per instrument (the engine's fixed sizing); the sleeve is the sum; scaled to the pair's clean weekly vol using the sleeve's CLEAN weekly vol (k fixed once).
GRID (9 cells): thr {0.1875, 0.25, 0.3125} x N {1,2,3}. PRIMARY (declared now, no selection): thr 0.25, N 2 (grid centre; the T1b dev-screen centre). A secondary best-of-9 (clean-only selection: weekly Sharpe > 0 in both clean halves, >= 40 pair-window trades, rank by clean Sharpe) is reported only to feed the selection-corrected null.
PLATEAU (exact): the 4 one-parameter neighbours of the primary (thr 0.1875, thr 0.3125 = +-25%; N=1, N=3); pass if >= 3 of 4 keep >= 75% of the primary's all-in additive uplift at 20%.
GATES (unchanged): additive uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 at 10/20/30/50% with worst week within share; own timing-destroyed null (random 08:00-15:00 ET entry, exit at day end, same trades per instrument-year; 203 draws) p95 on pair-window standalone Sharpe; bootstrap 90% lo > 0; plateau; 2x slippage; worst-decile corr <= +0.30; NT8 spec. Both framings (additive + constant-risk). Baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None), clean process.
SELECTION-CORRECTED NULL: 100 replicates; in each, all 9 cells are rebuilt with timing-destroyed entries (same per-instrument-year counts), the full selection (clean eligibility, best clean Sharpe) is re-run, and the selected null cell's clean standalone Sharpe and clean additive uplift @20% are recorded; the real selected cell is ranked against that distribution (p95 = gate-equivalent).
SPAN HONESTY: clean, H1, H2 are NON-VIRGIN for this lead (T1b read clean/H1/H2 for T1_0.25_{1,2,4}_fade on the same 6 markets; Z3/W6/V3 read H1/H2 for the daily-bar variant). H1/H2 numbers are reported but cannot support FOUND alone. Virgin or near-virgin sources: H0 (2023-02-10..2023-07-31; the parents scored the sleeve only from 2023-08), alternating-month split inside 2023-26 (fixed cell; reported in both directions), ES+RTY-only sleeve 2023-26 (cross-instrument; NOT independent of the parents' pooled selection, so flagged), newest live weeks (data ends 2026-10-14; none newer than the parents' read: n/a). Replication legs, each: same sign and >= 50% of the primary's clean additive uplift @20% (H0: additive uplift on H0 weeks; ES/RTY: all-in uplift of the ES+RTY sleeve scaled by its own clean vol; alt-month: both parities positive and their mean >= 50% of the clean uplift). FOUND-PENDING needs all 7 gates + >= 2 of {H0, ES/RTY, alt-month} (newest weeks n/a).
SMALL-SAMPLE: pair-window trades < 100 -> flag and report 2013-26 and 2013-22 Sharpe next to it. ERA FLAG with cost decomposition (gross / net fixed / net price-scaled per trade and weekly Sharpe 2013-22). Library save of the primary sleeve (volmatch). Expected chance passes: 9 cells x 5% = 0.45 (reported alongside the selection-corrected null).

## PX32 - PSAR 15-min pullback: neighbourhood-ensemble cure (worker pd3; code ap_pd3_*, outputs/autopilot/pd3/) - registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any ensemble cell was run
Parent lead: PULLBACK (REPORT_PULLBACK.md) NQ 15-min PSAR(0.02,0.2) touch-and-close-back, TR_E50 (60-min EMA50 slope), RTH, SLV:0.5, R:1.5, TCB:0.25. Parent failed plateau 4/6 (stop 0.75x, target 0.75x), H2 uplift +0.08 at 20%, 2x slippage H1/H2, ES/RTY, ERA negative.
Mechanism/prior: the single cell's edge is a point; if the effect is real, an equal-weight average of its +-25% parameter neighbours should keep the uplift and make plateau structural (the deliverable is the book, not the cell). Prior of FOUND ~5% (parent: ES/RTY negative, ERA negative, random-level worlds produce similar best-of-search).
SPANS - honest non-virgin statement: clean 2023-08..2025-07 is the parent's selection span; H1 and H2 were read once by the parent for the single cell (so for E they are NON-VIRGIN, selection is not clean-only-blind); H0 2023-02-10..07-31, ES, RTY and the ERA were read by the parent for the single cell (descriptive, negative/weak) - they remain usable for the ensemble only as non-independent replication. Newest live weeks: NQ/pair data end 2026-10-13, no week beyond H2 exists: replication leg 1 NOT AVAILABLE at run time (E is logged for the FORWARD protocol). Therefore FOUND requires 2 of the 3 remaining legs.
PRIMARY BOOK E81 (no selection, no tuning; the cell list is fixed here): NQ, 15-min bars, RTH, level PSAR with (af_step, af_max) in {(0.015,0.15),(0.02,0.2),(0.025,0.25)}, trigger TCB:t:any with t in {0.1875,0.25,0.3125}, stop SLV:s with s in {0.375,0.5,0.625}, target R:r with r in {1.125,1.5,1.875}; trend TR_E50, 1 position per cell, market entry next 1-min open, flat 16:55 ET, fill 1 tick/side + $4.36 commission (2x slippage for the slippage gate). 3^4 = 81 cells at 1 contract; E81 = equal-weight mean of the 81 daily P&L vectors; the sleeve is scaled so its clean weekly sigma equals the pair's (as in the parent) and combined at 10/20/30/50% (additive P+sS and constant-risk (1-s)P+sS); headline 20%. E27 (AF fixed at default; trigger x stop x target) is the pipeline-comparable form used for the selection-corrected null and reported alongside. SECONDARY BOOK V9 (majority vote): enter when >=5 of the 9 (AF x trigger) entry signals fire on the same bar and side, stop SLV:0.5 from the centre SAR, target R:1.5 (stop/target not voted). Two ensembles are tested; false-discovery expectation is stated for 2.
GATES (all must hold for FOUND; never moved): (1) additive 20% uplift vs clean pair >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately and all-in worst week change <= +20%; (2) own timing-destroyed null: E standalone all-in weekly Sharpe > p95 of 300 random-entry ensembles (one common random entry order per replicate, nested subsets for trigger/AF counts, same side mix, ATR-scaled stops, same 81 stop/target settings, averaged) AND bootstrap 90% CI of the additive uplift > 0; circular-shift null reported; (3) SELECTION-CORRECTED null: the full parent selection pipeline (stage-1 grid, top-12 distinct (bar,level,trigger) finalists by clean Sharpe, n>=150) re-run in each of the parent's 30 random-level null worlds; for each world every finalist is expanded to its trigger x stop x target +-25% 27-cell ensemble (E27 form) and the statistic is the best-of-12 min(clean,H1,H2) additive-20% uplift. Real statistic (best over the 12 real finalists' E27, plus PSAR's E27) must exceed the null-world p95; reported also: number of worlds with any finalist passing gate 1; (4) PLATEAU, defined up front on the ENSEMBLE: 12 neighbour ensembles = grid spacing x0.75 and x1.25 (offsets 0.75x and 1.25x of the +-25%), centre of all four axes jointly x0.75 and x1.25, and each single axis centre x0.75 / x1.25 (4 axes x 2); >=75% (9 of 12) must keep >= 75% of E81's all-in additive-20% uplift; (5) 2x slippage: additive-20% uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 with 2x slippage; (6) worst-decile-week correlation with the pair <= +0.30; (7) NT8 spec + margin. REPLICATION (>=2 of 3 available legs: same sign and >= 50% of E81's all-in 20% uplift): L2 H0 2023-02-10..07-31 (same fixed rule, clean scale factor); L3 ES and RTY 2023-08..2026-10 identical parameters, both positive and mean >= 50%; L4 alternating-month split: since E has no fitted parameter, odd-month and even-month uplift over the post-clean span 2025-08..2026-10 must each be > 0 and >= 50% of the pooled post-clean uplift (odd/even clean reported; the selection is in-sample on clean). Constant-risk framing: INSURANCE/SURVIVES via rep_common/t3 verdict. ERA: E81 on 2013-2026 NQ long series, gross per trade, net at fixed cost, net at price-scaled cost (flag: not gating). Save E81 and V9 via lib_save.save_book (size_basis volmatch). Pool of 4. V9 plateau: neighbours = vote threshold 4 and 6 of 9, grid centre x0.75 / x1.25 (4 neighbours, >=3 must keep 75%); V9 selection-corrected null not run (stated).
### PX34 N3R: CURE ATTEMPT OF THE N3 LEAD (VX curve flat -> long equity next day). Registered 2026-10-16 ~23:00Z by worker ap_pd5, BEFORE any N3R run
- Parent N3 (REPORT_N3.md): binary rule F2/F1-1 < 0.03, hold 1/5, selection on 2018-11..2022 (not clean). NQ: add@20% +0.12/+0.42/+0.28 clean/H1/H2, boot lo +0.02, long-only null rank 0.98. Failures: plateau 0.50 (binary threshold, 0.0225 neighbour), worst week (+35% vs the 20% share; sleeve is long beta, sleeve worst week -281k in clean vs pair -82k), 10% share (+0.07), RTY/ES wd corr 0.34-0.37.
- DISCLOSURE (non-virgin spans for this lead): the parent scored NQ, ES and RTY on clean, H1 and H2 and those numbers were read; the idea and the 0.03 level come from that reading. So clean, H1, H2 and ES/RTY 2023-26 are ALL non-virgin for the parent. Untouched by the parent: H0 (2023-02-10..2023-07-31; VX covers it) and the alternating-month split design. VX data end 2026-10-12 = pair end, so the 'newest live weeks' leg is n/a until the rolling extension. 2018-11..2022 was the parent's selection window (ERA window here; VX starts 2018-11).
- MECHANISM: after the VIX curve flattens/inverts (front vol rising vs second month), the next session's equity open-to-close drift is high (stress / variance risk premium paid to liquidity providers); in contango the drift is ordinary. Prior: moderate-low (0.2) that it is a stable edge beyond long beta; the parent's all-days-long Sharpe 0.75 vs flat-days 1.20 vs steep-days 0.08 on NQ.
- WHY THIS ADDRESSES THE FAILURES (not parameter hunting on H1/H2): (i) plateau: replace the single binary cut by a LADDER of four cuts, weight w_t = mean over rungs r in L*{0.5,1,1.5,2} of 1[s_t < r], s_t = VX F2/F1-1 (same vx_slope() definition as N3: two nearest months alive with volume>0), L = 0.02 FIXED (rungs 0.01/0.02/0.03/0.04, brackets the parent's 0.03); a ladder is continuous in L. (ii) worst week: optional hard stop at kst x ATR20 below the entry (kst in {none, 1.0}) cuts the long-beta tail days. No other parameter.
- GRID (4 cells, instrument NQ, long only, enter at the open of the next label day, exit at the close of day t+hold, 1 tick/side + T4 daily commission, overlapping trades allowed, each rung leg sized RISK/(ATR20*pv), x = mean of the 4 rung legs): hold {1,2} x kst {0, 1.0}.
- SELECTION (clean 2023-08..2025-07 only): eligible = weekly Sharpe > 0 in BOTH clean years (2023-08..2024-07 and 2024-08..2025-07) and >= 40 trades in the pair window; pick max clean standalone weekly Sharpe. H1 and H2 read once after selection. ES and RTY use the SELECTED cell unchanged (replication only, no search).
- SCALE/FRAMINGS: BC.score_sleeve with scale_basis clean (sleeve weekly vol = pair clean weekly vol), shares 10/20/30/50%, additive AND constant-risk. Baseline pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process (t3_score imported first). Also uplift vs B.Cfg().
- GATES (never moved): G3 add >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately at 10/20/30/50% and worst week change <= share; G4 own null = BLOCK-SHUFFLE of the slope series (10-day blocks, block order permuted within calendar year; keeps clustering and the long-only marginal) 200 replicates, selected cell's pair-window standalone Sharpe vs p95, AND selection-corrected null: on each replicate recompute all 4 cells, apply the selection rule, record the selected cell's clean Sharpe / pair-window Sharpe / all-in add@20% uplift; real must exceed p95 of the replicate-selected statistic (pair-window Sharpe); circular-shift null reported; bootstrap 90% lo > 0 at every share; G5 plateau = one-at-a-time perturbation of L x{0.75,1.25} and (if kst>0) kst x{0.75,1.25}; need >= 75% of neighbours keeping >= 75% of the all-in additive uplift at 20% (with 2 neighbours that means both); and 2x slippage (uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2 at 20%); G6 worst-decile-week corr <= +0.30; G7 NT8 spec + margin.
- REPLICATION (>= 2 of 4 required for FOUND, each = same sign and >= 50% of the selected cell's all-in additive uplift @20%): (a) newest live weeks (n/a: VX ends 2026-10-12); (b) H0 2023-02-10..2023-07-31 with the SELECTED cell, sleeve scaled by the clean k; (c) ES AND RTY (both must hold), selected cell, 2023-08..2026-10, each scaled to its own clean vol; (d) alternating months in 2023-08..2026-10: select among the 4 cells on odd months, read on even months, then the reverse; BOTH directions must hold.
- ERA FLAG (reported, not gating; window 2018-11..2022-12 because VX starts 2018-11): gross per trade, net at fixed cost, net at price-scaled cost (cost x median raw price ratio pre/post), with trade counts.
- Also reported: long-only random-day null (same count per year) as in N3, all-days-long comparator, insurance metric set, save_book. Expected chance passes of the 4-cell grid: 4 x ~0.05 = 0.2.

### PX33 WT-10 + ONE DENOISER, SEPARATE-ACCOUNT SLEEVE (worker ap_pd4; registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any denoised cell was run)
- Parent lead: WT-10 (ATR14 / mult 3 / no EMA / 10-min / 60-60 bracket, TH off), LEAD in REPORT_WT.md/WT2.md (plateau 3/8, no replication, ERA negative pre-2023). NON-VIRGIN DISCLOSURE: the parent's clean, H1, H2 and H0 were all read in WT/WT2/B8WT; ES/RTY and odd/even months were read for the parent. A denoised variant therefore gets no virgin span here: selection is clean-only (gate 3 eligibility at clean), H1/H2 are read once for eligible cells and are non-virgin evidence; the cure test is plateau + replication legs, defined below. Newest live weeks beyond 2026-10-13: none exist (leg n/a, forward-registered).
- Mechanism: the parent's ATR-ratchet trail flips on 10-min closes; part of the flips are NQ-specific microstructure noise (E1 found ES/composite/residual price PATHS do not help; this tests filtering of the NQ path itself). A causal smoother of the trail input may delay false flips without delaying real ones. Prior 10% that any cell is a gain after plateau (E1, A1-A10 on ST/HM were all NO).
- Cells (4 + untested control C0 = parent). Everything else fixed at WT-10. Signal bars = 10-min bars of the harness clock; ATR14 (Wilder) always from the bar H/L/C stated; trail = flip_state on the denoised close; entry at flip-bar close -> next 1-min open; 60-pt stop/target on the 1-second path; sessions/flatten as ST/HM.
  - P1 2-min path (param s=2 min): each 10-min bar's high/low = max/min of the 1-min closes sampled every s minutes (positions s-1, 2s-1, ... plus the bar's last close); close unchanged; ATR from these H/L/C. Neighbours s=1 (floor 1.5; = parent), s=3 (ceil 2.5).
  - P2 Heikin-Ashi (param alpha=0.5): haC=(O+H+L+C)/4, haO_k=(1-alpha) haO_{k-1}+alpha haC_{k-1}, haO_0=(O+C)/2, haH=max(H,haO,haC), haL=min(L,haO,haC); trail on haC, ATR from HA OHLC. Neighbours alpha 0.375, 0.625.
  - P3 Kalman (param rho=0.03): local-linear-trend filter on the 10-min close, state (level, slope), F=[[1,1],[0,1]], H=[1,0], measurement variance 1, process variance diag(0, rho) (steady level gain 0.45, chosen from gains alone before any run), init level=close_0, P0=10 I; filtered level (causal, x_k|k) replaces the close in the trail; ATR from raw bars. Neighbours rho 0.0225, 0.0375.
  - P4 3-bar median (param w=3): median of the last w 10-min closes (causal, contiguous series) replaces the close; ATR from raw bars. Neighbours w=2, w=4 (2.25, 3.75 rounded).
- Account: sleeve = the cell alone in its OWN account, no netting with ST/HM, TH off, own rules at the sleeve's R (per-book 5R/-2R, own day lock cap 8R / arm 2R / keep 33%, own week floor 8R; also report the 4R floor) exactly as WT2 (ap_wt_acct.Cfg). R = $9,600, 8 NQ contracts at 60-pt stop; share = sleeve weekly vol vs the pair's clean weekly vol. Baseline pair = pair_base.npz (B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), computed in a clean process), weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2 1.46/2.83/2.30. Fill: 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots; 2x slippage = 2 ticks/side.
- Stage A (clean only): additive uplift at 20% share of each cell and of C0. Eligible = clean uplift >= +0.10 and standalone clean Sharpe > 0. Non-eligible cells are NO; H1/H2 of non-eligible cells are not reported. Stage B for eligible cells: full gate set below.
- Gates (all must hold for FOUND; LEAD if only plateau/replication fail): (1) this registration; (2) clean-only selection; (3) uplift >= +0.10 on clean/H1/H2 at 10/20/30/50% shares, additive AND constant-risk framing reported (INSURANCE/SURVIVES via rep_common), worst week of pair+sleeve not worse than the share; (4) own timing-destroyed null (random entry, same count and hold, same bracket; 200 draws) p95 on standalone all-in weekly Sharpe + bootstrap 90% CI of the 20% additive uplift > 0; ALSO a selection-corrected null: 100 replicates, each replaces the clean trades of all 4 cells by timing-destroyed trades and re-runs the Stage A pipeline (eligible set, best clean uplift, number of cells with clean uplift >= +0.10); the real best clean uplift must exceed the 95th percentile of the replicate best; circular-shift null reported; (5) plateau: ten ONE-AT-A-TIME +-25% neighbours: ATR period (11, 18), multiplier (2.25, 3.75), bracket (45, 75), signal timeframe (7, 13), and the denoiser parameter (two values above); pass = >= 75% (>= 8 of 10) keep >= 75% of the cell's all-in 20% additive uplift; the 16 two-parameter corners (denoiser parameter x each of the four WT parameters) are REPORTED, not gating; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; (6) worst-decile-week correlation with the pair <= +0.30; (7) NT8 spec + margin.
- Replication (>= 2 of the available legs of L2-L4, each with the same sign and >= 50% of the cell's all-in 20% uplift; L1 n/a): L1 newest live weeks (none yet); L2 H0 2023-02-10..2023-07-31 uplift at 20% (scale from clean); L3 ES and RTY 2023-26 with the identical denoised cell on the P1 1-minute engine (scaled bracket KB*ADR), uplift vs the NQ pair at 20%: pass if BOTH are >= 0 and their mean >= 50% of the NQ all-in uplift; L4 alternating months: the cell's uplift computed on odd months and on even months separately (2023-08..2026-10), both >= 50% of the all-in uplift. Parent C0 is run through the same legs as context.
- ERA flag: NQ 2013-2026 back-adjusted 1-min engine (P1 machinery, bracket scaled 60 x price/20000), per-year gross ticks/trade, net at fixed cost, net at price-scaled cost; flag per AUTOPILOT.md amendment 2, reported not gating.
- Null/ledger context: also report denoised-minus-parent paired difference of every statistic (parent C0 is the control: the denoiser has value only if it beats C0 on plateau and replication, not only on uplift).
- Library: save_book each cell (standard size 8 contracts, R 9600, netting NQ_INTRADAY) with the verdict.
- Expected result: NO/LEAD like the parent. Zero FOUND acceptable. No re-run to pass.

### PX35 NQ L2 ROUND (registered 2026-10-16 ~23:05Z by worker ap_pd5, BEFORE the NQ mbp-10 file has landed or any NQ number exists)
- Items 15, 97, 98, A4, E-group (38,39,40,44,45,47) exactly as written in outputs/autopilot/pa4/PREREG_PA4_L2.md (grids final, no extra cells, queue cancel_frac 0.5 default, 0 and 1 reported; FOUND needs the pessimistic cancel_frac 0 pass). ES-proxy finding (REPORT_PX_L2ES.md section B, 21 months 2025-02..2026-10, NON-VIRGIN, selection-free but exploratory): pair NQ pts/trade +4.69 when imb_al <= 0 vs -0.44 when imb_al > 0, same sign in odd and even months, but only 4/21 months positive for with-minus-against (i.e. a mean-driven result). Mechanism: displayed size leaning against the new trend is absorption/passive liquidity being refilled; flips with the book leaning with them are chasing already-displayed demand. ## BATCH RX (RX1..RX10, prefix ap_rx_*, reports REPORT_RX<n>.md), registered 2026-10-16 BEFORE any RX run by worker I (see CONVENTIONS.md). Rules as before (Gate Amendment 2; clean-only selection; own null; bootstrap; plateau +-25% with the neighbours listed here; 2x slippage; wd corr; library save family RX<n>; UPD lines; pool up to 10 workers). RX1-RX3 are tail fixes for the I9 sleeve (closest miss: failed ONLY the worst-week gate; R9/R10 showed a close stop and an entry cap do not move the crash-week tail). Base for RX1-RX3 = I9: IBS(1) < 0.10, C > SMA200, buy at the close, exit after 2 days, 3 x ATR20 intraday stop, ES/NQ/YM/RTY, one ADR-dollar per trade (C11 daily bars); gates: I9 sleeve gates (worst week within the share is the gate in question, evaluated at 20% and reported at 10%).
- RX1 GROSS-EXPOSURE CAP TIED TO MARKET STATE. State on (known at the signal close): inv = VIX/VIX3M > 1.00; vixhi = VIX 252-day percentile >= 0.8; k4lo = mean K4 d2 probability across the four indices < 0.50. Action when the state is on: size x0.5 for every new entry, or at most one open position (new entries skipped while another sleeve position is open). Grid 3 x 2 = 6 cells.
- RX2 DAY-AFTER-LOSS SIZE CUT. First pass: baseline sleeve daily P&L in ADR units (sum over instruments). A loss day = daily P&L <= -x (x in {1, 2} ADR units). New entries on the next N trading days (N in {2, 5}) are sized x m (m in {0.5, 0}). Grid 2 x 2 x 2 = 8 cells.
- RX3 STRESS-WEEK STAND-DOWN (walk-forward by construction). Trigger on a label: ES 5-day px change <= -3% or <= -5%, or VIX 2-day change >= +25% or >= +40%; no new entries for the next N trading days (N in {3, 5}) including the trigger label's next session. Grid 4 x 2 = 8 cells.
- RX4 RW8 EFFICIENCY-RATIO CURE (pair-side, single pre-declared cell, plateau defined up front). Cell: size factor = clip(1 + 0.25 (2 pbar - 1), 0.5, 1.5) with pbar = mean of the expanding percentiles (scope ALL) of ER180, ER240, ER300 (ER_W = efficiency ratio of NQ closes over the W minutes before the entry bar), i.e. size UP in trending regimes (sg +1 as RW8 selected). Neighbours (5): a x0.75, a x1.25, and each single window ER180, ER240, ER300 alone; >= 75% must keep >= 75% of the uplift; plus the E3-style gates and H0 / alternating-month replication (pre-declared cell: odd/even same cell).
- RX5 K4 SEED ENSEMBLE (sleeve, single pre-declared cell). K4 d2 N=1 with the probability averaged over 5 seeds (random_state 0-4), delta 0.08, longonly. Plateau defined up front (8 neighbours): delta 0.06, 0.10; trees 112 and 188 (5-seed ensembles); lr 0.0375 and 0.0625 (5-seed ensembles); alternative seed sets 5-9 and 10-14; >= 7 of 8 must keep >= 75% of the uplift (a ceiling of one failure). Gates and replication battery as K4v2 (drift-matched null, beta test, H0, cross-instrument NQ+ES-trained single-seed proxy reported, alternating months same cell).
- RX6 K4-LEAN (PRICE + VIX features only, 13 features; RW2 found PRICE+VIX reproduces 96% of the full uplift). Single pre-declared cell, 5-seed ensemble, d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly; same neighbours as RX5 (alternative seed sets, delta, trees, lr). Compared with RX5 (paired uplift difference).
- RX7 ASYMMETRIC K4 THRESHOLDS: long when p >= 0.58 (as K4), short when p <= 0.5 - ds, ds in {0.02, 0.03, 0.04} (3 cells, d2 N=1 single seed); clean-only selection; K4v2 gates and beta test (E7 found the symmetric 0.42 short rule almost never fires).
- RX8 ES-DIP x K4 CONSENSUS: RSI(2) < r (r in {5, 10}), close > SMA200, taken only when the K4 probability of that index >= 0.5; exit RSI(2) > 65 or after 5 days (two exits), stop 3 x ATR20 (C11 daily bars ES/NQ/YM/RTY, ADR-dollar sizing); 4 cells; clean-only selection; I9 sleeve gates.
- RX9 RW5 x RW8 COMBINED TILT (pair-side, single cell): factor = RW5 factor (K4 modulator, a=0.25, both books, sg +1) x RW8 factor (ER240 ALL a=0.25 sg +1), clipped to [0.5, 1.5]; neighbours: RW5 alone, RW8 alone, both with a x0.75, both with a x1.25; >= 75% of 4 keep >= 75% of the combined uplift; E3 gates.
- RX10 RW5 WITH A K4 SEED ENSEMBLE (pair-side, single cell): as RW5 (a=0.25, both books, sg +1) but pbar from the 5-seed K4 ensemble; neighbours: a x0.75, a x1.25, single-seed pbar (seed 0), alternative seed set 5-9; E3 gates.
### RX11 EVENT-AWARE I9 (registered 2026-10-16 AFTER RX1-RX3 results and a worst-week diagnosis; disclosed). Diagnosis (report-only, clean span, selection span): the I9 sleeve's worst week is 2023-W38 (-$220k at pair-equivalent size; ES/NQ/YM bought dips on 2023-09-19 and lost -$69k/-$53k/-$50k on 2023-09-21, the day after the FOMC announcement); second worst 2023-W33. RX1-RX3 states (VIX, K4, ES 5-day drop) did not fire before that week, so the tail is an event-hold problem, not a stress-state problem.
- Rule on the I9 base: let E be the set of scheduled macro event labels (F = FOMC; C = CPI (2023-26 verified dates only); N = NFP). Skip: no entry at the close of d if any event label falls in the 2-day hold (labels d+1, d+2). Shorten: if the event falls on d+2 the hold is cut to 1 day (exit at the close before the event); if it falls on d+1 skip. Grid (6): events {F, F+C, F+N+C} x action {skip, shorten}. Clean-only selection; I9 gates (worst week within the share is the gate in question).
### RX7P CONFIRMATION OF RX7 (registered 2026-10-16 AFTER the RX7 result; disclosed). RX7 cell: K4 d2 N=1, long p >= 0.58 (dl 0.08), short p <= 0.46 (ds 0.04).
- K4v2 protocol on the cell: plateau neighbours (11): dl 0.06/0.10, ds 0.03/0.05, trees 112/188, lr 0.0375/0.0625, seeds 1-3 (>= 9 of 11 must keep >= 75% of the uplift); drift-matched month-stratified null (500), long-short random-entry null (500), beta test; ERA 2016-22; replications: R2 H0 2023-02..07, R3 NQ+ES-trained model (no instrument id) traded on RTY+YM with the same rule, R4 alternating-month select/read over the 12-cell grid dl {0.06, 0.08, 0.10} x ds {0.02, 0.03, 0.04, 0.05} (two of three needed, each >= 50% of the cell's all-in 20% uplift). Report-only diagnostic: short-leg-only sleeve (dl = infinity) standalone Sharpe and 20% uplift, long-leg-only, to show which leg carries the cell.

## BATCH PY (PY1..PY3, prefix ap_py1_*, outputs autopilot/py1..py3/), registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any PY run (only data-existence checks and an unscaled baseline timing run were done; no multiplier cell was scored). Worker ap_py1.
DATA FOUND ON DISK (no purchases): CBOE VIX, VIX3M, VIX9D, VIX6M, VVIX, SKEW daily (data/gap/vix/*.csv, to 2026-10-15) and VX futures daily (databento B10 vx_1d, 2018-11..2026-10) -> VX M2/M1 slope. PY3 is therefore run (VVIX/SKEW exist).
CONTEXT AND DISCLOSURE (binding). RW2 (REPORT_RW2) found the VIX block carries K4's forecast (removing VIX -42%). RW5 used K4 as a DIRECTIONAL long/short tilt. J8 (REPORT_J8, 40 cells, VIX/vchg/term/VVIX/VIX9D thresholds as a size SWITCH, trailing 756d quantiles) read clean/H1/H2 for its carried cell (vchg high tail q0.9 m0: +0.20/-0.11/-0.13); H8-C (VX slope weekly floor) was read on all spans. PY tests a different action (continuous, sign-symmetric, mean-1 linear tilt of every trade, not a threshold switch) on overlapping observables, so H1/H2 and all-in on NQ are NON-VIRGIN for the VIX/term/VVIX family: the maximum attainable status is FOUND-PENDING pending newest live weeks (n/a: pair rows end 2026-10-13). H0 (2023-02-10..2023-07-31) is available in the engine rows and untouched for these observables.
MECHANISM/PRIOR: a trend pair's per-trade payoff varies with the implied-vol regime (stress/vol-of-vol/skew/term inversion change trend persistence vs whipsaw). A continuous tilt avoids the J8 failure mode (thresholds on trailing quantiles fitted on clean, dropping to m=0 on 12% of days). Sign is NOT assumed: both signs are in the grid and a random-sign null and selection-corrected null price that freedom. Prior 3% per family.
STATE (identical construction in all families). For each feature x_t (daily, known at the close of date t): z_t = (x_t - mean(x_{1..t})) / std(x_{1..t}) over an EXPANDING window of all history available at t (min 250 obs; VX min 126), clipped to [-3,3]. A pair trade whose entry label day is d uses z at the last date STRICTLY BEFORE d (one-day lag, no look-ahead; weekend/holiday uses the prior session). Multiplier raw = clip(1 + s*b*z, 0.5, 1.5); m = clip(raw / mean(raw over CLEAN trades), 0.5, 1.5) (mean-1 on the selection span, a constant scalar). s in {+1,-1}, b in {0.1,0.2,0.3}. Applied through the sizing function (contracts rounded by the engine).
FEATURES (each smoothed by p days, p given; SMA of the daily series before standardising): PY1: LVL = ln(VIX) p=3; CHG = ln(VIX_t) - ln(VIX_{t-5}) p=5 (horizon). PY2: IDX = ln(VIX3M/VIX) p=3; VXS = ln(VX_M2/VX_M1) (5-trading-day-pre-expiry roll, as t3_s6_vx) p=3. PY3: VVIX = ln(VVIX) p=3; SKEW = SKEW index p=3. No composite (the VIX-block composite is the union of PY1-PY3).
GRID per family: 2 features x 2 signs x 3 b = 12 cells, 36 total, no other cells. Selection per family on CLEAN 2023-08-01..2025-07-31 weekly-Sharpe uplift vs the clean baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) (asserted 1.46/2.83/2.30, 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots, 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on both). Eligibility: mean(m) on all trades within [0.9,1.1]. H1 and H2 read ONCE for the selected cell (the other cells' H1/H2 are shown after selection for transparency only).
CONTROLS/NULLS: (c1) flat control = constant multiplier equal to the selected cell's mean multiplier over all trades; gate g2: uplift minus flat uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2. (c2) shuffled-state null: the selected cell with the state z permuted across label days (global permutation of the daily z series, 100 draws); p95 gate on clean and all-in uplift. (c3) random-b-sign null: the selected cell with |b| and feature fixed, sign drawn at random per ... (100 draws of a random permutation of per-DAY signs is NOT used; here: a global sign flip is a 2-point distribution so instead per CALENDAR MONTH an independent random sign, 100 draws); reported with rank, gate rank >= 0.95 on clean and all-in. (c4) selection-corrected null: the full 12-cell grid and clean-only selection re-run on 60 shuffled-state replicates per family; gate: real clean uplift >= p95 of the replicate best-clean uplifts. 
GATES (selected cell; baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None), clean process, b11_nt8 before b10_core never): g1 uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2; g2 vs flat control as above; g3 worst week not worse (all-in); g4 nulls c2,c3,c4; g5 bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; g6 plateau: 7 neighbours = b x0.75, b x1.25, rolling-756d standardisation instead of expanding, one extra lag day, clip [0.6,1.4], smoothing/horizon p x0.75 and x1.25 (rounded: p=3 -> 2 and 4; CHG p=5 -> 4 and 6); every neighbour re-normalised; PASS = >= 75% of neighbours keep >= 75% of the primary's all-in uplift (needs >= 6 of 7); g7 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; g8 replication needs >= 2 of 4 legs with same sign and >= 50% of the all-in uplift (legs: newest live weeks = n/a; H0 2023-02-10..07-31 pair rows; ES/RTY standalone ST+HM books d1_f4_core ADR20 k=0.75 with the same cell and the same state, both books all-in positive and mean >= 50%; alternating-month split: select among the family's 12 cells on odd months read on even and reverse, and the carried cell's held-out uplift > 0 in both directions and >= 25% of its selection half); g9 insurance metrics (CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, weekly Sharpe, MAR, %neg weeks; clean/H1/H2/all); g10 NT8-codable + margin (a daily CSV of z or constants is not needed: NT8 reads VIX/VVIX/SKEW via an external data series or a daily file refreshed at 16:15 ET; margin unchanged for multipliers <= 1, peak +50% at 1.5). Constant-risk framing: pair-change framing IS constant-risk at mean multiplier 1 (reported with the flat control). ERA FLAG: pair rows start 2023-02; pre-2023 not testable for the pair; the 2013-22 gross NQ-daily check is NOT run (no pair replica). Verdict classes: FOUND-PENDING (all gates, max status given non-virgin) / LEAD (>= 5 of the measured gates) / NO.
Outputs: outputs/autopilot/py1/ py2/ py3/, code/census/night_ins/ap_py1_*.py, REPORT_PY1..PY3.md ending 'DONE PY<n>', DECISIONS.md line, status/PY<n>.txt, UPD lines.

## BATCH 7 (worker ap_py2): PY4, PY5, PY6 - per-book and health-driven continuous size modulators; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any scored run (only a baseline reproduction 1.46/2.83/2.30 and timing were run)
Common: engine = live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in the clean process (ap_h7_core -> ap_g6_core), sizefn multiplier per trade, fill 1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots, 2x slip = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant, overlay NOT renormalised (tilt is mean ~1 by construction). Tilt recipe (E3): f = clip(1 + sg*a*(2p-1), 0.5, 1.5), p = walk-forward EXPANDING percentile of the state among earlier same-book trades (PY6: earlier weeks), factor 1 until 100 book trades (PY6: 10 prior health values). Entry-time states use only bars closed before the entry bar; daily states use the last RTH session closed at or before entry time. Selection on clean 2023-08..2025-07 only (max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift among the cells, reported even if < +0.10); H1/H2 read once for the carried cell. Gates unchanged (uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2 vs B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None); worst week not worse; own shuffled-state null p95 on clean and all-in; bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau >= 75% of 4 neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift; 2x slip >= +0.10 each span). Also reported: selection-corrected null (30 replicates, every state shuffled, full cell grid re-selected on clean, chance that the best cell passes clean/H1/H2 +0.10), flat-size control (constant book multiplier = clean mean of the carried factor; 0.9 and 1.1 for context), alternating-month split over all cells, ES/RTY 2023-26 cross-check where the state is a daily NQ state, insurance metrics. Replication legs for FOUND (>= 2 of 4): newest live weeks (none newer than 2026-10-13: not creditable), H0 (pair rows start 2023-02-10, H0 2023-02..07 is the span before clean; reported if the state exists there), ES/RTY, alternating-month. ERA flag: not testable (pair rows 2023+). Pair-change framing only (a modulator of existing books has no stand-alone book; no library save).
PY4 (HM-only modulator; M8 x D08 vol regime, D06 gap; H2-H4). Mechanism: HM (VWAP-relative trend) carries ~90% of the last-14-months P&L; if its edge scales with intraday/overnight volatility expansion (trending days) the size should follow, and ST (whipsaw-prone) is unchanged. Prior 6% (pair-wide analogues G8-A, PA5, PX26, PX27 all NO; per-book split and continuous form are the new part). States (fixed): VR60 = ln(sqrt(sum r^2, last 60 1-min bars) / sqrt(60 x mean r^2 over trailing 7,800 bars)) [PX27 construct, W=60]; GAP = |overnight gap| / (ADR20/price) for RTH entries 09:31-15:59 ET, factor 1 otherwise [PX26 construct]; RV20 = std of the last 20 daily NQ close-to-close log returns (RTH 15:59 closes, roll days dropped). Cells: state{VR60,GAP,RV20} x sg{+1,-1} x a{0.25,0.5} = 12, applied to HM rows only (ST rows factor 1). Plateau neighbours: a x0.75, a x1.25, state window x0.75 and x1.25 (VR60 W 45/75; GAP and RV20 ADR/return window 15/25).
PY5 (ST-only modulator): identical 12 cells applied to ST rows only (HM unchanged). Mechanism/prior as PY4; the contrast with PY4 is the point (ST trades are 'many small' and the book most exposed to chop; a vol-expansion tilt could help ST more than HM). Prior 6%.
PY6 (pair rolling-health continuous size modulator; M8 x D00). Mechanism: weekly pair P&L has lag-1 autocorrelation -0.15 (B8 note), so either sign is plausible - momentum (size up after healthy 8 weeks) or mean-reversion (size up after weak 8 weeks); both signs pre-registered, clean selects. State at week w = metric over the 8 completed weeks w-8..w-1 of the BASELINE pair weekly P&L (one-week lag = weeks must be fully closed; the current week is never used): H1 = 8-week Sharpe (mean/std, ddof 1), H2 = 8-week P&L / (expanding weekly sd x sqrt 8). Cells: metric{H1,H2} x sg{+1,-1} x a{0.25,0.5} = 8, factor applied to both books for all trades entered in week w. Plateau neighbours: a x0.75, a x1.25, window 6 and 10 weeks. Null = shuffled health series across weeks. Disclosure: state uses the unmodulated baseline weekly P&L (live would use realised modulated P&L; factors lie in [0.5,1.5], difference second-order). Difference to tested cousins: C11-HM = binary HM-only OFF switch when HM's own 8-week Sharpe < expanding 10th percentile (NO, +0.20/-0.32/-0.23); B8 = a separate 5-min ensemble switched ON after trailing pair loss (adds a sleeve, does not rescale the pair; NO); G0b = multi-day equity-curve drawdown STEP-DOWN (one-sided, thresholded on drawdown from peak; NO); PY6 is two-sided, continuous, uses a rolling-window risk-adjusted health score and both books. Prior 4%.
Non-virgin disclosure: VR60 is read in PA5/PX27/G8-A, GAP in PX26, RV20-type vol sizing in B17 (pair-wide, on pair P&L vol) - their H1/H2 were read pair-wide; per-book or continuous-tilt variants are new but H1/H2 are non-virgin for the state families. PY6 health-state cousins C11-HM, G0b, B8, B19 read H1/H2.

## PY7-PY10 (worker ap_py3; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any PY run; only entry counts / feature definitions looked at, no P&L)
Common: live pair B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) via ROWS_corrected_engine in a CLEAN process (baseline must reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30); overlay = per-entry size multiplier through B.Cfg.sizefn (0 = skip), fill 1 tick/side + $34.88/8 lots, 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant; features use only 1-minute bars closed before the entry bar or earlier sessions; edges/terciles/rank-CDF frozen on clean entries (2023-08..2025-07). Selection: best clean weekly-Sharpe uplift among the family's cells; H1/H2 read once for the carried cell. NON-VIRGIN disclosure: the features of PY7/PY10 (gap/ADR, 60-min vol ratio), PY8 (J6a minute window) and PY9 (H11-B stop-out trigger) were read on clean/H1/H2 in PX26/PA5/PX1/J6a/H11-B; max status FOUND-PENDING; a FOUND needs >= 2 replication legs of {newest live weeks (none exist: rows end 2026-10-12/13), H0 2023-02-10..07-31, ES/RTY 2023-26 (run for PY8 only; NQ-only features otherwise), alternating-month split}. Gates (all must pass): (1) uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; (2) worst week not worse (all-in and per span); (3) own block null p95 on clean and all-in (100 draws: features permuted jointly within book/year/ET-hour blocks, cell rebuilt with re-derived frozen edges; PY9: random afternoon break on the same fired days, same action/scope); (3b) SELECTION-CORRECTED null: 40 worlds, the full cell grid re-run per world, best-clean cell picked, real clean and all-in uplift must exceed the p95 of the selected cells' clean and all-in uplifts; (4) bootstrap 90% CI of the all-in uplift > 0 (3000 weekly resamples); (5) plateau: the registered neighbours keep >= 75% of all-in uplift for >= 75% of them; (6) 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; (7) flat-size control: uplift minus the flat multiplier (mean multiplier of the carried cell on every trade) >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2; (8) family gate (below); (9) replication legs; (10) insurance metrics reported; NT8-codable and margin note. Zero FOUND is acceptable; no cell added after a result.
- PY7: z_gap = clean-frozen empirical-CDF rank-z (2u-1) of ALIGN = side x ln(09:30 close / prior 15:59 close)/(ADR20/price), defined on RTH entries 09:31-15:59 ET (0 elsewhere; roll days missing -> 0); z_vol = -(rank-z of q_W), q_W = ln(sqrt(sum r_NQ^2 over W bars ending EI-1)/sqrt(sum r_ES^2 ...)), |r|>2% zeroed (PA5/PX1 definition). m = (1 + lam*(z_gap+z_vol)/2) / mean over all trades. Cells W {45,60} x lam {0.25,0.5} (4). Neighbours: lam x0.75, x1.25, W {34,56} (for 45) or {45,75} (for 60). Family gate (8): clean uplift of the carried combination > clean uplift of gap-only and of vol-only at the same lam (reported controls).
- PY8: entry ET minute-of-hour in {0,15,30,45} (b15k1 of J6a), applied to entries of one book, multiplier m {0,0.5}; cells ST_m0, ST_m0.5, HM_m0, HM_m0.5 (4); reduce-only, not renormalised. Neighbours: depth (1-m) x0.5 and x0.75 (2). Reported controls: same m on both books (the J6a parent), window shifted by -1 and +1 minute. Family gate (8): both shifted windows keep < 50% of the carried all-in uplift (phase specificity). ES/RTY leg: standalone ST+HM books via d1_f4_core, same skip on the selected book/minutes, uplift > 0 both and no span below -0.10, mean >= 50% of NQ all-in.
- PY9: trigger = N=2 stop-outs (Exit name contains Stop) of the pair on the label day with hold <= X minutes and exit time 12:00-16:59 ET (uses only trades closed before the entry), action HALT (x0) or HALF (x0.5) on all later entries of the label day (SAME) or also on the next label day's entries with ET hour in [18,24) or [0,12) (NEXT). Cells X {10,20} x act {HALT,HALF} x scope {SAME,NEXT} (8). Neighbours: N=3, X x0.75, X x1.25. Reported control: the other scope at the carried X/act. Family gate (8): the selection-corrected null IS the truncation control (random afternoon break); additionally the carried cell must have >= 20 fired days in 2023-02..2026-10 else 'small-sample'.
- PY10: R_L = mean over the previous L sessions (shifted one) of gc_d = (gap_d/(ADR_d/price)) x ((RTH close - RTH open)/ADR_d), at least 0.75 L valid; sgn = sign(R_L) (0 if undefined); t = clean-frozen tercile code of ALIGN (+1 top, -1 bottom, 0 mid; RTH entries 09:31-15:59 only); m = (1 + lam*sgn*t)/mean over all trades. Cells L {10,20} x lam {0.25,0.5} (4). Neighbours: lam x0.75, x1.25, L {8,13} (for 10) or {15,25} (for 20). Reported controls UNFLIPPED (sgn=+1) and INVERTED (sgn=-1). Family gate (8): flipped clean uplift > unflipped clean uplift.
Replication leg definitions: H0 holds = uplift on 2023-02-10..07-31 weekly Sharpe >= 50% of the clean uplift and > 0; alt-month holds = carried cell on held-out months > 0 and >= 25% of the selection-half in BOTH directions (selection among the cells on one half, carried cell read on the other as in PX1); plateau as in (5).
Code: code/census/night_ins/ap_py7_core.py, ap_py7_run.py (PY7/PY8/PY10), ap_py9_run.py, ap_py7_xi.py (PY8 ES/RTY), ap_py7_doc.py; outputs outputs/autopilot/py7..py10/; status/PY7..10.txt. Pool of 4.

## BATCH S, part 3 (SW21..SW30), registered 2026-10-16T23:19Z BEFORE any SW21-SW30 scored run (only first-cell trade-count smoke tests on NQ; no Sharpe or uplift read). Rules, fill model, selection, nulls, gates, replication as BATCH S part 1; 100-replicate false-survivor test. Priors 2% each.
- SW21 live pair engine as standalone sleeves on other instruments (D00); SW22 daily option-state direction (D03 H5); SW23 autocorrelation-regime gating (D02); SW24 pooled-transfer GBM (D12); SW25 real aggressor-flow imbalance (D01/D02, NQ/ES, 2023+ only); SW26 correlation-gated lead-lag (D07); SW27 1-min jump shocks (D08/D10 H1); SW28 opening-activity regime (D08 H4); SW29 session-specific signals (D06 H3); SW30 VIX-state gating (D08 H3).
- Calibration disclosure for the final count: a random-entry calibration of the chance rate of 'gate-3 at 20%' (uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2) is run after all SW results exist, for the summary only; it does not change any verdict.

## BATCH RY (RY1..RY10, prefix RY, ap_ry_*), registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any RY run by worker I. Question: is the K4 VIX-block timing real and independent? K4 family = K4, K4v2, RW5, RW12P, RX5, RX7P (ONE signal; promotion of one counts for the family). Rules as before (Gate Amendment 2; clean-only selection where a grid exists; own null; drift-matched null and beta test for long-biased sleeves; bootstrap; plateau +-25%; 2x slippage; wd corr; library save family RY<n>; UPD lines; pool 10). RW1 CRITERION (used by RY1, RY2, RY3, RY9): sleeve vs exposure-matched buy-and-hold of the same instruments (each scaled by the sleeve's own exposure fraction): PASS = paired weekly bootstrap 90% CI lower bound of the Sharpe difference > 0 AND the sleeve's additive 20% uplift exceeds the buy-and-hold's on clean, H1 and H2 separately.
- RY1 VIX-STATE TIMING, NO ML. Each label at the 16:16 ET px, if the rule is ON go long ES, NQ, RTY, YM (unit ADR risk each) for one day. Rules (6): r1 VIX < median of its prior 252 labels; r2 VIX/VIX3M < 1; r3 VIX < VIX 5 labels earlier; r4 VIX < VIX 1 label earlier; r5 r1 and r2; r6 r2 and r3. Clean-only selection of 1 of 6; sleeve gates + RW1 criterion + drift-matched null + beta test. K4/RX5 reference numbers quoted from RW1.
- RY2 PER-INSTRUMENT STANDALONE (no selection): for each of ES, NQ, RTY, YM alone: (i) the RX5 cell (5-seed K4 ensemble, d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly, signal restricted to that instrument), (ii) the RY1-selected rule. Each of the 8 cells is scored as its own sleeve (scaled to the pair's clean weekly sd, additive 20%), with the RW1 criterion against its own exposure-matched buy-and-hold and the drift-matched null. An instrument "stands alone" if the cell passes uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 AND the RW1 criterion.
- RY3 OTHER EQUITY INDICES ON DISK: NKD (Nikkei, CME) only (DAX/FDAX not on disk: skipped). (i) the RY1-selected VIX rule applied to NKD (long 1 day at its 16:16 ET last price); (ii) transfer: LightGBM d2 trained walk-forward (quarterly from 2016, as K4) on ES/NQ/RTY/YM with PRICE+VIX features (no instrument id, no COT) and applied to NKD, long when p >= 0.58. Reports: standalone Sharpe, RW1 criterion vs NKD buy-and-hold at equal exposure, own-scaled 20% additive uplift per span, drift-matched null.
- RY4 REGIME-CONDITIONAL RX5 (hedge claim). RX5 signal taken only when the pair's own trailing P&L (library PAIR daily, known at the label) is bad: window w {8, 13} weeks x rule {sum < 0, sum below its expanding 33rd percentile}: 4 cells; clean-only selection; standard gates AND hedge-claim gates (worst-decile correlation with the pair < 0 and circular-shift null rank >= 0.95).
- RY5 K4 PROBABILITY SCALING OF THE FVG E1 SLEEVE (limit-order sleeve; E1 = tf3+tf5+tf15 FVG pullback tick-replay members from ap_fv_e1.trades, R-sized, belongs to the ICT worker): each E1 trade's quantity x clip(1 + side sg a z, 0.5, 1.5) with z the K4 5-seed ensemble z = clip((pbar-0.5)/0.1,-1,1) at the previous label of the trade's placement; a {0.25, 0.5} x sg {+1 (the K4 hypothesis), -1 (control)} = 4 cells, no selection; report E1 unmodified vs modified: 20%-share additive uplift per span and the paired weekly bootstrap CI of the difference in all-in additive uplift ("does K4 help or hurt a limit-order sleeve").
- RY6 RX5 FRESH PRE-REGISTRATION with seeds 10-14 and the STANDARD plateau (>= 75% of 8 neighbours keep >= 75% of the uplift): primary = 5-seed ensemble over random_state 10-14 (d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly); neighbours: delta 0.06/0.10; trees 112/188; lr 0.0375/0.0625 (all 5-seed ensembles on seeds 10-14); alternative seed sets 0-4 and 5-9. K4v2 replication battery (H0, NQ+ES-trained proxy, alternating months same cell), drift-matched null, beta test.
- RY7 FAMILY-WISE ACCOUNTING: deflated Sharpe ratio (Bailey-Lopez de Prado) and probability of backtest overfitting (CSCV, S = 16 blocks of the 167 pair-window weeks) over the full K4-family trial set: all ES/NQ/RTY/YM daily GBM sleeve cells run in K4, K4v2 (32), RW12P (32 extended-feature cells), RW2 (8 models), RW3 (6), RW4 (5), RX5 (cell + 8 neighbours), RX6 (cell + 8), RX7P (12-cell grid), L3 (8), L4 (16), L9 (8). Effective number of independent trials from the eigenvalue participation ratio of the trial correlation matrix; DSR evaluated for the K4v2 cell, the RX5 cell and the RW12P cell at N_total and N_eff; PBO for the K4v2 grid, the RW12P grid and the pooled set. Report only.
- RY8 VIX-RULE DIRECTIONAL PAIR MODULATOR (rule-based analogue of RW5): z = +1 when the state is benign, -1 when stressed, 0 otherwise; factor = clip(1 + side a z, 0.5, 1.5), applied to rows whose lab_in is the next label. States (3): r_term (VIX/VIX3M < 1 -> +1, > 1 -> -1); r_chg (VIX below its value 5 labels ago -> +1, above -> -1); r_both (term < 1 and falling -> +1; term > 1 and rising -> -1; else 0); a {0.25, 0.5}: 6 cells, clean-only selection; E3 gate set.
- RY9 K4 CONTINUOUS SIZING (sleeve): units per signal = min(cap, max(0, (p - 0.5)/sc)) with the 5-seed ensemble p; sc {0.10, 0.15}, cap {1, 2}; 4 cells, clean-only selection; sleeve gates + RW1 criterion; compared with RX5 (binary delta 0.08).
- RY10 RETRAIN CADENCE SENSITIVITY (3-seed ensembles, d2, N=1, delta 0.08, longonly): retrain monthly / quarterly (RX5 convention) / semiannual (Jan, Jul) / annual (Jan), training on all realised data, predictions from 2016-01. 4 cells, no selection; sleeve gates reported for each; question: does the edge need adaptive retraining (non-stationary) or survive a stale model (structural)?

## BATCH RZ (RZ1..RZ10, prefix RZ, ap_rz_*), registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any RZ run by worker I; NOT K4-derived (K4 family closed to new variants). Template: the ICT worker's FVG pullback finding (limit orders at structural levels, tick-level fills, high-range-day dependence, edge persistent pre-2023). NQ limit-order setups are owned by the ICT worker (prefix ap_fv_*) and are NOT duplicated; RZ1/RZ2/RZ9 cover other markets, RZ3/RZ4/RZ5/RZ8/RZ10 are NQ range-regime items, RZ6 conditions the ICT E1 sleeve, RZ7 is the dip-buy limit variant. Rules as before (Gate Amendment 2; clean-only selection; own null; bootstrap; plateau +-25%; 2x slippage; wd corr; replication H0/alternating months for pair-side; library save family RZ<n>; UPD lines; pool 10).
- SIMULATOR (RZ1, RZ2, RZ9): 1-minute caches (adjusted), tf-minute bars (epoch buckets) with Wilder ATR14. Setup FVG: 3-bar imbalance (bar i low above bar i-2 high, or mirrored) of size >= m x ATR; limit at 50% of the gap; stop = candle-1 extreme -/+ 0.05 ATR. Setup DISP: bar with range >= b x ATR and body >= 70% of range; limit at 50% of the bar's range; stop beyond the bar's far extreme by 0.05 ATR. Stop floor max(0.75 ATR, 30 ticks); setups with stop distance > 4 ATR skipped (so markets where 30 ticks exceeds 4 ATR - e.g. ZN - have no trades: reported, not tuned); order placed at the next 1-minute row after the tf bar closes, valid 20 tf bars and not past the 16:55 ET flat; fills only if a 1-minute row trades through the limit by one tick; same-row stop = loss at the stop; stop fills at the stop (gap: open) with 1 tick slippage; target 2R fills on touch; hold cap 360 minutes then close - 1 tick; flat at 16:55 ET; one open trade at a time. Net R = (gross - $4.36/contract-equivalent)/stop distance. Unit-risk sleeve (1 R per trade) scaled to the pair's clean weekly sd.
- GRID per instrument (8 cells): setup {FVG (m 0.25, 0.5), DISP (b 1.5, 2.0)} x tf {15, 30}. Clean-only selection (>= 40 clean trades); plateau neighbours: thr, R (target 1.5/2.5), L (15/25 bars) each x0.75/x1.25; null = random setups (same count, random sides, entry depth and stop distance equal to the real medians) through the same simulator. ERA 2013-22 (equities, FX, metals, energy) from the same simulator; fixed cost only (the price-scaled decomposition is not defined for R-denominated trades and equals the fixed row); range-tercile table of net R per trade by realised day-range/ADR tercile for 2013-22 and 2023-26.
- RZ1 instruments: ES, RTY, YM, GC, CL, ZN, 6E (per instrument selection and gates; ES/RTY replicate the ICT worker's NQ-adjacent replication on a different simulator and window). RZ2: BTC and ETH futures (CME crypto, data/t4; history from 2022-01 only, so ERA is 2022 only).
- RZ9 EQUAL-RISK BASKET of the RZ1/RZ2 instruments whose selected cell has a clean weekly Sharpe >= 0.5 (rule fixed now): equal-risk average of those daily R series, scaled to the pair; full sleeve gates (selected cells are already clean-selected: disclosure) ; compared with the best single instrument.
- RZ3 PRE-OPEN RANGE-EXPANSION FORECAST (NQ, simple rules, no trees): score for label day D = mean of expanding percentiles (same-sample, only prior days) of (a) overnight range (18:00-09:29 ET) / ADR20, (b) prior-label full range / ADR20, (c) VIX close percentile (prior label); available features only (RTH-time score uses a, b, c; earlier-in-day score uses b, c). Forecast skill: Spearman and top-third AUC against the realised RTH range / ADR20, 2013-22 and 2023-26. Conditioning of an intraday sleeve: NQ opening-range breakout (stop entries at OR high + 1 tick / OR low - 1 tick after the OR ends, first break per day and side, stop at the opposite OR boundary (min 0.3 ADR20, max 1 ADR20), target 2R, flat 16:55; OR {15, 30} min) taken only when the forecast score is >= {none, 0.5, 0.67}: 6 cells; clean-only selection; sleeve gates. 
- RZ4 PAIR FLIPS TILTED BY THE RANGE FORECAST (pair-side, walk-forward): factor = clip(1 + sg a (2p - 1), 0.5, 1.5) with p = RZ3 score percentile for the entry's label (RTH entries: full score; Globex entries: score without the overnight leg), a {0.25, 0.5}, sg {+1 size up in forecast-high days, -1 control}, scope {RTH entries only, all entries}: 8 cells; E3 gate set.
- RZ5 RANGE DEPENDENCE OF THE PAIR (report only): net R per trade (R = $9,600) of the live rows (2023-26) and the 1-minute engine rows (2013-26, p1 run_fixed_real) by realised day-range/ADR tercile and by forecast tercile (causal, RZ3 score), with bootstrap se; conclusion: is a range tilt possible without look-ahead.
- RZ6 RANGE FORECAST CONDITIONING OF THE ICT E1 SLEEVE (report only, paired as RY5): E1 trade quantity x {0 (skip), 0.5} on placement labels with forecast score below {0.5, 0.33}: 4 cells; paired weekly bootstrap CI of the all-in additive-uplift difference vs unconditioned E1.
- RZ7 DIP-BUY WITH A LIMIT AT A STRUCTURAL LEVEL (ES, NQ, RTY, YM; C11 daily bars for the signal): signal at the close of day d: RSI(2) < 5 or IBS < 0.10, with close > SMA200; instead of buying the close, place a buy limit on the next session (label d+1, valid to 16:55 ET) at the prior-day low or at 50% of the prior-day range (CE); fill = one-tick trade-through; stop 3 ATR20 below the limit (intraday, 1 tick slip), exit at the 16:16 ET price three labels after the fill label or at the stop; unit ADR risk; 8 cells (signal 2 x level 2 x ... exits fixed) = signal {RSI2, IBS} x level {PDL, CE}: 4 cells. Clean-only selection; I9 sleeve gates.
- RZ8 PRIOR-DAY HIGH/LOW BREAK CONDITIONED ON THE RANGE FORECAST (NQ): stop entry at the prior RTH-label high + 1 tick (long) / low - 1 tick (short) during 09:30-15:30 ET, first break per side per day, stop 0.5 ADR20 (or 0.75), target 1 ADR20, flat 16:55; taken only if forecast score >= {none, 0.5, 0.67}: 2 stops x 3 filters = 6 cells; clean-only selection; sleeve gates. (T4 D / D2 prior-day break lead was selected after the fact among 276; this is a clean-only re-test.)
- RZ10 CROSS-ASSET FORECAST EXTENSION: add a macro leg to the RZ3 score: the overnight range / ADR20 of ZN, 6J, CL, GC (mean of their expanding percentiles) as feature (d); compare forecast skill (Spearman/AUC) with and without (d) on 2013-22 and 2023-26, and rerun the RZ4 grid with the extended score (8 cells); E3 gates; report whether (d) improves the forecast.

## BATCH RV (RV1 = coordinator follow-up on the RZ FVG-retrace sleeves for GC, CL and the 9-instrument basket; prefix RV, code ap_rv_*), registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any RV run by worker I. Cells are FIXED to the RZ selections (GC FVG tf15 thr0.25, CL FVG tf15 thr0.25, basket = RZ9 members); nothing is re-selected. Does not touch NQ FVG (ICT worker). Same 1-min simulator as RZ1 (ap_rz_sim.py, extended with parameters fr, ft, tt, es; defaults reproduce RZ1).
- RV1a H0 (2023-02-10..2023-07-31, first PAIR book day to H1 start): trades by exit label; report n, net R per trade, t, weekly Sharpe, additive-20% uplift vs PAIR. Pass: net R/trade > 0 and uplift > 0.
- RV1b alternating months (calendar month parity of exit label, 2023-02..2026-10, both halves, both directions): net R/trade, t, monthly-total Sharpe per half. Pass: both halves net R > 0 and monthly t > 1.
- RV1c exact +-25% plateau, one parameter at a time, six parameters: FVG min gap thr 0.25 -> 0.1875/0.3125; stop floor 30 ticks -> 22/38; retrace fraction fr 0.5 -> 0.375/0.625 (fr measured from the far edge of the gap); target R 2 -> 1.5/2.5; hold cap H 360 -> 270/450 min; order life L 20 -> 15/25 tf bars: 12 neighbours. A neighbour passes if clean weekly Sharpe >= 75% of the base AND additive-20% uplift > 0 on H1 and on H2. Plateau pass: >= 75% of neighbours pass. Per market and basket (basket neighbours move all members together).
- RV1d family-wise null, depth permutation: statistic = maximum over the 72 RZ cells (9 instruments x 8 cells) of clean weekly Sharpe (R units). Null world = every setup keeps its detection, stop and sizing but its entry fraction is redrawn U(0.25,0.75) of the gap/range (destroys the structural 50% level; DISP cells use the same redraw on the 50% retrace), run through the same simulator, 100 worlds. Report the rank of the observed maximum and of GC and CL's own market statistic (max over that market's 8 cells) against the matching null maxima. Pass: observed rank >= 0.95.
- RV1e stress set (per market and basket; clean/H1/H2/all weekly Sharpe and additive-20% uplift): S1 2x slippage (2 ticks on stop/market exits); S2 1-tick adverse entry (fill 1 tick worse, R denominator unchanged); S3 20% of filled trades missed at random (mean of 50 draws); S4 = S1+S2+S3. Fill bounds as the ICT worker: TOUCH (fill on touch, ft=0), THROUGH1 (base), STRICT (fill needs 2 ticks through, target needs 1 tick through, 1-tick adverse entry). Pass: S4 additive-20% uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 and STRICT clean weekly Sharpe > 0.
- RV1f sizing: contracts per trade at 10/20/30% share = share x f / (median stop in $ per contract), f = pair clean weekly sd / sleeve clean weekly sd in R; micro (MGC 10 oz, MCL 100 bbl) equivalents; margin ASSUMPTIONS flagged (not verified with the broker): GC $11,000, MGC $1,100, CL $6,500, MCL $650 initial per contract.
- RV1g independence: daily and weekly correlation of GC, CL, basket with the PAIR book, ICT_FVG_E1_tickreplay and ICT_FVG_IMP3_tf3_tickreplay (library), plus worst-decile pair-week behaviour.
- RV2 price quote only (Databento metadata.get_cost, no purchase): GLBX.MDP3 trades for GC.v.0 and CL.v.0 2023-02-01..2026-10-14; written to SPEND.json notes.

## BATCH RU (RU1..RU10, prefix RU, code ap_ru_*), registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any RU run by worker I. Not K4-derived. NQ FVG/IMP3 are the ICT worker's; ICT code and pickles (ap_fv_*, fv/g7_x.pkl) are read-only references; no duplicate runs of its jobs. Rules as before (Gate Amendment 2; clean-only selection; own random null; bootstrap; +-25% plateau; 2x slippage; worst-decile corr; library family RU<n>; pool 10).
- SIMULATOR for RU1/RU3: the RZ 1-minute limit simulator (ap_rz_sim), extended with setup IMP (nb=3 consecutive same-direction tf bars, net move c[i]-o[i-2] >= m ATR, limit at o[i-2]+f x net, stop beyond bar-1 extreme by 0.05 ATR, floor max(0.75 ATR, 30 ticks), cap 4 ATR, 2R, life 20 bars, hold 360, flat 16:55 - the ICT IMP3 definition except for the 30-tick floor which is inactive on NQ at tf3) and depth tools.
- RU1 SIDE-BY-SIDE DEPTH-PERMUTATION NULL, markets NQ, GC, CL (ES, RTY as extra rows). Cells (fixed, no selection): IMP3 tf3 m1.5 f0.5 (ICT cell); FVG tf5 m0.25 f0.5 (ICT P1 definition); FVG tf15 m0.25 f0.5 (RZ cell). Statistic: clean (2023-08..2025-07) weekly Sharpe of R series, sample 2023-02..2026-10 trades. Null A (ICT's permutation, ap_fv_gates.depth_perm_one): the (depth below the signal close, stop distance) pair of every setup is permuted jointly across setups; null B (RV1): entry fraction redrawn U(0.25,0.75) of the gap/move with the original stop. 200 worlds each. Report real, null median/p95, rank, per market and cell, next to the ICT tick-replay figures (IMP3: real 4.37 vs best-of-20 null mean 1.93, p95 2.62; read from fv/g7_x.pkl). Verdict per cell: DEPTH MATTERS if real > null A p95 and > null B p95; else DEPTH-FREE.
- RU3 WHY DOES DEPTH MATTER (explanatory table, report only): for NQ, ES, RTY, YM, GC, CL, 6E, BTC, ETH (FVG tf15 m0.25 and, for NQ/GC/CL, IMP3 tf3): median stop in ticks and USD, round-trip cost in R (commission + 2 ticks), median ATR in ticks, gross and net R per trade and clean weekly Sharpe as a function of entry fraction f in {0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0} (f=0 far edge of gap, 1 near edge; fills use the standard rule; f=1.0 is closest to a market-on-pullback), plus Spearman of (depth-effect = Sharpe(f=0.5) - mean Sharpe(f=0.25, 0.75)) against stop-in-ticks and cost-in-R across markets.
- RU2 DISPLACEMENT CONTINUATION (own mechanism, level-free): markets GC, CL, SI, HO, RB, NG, HG, 6E, ES, NQ. Setup: tf bar (15 or 30 min) with range >= k ATR14 (k 1.5 or 2.0) and close within 20% of range of the extreme in the bar's direction; trade WITH the bar. Entry: market at the next 1-min open + 1 tick (mkt) or stop at bar extreme + 1 tick valid 20 tf bars (stop). Stop S x ATR (S 1.0 or 1.5; floor 30 ticks; skip if > 4 ATR), target R x stop (R 1 or 2), hold cap 360 min, flat 16:55, 1 tick slip on stop/market exits, $4.36/contract; one trade at a time. 32 cells per market (ent 2 x k 2 x tf 2 x S 2 x R 2); clean-only selection (>= 40 clean trades); I9-style sleeve gates; neighbours k, S, R, L x0.75/1.25. Null: random tf bars with range >= k ATR (displacement magnitude kept, close position ignored) and random side, same count, same geometry. Fill bounds reported on the selected cell: entry slippage 1/2/3 ticks. Comparison: the RZ retrace-limit DISP cells on the same market (REPORT_RZ1/RZ2 selection tables are not the same family; the comparison re-runs DISP b=k with f=0.5 through the RU2 report).
- RU4 PAIR TILT BY DISPLACEMENT-CONTINUATION STATE (NQ): state at each pair flip entry = direction dir (+1/-1) of the last 5-min NQ bar with range >= 1.5 ATR14 and bars-since n (causal, closed bars only). Factor = clip(1 + a x sg x side x dir x w, 0.5, 1.5), w = 1 if n <= N else 0; cells: a {0.25,0.5} x N {12,36} x sg {+1 (confirmation), -1 (control)}: 8 cells; no fitting, so walk-forward = the clean-only selection; E3 gates via ap_rz_pair machinery (null = np.roll of the factor, neighbours a, N x0.75/1.25, 2x slip, H0, alternating months).
- RU5 equal-risk basket of the RU2 selected cells whose clean weekly Sharpe >= 0.5 (RV/RZ9 template): full sleeve gates; compare with best single.
- RU6 SESSION SPLIT of the RU2 selected cell per market: cells {ALL, RTH 09:30-16:00 ET, non-RTH} by placement time: 3 cells, clean-only selection, sleeve gates (tests whether continuation is an overnight/low-liquidity effect, which would make fills unreliable).
- RU7 TRAILING EXIT VARIANT of RU2: no fixed target; trail S ATR from the best price since entry (S 1.0 or 1.5), ent {mkt, stop} x k {1.5, 2.0} x tf {15, 30} x S {1.0, 1.5} = 16 cells; same markets/gates/null.
- RU9 MULTI-BAR CONTINUATION: nb {2, 3} consecutive same-direction tf bars with net move >= k ATR (k 1.5 or 2.5), entry mkt, S 1.0, R 2, tf {15, 30}: 8 cells; same markets/gates/null.
- RU10 CROSS-MARKET CONFIRMATION: RU2 setups taken only if the partner market printed a same-direction bar of range >= 1.0 ATR in the same or previous tf bar; pairs GC|SI, SI|GC, CL|HO, HO|CL, RB|CL, NG|CL; cells ent {mkt, stop} x k {1.5, 2.0} x tf {15, 30} (S 1.0, R 2): 8 per pair; same gates/null.
- RU8 FORECAST x CONTINUATION on NQ: RU4 factor restricted to high-forecast days is NOT run (RZ4 showed no usable forecast). RU8 instead = the RU2 selected cell on ES and NQ in the RZ3 forecast terciles (report only, tercile table of net R per trade).
- RU11 (added 2026-10-17 after the RU1 table, BEFORE any RU11 run; follows from RU1 where IMP3 shows a depth effect on GC and ES as on NQ): IMP3 impulse-retrace limit on GC and ES (NQ is the ICT worker's): setup IMP, nb=3, tf {3, 5} x m {1.5, 2.5} x f 0.5 = 4 cells per market, other rules as RU1/RZ simulator; clean-only selection; full sleeve gates; depth null B (U(0.25,0.75), 200 worlds) on the selected cell as an extra gate. Cross-market support for the single FVG-family lead only (not an independent FOUND).

## SW4-REPL (replication battery for the SW4 FOUND-CANDIDATE RTY cell 39 'RTHopen+15m ref=ft onrange hi0.67 dir=against hold=120m'), written 2026-10-17T01:48Z AFTER the battery had already been run once in a single step (ap_sw_repl.py) and its output seen; DISCLOSED: the criteria below were fixed in the script before running (R1 H0 2023-02-10..2023-07-31 sleeve Sharpe > 0 and uplift > 0; R2 odd and even calendar months of 2023-08..2026-10 each with uplift >= 50% of the all-in uplift 0.36 -> >= 0.18; R3 same cell on ES/NQ/YM with positive uplift on clean, H1, H2 and positive standalone Sharpe, 2 of 3 instruments), two of three replications needed, but this text is post-hoc and the verdict is therefore capped at FOUND-PENDING-REPLICATION. No parameter of the cell was changed. H1/H2 were read once for the selection read; the R3 instruments' H1/H2 are not independent of RTY's (same days, correlated index futures).

## BATCH 3 Q-FAMILIES Q11-Q20 (registered 2026-10-17 before any runner launched). Rules: clean-only selection (>= 100 trades, positive weekly Sharpe in both clean halves, top 10), 200 timing-destroyed replicates (NO reduction), H1/H2 once, own null, bootstrap, plateau (+-25% hold and parameter neighbours), 2x slippage, wd corr, ERA flag with cost decomposition, library save, replication battery for leads. Fill model/cost as CYCLE 12 (1 tick/side, $4.36 RT, next 1-min open, time exit at the open of the row after the hold, no stop, full hold before 16:55 only), 1 contract vol-matched to the pair clean weekly sigma.
