Coverage reasoning: BACKLOG2 groups are all owned (A/D/E1-4/E7 ideation, B/C/M pair-side, F-J and E5/E6 GAP worker, K/L mine, done); so these ten are NEW signal families for the untested part of the matrix (D11 price patterns / D06 clock at H2-H4 on instruments other than NQ) plus an untested class (livestock). Each is intraday signal -> time exit on 8 instruments {ES, NQ, RTY, GC, CL, ZN, BTC, HO} (Q16: RTY YM GC CL ZN BTC ETH SI HG; Q17: HE LE). Prior 2-3% each.
- Q11 volatility burst: bar range > p2 x mean range of the previous p1 bars (p1,p2 in {(10,2.0),(10,3.0),(20,2.5)}), event direction = bar direction.
- Q12 Bollinger cross-out: close crosses beyond +-p2 SD of the p1-bar mean from inside ((20,2.0),(20,2.5),(40,2.5)).
- Q13 run: p1 consecutive same-direction closes (3,4,5), event on the p1-th.
- Q14 squeeze breakout: n-bar (6 or 12) range below p2 bar-ATRs ((6,2),(12,3),(12,4)) and the close breaks the n-bar high/low.
- Q15 volume spike: bar volume > p2 x mean of the previous p1 bars ((20,3),(20,5),(40,4)), direction = bar direction.
- Q16 time-of-day drift buckets (C8-Q2 protocol on new instruments): long or short at each 30-minute start 08:00..14:30 ET, hold 30/60/120 min, 84 cells; direction not fitted on history.
- Q17 livestock HE, LE: B7 (Q17A), B1 (Q17B), B11 (Q17C) grids, session open 10:05 ET.
- Q18 RTH-open distance: |price - RTH open| / ADR20 crosses {0.5, 0.75, 1.0}, direction sign of the distance.
- Q19 EMA distance: (close - EMA(p1))/ATR14 crosses +-p2 ((50,2),(50,3),(100,3)).
- Q20 prior-close distance: (price - prior label-day close)/ADR20 crosses {0.5, 0.75, 1.0}.
Grids (Q11-15, Q18-20): 2 bar sizes {5,15} x 3 parameter pairs x direction {with, against} x hold {60,120,180,240} x session {all, day} = 96 cells; neighbours: hold +-25%, p2 +-25%. R5 for the GC fade is run from ap_g93_r5.py when the extension lands; K-L2 when outputs/a5/L2-DONE appears.

## BATCH 8 PZ1-PZ5 (worker ap_pz2; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any scored run of these families; code prefix ap_pz_*, outputs autopilot/pz1..pz5)
Common: baseline = B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) on ROWS_corrected_engine in a clean process (reproduce 1.46/2.83/2.30); selection on clean 2023-08..2025-07 only (max clean weekly-Sharpe uplift among the family's cells), H1/H2 read once for the carried cell; additive framing = pair-change (size/exit overlay of the pair, not a sleeve; constant-risk framing not applicable, overlay is not renormalised except where stated mean-1); fill model as live (1 tick/side + $34.88 per 8 lots; 2x slippage SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant); gates (all must pass): uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week (all-in) not worse; own null p95 on clean and all-in (200 replicates); bootstrap 90% CI of all-in uplift > 0; plateau >= 75% of the +-25% neighbours keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 on each span; selection-corrected null (200 worlds, the whole family grid re-selected on clean in each world; real clean uplift must exceed the 95th percentile of the best-of-grid clean uplifts); insurance metric set reported. Replication legs (>= 2 of 4 for FOUND; FOUND-PENDING otherwise): (L1) newest live weeks (none after 2026-10-13 yet); (L2) H0 2023-02-10..07-31 uplift > 0 and >= 50% of all-in uplift (credited only where the rule is active in H0); (L3) ES and RTY 2023-26 books (d1_f4_core, k=0.75, own-instrument state, same recipe) both all-in uplift > 0 and mean >= 50% of the NQ all-in; (L4) alternating-month split, select on odd months read on even and reverse, both held-out uplifts > 0 and mean >= 50% of all-in. ERA: pair rows start 2023-02, pre-2023 not testable (reported n/a). Non-virgin disclosure: H1/H2 of the pair were read for every cousin listed in RESEARCH_LOG 'Batch 8 ideas'; PZ2/PZ3 observables (partner exit events, partner open P&L) are new; PZ1/PZ4 states (20-day trend strength, drawdown, vol term, index dispersion) are new but sit in families (RV20 PY4, VIX blocks PY1-3, E6) whose H1/H2 were read.
Engine speed-up (disclosed): ap_pz_fast.py replaces the O(N^2) pass1 with an O(N) version; verified bit-identical (max abs diff 0.0 on 4 configurations incl. a hook run) before any PZ scoring.
PZ1 NQ 20-day regime tilt. States from NQ daily closes (last close at/before 16:59 ET, roll days NaN), value at the last trading date strictly before the entry's label day: TS(W)=|sum of last W log returns|/(std_W*sqrt W) (W=20), DD(W)=max over W days of cumulative log return minus its latest value (W=60), VT(W)=ln(std_W/std_120) (W=20). z = clean-frozen empirical-CDF rank in [-1,1] (NaN -> 0). m = (1 + sg*lam*z) / mean_all(1 + sg*lam*z). Cells 12 = state {TS20,DD60,VT20} x sg {+1,-1} x lam {0.25,0.5}. Plateau neighbours of the carried cell: lam x0.75, lam x1.25, window x0.75, window x1.25 (TS/VT W 15/25, DD W 45/75). Controls: flat 0.9x and 1.1x of all rows, inverted sign. Own null: state series permuted in 20-trading-day blocks (block order random, random start offset; keeps the persistence of month-level states) applied jointly to all states, 200 replicates, cell fixed; secondary iid-day permutation null reported, not gating. Selection-corrected: 200 block-permuted worlds x 12 cells. Prior 3%.
PZ2 partner-event exits. Baseline-accepted rows only. Event: a partner-book row p (other book, same side) exits at bar XI[p] while row j (accepted, EI[j] < XI[p]) is still open; trigger PSTOP = p exit name contains Stop, PWIN = p exit name ends Target (time/netting exits are not events); earliest event per victim. Action at k = XI[p]+1: FLAT = exit j at the open of bar k (pts = side*(o[k]-entry)); BE = stop of j moved to entry price (T=0), exit at the first bar m >= k with low <= entry (long) / high >= entry (short), fill = min(entry, o[m]) side-adjusted (gap fills at the open), stop-first when the same bar also holds the original exit. 4 cells = trigger {PSTOP,PWIN} x action {FLAT,BE}. Plateau neighbours: FLAT delay +1 and +2 min; BE T=-0.25 (lock 15 pts) and T=+0.25 (stop 15 pts beyond entry). Own null: same victims, random event bar uniform over the victim's life after entry, same action (200 replicates); selection-corrected: 200 worlds with every cell's victims given random event bars. Events and exits use 1-min bars (fill model approximation disclosed: real stop fills are intra-bar). Prior 3%.
PZ3 partner open-P&L state. Baseline-accepted rows; stacked row = accepted row i entered while an accepted partner-book row is open; partner P&L in R at the entry bar = side*(o[EI[i]] - entry_p)/60 pts. Cells 6: UW theta {0.25,0.5} x {SKIP,HALF} (partner P&L <= -theta R) and PP theta {0.25,0.5} x pyramid 1.5 (partner P&L >= +theta R). Not renormalised (rare, conditional switch). Plateau: theta x0.75, x1.25, deviation x0.75, x1.25. Controls: the same multiplier on ALL stacked rows regardless of P&L (STACK_ALL), flat mean. Own null: partner P&L values permuted among stacked rows (200 replicates); selection-corrected: 200 permuted worlds x 6 cells. Prior 2%.
PZ4 month-level cross-index tilt. Same form, grid (12 cells), neighbours, null and controls as PZ1 with states DISP(W)=|sum of last W (rNQ-rRTY)|/(std_W(rNQ-rRTY)*sqrt W) (W=20), VNE(W)=ln(std_W NQ / std_W ES) (W=20), VRN(W)=ln(std_W RTY / std_W NQ) (W=20). ES/RTY legs use the instrument's own analog (partner sets: NQ->RTY,ES; ES->RTY,NQ; RTY->NQ,ES). Prior 2%.
PZ5 forward monitor (no gates; infrastructure): ap_pz5_forward.py -> watch/FORWARD_OVERLAYS.md; overlays H11-B N2_X10_HALT, J6a b15k1_m0.0, PA5 L60_TWO_V, PX1 W60_MEAN1 (mean frozen at the watermark), PX26 ALIGN_COUNTER_DOWN, K96b HM stop-entry; watermark label day 20261013; promotion reading unchanged (>= 8 new weeks, new-week weekly-Sharpe uplift >= +0.10, cumulative new-week net difference > 0, worst new week not worse); script validated by a back-test mode that moves the watermark inside H2.
PZ AMENDMENT 1 (2026-10-17, before any scored PZ run of record; disclosed): (i) a smoke test of the engine plumbing printed the weekly Sharpe of ONE PZ2 cell (PSTOP_FLAT: clean 1.35 / H1 3.30 / H2 2.92 vs pair 1.46/2.83/2.30) - selection stays clean-only over all 4 cells so this cannot change the carried cell, but it is a disclosure item: that cell's H1/H2 were seen before selection. (ii) Event-count check (no performance) showed that PZ3 underwater cells at theta 0.25/0.5 R would have only 62/7 clean rows (partners that are still open are mostly in profit); UW thetas are changed from {0.25,0.5} to {0.1,0.2} R (139/77 clean rows; neighbours 0.075/0.125 and 0.15/0.25); PP thetas stay {0.25,0.5} (452/193 clean rows). Everything else as registered.
PZ AMENDMENT 2 (2026-10-17, after the first PZ2 run; disclosed): the PZ2 own-null as registered (same victims, random event bar) is invalid for the PSTOP trigger - victims are defined by the partner stop that ends them, so a random earlier exit cuts trades that are about to stop and leaks the future (first run: null p95 clean uplift +1.88, impossible for a causal rule). The gating null is replaced before any conclusion rests on it by a random-victim null: same number of victims per trigger drawn at random from ALL baseline-accepted rows, event bar at the same elapsed fraction of the victim life as the real events (200 replicates; the selection-corrected null uses 200 such worlds x 4 cells). The leaky null is kept as a secondary, labelled column. Cell results, selection and all other gates are unchanged (the PZ2 cells are deterministic); PZ2 clean uplift is negative so the verdict cannot depend on the null.

### Q20-ENS (registered 2026-10-17 before any ensemble run; contaminated neighbourhood - the RTY cell's three spans were read in Q20)
Grid: the Q20 prior-close-distance FADE cells with direction 'against' and session 'day' = 2 bar sizes {5,15} x thresholds {0.5,0.75,1.0} ADR20 x holds {60,120,180,240} = 24 cells, on ES, RTY, YM, GC (and CL, NQ shown as controls). Objects: per-instrument equal-risk ensembles (each cell scaled to unit clean weekly sigma, averaged, vol-matched to the pair) and the 4-instrument combined ensemble (instrument ensembles equal-risk). Gates: full additive gate set at 10/20/30/50% with bootstrap, timing-destroyed null (30 replicates), 2x slippage, leave-one-value-out plateau, alternating months, H0; cross-market replication = positive uplift on >= 3 of the 4 core instruments; ERA flag; sizing and worst-trade dollars. Not independent of the Q20 selection for RTY; ES/YM/GC were only read as 'x' cross-sections of the RTY cell.

## BATCH 4 Q21-Q30 (registered 2026-10-17 before any runner launched; 200 replicates; clean-only selection; all gates; time exits, no stops unless stated)
Excluded as duplicates / closed: the 'fade a fresh trend event' cluster (closed as one observation; no fade-of-fresh-extension rule is used below except where the object is a calendar/level event rather than a trend flip: Q27 round numbers is a LEVEL event with both reject and break modes, flagged as adjacent), weekend-gap fade (T1/T1b/U2/U3/Z3/W6), roll-week drift (S9 daily), month-end/opex (S7/S8), Asia->London (S2), overnight-range at RTH open (N6), event-day NQ breakout/follow-fade (C2-G3, N2 on NQ), daily seasonals (C2-G4 daily universe), index spreads ES/RTY/NQ (C1-F4). Roll-week calendar spreads need front/second contract series not on disk for the whole universe: SKIPPED (data not on disk). Month-of-year intraday seasonality cannot be selected on a 2-year clean span (each month appears twice), so Q25 uses literature-prior rules fixed in advance, no selection.
Look-ahead checks registered with the code: every signal uses rows <= the signal row; Q21 uses the 5-minute post-release return completed at T+5; Q23 day direction = price at T-offset minus the RTH-open price (RTH open precedes the decision); Q24 first w minutes of the week; Q28 gap = reopen price minus the previous label day's last close observed before the reopen; Q29 London return ends 6+ minutes before the signal row; Q30 week-to-date return uses the Monday open and the price at the signal row. Calendars: FOMC, NFP, CPI 2023-2026 VERIFIED lists of ap_c2_g3_events.py (event families therefore have selection data only from 2023-08; clean-span event counts: FOMC 16, NFP ~24, CPI ~24).
- Q21 scheduled-release windows (FOMC 14:00; NFP and CPI 08:30; 'ALL' = the three): the 5-minute post-release return r5 (open at T to close of T+4); trade WITH or AGAINST sign(r5) if |r5| >= k x ADR20 (k in {0,0.05,0.1}); entry at T+5 open; hold {15,30,60} min. 72 cells. Instruments ES NQ RTY YM GC CL ZN 6E 6J BTC. Selection minimum trades 12 (event counts), halves rule unchanged. Prior 3%.
- Q22 cross-market dislocation convergence: synthetic spread S = pvA x A - r x pvB x B (back-adjusted, r = ADR$ ratio of clean-span medians) for pairs ZN-ZB, ZN-ES, ZB-ES, GC-SI, CL-HO, HO-RB, 6E-6B, ZN-6E; Bollinger(n, k) cross-out of S on {5,15}-min bars with n in {96,480}, k in {2.0,2.5} ((96,2.0),(96,2.5),(480,2.5)), trade the convergence (AGAINST) or divergence (WITH), hold {60,120,180,240} min, session {all, day}: 96 cells per pair. Costs: 1 tick per side per leg and $4.36 per contract per leg (TICK = tickA$ + r x tickB$ per side, COMM = 4.36 x (1 + r)). Prior 3%.
- Q23 settlement-window drift: decision at S-offset (S = instrument settlement time ET: equity 16:00, metals 13:30, energy 14:30, rates 15:00, FX 14:00), direction = sign of (price - RTH open) if |.| >= k x ADR20 (k in {0,0.25,0.5}), WITH or AGAINST, offset {30,15}, exit at S or S+15: 24 cells; instruments ES NQ RTY YM GC SI HG CL HO RB NG ZN ZB 6E. Prior 3%.
- Q24 Sunday-open week structure: first w minutes (60,120) of the week's first Globex session; direction = sign of the return over the window if |.| >= k x ADR20 (k in {0,0.1}); WITH or AGAINST; hold {120,360,720} min (flat at the break): 24 cells; GC SI HG PL ZN ZB ZF 6E 6J 6B. Selection minimum trades 40. Prior 3%.
- Q25 (no selection) literature-prior intraday seasonal drift on energy and grains: fixed calendar rules (RB long Feb-May and short Sep-Nov; HO long Oct-Jan; NG long Oct-Jan and short Mar-May; ZC and ZS short Jun-Aug and long Feb-Apr; ZW short Jun-Jul), trade the day-session drift (08:30 to 14:00 ET, 10:30 to 13:00 for grains) every in-season day; reported against random-month nulls on the pair window and 2013-22 as ERA; an evidence-free cell is any rule not in this list. Prior 2%.
- Q26 intraday cross-sectional momentum / reversal across 14 outrights (ES RTY YM GC CL ZN ZB 6E 6J HG SI NG HO RB): rank each instrument's return from 08:00 ET to 10:30 ET (or to the 1-hour mark) scaled by its ADR20; long top-3 / short bottom-3 (momentum) or the reverse, equal-ADR sizing, exit 15:00 ET; cells: ranking window {to 10:30, to 12:00} x direction x group size {2,3,4}: 12 cells, daily P&L aggregated; clean-only selection; Prior 3%.
- Q27 round-number reaction: levels at multiples of s x {1,2} (s = a 'nice' 1-2-5 number near 0.5 x ADR20 of the clean span); events: close crosses a level (break) or the bar pierces the adjacent level and closes back (reject); WITH or AGAINST the touch direction; bars {5,15}; hold {60,120,180}: 48 cells; instruments ES NQ RTY GC CL ZN BTC HO. Adjacent to the closed fade cluster for the reject mode and flagged. Prior 2%.
- Q28 daily maintenance-break gap: Mon-Thu 18:00 reopen vs the previous label day's last close; decision w minutes (5,15) after the reopen; direction sign(gap) if |gap| >= k x ADR20 (k in {0.02,0.05,0.1}); WITH or AGAINST; hold {60,120,180}: 36 cells; ES NQ RTY GC CL ZN HO 6E. Prior 3%.
- Q29 London-to-New-York handoff: London return (03:00 ET open to RTH_open-6 min) sign if |.| >= k x ADR20 (k in {0,0.15,0.3}); trade the instrument's RTH open (signal w in {0,5} minutes before it) WITH or AGAINST, hold {60,120,180}: 36 cells; ES NQ RTY GC CL ZN HO 6E. Prior 3%.
- Q30 Friday afternoon position squaring: week-to-date return (Monday open to the Friday signal) sign if >= k x 5 x ADR20 x 0.2 (k in {0,0.5,1.0}); WITH or AGAINST; entry at 13:00 or 13:30 ET; hold {60,120,170}: 36 cells; ES NQ RTY YM GC CL ZN 6E. Selection minimum trades 40. Prior 3%.

## BATCH S, part 4 (SW31..SW36), registered 2026-10-17T08:38Z BEFORE any scored SW31-SW36 run (only first-cell trade-count smoke tests on NQ, no Sharpe or uplift read). Coordinator instruction 2026-10-17: continue after GAPMAP re-merge. Re-merge result: 56 unclaimed on-disk UNTESTED cells remain; the sleeve-type ones are D00 H5 M1-M3 NQ, D10 H2-H4 M1-M3 BTC and D12 H6 M1-M3 (NQ, ES, RTY, YM, GC, CL, BTC); the rest are pair-side NQ execution/sizing modes (M4-M8), QQQ and VX cells with no data on disk. That is fewer than three batches of ten, so this batch is SIX families (not ten) and I will not invent filler families.
- Rules, fill model, selection (clean 2023-08..2025-07, Gate Amendment 2), H1/H2 once, own null, bootstrap, plateau, 2x slippage, worst-decile correlation, ERA flag, gates: exactly as BATCH S part 1. AMENDMENT (logged here before running): the false-survivor test uses 200 replicates (NREP=200, not the 100 of parts 1-3), and MINTR (minimum clean trades) is 60 for SW32, SW34, SW35, SW36 (weekly/pair-history cadence cannot reach 100 clean trades; B2356 precedent), with the small-sample flag (< 100 trades in the pair window) so such cells cannot be FOUND; SW31 and SW33 keep 100.
- New machinery: a daily-hold simulator (ap_sw_common.daily_attr/dayrow): entry at the RTH open (next 1-min open + 1 tick), exit at the open of the first row at the stated clock on the label day d+N (path-resolved through the 1-min simulator, rolls via the back-adjusted series, one position at a time, no stop). Daily features are causal (state at the previous label day's close).
- Direct full-pipeline random-entry calibration (replaces the scaled estimate): ap_sw_chance2.py draws worlds of timing-destroyed grids, applies the real selection, then the FULL gate chain (add-gate at 10/20/30/50%, bootstrap, own null, wd corr, plateau on the neighbours' random series, 2x slippage) and counts FOUND-CANDIDATEs; reported per grid. Frozen: the SW4 and SW20 RTY definitions are not touched.

## BATCH BK (BACKLOG 2 residue: groups F, G, J), registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any scored BK run (only data-shape peeks: array shapes, shock-minute frequency of the basket data with no P&L read)
Selection of items (from DECISIONS/GAPMAP_UPDATES/REPORT_* audit): NOT yet run among F/G/H/I/J/K/L on data on disk: 49, 50, 53, 54, 55 (F); 57, 58, 59, 60, 61 (G); 76 (J). Already covered or duplicate and SKIPPED (logged in DECISIONS.md): 48/52 (OPT0DTE cells 4-5, OPTIONS), 51 (no greeks on disk beyond flow-implied inventory; folded into OPT0DTE cell 4), 56 (J6/J7), 62-63,65-66,69 (PX20-24), 67,68 (H7-A, PA4_NKD), 64 and 71 (no VX intraday / VVIX 1-min on disk; J8/PY3 daily), 70,72,73,74 (PX25-27), 75 (J8/PY2/PX34), 77 (J4/J5), 78,79 (no calendar on disk; J79 driver covered 79 partially), 80,81 (fewer than 60 affected pair rows in the pair window: untestable, small-sample), 82-89 (K, K84, K), 90-95 (L90-L93, PX31, W6/T1b/Q5ENS), 96,99,100 (K96,K99,K100), 97/98 (need L2 queue model), E (L2 group, out of scope). Ten families run: BK1 (59), BK2 (57), BK3 (58), BK4 (60 + E6), BK5 (61), BK6 (54), BK7 (49 + 53), BK8 (55), BK9 (50), BK10 (76).
COMMON PROTOCOL (all ten). Engine: live pair rows (ROWS_corrected_engine, 13,184 rows to 2026-10-16) through ap_k_lib (K100/K18 machinery: 1-second replay, live account rules 5R/-2R per book, day lock, week floor 8R), baseline B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) in a clean process; MUST reproduce clean 1.46 / H1 2.83 (H2 2.48 on data to 2026-10-16) or the run aborts. Fill: market at the bar after the signal, 1 tick/side, $34.88 per 8-lot round trip; 2x slippage = SLIP_PT 1.0 on baseline and variant. Selection on CLEAN (2023-08..2025-07) only: the eligible cell with the highest clean weekly-Sharpe uplift vs the baseline; H1 (2025-08-01..2026-02-06) and H2 (2026-02-07..2026-10-16) read once for the carried cell; UNTOUCHED never. Eligibility for tilt/skip/bracket cells: flagged share of clean rows between 1.5% and 45%. Gates (identical to AUTOPILOT.md, none softened): (3) uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 separately; worst week (all-in) not worse than the baseline's; (4) own timing-destroyed null = feature permuted within (book x year-month) blocks, same flagged share and bracket structure, 200 replicates, exact account re-runs, p95 on clean AND all-in uplift; bootstrap 90% CI (3,000 week resamples) of the all-in uplift above zero; (5) plateau: the +-25% neighbours (4-6 per cell, defined below) must keep >= 75% of the all-in uplift in >= 75% of neighbours; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 on each span; (6) worst-decile correlation reported (pair overlays are changes of the pair itself, so not a hedge claim: weekly corr with pair and worst-decile corr of the candidate-minus-pair difference reported); (7) NT8 spec in the report. Replication leg (>= 2 of 4 must hold with the same sign and >= 50% of the clean uplift): H0 2023-02-10..07-31, pre-registered alternating-month split (select on odd months read on even, then reverse; both directions must hold), newest live weeks (the 2026-10-09..16 extension; reported), cross-instrument ES/RTY (pair-row overlays have no ES/RTY pair rows in this harness; run via the J7V-style d1_f4 ES/RTY books only if the family reaches LEAD, otherwise 'n/a, family did not pass the Sharpe gate'). ERA FLAG: the state data start 2022 (basket, options) so pre-2023 is not testable (reported 'not testable'); BK6 GEX history from 2019 is read descriptively. Small-sample flag when the flagged rows in the pair window are < 100. Insurance metrics CVaR5/10, maxDD, worst week, Sortino, MAR, %neg weeks on clean/H1/H2/all-in. Sized dollars: R = $9,600 per full 60-pt stop at 8 contracts (+$34.88 round-trip cost); the report states worst single trade at the maximum multiplier of the carried cell, worst day and worst week in dollars. Causality check: every feature is computed from data with label <= entry minute - 1; automated poison test (25 random rows: all data from the entry minute onward replaced by noise, feature recomputed, must be unchanged) stored in the report. Library: the carried cell's daily pair-variant series saved with lib_save.save_book (family BK<n>, netting PAIR_ACCT, tag pair-variant). Pre-registration prior: probability of FOUND for each family <= 3% (expected false FOUND across ten families ~0.1-0.3 at the 200-replicate null).
- BK1 (item 59) SINGLE-NAME SHOCK PAUSE. Mechanism: a >6-sigma 5-minute move in one mega-cap (earnings, news) makes NQ's next 15-30 min headline-driven; the trend books whipsaw. Data: XNAS.ITCH 1-min closes of the ten year-start top names (AAPL MSFT NVDA AMZN META GOOGL AVGO TSLA COST NFLX), 2022-01..2026-10-13 (data/basket via outputs/autopilot/basket/dense_itch.npz); z5 = ln(C_t/C_{t-5}) / (sd1 x sqrt5), sd1 = mean of the previous 20 sessions' 1-minute return sd; shock = max over names |z5| > thr, evaluated at minutes 09:35-15:59 only; entries (any session) within W minutes after the shock minute are halved/skipped (cash hours only, entries outside RTH unaffected). Grid: thr {6, 8} x W {15, 30} x {HALF, SKIP} = 8 cells. Neighbours: thr x0.75/x1.25, W x0.75/x1.25 (4). Prior 2%.
- BK2 (item 57) QQQ-vs-NQ BASIS-DRIFT REGIME. Mechanism: when the futures-minus-cash spread has been drifting (arbitrage pressure / carry / dividend), index flow is dominated by basis traders and the trend books face mean-reverting prints. State: e_d = ln(NQ_adj close 15:59 d / NQ_adj close 15:59 d-1) - ln(QQQ close 15:59 d / QQQ close 15:59 d-1) per ET date (|e_d| > 0.5% treated as roll/split artefact = missing); E_L = (sum of e over the L dates strictly before the entry's ET calendar date) / (sd_60 x sqrt L) with sd_60 the trailing 60-date sd; terciles frozen on clean; schemes HIGH_DOWN (top tercile |E| x0.5), HIGH_UP (top tercile 1.5), TWO_V (bottom 1.25 / top 0.75), applied to |E_L|; L {5, 20}; 6 cells; direction-neutral. Neighbours: L x0.75/x1.25 and tercile share 25%/42% (4). Prior 2%.
- BK3 (item 58) INTRA-NDX DISPERSION (SEMIS VS SOFTWARE). Mechanism: when semis and software diverge intraday, NQ carries a rotation, not a trend; the 1-min trend signal is mostly relative-value noise. State at entry (RTH entries 10:30-15:59 only): spread series s_t = mean r(NVDA, AVGO, AMD) - mean r(MSFT, ADBE) per minute; feature ABS = |sum s over last 60 min| / (sd_s x sqrt60); VOL = ln( sqrt(sum s^2 last 60) / sqrt(60 x mean s^2 over the previous 20 sessions) ); terciles frozen on clean; schemes HIGH_DOWN / HIGH_UP / TWO_V; 2 features x 3 schemes = 6 cells. Neighbours: window 45/75 min, tercile share 25/42% (4). Prior 2%.
- BK4 (item 60 + E6) CONCENTRATION REGIME. Mechanism: when the index is carried by a few names (high top-10 variance share, high dispersion), NQ's 1-minute path is single-name flow and the pair's trend edge differs from broad-index days. States at the previous session close (causal): S10 = ln( Var(EQ10 1-min return, trailing 20 sessions) / Var(QQQ 1-min return, same) ); DISP = mean over trailing 20 sessions of the cross-sectional sd of the ten names' daily close-to-close returns. Terciles frozen on clean; HIGH_DOWN / HIGH_UP / TWO_V; 2 x 3 = 6 cells. Diagnostic (not a gate): rank correlation of the 13-week rolling pair Sharpe with each state over 2023-02..2026-10. Neighbours: window 15/25 sessions, tercile share 25/42% (4). Prior 2%.
- BK5 (item 61) BREADTH-THRUST DAY, LONGER TARGETS. Mechanism: when >= 8 of the ten names are up (or <= 2) versus the prior close at 10:30 ET the index is in a one-sided institutional programme; the trend books' 60-pt target is too near. Rule: on thrust days, RTH entries after 10:30 whose side equals the thrust direction get target x m (stop fixed 60), or size x1.25 (alternative action); other entries unchanged. Grid: n_up threshold {8, 9} (down: <= 2, <= 1) x action {target 1.25, target 1.5, target 2.0, size 1.25} = 8 cells. Neighbours: threshold +-1, multiplier x0.75/x1.25 on the multiplier-above-1 (4). Prior 3%.
- BK6 (item 54) GEX-SIGN BRACKET SCALING. Mechanism: negative dealer gamma amplifies moves (wider swings: a 60-pt stop is inside the noise, targets are reached faster), positive gamma damps them (tighter ranges): scale stop AND target by a (negative GEX) / b (positive GEX). State: gex_norm of the PREVIOUS trading date (outputs/autopilot/opt/feat_NQ.parquet, NQ options open interest, 2019-2026), sign cut at 0. Grid (a,b): (1.25,1), (1,0.8), (1.25,0.8), (1.5,1), (1,0.67), (1.5,0.67) = 6 cells. Neighbours: a-1 and b-1 each x0.75 / x1.25 (4). Prior 3%. (Sizing is not changed; contract count constant, so risk per trade scales with the stop.)
- BK7 (items 49 + 53) PIN/CLUSTER STRIKE AS TARGET CAP. Mechanism: the largest 0DTE strike-volume cluster acts as a magnet/barrier (dealer hedging, round-number order concentration); a target beyond it is rarely reached. State (RTH entries only, 0DTE era 2022+): from outputs of data/autopilot/opt0dte/min NDXP *_strk.parquet (bucket exp_lab == lab, all completed 30-min slots of the entry date before the entry's slot), pin = strike with the largest cumulative 0DTE traded size (calls + puts), NQ-equivalent level = K + (S_NQ - Fp) with S_NQ the harness NQ close at the entry minute - 1 and Fp the put-call-parity forward of the last available minute record of the entry date's NDXP file (basis fixed per date); cluster2 = second-largest strike. Rule: if a pin lies ahead of the entry in the trade direction at distance D with dmin <= D < 60, the target becomes max(D - buf, 8) pts (stop 60 fixed); else unchanged. Grid: dmin {10, 20} x buf {0, 5} x pin definition {top1, nearest of top2} = 8 cells. Neighbours: dmin x0.75/x1.25, buf +-5 clipped at 0 (4). Prior 2%.
- BK8 (item 55) LATE-DAY FADE TO THE 0DTE PIN (standalone sleeve on NQ). Mechanism: 0DTE hedging and pinning pull the index toward the largest-volume strike in the last 2 hours. Signal at entry time T in {14:00, 14:30} ET each RTH day: pin as BK7 (top1, cumulative 0DTE size through the last complete slot, NQ-equivalent); if |P - pin| >= x then fade toward the pin: long if P < pin, short if P > pin; x in {0.15, 0.25} x ADR20 (previous 20 RTH sessions' high-low mean, NQ points); target = pin level (touch), stop = k x x with k in {1, 2} from entry, time exit 15:55 ET; 1 trade/day max; entry at the next 1-minute open + 1 tick, stop-market with 1 tick slippage, $4.36 per contract round trip; size so a full stop = $9,600 at standard (library) size. Grid 2x2x2 = 8 cells. Selection on clean standalone weekly Sharpe with >= 60 clean trades; additive framing at 10/20/30/50% (sleeve scaled to the pair's clean weekly sd; share = fraction of pair risk), primary share 20% for the +0.10 test on clean/H1/H2, worst week of the combination not worse than the pair's by more than the share x |pair worst week|; own null: the same days' trade parameters (direction, distance, stop, target) reassigned to random other RTH days (200 replicates), p95 on standalone Sharpe; bootstrap; plateau x +-25% (x, k, T +-15 min); 2x slippage; worst-decile corr with the pair <= +0.30; cross-instrument: ES via SPX/SPXW 0DTE strike tables (ES_* files hold ES.OPT only, so the SPX family is mapped to ES with the same rule) if the NQ cell reaches LEAD; ERA: not testable pre-2022. Small-sample flag < 100 trades in the pair window. Prior 3%.
- BK9 (item 50) 0DTE PUT/CALL PREMIUM IMBALANCE ALIGNMENT. Mechanism: when 0DTE put premium traded dominates call premium over the last 30-60 min the index is being hedged/sold; trend entries aligned with the dominant side carry more follow-through. State (RTH entries 10:00-15:59 only): NDXP bucket bk=0, minute records m < entry minute: PCI_L = (sum prp - sum prc) / (sum prp + sum prc) over the last L minutes; feature F = side x (-PCI) (positive = trade side aligned with call-heavy tape); terciles frozen on clean; schemes COUNTER_DOWN (bottom tercile x0.5), ALIGN_UP (top tercile 1.5), TWO (0.75/1.25); L {30, 60}; 6 cells. Neighbours: L x0.75/x1.25, tercile share 25/42% (4). Prior 2%. (OPT0DTE cell 2 tilted by ET hour of unsigned flow; this tests side-aligned premium imbalance and is not a duplicate.)
- BK10 (item 76) MEGA-CAP EARNINGS NIGHTS. Mechanism: after an after-close release by one of the ten names (EDGAR 8-K item 2.02 acceptance >= 16:00 ET, data/gap/edgar) Globex NQ is gap-and-reverse driven by the single name; the trend books' overnight entries are low quality. Rule: entries from 16:00 ET of the release date until scope end are halved/skipped; scope A = until 02:59 ET next calendar day, scope B = until 09:29 ET next calendar day. Grid: scope {A, B} x {HALF, SKIP} x names {all ten, AAPL+MSFT+NVDA+AMZN+GOOGL+META (six largest)} = 8 cells. Neighbours: scope end +-60 min, and names set swap (4). Eligibility 1.5-45% touched share replaced by minimum 40 flagged pair rows in the pair window (earnings are sparse; below 100 = small-sample flag). Prior 2%.
- BK AMENDMENT 1 (2026-10-17, before any scored BK run; made after looking only at the distribution of pin-minus-price distances, no P&L): the BK7/BK8 pin is the strike with the largest cumulative 0DTE traded size AMONG STRIKES WITHIN 100 NQ points of the NDXP put-call-parity forward at the entry (the unrestricted maximum sat a median 164 points from price, i.e. far-OTM lottery strikes, and only 3% of clean entries had a pin within the 60-pt target; with the band 10% do). 'top2' = second-largest within the band. Distance in NQ points = K - Fp (Fp = median forward of the bk0 records in the 5 minutes before the entry; Fp is NDX-equivalent, so no futures basis or back-adjust offset is needed). Nothing else changes.

## BK11 (coordinator follow-ups, registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any scored BK11 run)
Part 1 SPLIT AUDIT (analysis only, no selection): list every code path/report that read the raw basket panel (outputs/autopilot/basket/dense_itch.npz) and state whether it compares prices across sessions (split-sensitive) or only within a session; confirm empirically (max intraday 1-min return, list of cross-day |ret|>40% = split dates). A verdict is re-run only if a cross-session comparison was unguarded. Part 2 CROSS-INSTRUMENT HARNESS: ES/RTY pair-row books from d1_f4_core (ST+HM, ADR20 k=0.75, live account rules, 1 tick/side) as used by J7V; a BK overlay of the weight type (HALF/SKIP/tail-tilt) is applied to ES/RTY entries by the same entry minute, ET date, side; tercile edges are FROZEN from the NQ pair's clean rows (never re-fit on ES/RTY); null = 200 replicates of the feature permuted within (book x month x session) blocks, exact account re-runs; replication criterion (declared): same sign on both instruments and mean uplift >= 50% of the NQ clean uplift, counted as ONE of the >= 2 replications. Bracket-type overlays (BK5, BK6, BK7) need an ES/RTY 1-second replay and are NOT supported (declared limitation). Validation of the harness: run the selected cells of BK1, BK2, BK3, BK4, BK9, BK10 report-only (all failed the NQ gates, so no verdict changes). Items among the 26 skipped whose reason was 'harness lacked X': none qualifies (reasons were: covered, duplicate, no data on disk, calendar not on disk, L2 queue model); stated in the report.

## BATCH BI, registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any BI run (full text of the ideas, keyword search and frozen grids: IDEAS_BI.md; gates as AUTOPILOT.md and Gate Amendment 2, 200 replicates, no gate softened; selection clean-only; H1/H2 once)
## Grids, frozen (ten runs)
Pipeline for BI1-BI8: the SW sleeve pipeline (clean-only selection: >= 100 clean trades (MINTR 60 for the daily-cadence BI4-BI8, small-sample flag < 100), weekly Sharpe > 0 in BOTH clean halves, top 10 per instrument; H1/H2 read once; own random-entry null (200 replicates on the selected cell, 200 replicates of the whole-grid false-selection test); additive framing at 10/20/30/50% with bootstrap, worst-decile corr, plateau +-25%, 2x slippage, ERA flag with gross / net fixed / net price-scaled cost; instruments ES NQ RTY YM (cross-instrument replication is built in: a cell that is a LEAD on one instrument is read on the others in the table); sizing: 1 contract scaled to the pair's clean weekly sigma). Fill: signal at bar close, entry next 1-min open, 1 tick/side + $4.36/contract; time exits at the open of the exit minute. Replication leg: H0 2023-02..07 and alternating-month split computed for any LEAD by ap_bi_rep (reported when needed).
- BI1: slots start {09:30,10:00,...,15:00} (13) x K prior days {1,5} (mean of the same-slot 30-min open-to-close return) x dir {with, against}; hold 30 min; 52 cells. Neighbours: K x0.75/x1.25 (rounded).
- BI2: leg RTH (entry 09:30, sign of the prior Globex return 18:00-09:29; hold {120 min, flat 16:55}) or ON (entry 18:00, sign of the prior RTH return 09:30-15:59; hold until 09:25 = 925 min) x threshold {0, 0.15 x ATR20 of RTH range} x dir {with, against}; 12 cells. Neighbours: threshold x0.75/x1.25, hold x0.75/x1.25 (RTH 120 only).
- BI3: L {30,60,120} min x thr {1.5,2.5} (|own_move - driver_move| in own sigma units, move z as ap_sw_common.own_move/driver_move; driver ES for NQ/RTY/YM, NQ for ES) x H {15,30,60} x dir {against, with}; crossing events only; 36 cells. Neighbours: L, thr, H x0.75/x1.25.
- BI4: day volume (label-day sum) percentile among the previous N label days {20,60} >= p {80,90} x mode {long, with-day-sign, against-day-sign}; enter next RTH open, hold flat; 12 cells. Neighbours: p +-10 points (70/90, 80/100 clipped) and N x0.75/x1.25.
- BI5: level = prior ISO-week high (long break / short fade) or low (short break / long fade); event = first 1-min close beyond level +- b x ATR20(range) during RTH 09:35-15:30; b {0,0.1} x dir {with, against} x hold {60,120,flat}; 12 cells. Neighbours: b x0.75/x1.25 (b=0: +0.05), hold x0.75/x1.25.
- BI6: driver {RTY,YM} (target ES/NQ; ES,YM for RTY; RTY,NQ for YM) prior label-day RTH return z (/ 20d sd) with |z| >= th {0,0.5} x dir {with, against} x hold {flat, 120 min}; entry next RTH open; 16 cells. Neighbours: th x0.75/x1.25 (0.5 only), hold x0.75/x1.25.
- BI7: anchor {1st trading day of month, first trading day on/after the 15th} x offset k {-1,0,+1} trading days x hold {flat (open to close), 2 days} x dir {long, short}; 24 cells. Neighbours: hold only (2d -> 1.5 not defined; offset +-1 as neighbours).
- BI8: range/ATR20 of the previous label day >= x {1.5,2.0} x mode {long, with-day-sign, against-day-sign} x hold {flat, 2 days}; 12 cells. Neighbours: x x0.75/x1.25.
- BI9 (pair overlay 1): states at the entry (strictly before): ON = (price at entry minute-1 / last RTH close - 1) / ATR20-of-RTH-range-in-%; RTHP = (last completed RTH close / its open - 1) / same; features side x ON and side x RTHP; terciles frozen on clean; schemes COUNTER_DOWN / ALIGN_UP / TWO; 6 cells; ES/RTY cross-leg via ap_bk_xi-style harness. Neighbours: share 25/42% and ATR window 15/25.
- BI10 (pair overlay 2): features g1 = -ln(minutes since the previous entry of the same book), g2 = -ln(minutes since the previous entry of either book), g3 = -ln(minutes since the previous entry in the opposite direction) (all from earlier rows only); tercile edges frozen on clean; schemes HIGH_DOWN (short-gap tercile x0.5) / HIGH_UP (x1.5) / TWO_V; 9 cells; ES/RTY cross-leg likewise. Neighbours: share 25/42%.
Priors: each <= 3% for FOUND. Expected false FOUND across ten ~0.1-0.3.

## BATCH 5 (registered 2026-10-17 before any batch-5 runner launched; 200 replicates; no purchases; <= 8 processes)
### Part 1: COST DECOMPOSITION AND PASSIVE-FILL EXECUTION TEST (no new signal selection)
Objects (fixed, already identified in earlier batches; their selection is not repeated): B7 GC cell (5m ST(14,2.0) fade, 240 m, no stop, day) = 'GCB7'; Q2 RB and HO cells (5m Donchian-20 fade, 180 m, day) = 'RBQ2', 'HOQ2'; Q14 HO (5m squeeze(12,4.0) fade 120 m day) and GC (squeeze(12,3.0) fade 240 m day) = 'HOQ14', 'GCQ14'; Q12 NQ (15m Bollinger(20,2.5) fade 120 m day) = 'NQQ12'; Q1B NQ (10m ST(14,2.0) WITH, 180 m, all) = 'NQQ1B'; Q20 RTY/ES (15m prior-close distance 0.75 ADR fade 240 m day) = 'RTYQ20', 'ESQ20'; Q29 CL (London->NY fade, 120 m) = 'CLQ29'. Cost decomposition only (no passive test, multi-leg or non-time exits): Q26 momentum (3 cells), Q25 NG, B11 CL OR15 fade.
(1a) Cost decomposition per round trip for every object over 2013-22 (RTY 2017-22), clean and H1+H2: trades, gross $/trade, slippage $/trade (2 sides x 1 tick), commission $/trade, net $/trade, cost as % of gross, breakeven slippage per side in ticks (gross/(2 tick $) after commission), and the largest possible passive saving (entry tick only: 1 tick/side x 1 side).
(1b) 1-minute passive-entry replay, all time-exit objects, all instruments (bars only): at the signal bar close place a LIMIT at the signal level P (the signal row close) improved by d ticks in our favour, d in {0,1}; active for W minutes after a 1-minute latency, W in {5,15}; fill models: touch (q0: low <= P for a buy), through1 (price trades 1 tick through), q50 (touch fills kept with probability 0.5, deterministic hash of the order; through fills always kept), q100 (through1 required, same as through1 but also demands the bar close not above P: i.e. strictly through); the entry price is P (no slippage) and commission applies; exit = the same absolute time exit as the market-order version (open of the row after the hold), market exit with 1 tick adverse slippage; unfilled orders are missed trades (counted as zero); one position at a time. Variants per object = 2 d x 2 W x 3 fill models (touch, q50, through1) = 12; with 12 objects the family has 144 variants. 
(1c) Family null: for each object 40 timing-destroyed event sets (same count, random valid rows, alternating sides) are run through the SAME passive machinery and the market machinery; the object's passive-minus-market net per signal is compared with the null distribution of that difference (p95), and standalone pair-window Sharpe of the passive sleeve against the null p95; a variant is a RESCUE only if (i) per-signal net improves versus market and beats the null p95 of the improvement, (ii) passive sleeve uplift at 20% >= +0.10 on clean, H1 and H2 (vol-matched with the market version's clean sigma), (iii) fill rate >= 50% and the missed-trade net (what the unfilled signals would have earned at market) is reported (adverse-selection check), (iv) it also holds at the 'through1' fill model. The Bonferroni-style count of 144 variants is stated next to the result.
(1d) Tick replay on actual trades (data/databento NQ and ES trades 2023-02..2026-10; other instruments are data-gated: no trade prints on disk): objects NQQ12, NQQ1B, ESQ20; fill models through1 / q0 / q50 / q100 as in ap_fv_fill.py (queue ahead = trailing 15-minute volume at the price), latency 1 minute, W in {5,15}, d in {0,1}; exits market at the first print after the hold time with 1 tick adverse; null 20 timing-destroyed event sets per object in the same pass.
### Part 2: ten new families (keyword-searched against LEDGER/GAPMAP; dropped as duplicates: VX vol-of-vol (J8/PY/K4V2/RW12P), variance-ratio pair regime (C6-N1), QQQ-constituent dispersion (BK3/BK4/BASKET), outside-day by close location (Z5/R7), overnight gap/range pair filters (SW4/PY7/PY10/BI9), first-hour range tilt (PX25), weekly compression (Z2), weekend-gap family (U3/PA8)). Clean-only selection, standard gates, ERA flag, library save; sleeves 1 contract vol-matched; time exits unless stated.
- BQ1 (overlay on the live pair, ap_bk harness copy ap_bq_run.py; features lagged one trading day; selected on clean among 6 eligible cells, then H1/H2 once, 200 replicate null, exact account re-runs; ES/RTY legs through ap_bq_xi.py): cross-sectional dispersion across the 26 outrights = 5-day mean of the cross-sectional SD of ADR20-scaled daily returns (feature A) and the 20-day average pairwise correlation of daily returns (feature B); cells {A, B} x {HIGH_DOWN, HIGH_UP, TWO_V} tilt (tercile edges frozen on clean rows). Prior 3%.
- BQ2 (second overlay): realised skew (A) and excess kurtosis (B) of NQ 1-minute returns over the previous 5 sessions; same six cells. Prior 3%.
- Q31 weekly time-series momentum/reversal switched by the variance-ratio regime: for each of 14 outrights (ES RTY YM GC CL ZN ZB 6E 6J HG SI NG HO RB), VR(5) of daily returns over {40,60,100} days; if VR > 1 follow, if VR < 1 fade the previous {1,4}-week return; hold one week (Monday open to Friday 15:00 ET), equal ADR risk; 6 cells; portfolio daily P&L.
- Q32 morning-range state -> afternoon: at 11:00 ET (instrument-session adjusted) range since the RTH open / ADR20 in {<0.4 narrow, >0.8 wide}; trade the afternoon WITH or AGAINST the open-to-11:00 direction (narrow day: breakout expectation; wide day: exhaustion), hold {120, to 15:00}; 8 instruments x cells; selection min 100 trades. Prior 2%.
- Q33 intraday time-series momentum on non-index markets: sign of (RTH open -> window end) return (window end 10:30/11:30/12:30) if >= k x ADR20 (k in {0,0.15}), trade with/against until 15:00 (Gao-style, extended beyond C3-H1's NQ/ES/RTY first-30 -> last-30). 24 cells. Prior 2%.
- Q34 overnight-to-day: Globex return (18:00 -> RTH open - 1) sign if >= k x ADR20, trade the RTH day WITH/AGAINST, hold to 15:00 or 240 min; 24 cells. Prior 2%.
- Q35 prior-day-break conditioned on yesterday's range state (range/ADR20 <0.7 contraction, >1.3 expansion): B11 PD cells (tf {5,15}, mode with/fade, exits b1/tr a in {0.1,0.2}) x state: 32 cells. Prior 2%.
- Q36 opening-range breakout with relative-volume confirmation: B11 OR15/OR30 WITH, breakout bar volume / mean volume of the previous 20 bars >= {1.5, 2.5} (or no filter as control): 24 cells. Prior 2%.
- Q37 prior-WEEK high/low breakouts (entries on 15-min bar close beyond the prior ISO week's extreme, first per week), WITH/FADE, exits trail/bracket a in {0.2,0.3} ADR20: 16 cells. Prior 2%.
- Q38 time-at-extreme: a 5-minute bar makes a new day-session high (low) after the previous extreme was N bars ago (N in {6,12,24}); WITH/AGAINST; hold {60,120,180}: 36 cells. Prior 2%.
Look-ahead rule: every feature uses data completed before the decision row; daily features are lagged one label day; the ap_bq overlays use strictly earlier label days than the entry's ET date.
- BATCH 5 amendments (before any Part-2 runner launched): Q38 grid has 18 cells (3 N x 3 holds x 2 directions), Q37 has 8 cells (15-min bars only, 2 modes x 4 exits), Q32 16 cells (narrow k in {0.3,0.4}, wide k in {0.8,1.0}, hold {120,240}, 2 directions; decision 90 minutes after the RTH open). Instruments for Q32-Q38: ES NQ RTY GC CL ZN HO 6E SI HG. Selection minimum trades: 60 for Q32, Q35, Q36; 40 for Q37; 100 otherwise. Part-1 variants: 12 per object with 1-min replay (as registered) plus 16 tick-replay variants for NQQ12, NQQ1B, ESQ20 (4 bounds x W{5,15} x d{0,1}).
