## BATCH BJ, registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any BJ run (full text IDEAS_BJ.md)
# IDEAS_BJ - batch BJ (worker BK, 2026-10-17), registered BEFORE any BJ run
Source: ideas 11-25 of IDEAS_BI.md that were not run were checked again (all covered by existing reports: flip-conditioned holds C2-G1/J0A, VIX slope N3/PX34, VRP K9, pre-FOMC G3/N2, pre-holiday X1, first30-last30 C3-H1, semivariance FV16, lag-1 autocorr SW23, volume spike SW6, ORB, NR7, Connors) and are NOT repeated. Ten NEW mechanisms (keyword search over LEDGER, GAPMAP*, BACKLOG*, IDEAS_*, PREREG, every REPORT_*: 0 hits unless noted):
1. BJ1 FOMC-cycle weeks: the equity premium is earned in even weeks (0,2,4,6) of the FOMC cycle (Cieslak-Morse-Vissing-Jorgensen 2019). Sign: LONG in even weeks, flat/short in odd.
2. BJ2 Power-hour momentum: the return into 15:00/15:30 predicts the last 25-55 minutes (institutional close-demand / MOC imbalance, Heston-Korajczyk-Sadka 2010 style, here conditioned on the prior 30-90 min rather than the first 30 min, which C3-H1 tested). Sign: WITH.
3. BJ3 RTH-open magnet: price displaced from the day's RTH open by >= x ADR at 11:00/13:00 reverts toward the open (opening price as anchor, George-Hwang anchoring at the daily scale). Sign: AGAINST.
4. BJ4 Open location vs the prior-day range: an RTH open beyond the prior day's high/low (outside the prior range) continues (acceptance/rejection of value, Dalton; distinct from the open-vs-prior-close gap sleeve I2). Sign: WITH.
5. BJ5 Prior-day close minus RTH VWAP: closing above the day's VWAP means late-day net buying by VWAP-benchmarked institutions that continues next day (Berkowitz-Logue-Noser VWAP benchmark pressure). Sign: WITH.
6. BJ6 VIX-settlement Wednesday: SOQ/VIX settlement-related option flows move SPX/NQ around the Wednesday settlement of the monthly VIX future (Griffin-Shams 2018). No sign prior; small-sample by construction (12/yr).
7. BJ7 Lunar cycle: returns are higher around new moons than full moons (Yuan-Zheng-Zhu 2006, mood). Sign: LONG new-moon window, weaker full-moon window.
8. BJ8 Month-of-year: Nov-Apr returns exceed May-Oct (Bouman-Jacobsen 2002 'sell in May'). Sign: LONG Nov-Apr. (C2-G4 excluded the indices.)
9. BJ9 Opening drive: the first 5-minute bar direction (09:30-09:34) continues over the next 30-120 min (order-imbalance persistence at the open). Sign: WITH. (Distinct from FOMC first-5-minute tests.)
10. BJ10 Globex volume share (informed after-hours trading, Barclay-Hendershott 2003): when overnight volume is high relative to its 20-day mean the overnight price move is informed and continues in RTH. Sign: WITH on high-volume nights.
## Machinery and protocol (frozen)
SW sleeve pipeline clone (ap_bj_*), instruments ES NQ RTY YM, clean-only selection (>= 100 clean trades; MINTR 60 for BJ5/BJ8 daily rows with < 100, MINTR 20 for BJ6 flagged small-sample so it cannot be FOUND; weekly Sharpe > 0 in both clean halves), H1/H2 once, 200-replicate false-selection test and 200-replicate own null on the selected cell, additive framing at 10/20/30/50% (+0.10 on clean/H1/H2, worst week), bootstrap, plateau, 2x slippage, worst-decile corr, ERA flag with cost decomposition, library save, replication leg (ap_bi_rep: H0, alternating months, other instruments). NEW (from REPORT_BI8B): the random-entry null is DRIFT- AND TIME-OF-DAY-MATCHED: the random entries are drawn from the same clock minute(s) as the rule (valid mask = the rule's signal clock) and take the same long/short mix as the real events, so a long-only rule must beat random long-only entries at the same clock and hold (the old alternating-side null let long-beta pass). Fill: next 1-min open, 1 tick/side + $4.36/contract, entry/exit clocks as stated.
## Grids
- BJ1: parity {even weeks 0,2,4,6; odd weeks 1,3,5,7 after the last FOMC statement date, dates: 2023-26 verified, 2013-22 from memory (unverified, flagged)} x dir {long, short} x hold {RTH open to 16:55; close-to-close (15:55 entry, next day 15:55 exit)}; 8 cells.
- BJ2: entry {15:00, 15:30} x prior-return window L {30,60,90} x dir {with, against}; time exit 15:55; 12 cells. Neighbours L x0.75/x1.25.
- BJ3: clock {11:00, 13:00} x |displacement from the RTH open| >= x {0.3, 0.6} ADR20 x hold {60,120} x dir {against, with}; 16 cells. Neighbours x, hold x0.75/x1.25.
- BJ4: buffer b {0, 0.1} ADR20 beyond prior-day high/low (price at 09:29) x dir {with, against} x hold {120, flat}; 8 cells.
- BJ5: prior-day (close - RTH VWAP)/ADR20 >= th {0, 0.05} x dir {with, against} x hold {120, flat}; 8 cells. Neighbours th, hold.
- BJ6: day offset to the VIX settlement Wednesday {-1,0,+1} x dir {long, short} x hold {RTH, close-to-close}; 12 cells.
- BJ7: window {new moon, full moon} x half-width w {2, 3.5} days x dir {long, short} x hold {RTH, close-to-close}; 16 cells. Phase from the synodic month 29.530588853 d from the 2000-01-06 18:14 UTC new moon.
- BJ8: season {Nov-Apr, May-Oct} x dir {long, short} x hold {RTH, close-to-close}; 8 cells.
- BJ9: first-bar threshold x {0, 0.03} ADR20 x dir {with, against} x hold {30, 60, 120}; entry 09:35; 12 cells.
- BJ10: Globex (18:00-09:29) volume / its prior-20-day mean >= q {1.2, 1.5} x dir {with the overnight move, against} x hold {120, flat}; entry 09:30; 8 cells.
Priors <= 3% each; expected false FOUND ~0.1-0.3.

## BATCH SE (SE1: pair ENTRY-EXECUTION TIMING on the 1-second order-level engine; GAPMAP D01/D02 H1-H2 M6 NQ), registered 2026-10-17T11:06Z BEFORE any scored SE run. Only the control was run (market replay of the live signals on the 1-second engine reproduces the live account: clean 1.46, H1 2.83 weekly Sharpe, 7,346 vs 7,361 accepted trades; H2 on this axis 2.48 because the span runs to 2026-10-16, the 2.30 quoted elsewhere ends 2026-10-13). Clean process (ap_k96_core -> ap_k_lib -> ap_g11_c_core; b10_core is not imported before b11_nt8). Engine: ap_sw_exec.py (acct_se order-level account, per-second SC/SH/SL, second volume SV from outputs/b10/h2root/bars2 verified aligned to SS), disk use < 1 GB (guard 60 GB).
- Mechanism: the live strategy sends a market order at the next 1-min open after the bar-close signal; real emission-to-fill latency is not zero (outputs/h2/emission_latency_sep24_oct15.csv.gz is the model-emission latency, not the NT8 fill latency). Questions: what does latency cost, can a limit order at the signal-bar close or a sub-minute order-flow confirmation recover edge.
- Cells (11 selectable): delay market d = 1/5/15/30 s after the open (fill = close of the d-th second, then 1 tick/side in the account); limit at the signal-bar close -o ticks (o 0/1), resting W = 15/60 s from the open, no chase, unfilled = missed trade; flow-confirmed market after C = 5/15/30 s only if the tick-rule signed volume of those C seconds (sum sign(dSC) x SV, SV = traded volume per second) agrees with the side. Diagnostics (not selectable): delay 60 and 120 s, for the cost-of-delay curve. Limit fills are charged the engine's slippage like market fills (conservative; the no-slippage credit is reported as a diagnostic line).
- Selection: best clean weekly-Sharpe uplift of the 11 cells; H1/H2 read once. Gates: uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2, worst week not worse, own timing-destroyed null (200 replicates: entry at a uniformly random second inside the entry bar for delay cells; same-count random skip at the cell's mean delay for limit/flow cells) p95 on clean and all-in, bootstrap CI > 0, plateau +-25% (d, W, C; limit offset has no +-25% neighbour) >= 75%, 2x slippage >= +0.10 each span, alternating months, H0 2023-02-10..07-31 (replication legs counted as in K96). Framings: pair-change (execution replaces the live fill) with insurance metrics; the constant-risk reading is the same trades at the same size, so the INSURANCE flags (M.score: cost %, CVaR5/maxDD/worst-week improvements) are the constant-risk statement. Cost of delay in $ per trade (all-in net difference / accepted trades, 8 lots) and the break-even delay (delay at which a linear fit of net P&L vs delay reaches zero; and the delay at which all-in weekly Sharpe falls to 1.0) are reported for the delay curve 0/1/5/15/30/60/120 s.
- Prior 2% for a FOUND (a delay cannot help by construction; the limit and flow cells can only help if the first seconds after a flip mean-revert). Pool of 5 workers x 2 at most (10 processes).

## BATCH BM, registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any BM run (full text IDEAS_BM.md)
# IDEAS_BM - batch BM (worker BK, 2026-10-17), registered BEFORE any BM run
Ten literature mechanisms with no hit (keyword search over LEDGER, GAPMAP*, BACKLOG*, IDEAS_*, PREREG, every REPORT_*) for the key terms: leveraged-ETF rebalancing, Amihud illiquidity, Faber/SMA-200 timing, weekly reversal, monthly reversal, ADR exhaustion, high/low time-order, up-day fraction, daily z-score reversion, overnight persistence. Excluded as covered: pivot points (12 hits), roll-week (15), run-length (17), key reversal/outside day (5), hourly boundaries (7), Friday-Monday (9), XS intraday momentum (2 hits, BASKET/XS), volume spike (SW6).
1. BM1 LETF end-of-day rebalancing (Cheng-Madhavan 2009; Tuzun 2013): leveraged/inverse index ETFs must buy after up days and sell after down days into the close, in proportion to the day's return; sign WITH the day's move since the prior close, entry 15:20/15:30/15:40 ET, exit 15:55. Indices only (ES NQ RTY YM).
2. BM2 Amihud illiquidity day (Amihud 2002; liquidity-provision premium): after a day of high |return|/volume the next day earns a premium (long) or reverts; general (8 instruments).
3. BM3 Faber timing (Faber 2007): long when the prior close is above its 100/200-day SMA, or short below; general. The classic drift-sensitive rule; the matched null is the test.
4. BM4 Weekly reversal (Lehmann 1990; Jegadeesh 1990): the prior week's return reverses at the start of the week; sign AGAINST; general.
5. BM5 Monthly reversal/continuation at month start (Jegadeesh 1990; Heston-Sadka 2008 seasonal return persistence): first 1-3 trading days of the month against/with the prior month's return; general.
6. BM6 ADR exhaustion: after the label day's range so far exceeds 0.8-1.0 x ADR20 by 12:00/13:30 ET the move is spent (ranges mean-revert to their average; Parkinson range dynamics); sign AGAINST the day's direction; general.
7. BM7 High/low time-order: when the prior day printed its high before its low (distribution then sell-off) vs low first; informed-flow sequencing (Dalton/market-profile; Lo-Mamaysky-Wang pattern literature is about shapes, this is time order); sign WITH the late extreme (low-first = bullish); general.
8. BM8 Up-day fraction (Kaplanski-Levy / sentiment-persistence): fraction of up days in the last 5/10/20 days beyond 70/80% (30/20%) continues or reverses; general.
9. BM9 Daily z-score reversion to the 20-day mean (Poterba-Summers variance-ratio mean reversion; Bollinger at the daily scale): sign AGAINST for |z| >= 1.5/2.0; general.
10. BM10 Overnight persistence (Lou-Polk-Skouras tug-of-war, the overnight-to-overnight leg: overnight returns persist across nights; BI2 tested RTH->overnight and overnight->RTH): next Globex session (18:01-09:25) in the direction of the prior Globex return or prior close-to-close return; general.
## Protocol (frozen)
Machinery: ap_bj_pipe.py clone (ap_bm_pipe.py): SW sleeve pipeline with the drift- and clock-matched random-entry null (real long/short mix, the family's valid mask pins the clock), 200-replicate false-selection test and 200-replicate own null on the selected cell, clean-only selection (>= 100 clean trades, MINTR 60 for the daily-cadence BM2-BM9, small-sample flag < 100; weekly Sharpe > 0 in both clean halves), H1/H2 once, additive 10/20/30/50% (+0.10 on clean/H1/H2, worst week within the share), bootstrap, plateau +-25%, 2x slippage, worst-decile corr <= +0.30, ERA flag with cost decomposition, library save, replication leg (ap_bi_rep). Instruments: BM1 ES NQ RTY YM; BM2-BM10 ES NQ RTY YM GC CL ZN BTC (the 8 instruments with a conforming series file); the extension to the other outrights (6A 6B 6C HG HO LE NG NKD PL RB SI ZC ZF ZS ZT ZW HE ETH) is run for any family that reaches LEAD. Fill: next 1-min open, 1 tick/side + $4.36/contract; time exits at the open of the exit minute. Priors <= 3%.
## Grids
- BM1: entry T {15:20, 15:30, 15:40} x |price - prior RTH close| >= x {0.4, 0.7, 1.0} ADR20 x dir {with, against}; exit 15:55; 18 cells.
- BM2: prior-day |close-open|/volume percentile among the previous N {60,120} days >= p {80,90} x mode {long, with day sign, against day sign}; enter next RTH open, hold flat; 12 cells.
- BM3: SMA length N {100, 200} daily closes x mode {long above, short below} x hold {RTH, close-to-close}; 8 cells.
- BM4: prior-week return z (/ ADR20 x sqrt5) >= th {0, 0.5} x dir {with, against} x hold {RTH Monday, 2 days}; 8 cells.
- BM5: first k {1, 3} trading days of the month x dir {with, against the prior month's return} x hold {RTH, close-to-close}; 8 cells.
- BM6: clock T {12:00, 13:30} x range so far (label day high-low) >= x {0.8, 1.0} ADR20 x dir {against, with the move from the label-day open} x hold {60, 120}; 16 cells.
- BM7: prior-day extreme order x range filter f {0, 1.0} ADR20 x dir {with the late extreme, against} x hold {120, flat}; 8 cells.
- BM8: window N {5,10,20} x threshold {0.7, 0.8} (and 0.3, 0.2) x dir {with, against the majority}; enter RTH open, hold flat; 12 cells.
- BM9: |z| of prior close vs SMA20 / sd20 >= k {1.5, 2.0} x dir {against, with} x hold {RTH, 2 days}; 8 cells.
- BM10: state {prior Globex return, prior RTH close-to-close return} x threshold {0, 0.15} ADR20 x dir {with, against}; entry 18:01, exit 09:25; 8 cells.

## BATCH SE2 (fill-delay fragility analysis + latency-robust execution tolerances), registered 2026-10-17T11:18Z BEFORE any SE2 run (only the event-attributed account was built and checked: its R0 ledger sums to the daily series, weekly Sharpe 1.46/2.83/2.48/1.90, and the 15 s delay all-in 1.43 reproduces SE1). Engine: ap_sw_se2.py (copy of the K96 order-level account with an event id on every lot and row; clean process, baseline asserted 1.46/2.83 in the runner), same data and fill model as SE1; up to 10 processes; disk < 1 GB.
- PART 1 (analysis, no gates): for delays 5/15/30 s decompose the all-in net change vs the control. Ladder: R0 control; R1 = delayed in time but filled at the control's price (isolates acceptance / netting / bracket-path changes); R2 = fully delayed (R2-R1 = price-drift effect incl. re-centred brackets). R1-R0 is split by event status transitions (opened/netted/busy in each run) and, for events opened in both, by exit class (stop/target/close, same or changed); stop-before-target ordering inside a second is tested by re-running R0 and R2 with the tie broken target-first (the engine's rule is stop-first). Top 20 events by |delta|, concentration by hour of day, month and day; share of the total delta carried by the top 20 / top 1% events, and the total with them removed; sign test on events opened in both. Bootstrap of the Sharpe-vs-delay curve (delays 0/1/2/5/10/15/30/60 s): stationary block bootstrap over weeks of the all-in span (mean block 4 weeks, 1,000 draws, the same resampled weeks for every delay), reporting the CI of Sharpe(d) and of Sharpe(d)-Sharpe(0). Fill-second noise benchmark: 300 replicates of the control with a random 0/1/2 s delay per signal (mean 1 s); the position of delay-5/15/30 within that band.
- PART 2 (counted family of FIVE cells; pure execution tolerances; signal logic unchanged): TOL-N (N 2/4/8 ticks): at submission time (delay d) enter at market only if the fill is not worse than N ticks versus the signal-bar close, else skip; CAP-N (N 4/8 ticks, rest W=30 s): like TOL-N but an unfilled order rests as a limit at the tolerance price for 30 s and fills if touched. Each cell is evaluated at delay d = 1/5/15 s against the delayed MARKET baseline of the same delay (uplift = cell Sharpe minus delayed-market Sharpe). Selection: highest mean clean uplift over d = 1, 5, 15 (clean only); H1/H2 read once. Gates at EACH of d=5 and d=15 (d=1 informational): uplift >= +0.10 clean/H1/H2 vs the delayed market, worst week not worse, own null p95 (200 random same-rate skips of the delayed market at the same delay) on clean and all-in, bootstrap CI > 0, plateau +-25% on N (4 neighbours incl. W for CAP) >= 75%, 2x slippage >= +0.10 each span, alternating months, H0. A cell that passes only by recovering the delayed market while staying below the undelayed live Sharpe is reported as such (a tolerance cannot recover the undelayed edge unless it also beats d=0). Prior 3%.
- PART 3: latency gate and the live measurement that would settle it, derived from the curve and the bootstrap; no test.

## BL3 (coordinator follow-ups after BM; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any BM11/BM12 run)
1. Q5ENS ensemble null with side-count-matched draws (ap_bl3_q5ens.py): for each of the 12 Q5 cells the 203 matched draws of ap_null_dm (same seeds as BL2), ensemble null = equal-risk book of draw j across cells (independent draws, understates the null), single-cell matched p95 and Bonferroni 98.75th percentile; verdict rule identical to the Q5ENS pre-registration (pass only if the real book exceeds the maximum of the three); legacy numbers recomputed on identical seeds.
2. BM1 deepening. BM11 = replication probe of the BM1 mechanism (fade of the move since the prior RTH close into the close), registered BEFORE running: instruments = ES NQ RTY YM plus every other outright with a conforming 1-minute series file (SI HG PL NG RB HO ZB ZF ZT 6E 6J 6B 6A 6C ZC ZS ZW LE HE and any of the others available, ETH); per-instrument RTH close C_i (ET minutes, ASSUMED settlement-window ends: equity indices and BTC/ETH 16:00; GC 13:30; SI 13:25; HG 13:00; PL 13:05; CL NG RB HO 14:30; ZN ZB ZF ZT 15:00; 6E 6J 6B 6A 6C 14:00; ZC ZS ZW 14:20; LE HE 14:05); exit E = C_i - 5 min; entry clocks E - {55, 45, 35, 25} min (for the equity indices 15:00, 15:10, 15:20, 15:30); signal |price - prior RTH close| >= x ADR20, x in {0.4, 0.7}; direction AGAINST only (the BM1 direction); 8 cells per instrument; the same pipeline, drift- and clock-matched null, 200 replicates, clean-only selection; the probe counts in the null through the pipeline's per-instrument false-selection test and through a family-level count: number of instruments with a gate-3 cell at 20% versus the chance rate 5.88% per clean-selected cell (SW chance calibration) -> binomial p.
   BM12 = reference test of BM1 on NQ ES RTY YM (entry 15:20 fixed, direction AGAINST, x in {0.4, 0.7}, exit 15:55): reference r in {prior RTH close (BM1), prior RTH VWAP (typical-price volume-weighted over the prior 09:30-15:59), today's RTH open, today's RTH VWAP so far, VWAP of the 30 minutes before entry, 'gap only' = (RTH open - prior close)/ADR20 with the same entry}; 12 cells. Decision rule fixed in advance: END-OF-DAY REVERSAL if the today-open / RTH-VWAP / 30-min-VWAP references each give uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1 and H2 at 20% with null rank >= 0.95 on NQ; PRIOR-CLOSE ARTIFACT (gap/overnight component) if only the prior-close and gap-only references do; MIXED otherwise.
   Analysis only (no gates): overlap of BM1 with BJ2 (done: daily corr 0.05), with ST/HM entries at 15:20 (share of BM1 trades with an open pair position, same/opposite direction, correlation of BM1 trade P&L with the pair's concurrent P&L), and with close-auction pressure proxies (NQ volume share of 15:30-16:00 and of 15:20-15:55 versus the 20-day average; mean BM1 P&L by tercile of the realised last-30-min volume share, diagnostic because it is measured after entry, plus the pre-entry share of 14:50-15:20).
   Feasibility: BM1 NQ cell re-run at 1, 2 and 4 ticks per side (B_SLIPX 1, 2, 4; the pipeline's 1 tick is the base case), standalone and additive results at 20% and 50% share with insurance metrics; participation = contracts / median NQ volume per minute at 15:20-15:55; impact estimate with a square-root model I = 0.5-1.0 x sigma_min x sqrt(Q/V_min) per minute of exposure, reported as an equivalent in ticks.
- BL3 AMENDMENT 1 (2026-10-17, after BM12 and before BM13 ran; no P&L of the new cells seen): in BM12 the vwap30 reference produced ZERO events at x >= 0.4 (price is within 0.2 ADR of its own 30-minute VWAP), so the pre-registered decision rule cannot be evaluated for it. BM13 = same machinery and decision rule with ref in {vwap30, sess_vwap} x x in {0.1, 0.15, 0.25} ADR20 (12 cells), NQ ES RTY YM, entry 15:20, against, exit 15:55.

## BLOCK 'SD' (registered 2026-10-18 before any SD computation; no new selection; up to 12 processes; no purchases)
### SD-1 ES dip-buy (C11-DIP: ES RSI(2)<5 & close>SMA200, entry at the signal-bar close, exit after 5 trading days or a 3 x ATR20 stop, 16:00-ET daily bars, 1 tick/side + $4.36, extra adverse tick on stop fills) - broadest honest evidence
(a) Drift-matched random-date null: 5,000 worlds; each draws random entry days from the days on which close > SMA200 (the regime filter of the rule, so the long drift is matched), the same number of trades as the real rule has in the comparison window (windows: pair window 2023-08..2026-10; 2013-2026 full), identical exits (5-day hold, 3 x ATR20 stop, sequential, no overlap, same costs); statistics: total net $, mean net $/trade, win rate, weekly Sharpe of the daily P&L; one-sided p of the real value.
(b) Universe replication with identical parameters and no re-selection: ES, NQ, RTY (2017+), YM and the 26 outrights (6A 6B 6C 6E 6J CL ES GC HE HG HO LE NG NQ PL RB RTY SI YM ZB ZC ZF ZN ZS ZT ZW) on daily bars built from the 1-minute files in the same way (16:00-ET key, difference back-adjusted); per instrument and pooled: trades, win rate, mean $/trade in ATR units (net $ / (ATR20 x $/pt)), t-stat, 2013-22 and 2023-26.
(c) The same rule on ES 2013-2022 (ERA), with the cost decomposition.
(d) Bayesian posterior for the win rate: Beta prior set from the universe EXCLUDING ES (pooled win rate of the other markets, prior strength = 20 pseudo-trades, sensitivity 10 and 40); posterior for ES given its own wins/trades in (i) 2023-26 and (ii) 2013-26; report P(win rate > 0.5), the posterior mean and 90% interval; for expectancy (net $/trade in ATR units) a normal-normal posterior with the universe mean as prior and between-market variance from the universe. Minimum extra trades: the number of additional ES trades n such that, if the true win rate equals the posterior mean, the posterior P(win > 0.5) >= 0.95 with probability >= 80% (simulation), and the calendar years needed at the rule's trade frequency.
### SD-2 Q34 ES cell 1 (Globex return faded from the 09:30 open, hold 180 min, ES) and BM1 cell 1 (NQ 15:20 fade of >= 0.4 ADR20 move from the prior RTH close, exit 15:55)
(a) 2,000-world drift- and clock-matched nulls: the real event set (same days, entry clock, hold) is kept; the trade sides are re-assigned by random permutation preserving the number of long and short trades (drift match; clock match by construction); statistics: net $ per trade and weekly Sharpe in clean, H1, H2 and all-in; one-sided p of the real value. (b) Shifted-clock surfaces: Q34: entry offset {-60,-45,...,+60} minutes from 09:30 (the overnight return is measured to the signal row before the entry) x hold {60,120,180,240,300}; BM1: entry clock {14:20 ... 16:20 step 15} x exit rule {fixed 15:55 clock (entries before 15:55) ; constant 35-minute hold}; reported as surfaces of standalone weekly Sharpe (clean/H1/H2) and additive uplift at 20% (clean/H1/H2). (c) Decompositions of the real trades 2023-26: by weekday and by VIX tercile (VIX close of the previous trading day; terciles over 2023-08..2026-10), with pre-2023 comparatives. (d) The same rules on the 26 outrights: Q34 ES1 exact 1-minute rule on all 26 (no daily-bar form exists because the 16:00-keyed daily bars contain the Globex session, so the overnight return is not separable); BM1 is defined only for the four equity-index futures (needs the 15:59 RTH close) and is run on ES NQ RTY YM.
### SD-3 Sizing/insurance table for Q34 ES1 and BM1 each, together (equal-risk, each vol-matched to the clean weekly sigma then averaged) and with the pair: additive 10/20/30/50% and constant-risk 30% via t3_score.score_one on B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None); metrics clean/H1/H2/all-in (weekly Sharpe, worst week, CVaR5, max DD, Sortino, MAR, % negative weeks); sized dollars (contracts, worst day, worst week, margin note). No gates are applied: both are LEADS (ERA negative pre-2023); the table is for allocation.

## BLOCK 'SK' (stress test of the K4 family; no new selection, no new cell), registered 2026-10-17T12:10Z BEFORE any SK run. Only reproduction checks were run: the walk-forward in ap_sk_lib.py reproduces ap_k_fams.ml_preds bit-for-bit (max abs difference 0.0) and the K4v2 cell (d2, N=1, delta 0.08, long-only, p >= 0.58, 16:16 ET, 1-day hold) gives additive-20% uplift +0.183 / +0.392 / +0.197 / +0.203 (clean/H1/H2/all-in), 267 pair-window trades. Code: ap_sk_lib.py, ap_sk_run.py, ap_sk_rw5.py; outputs/autopilot/sk/. Instruction: coordinator 2026-10-17; up to 12 processes; disk < 1 GB.
- Common: data, features, fill model (last 1-min close at/before 16:16 ET, 1 tick/side + $4.36 per contract RT, 1 ADR-dollar of risk per signal, scaled to the pair clean weekly sd), pair = B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) series of t3_score (clean process), spans clean 2023-08..2025-07, H1 2025-08-01..2026-02-06, H2 2026-02-07..2026-10-16; additive 20% share unless stated. All K4 definitions (features, quarterly purged retrain from 2016, thresholds) are FROZEN; nothing is selected.
- SK1 (seeds): LightGBM random_state = 0..999 (bagging and feature-subsample seed; seed 0 is K4v2), same data, K4v2 feature column order. Reported: distribution (mean, sd, 5/50/95%) of uplift clean/H1/H2/all-in and standalone pair-window Sharpe; FRACTION of seeds with uplift >= +0.10 on clean AND H1 AND H2; fraction with all-in uplift >= 75% of seed 0 (+0.152) = seed plateau P1; fraction with all-in > 0. Pre-registered seed-robustness criterion for the K4 family claim: P(all three >= +0.10) >= 0.75 and P1 >= 0.75 (otherwise the K4v2 headline is a lucky seed). Parameter x seed plateau: the six exact +-25% neighbours (delta 0.06/0.10; trees 112/188; learning rate 0.0375/0.0625) x 100 seeds each; criterion: >= 5 of the 6 neighbour configurations have a seed-median all-in uplift >= 75% of the base configuration's seed-median. Seed ensembles: means of 5 and 25 seeds (disjoint groups from the 1,000), distribution of their uplift (RX5 analogue).
- SK2 (selection-aware family null, 1,000 worlds): the family {K4v2, RW12P, RX5 sleeves; RW5 pair modulator} is treated as one family. Sleeves: month-stratified drift-matched null = random long entries (pair window, same number per instrument and calendar month as the real sleeve, same 1-day hold, same sizing) -> standalone Sharpe and additive-20% uplift (clean/H1/H2/all-in); statistic W = min(clean,H1,H2 uplift) and the all-in uplift. RW5: the K4 probability series permuted among the days of the same calendar month (month-stratified) feeding the same pair modulator (a=0.25, both books, z = clip((pbar-0.5)/0.1,-1,1)). Per world one independent draw per family member; family statistic = best member (max W; max all-in); family-wise p = fraction of worlds whose best member >= the real best member; also each member's marginal rank, and a Westfall-Young min-p (marginal ranks converted to p, min over members). Independent draws are conservative (the real members share one signal); marginal ranks are the liberal bound; the final rank is stated as the bracket.
- SK3 (model classes, same features/purge/quarterly protocol): logistic (C 0.1), ridge (alpha 100, clipped 0-1), random forest (200 trees, depth 3, min leaf 100, sqrt features) and extra-trees (same), tree models over 20 seeds; two trading rules per class: (a) p >= 0.58 long-only as K4, (b) frequency-matched: long when p exceeds its rolling 250-day (shifted) quantile at the K4v2 selected-day frequency. Criterion for 'reproduces': same-sign uplift on clean/H1/H2 and median all-in >= 50% of +0.203. If only LightGBM works this is stated.
- SK4 (ablation + VIX-date null): feature sets PRICE, VIX (four VIX-state features), VIX+iid, PRICE+VIX, FULL-VIX (price+COT+calendar), FULL x 200 seeds each (set column order of ap_r_fams.fset). Permuted-VIX null: the whole VIX frame (VIX, VIX3M, VVIX, VIX9D, VIX6M, SKEW) is cyclically rolled by a random 6..60 months (either sign) in trading days before the VIX features are built (FULL features, K4 column order), random_state drawn per permutation; 500 permutations. Question: does the real-VIX seed distribution beat the permuted-VIX distribution (rank of the real median; fraction of permuted runs >= real median; fraction of permuted runs with all three spans >= +0.10)?
- SK5 (trade level, K4v2 seed 0 and RX5): signals per year (2016-2026 and pair window), long exposure (fraction of days with >= 1 open position, mean positions), contribution of the top 5% of days by P&L to the pair-window total, uplift with the top 5% of days removed, correlation with always-long, sized dollars at 20% and 30% (contracts per signal, annual and weekly $, worst week, CVaR, maxDD), and the standard insurance metric set.
- Priors: none claimed; this block can only lower or confirm the family status (maximum FOUND-PENDING, H1/H2 non-virgin).

## SI1 (forward-distribution study of the stated objective; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE the bootstrap ran)
Data: label days 2023-08-01..2026-10-16 (167 weeks, ISO weeks), daily USD series: pair (ap_ict_core PAIR, = ROWS_corrected_engine live 8/8), IMP3 incumbent (tf3 m1.5) and VC (k1 m1.125) on FV18 basis B (+1 s latency, +1.4 ticks on stop exits), BM1 NQ cell (15:20 fade), Q34 ES1 (onr (0,0.0,180,-1)). Books: (1) pair 8/8; (2) pair 6/6 = 0.75 x pair (contract rounding ignored); (3) pair + 20% IMP3; (4) pair + 20% VC (additive = pair + 0.2 x sleeve as in FV18, sleeve R-sized); (5-8) constant-risk 80/20 and 70/30 with IMP3 and with VC (kappa = clean weekly SD pair / sleeve as in FV18); (9) pair + 10% VC + 10% BM1 + 10% Q34 ES1 (VC raw x0.1, BM1 and Q34 ES1 x0.1 x kappa), flagged lead-only sensitivity, NOT counted. Method: moving-block bootstrap of the 167 joint weekly vectors (4-week blocks, circular wrap, 20,000 draws, common random numbers across books), horizons 13/26/52 weeks. Metrics per book and horizon: median / 5th / 95th percentile cumulative P&L; P(quarter < 0) (all non-overlapping 13-week segments); P(max drawdown of the path > $200k, > $300k, > $280k = 40% of $700k); mean of the path's worst week; P(>= 8 of 13 weeks positive); weekly SD and the share of weeks below -$25k (in-sample). Shrinkage: each sleeve's weekly series minus (1 - lambda) x its in-sample mean, lambda in {1, 0.5, 0.25} (pair untouched), plus a lambda grid 0..1 step 0.05 for the break-even where adding the sleeve stops improving P(drawdown > $200k) (52-week horizon, and 26); an additional joint-shrink row (pair and sleeves at 50%) as context. Margin view: peak concurrent contracts from the trade intervals of each book (pair rows, IMP3/VC tick-replay trades, BM1 and Q34 intervals) in the day session (09:30-16:00 ET) and outside it, under two UNVERIFIED margin assumptions (intraday/day-trade margin NQ $1,500, ES $1,200 per contract; exchange-style full margin NQ $20,000, ES $14,000), as % of $700k. Caveat stated in advance: the window is the pair's best period and the bootstrap replays that history; shrinkage rows are the sensitivity.

## BLOCK 'FVG_GCCL' (directive FVG-PAPER item 5; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE the first tick-replay run; no new selection)
Data: GC.FUT and CL.FUT (parent symbology) GLBX.MDP3 trades 2023-02-01..2026-10-12, already on disk (data/databento/{GC,CL}/trades/GLBX-20261013-*); NO purchase made (SPEND.json unchanged, 6,938.18).
Cells (frozen from RZ1; no re-selection): GC FVG tf15 thr0.25 and CL FVG tf15 thr0.25, R=2.0, L=20 bars, H=360 min, stop floor 30 ticks; RZ9 basket members as in REPORT_RZ9 with GC and CL members tick-replayed (other members stay on the 1-min model; no trades data for them).
Replay: setup placement = first minute after the setup bar close; entry limit at the 50% retrace price converted to raw contract prices by the series offset at the placement bar; queue bounds through1/q0/q50/q100 (q50 = headline), latency 1 min, target filled on 1 tick through, stop-market at first print at/through the stop (actual slippage), flat at the series flat bar; sequential one position; 4.36 USD RT per contract.
Roll rule: drop setups whose placement-bar instrument id differs from the id at the flat bar, whose series offset changes before the flat bar, or whose placement day's max-volume trade instrument differs from the series instrument id; count reported.
Gates (as IMP3): headline q50 additive 10/20/30/50 and constant risk, clean/H1/H2 uplift >= +0.10 at 20%, worst week within share, own nulls (200 depth-permutation replicates = random entries with matched depth and count; 200 entry-fraction replicates = fraction uniform 0.25-0.75), bootstrap CI, plateau (6 neighbours thr/R/L +-25%), 2x slippage (extra tick on entry and stop exits), worst-decile corr <= +0.30, replication (H0 2023-02..07, alternating months, ES/RTY cross with 1-min model, live weeks to 2026-10-12), q0/q100 bounds, latency 0/1/2 min and 1-tick-through vs touch as sensitivities, sized dollars, insurance metrics, library save. Pre-2023 history/era flag = 1-min model (no tick data).

## BLOCK 'DAYRULES' (DIRECTIVE DAYRULES, pair-side daily-rule scoring), registered 2026-10-17T15:29Z BEFORE any cell was scored (only the baseline reproduction and run-time checks: B.Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None) = 1.46/2.83/2.48/1.90 weekly Sharpe clean/H1/H2/all-in reproduced to the dollar by the new per-book engine (max daily difference 0.0)).
- Engine: code/census/night_ins/ap_dr_core.py (clean process, b11_nt8 first): the pair rows of the NT8-faithful 1-second engine (ROWS_corrected_engine, 13,184 rows) with per-book daily profit target / loss floor (own threshold per book, realised closed-trade P&L of the book's day, rule blocks new entries), the combined day lock (cap, give-back arm 2R / keep 33% = give back 67%, active from 00:00 on mark-to-market minute equity, flattens and blocks the rest of the label day) and the combined week floor -8R (all cells), per-book week rule OFF. R = $9,600 per book at 8/8; 6/6 = 0.75 x every dollar figure (6 lots; linear, no rounding).
- TH: the corrected-engine rows contain no Trade Health exits (exit names Target / Stop / Entry flip / TimeBlock only) and the NT8 list used for the reconciliation was recorded with TH off; the baseline above is therefore TH-OFF for both books. The directive's 'ST TH on' base needs a TH-on ST row set that does not exist for the corrected engine (B3-TH on the older engine: ST TH 'NEUTRAL', MAR +0.60 vs off); it is NOT scored here and the difference is reported as unquantified (limitation).
- Groups exactly as the directive: G1 cap {off,6,8,10,12,15,20}R x give-back {2R/67%, off} (14); G2 ST target {off,2,3,4,5,6}R x HM {off,3,4,5,6,8}R, floors -2R/-2R, combined lock as live (36); G3 ST floor {off,-1,-1.5,-2,-3}R x HM same, targets 5R/5R, combined lock as live (25); G4 = best 5 of G2 x best 5 of G3 x cap {off,8,12} (75, give-back arm 2R/67% kept) + the exact live cell + a plain cell (no daily rules, week floor kept); G5 = G2 and G3 with the combined day lock OFF (cap and give-back off; per-book rules only) + the single G1-off cell (per-book 5R/-2R only).
- 'Best 5' criterion (clean span ONLY): rank the cells of the group, excluding the live cell, by clean weekly Sharpe (ties: lower clean max drawdown); H1/H2 are printed in every table but play no role in the selection.
- Metrics per cell and span (clean / H1 / H2 / all-in): net, weekly Sharpe, max DD, worst week, CVaR5, % negative weeks, days a rule fired (per-book blocked book-days; combined-lock days), delta vs the live cell with sign consistency across the three spans.
- Ranked lists: A = beats live on all-in CVaR5 OR all-in max DD with all-in net >= 95% of live; B = all-in net > live with all-in max DD <= live and all-in worst week no worse than live. Verdict rule fixed now: SURVIVES = list B, Delta net >= 0 in >= 2 of 3 spans (none worse than -5% of live span net), plateau >= 0.75 and random-halt null rank >= 0.95; INSURANCE = list A, the claimed metric (CVaR5 or max DD) improves in >= 2 of 3 spans, plateau >= 0.75 and null rank >= 0.95; otherwise NO.
- Plateau: exact +-25% on every active numeric parameter of the cell, one at a time (targets, floors, cap, give-back arm); share of neighbours keeping >= 75% of the cell's improvement in the claimed metric. Matched random-halt null: 200 replicates in which all daily rules are replaced by random combined halts at the same firing rate (share of trading days on which any of the cell's rules fires), on the plain-rules book with the week floor; null rank = share of replicates whose improvement vs live is smaller than the cell's. Bootstrap: block bootstrap (4-week blocks, 2,000 draws) of the weekly delta vs live (net per week) and of the all-in CVaR5 difference.
- Chance expectation: with ~250 scored cells and a 5% null criterion about 12 cells pass the null by chance; plateau and sign-consistency filters apply on top. Prior from the earlier finding: the day lock is +clean, -H1, +H2 (regime dependent).

## POWER (DIRECTIVE CALIBRATE item 2; worker FA; registered 2026-10-17 BEFORE any planted book was scored)
Question: would the full gate chain find a book of known edge? Planted books on real NQ 1-min bars 2013-26.
* **Noise worlds (zero edge, real bars).** World w: 3 random base entry rows per label day (Globex+RTH entry-allowed rows), random sides; each of 50 cells is run through the real simulator ap_b_base.run_cell (entry at next 1-min open, 1 tick/side slippage, $4.36 commission per contract round trip, one position at a time, time exit, flatten 16:55). Neighbourhood: 5 entry-row offsets {-4,-2,0,+2,+4} x 5 time-exit holds {36,48,60,72,84} min x 2 session windows (all / 08:00-15:30 ET) = 50 cells; cells share the base entries so neighbouring cells are correlated as parameter neighbours are. 2,040 worlds (code ap_power_pool.py); worlds 1,840-2,039 are reserved as the null pool, worlds 0-1,839 carry books (each world used about 3.6 times, never twice in the same cell of the design).
* **Planted edge.** A constant drift d per trade (dollars, added to the real trade outcomes via the weekly trade count) on the centre cell (offset 0, hold 60, all-session); d is solved so the realised weekly Sharpe of the final book over its active window equals the target {0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0} (annualised sqrt52). Off-centre cells get d x m with m = exp(-(di^2+dj^2)/(2 x 1.5^2)) (di, dj = steps from the centre along offset and hold; adjacent step m = 0.80, diagonal 0.64, two steps 0.41), the RTH-only variant x 0.9. Regime types: (a) stationary, active window 2013-W01..2026; (b) regime-bound, drift only on trades in weeks >= 2023-W01, zero before (the pair's profile). Zero-edge control: target 0 (200 books per correlation level).
* **Correlation with the pair.** A shared component a x sigma x z (z = standardised weekly pair: real library PAIR from 2023-02, engine proxy c3/h23/h2_pair_long rescaled to the same SD before) is added to every cell of the book, a solved so the centre cell's realised weekly correlation with the pair over the 167-week pair window equals {0, +0.3, +0.6} (zero-mean, adds variance not mean; the drift is solved after it so the Sharpe target is on the final series).
* **Design.** 5 sizes x 2 regimes x 3 correlations x 200 = 6,000 planted books + 600 zero-edge books (3 correlations x 200) = 6,600; realised Sharpe and correlation of the planted centre cell are recomputed and reported.
* **Chain (per book, cumulative, thresholds as in ap_sw_pipe/ap_sw_nullcal and FA4):** S selection on clean 2023-08..2025-07 only (>= 100 clean trades, weekly Sharpe > 0 in both clean halves, best clean Sharpe of the 50 cells = the candidate); H standalone net > 0 on H1 and H2; G3 gate 3 as a 20% additive sleeve to the live pair (sleeve scaled to the pair's clean weekly SD x 20%): uplift >= +0.10 on clean, H1, H2 and worst week not worse by more than 20%; N own timing-destroyed null: the same cell in 200 zero-edge worlds, all-in (167-week) Sharpe > p95; B 4-week block bootstrap (2,000 draws) 90% lower bound of the additive uplift > 0; W worst-decile correlation with the pair <= +0.30 (17 worst pair weeks); P plateau: the 2-4 adjacent cells (offset +-1 step, hold +-1 step, same session) keep >= 75% of the uplift for >= 75% of neighbours (base uplift must be > 0); X 2x slippage (+1 tick/side more per trade = $10 per trade) uplift >= +0.10 on each span. Full chain through X = FOUND-PENDING-equivalent (FA4). Standalone track in parallel: S, H, N, bootstrap of the standalone weekly mean > 0, plateau on standalone Sharpe (neighbours >= 75% of base for >= 75%), 2x-slip standalone net > 0 on H1 and H2. Reported not gating: H0 (2023-02..07 Sharpe > 0), alternating months (odd/even > 0 on the pair window), ERA (2013-22 Sharpe > 0), executability (market entries: passes by construction), ES/RTY/YM replication (NOT APPLIED: the plant exists only on NQ by construction; a cross-market test would measure the assumed transfer ratio, not the pipeline).
* **Expectations written before running.** Gate 3 needs the sleeve to add >= 0.10 to a pair of Sharpe 1.4/3.1/2.4; with sleeve Sharpe s and correlation r a 20% sleeve changes the Sharpe by about 0.2(s - r Sp)/sqrt(1+0.04+0.4 r) - 0.02 Sp, so small edges (s <= 0.75) should fail on H1 and H2 even when real, and rho = +0.6 should fail almost everything. Zero-edge chain pass rate expected ~0 (FA4: 0 of 25,664).

## BLOCK 'CONTROL' (directive CALIBRATE item 1; registered 2026-10-17 before ANY cell result was read, selected or scored; the grid daily series were computed minutes earlier by ap_ctl_grid.py and written to pickles without being opened)
Question: would the candidate gate chain have found ST, HM, WT-10 and the ST+HM pair if they were candidates? Engine: NT8-faithful harness (nt8sim.acct order-level account with netting, 1-second bracket path, B11 account rules wk_floor 8 via ap_g11_c_run.acct), clean process; the live pair is asserted 1.46/2.83/2.48 (clean/H1/H2) by the BASE-rows control and reproduced exactly by the harness from parameters (clean 1.4599, H1 2.8276, H2 2.4840, 7,361 vs 7,346 trades, daily corr 0.99998). WT via ap_wt_gate.cell_trades + ap_wt_acct.Acct (WT-10 config reproduces forward/series/WT10_live.csv, ap_wt10_verify.py).
Grids (list of 975 cell ids in outputs/autopilot/control/CONTROL_CELLS.json): ST 229 = Supertrend ATR period {60,90,120,150,180} x multiplier {1.5,1.75,2.0,2.25,2.5} x EMA {100,200,300} x bracket {50,60,70} (+4 entry-window variants W1-W4 of the live cell); HM 229 = HMA fast {14,17,20,23,26} x slow {40,48,55,63,70} x EMA {100,200,300} x bracket {50,60,70} (+4 windows); PAIR 247 = ST (period {90,120,150} x mult {1.75,2.0,2.25}) x HM (fast {17,20,23} x slow {48,55,63}) x bracket {50,60,70} at EMA 200 (+4 windows); WT 270 = Wilder ATR {10,14,20} x multiplier {2,2.5,3,3.5,4} x timeframe {5,10,15} min x bracket {45,60,75} x EMA {0,200}, exit X1 (entry-window variants not available in the WT core). Live cells: ST (120,2.0,200,br60), HM (20,55,200,br60), WT-10 (14,3.0,0,10min,br60), pair = both.
Selection: clean span only (2023-08..2025-07), >= 100 clean trades, standalone weekly Sharpe positive in both clean halves, highest clean standalone weekly Sharpe; H1/H2 read once for the selected cell; the LIVE cell is also taken through the chain as a second candidate (the question is whether the chain passes a book of the pair's quality). CAVEAT stated in advance: live parameters were chosen on 2023-26 data, so clean selection near the live cell is in-sample for the live pair and H1/H2 are not virgin for it.
Gates (candidate definitions): gate 3 = additive 20% sleeve (vol-matched to the base's clean weekly sd) on the OTHER book (ST on live HM, HM on live ST, WT on the live pair), the pair standalone against an empty base (uplift = standalone Sharpe): +0.10 clean/H1/H2 and worst week within share; own timing-destroyed null p95 (200 replicates of random timing, same counts, standalone all-in Sharpe); bootstrap 90% CI of the all-in uplift (pair: of the Sharpe) lower bound > 0; plateau: exact +-25% one-at-a-time neighbours keep >= 75% of the metric for >= 75% of neighbours; 2x slippage uplift >= +0.10 each span; worst-decile correlation <= +0.30 (sleeves); replication = at least 2 of {H0 2023-02..07, ES/RTY/YM same cell (1-minute approximation: no second data), alternating months (select odd read even and the reverse), newest 4 live weeks}; 2013-22 ERA with cost decomposition (1-minute approximation, bracket scaled by ADR); executability. Labels as the pipeline: FOUND-PENDING = all measured gates + replication 2 of 4; LEAD = >= 5 of 7 measured gates incl. gate 3; else NO. Pass margins are reported for every gate.

## BLOCK 'WEEKGB' (registered 2026-10-17, before any WEEKGB cell was scored) - DAYRULES add-on: combined WEEK give-back lock and per-book weekly profit target
- Engine/base: identical to 'DAYRULES' (clean process, b11_nt8 first, ROWS_corrected_engine = TH-OFF rows, live config 8/8 with per-book day 5R/-2R, combined day lock cap 8R + give-back arm 2R keep 33% from 00:00, combined week floor -8R, per-book week rule OFF); baseline asserted 1.46/2.83/2.48/1.90 (ap_dr_core LIVE == B.run(Cfg(wk_floor=8,wk_loss_pb=None))). Dollars at 8/8; 6/6 = 0.75x. R = $9,600 per book, i.e. the SAME convention as the live combined locks (combined cap 8R means combined mark-to-market day P&L >= 8 x $9,600).
- Week give-back (cells W1): week = ISO week of the exit label (same grouping as the live week floor); the week equity is the combined mark-to-market equity of all open and closed rows after the day-lock stage, evaluated on 1-MINUTE bar closes (the engine's resolution); the week peak is the running maximum of that equity INCLUDING intraday marks. Arm when the peak >= A x R, A in {2,3,4,5,6}; lock when equity <= K x peak, K in {0.33,0.50,0.67} (i.e. the week has given back 67/50/33%). On lock: open rows are closed at the next 1-minute open, new entries blocked. Variant (a) halt until Monday (the lock is the same as the live week lock, so the week floor -8R is evaluated in the same pass); variant (b) halt for the rest of that SESSION (label day) only; the same week peak is retained; the rule RE-ARMS on the downward cross: the lock may fire again on a later session only after the week equity has first recovered above K x peak (otherwise (b) would re-fire at the first bar of the next session and equal (a)); the week floor -8R remains a halt-until-Monday in both variants. 5 x 3 x 2 = 30 cells.
- Per-book weekly profit target (cells W2): realised net of the book's CLOSED trades in the same ISO week (exit-label week, as pass1 in b11_nt8) >= T x R, T in {4,6,8,10}, blocks that book's new entries until Monday (open positions continue); books ST, HM, BOTH (each book against its own target) = 12 cells.
- Scoring: net, weekly Sharpe, max DD, worst week, CVaR5, % negative weeks per span (clean/H1/H2/all-in); weeks/sessions in which the rule fired; (net - live net) per firing (all-in). Plateau: the adjacent grid points (A +-1 step, K +-1 step within the same variant; T +-1 step within the same book set); a neighbour keeps if its improvement of the claimed metric is >= 75% of the cell's; plateau = share kept (SURVIVES/INSURANCE need >= 0.75, as DAYRULES). Matched random-halt null (200 replicates): the live book plus an extra random halt at the cell's own firing rate (week-level halts until Monday for W1a; session-level halts for W1b; per-book-week random cut-off for W2), claimed metric compared; null rank = share of replicates beaten (>= 0.95 needed). Block bootstrap of the weekly delta (2000 draws, 4-week blocks). Per-span sign consistency printed.
- Lists/verdicts: exactly as DAYRULES: list A = beats live on all-in CVaR5 or max DD at <= 5% net cost; list B = beats live on all-in net with max DD and worst week no worse; SURVIVES = list B, dnet >= 0 in >= 2 of 3 spans (none worse than -5% of live span net), plateau >= 0.75, null rank >= 0.95; INSURANCE = list A, claimed metric improves in >= 2 of 3 spans, plateau >= 0.75, null rank >= 0.95; otherwise NO. Chance expectation: 5% of the evaluated qualifying cells pass the null by chance (cells are highly correlated, so far fewer independent trials).
- Week 2026-W42 (10-12..10-16): for every cell the firing time (ET), the week peak, the lock level (equity at the lock) and the final week P&L are listed; live W42 replay +$38.0k (engine) is checked.
- TH: base is TH-OFF as established in DAYRULES; no TH-on rows exist. No live change.


## L2-M2 (registered 2026-10-17 ~17:50Z by the L2-M2 worker, BEFORE the NQ mbp-10 file has landed and before any NQ L2 number exists; supersedes the cell-level 'PX35 NQ L2 ROUND' chain above, which is WITHDRAWN as a verdict path: its cells are now the cells of the families below, not candidates)
Authority: DIRECTIVE METHOD-2 and the L2 AMENDMENT (AUTOPILOT.md, verbatim). Code: code/census/night_ins/ap_l2m2_build.py (grids = the constants GRIDS in that file, final), ap_l2m2_score.py (thin wrapper over ap_m2.py). No API/key use, no purchase, nothing resubmitted; the NQ job GLBX-20261013-Y3PCQ4MKV3 has not landed.
**Status rule: NO PASS/FAIL label and NO verdict is issued for any family below unless REPORT_POWER2.md contains 'DONE POWER2' and 'POWER2 MET'. Until then every number is a REPORT-ONLY descriptive statistic (ap_l2m2_score.py --report-only). FOUND/LEAD labels are retired; ES and RTY proxy results are report-only evidence that motivated cells and are NOT selection evidence and NOT independent replication (the ES finding generated the DIA hypothesis).**
Common to all families. Instrument NQ (pair trades of ROWS_corrected_engine, 2023-02..2026-10; L2 span 2023-08..2026-10). Each family is a DELTA family: cell-by-day matrix = (modified book minus live pair) in USD at live size (R = $9,600 / 8 NQ-equivalent), 1 tick/side + commission, clean-process pair harness b11_nt8 (B.Cfg(wk_floor=8, wk_loss_pb=None); baseline 1.46/2.83/2.484 asserted, identity cell asserted); the delta is exactly 0 before the first L2 month (modifier inactive; declared, not a silent fill). pair_dependent=1 except L2_ESKIP. Every cell of the grid is kept (cells that never fire stay as zero columns and drop out of the equal-risk ensemble by sd=0). Timing: every L2 state variable is sampled 1 s BEFORE the decision time (flip entry time / bar close), trailing windows only; direction of every rule fixed here, never flipped after a result. Grid size is final; no cell is added or changed after the first NQ L2 number.
OOS read: ap_m2.py walk-forward, fit 9 / read 3 / step 3 months (or the configuration CHOSEN in REPORT_POWER2.md), equal-risk ensemble with the ensemble rule chosen there, fit window only for the pre-registered weighting; family null = the null chosen by REPORT_POWER2 (default signflip, 200 replicates; the literal shift null is reported only); 4-week block bootstrap; both OOS halves; best-of-F with the 8 L2 families counted in F (nominal F = all registry families with matrices; the L2 pass also prints F_L2 = 8 as a reference); reported not gating: pair correlation / worst-decile correlation, size and DD at pair 6/6 DD, median cell and the clean-selected best cell (2023-08..2025-07) and its later Sharpe, a sensitivity run with the walk-forward started 2023-08-01. The first fit window (2023-02..10) holds only ~3 months of L2 days: accepted and disclosed. PASS (only if POWER2 met) = gates a-d and best-of-F rank >= 0.95, or an independent pre-registered replication: the @RTY legs below (same cells, RTY book at the same NQ pair trades) are registered here for that purpose, are not counted in F, and must show the same sign and >= 50% of the NQ ensemble net; the @ES legs are NOT independent for DIA.
Families (id; mechanism; state variables and timing; grid = cells; size; order form; engine):
1. L2_DIA (33 cells). Mechanism: when the EMA of the top-5 depth imbalance, side-aligned (imb_al = side x EMA_s(imb5)), leans WITH the new trend at the pair flip, the flip chases displayed demand (ES finding: against-the-flip book = absorption); skip / halve / tilt away. Sign fixed in advance. Grid: EMA span s in {5, 10, 30} s x threshold th in {-0.05, 0, +0.05, +0.10} x action {skip (size 0), half (0.5)} when imb_al > th = 24; tilt f = clip(1 - k x imb_al, 0.5, 1.5), k in {1.5, 2.5, 3.5} x s in {5, 10, 30} = 9. Size: pair size x factor. Order form pair_overlay; engine reconciled NT8-faithful (pair harness). Proxy (report-only): ES (21 months, REPORT_PX_L2ES section B): NQ pts/trade +4.69 when imb_al <= 0 vs -0.44 when > 0, same sign in both alternating-month halves, but only 4/21 months positive for with-minus-against (mean-driven); RTY (39 months, REPORT_PZ1_L2RTY): NOT reproduced (difference -0.25 pts, even months +1.42, odd months -1.93, 18/39 months positive).
2. L2_SPRDEP (15 cells). Mechanism: flips taken in a wide-spread or thin-depth book (low displayed liquidity, vacuum) have worse follow-through; skip or halve. State: spread_t (ticks, 1 s before entry), dep_ratio = top-5 total depth / its trailing 1800 s mean (min 300 s). Grid: wide spread spread_t > k, k in {1, 2, 3} x {skip, half} = 6; thin depth dep_ratio < q, q in {0.5, 0.7, 0.85} x {skip, half} = 6; vacuum (dep_ratio < q and spread_t >= 2) skip, q in {0.5, 0.7, 0.85} = 3. Pair_overlay, same engine. Proxy (report-only): ES: only 17.5% of flips have spread > 1 tick; wide-spread flips NQ pts/trade 1.10 (t 0.7) vs 2.57 (t 3.7): no evidence a skip helps (n = 1,391); RTY: 84% of flips are > 1 tick (0.10 tick), 2.08 vs 3.73 pts, uninformative; depth thinness was not measured on either proxy.
3. L2_MICRO (16 cells). Mechanism: if the microprice (depth-weighted mid offset, ticks, clipped at +-3) is drifting against the flip direction just before the flip, order flow does not confirm it; skip or halve. State: md_al_a = side x (EMA_a(micro) - EMA_60(micro)), sampled 1 s before entry. Grid: a in {3, 10} s x theta in {0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40} ticks x {skip, half} when md_al_a < -theta = 16. Pair_overlay. Proxy: none measured (ES/RTY fwd30/60/300 drifts at flips were reported, microprice drift was not).
4. L2_I15 (18 cells). Item 15 (BACKLOG2 B.15) exit on depth-imbalance flip: exit at the touch 1 s after the first second (after the minimum hold) where side x EMA_span(imb5) < -thr, before the original exit. Grid: span in {5, 15, 30} s x thr in {0.10, 0.20, 0.30} x min hold in {20, 60} s = 18 (the 4 PA4 cells span {5,15} x thr {0.10,0.20}, hold 20, are inside). Effect = price delta at the touch vs the original exit (mid -/+ half spread) added to the pair row's points and the exit time moved (engine PTS0/XI/TOUT overrides). Pair_overlay. Proxy (report-only): ES: all 4 cells lose vs the original exit (-0.21 to -0.28 ES pts/trade, t about -2 on changed trades), modestly better than a random-time-exit null for thr 0.2, same sign in even and odd months, early exit cuts stops (+7..+10) and forfeits targets (-6..-9) nearly one for one; RTY: all 4 negative (-0.08 to -0.20, t -1.6 to -3.3), no better than random exit in 3/4.
5. L2_WHIP (48 cells). K-L2 whipsaw cure (H11-B / J5W mechanism with L2 state): after N pair trades stopped out within X minutes of entry on the same label day (state from trades closed before the entry), halt (size 0) or halve (0.5) the NEXT entry only when the L2 state at that flip is choppy. Grid: N in {2, 3} x X in {10, 20, 40} min x gate in {thin: dep_ratio < 0.7; wide: spread_t >= 2; with: imb_al_10 > 0; cancel: cancel_z > 1 (z of the 30 s cancelled size vs trailing 3600 s)} x action {HALT, HALF} = 48; a missing feature = gate false (no action). Reference arm kept in the npz (ctrl_X, 12 cells, gate always true = the L2-free H11-B/J5W cells), reported only, NOT part of the family. Pair_overlay with a state-carrying size hook (engine pass1 sizefn). Evidence without L2 (report-only, non-L2): H11-B N2_X10_HALT clean +0.20 / H1 +0.47 / H2 +0.19 uplift, plateau 0/4, random-halt null rank 0.94 (REPORT_H11_B); J5W A2_T2 +0.05 / +0.30 / +0.35 (REPORT_J5W_A). ES/RTY L2 proxy: none (no whipsaw-specific L2 result exists).
6. L2_EXEC (15 cells). Items 97 and 98 merged into one execution family (each alone has < 10 cells): mechanism = queue-model price improvement of exits. 97: target rested as a limit 1 tick better in a deep book (dep5 >= trailing 30-min 60th percentile at the target touch), timeout after the original touch in {5, 15, 30} s then market; 98: stop-limit offset in {2, 4} ticks (5 s timeout, then market) instead of stop-market in thin Globex hours (17:00-08:30 ET). Queue-model cancel_frac in {0, 0.5, 1} is a cell factor (latency 5 ms, ap_pa4_queue). Grid: 3 x 3 + 2 x 3 = 15. Construction (disclosed deviation): only sampled event windows exist (<= 30 target trades and <= 30 thin-hour stops per month, placement 45 s before the exit, i.e. not 'rest from entry'); the daily delta is the sampled trade delta x the stratified inverse-probability weight (population count / sampled count per month and stratum) x baseline size x $20 x 8 pts, i.e. an EXTRAPOLATION with no lock interplay; high variance, family flagged 'resting', engine 'pair harness + queue model, sampled, IPW'. A full-coverage rebuild needs a second pass over raw NQ (re-download), NOT done; a family verdict from the extrapolated series is still only valid if POWER2 is met and is to be read as indicative. Proxy (report-only): ES 97 high-depth subset -0.10 to -0.21 pts/trade, fill 22-25%, cancel_frac irrelevant (bounds equal within noise); 98 +0.011 / -0.005 pts, ES timing contaminated (n = 142); RTY 97 -0.07 to -0.18, 98 -0.04 / -0.05.
7. L2_A4 (27 cells). Item 4 (A4) ST/HM on mid: ST/HM signals are computed on 1-min mid OHLC from the L2 book (first/max/min/last of the per-second mid; trade bars outside the L2 span and for minutes without mid), execution (next-open market entry + bracket) unchanged. Grid (CONTROL harness ap_ctl_grid PAIR cells, live ST period 120 / mult 2.0, HM 20 / 55, EMA 200, bracket 60 pts, W0): ST period {90, 120, 150} x ST mult {1.75, 2.0, 2.25} x HM slow {48, 55, 63} = 27 (live cell inside); delta = pair(mid signals) - pair(trade-bar signals) for the same parameters. State: mid bars, causal. The bar-minute alignment (label vs bucket) is chosen by the code from the correlation of 1-min close changes against the NQ bars (asserted > 0.5) and logged; an identity check (mid = trade bars) must reproduce the trade-bar run exactly. Pair_overlay, engine reconciled NT8-faithful (nt8sim), NOTE the CONTROL generator is not ROWS_corrected_engine. Proxy: none possible (an ES/RTY mid cannot substitute NQ bars); PA4 comparison step (ST flip-time agreement, 2% rule) is subsumed.
8. L2_ESKIP (288 cells). Family E mbp-10 skip filters (IDEAS-5000 family E, PREREG_IDEAS5000.md line 52 and REPORT_I5E 'what was not scored': 'resting depth at/beyond the limit, imbalance sign'; no separate a027 prereg text exists beyond these, so the grid below is registered here): a resting retrace limit is NOT placed when the L2 state at the decision bar (close of the signal bar, state 1 s before) predicts adverse selection. Cells = E cell x filter, delta = filtered minus the same unfiltered E cell: books {T3_k1.5, T3_k1.125, V1_k1.5, V1_k1.125} x R {1.5, 2.0} x forms {L0, LD4, CX5} x order-handling rules {ffw, ooat} x filters {imbalance against the order side: side x EMA10(imb5) < -theta, theta in {0, 0.05, 0.10}; own-side top-5 depth ratio < q, q in {0.5, 0.7}; spread >= 2} = 4 x 2 x 3 x 2 x 6 = 288. Deviation: depth at the limit PRICE level is not in the reduced per-second tables, so the own-side top-5 aggregate is used; the order-handling rule is a cell factor and named in the notes. Engine exec_engine v2 ECON via ap_i5_ej (R-sized $9,600, 24-contract cap, 1 tick/side + $4.36), resting. pair_dependent=0 (library-standard USD). Proxy: none (E is an NQ order program).
Replication legs: the same builder on the RTY (and ES) mbp-10 reductions writes L2_<fam>@RTY / @ES (families 1-6, 8) as report-only legs; they are never counted in F and never scored as families.
Chance accounting stated in advance: 8 families, 33+15+16+18+48+15+27+288 = 460 cells, strongly dependent within a family; a family with no edge passes a one-sided null at about 5% and the best-of-F rank corrects across the registry; per-cell results are not reported as findings.
PX35 chain: the cell-level PX35 gate chain and its per-cell hooks (ap_pd5_score.py, the 'DIA / E-group REPLICATION' legs above) are withdrawn as a verdict path by the L2 AMENDMENT; the PX35 text above remains the record of the cell definitions only. The watcher v2 (ap_pz9b_watch.sh) runs download+reduce, ap_pd5_agg (descriptive), ap_l2m2_build.py and, only if POWER2 is met, ap_l2m2_score.py.
L2-M2 ADDENDUM (17:55Z, same day, before any NQ L2 number; REPORT_POWER2.md was completed at 17:49Z and now exists): (i) the walk-forward configuration and ensemble rule for the L2 families are those RECOMMENDED in REPORT_POWER2 section 1 (fit 12 / read 3 / step 3, rule `tilt`; first OOS week 2024-02, ~142 OOS weeks); ap_l2m2_score.py parses them from that line (default 9/3/3 ew only if the line is absent); (ii) null = sign-flip (POWER2 prescribes (b) sign-flip for P&L-only two-sided families; the L2 delta families have no random-entry simulator for the custom null), 200 replicates; the literal shift null is not used; (iii) REPORT_POWER2.md ends with 'DONE POWER2' but contains no 'POWER2 MET' line (power at true Sharpe 1.0 is 'NOT met'), so under the watcher's rule the L2 families are REPORT-ONLY: no PASS/FAIL, no verdict, no best-of-F claim, until a 'POWER2 MET' line is written. The 9-month-fit sensitivity of the first paragraph becomes the 'walk-forward started 2023-08-01' sensitivity (reported only).

## BLOCK 'M2-REPL' (worker intraday-other, 2026-10-17; registered BEFORE any replication cell below was read; coordinator decision: the six METHOD-2 single-family passers have no best-of-F >= 0.95 and need an independent pre-registered replication)
The six passers: BM13_NQ, BM11_NQ, BM12_NQ, BM8_RTY, Q37_RTY, BI8_YM (METHOD-2 12/3/3 tilt, sign-flip family null, gates a-d; OOS Sharpe 1.77 / 1.46 / 1.28 / 1.32 / 1.24 / 1.10; best-of-F rank against 567 intraday-other families 0.48 / 0.03 / 0.00 / 0.00 / 0.00 / 0.00). Same-bet map from the OOS series (daily corr): BM13, BM11 and BM12 are ONE bet (late-day NQ fade of the move away from a reference price; pairwise OOS corr 0.54 / 0.61 / 0.81, weekly 0.53 / 0.58 / 0.81); BM8_RTY, Q37_RTY and BI8_YM are three further independent bets (|corr| < 0.2 with every other passer). Four independent bets are counted.
Already-read sibling evidence (not independent of the selection, shown for honesty; METHOD-2 OOS Sharpe / null rank): BM13 ES -0.55/0.25, RTY -0.20/0.28, YM -0.61/0.15; BM12 ES +0.32/0.66, RTY +0.53/0.80, YM +0.35/0.66; BM11 ES +0.24/0.63, RTY -0.18/0.34, YM -0.87/0.07; BM8 ES -0.85/0.13, NQ -0.50/0.27, YM +0.58/0.86; Q37 ES -0.42/0.28, NQ -1.30/0.01; BI8 ES +0.19/0.60, NQ +0.39/0.66, RTY +0.48/0.79.
R1 FORWARD (genuinely independent): for each passer the ensemble is FROZEN as of the last METHOD-2 fold (weights = tilt on the fit window 2025-08-01..2026-07-31, cells scaled to unit SD on that window; weights written to outputs/methods2/repl/M2-REPL_weights_<family>.csv before any forward week is read). Forward weeks = ISO weeks with label days after 2026-10-16, appended by the weekly extension; the cell definitions and costs are the registered ones (1 tick/side + commission). Decision rule, fixed now: at 26 and 52 forward weeks compute the forward weekly mean of the frozen ensemble and its z (mean / (SD / sqrt(weeks))). PASS at 52 weeks: z >= 1.64 (one-sided 5%) and forward net > 0; early PASS at 26 weeks: z >= 2.33; KILL at 26 weeks: z <= -1.0 (the family is retired as a lead). Between: continue. The BM13/BM11/BM12 group counts as one test, so its pass is judged on the BM12 ensemble (the 12-cell parent grid); the four independent bets are each tested at 5% (no further correction pre-registered; the expected chance passes among four independent null bets are 0.2).
R2 SHIFTED CLOCK / PARAMETER FAMILIES (cells registered here, same data window, never read before this block; code ap_m2_repl.py): BM12S = BM12 grid (6 references x |dist| in {0.4, 0.7} ADR) at entry clocks 15:10 and 15:30 (exit 15:55 unchanged) = 24 cells; BM13S = BM13 grid (vwap30, sess_vwap x {0.10, 0.15, 0.25}) at 15:10 and 15:30 = 12 cells; BM8S = up-day-fraction grid with lookbacks N in {7, 15, 30} x threshold {0.7, 0.8} x {with, against} on RTY = 12 cells; Q37S = prior-week high/low breakout (with / fade) on RTY at 5-minute and 30-minute bars x the 4 registered entry rules (2 x 2 x 4) = 16 cells; BI8S = range-shock next-day drift on YM with range multiple {1.25, 1.75, 2.5} x {long, with, against} x hold {1, 2} days = 18 cells. Test: each S family run through ap_m2 exactly as the originals (12/3/3 tilt, sign-flip null, 200 replicates, gates a-d). Replication PASS = gates a-d pass AND OOS Sharpe >= 0.5 x the parent's OOS Sharpe; FAIL otherwise. An S family that fails is reported, not re-tuned. BM11 already contains the clock shifts 15:00-15:30 for the prior-close reference (read), so BM11 is replicated only through R1.
Untouched instruments: none exist for these mechanisms inside the data (ES, NQ, RTY, YM, all other outrights and BTC/ETH were scored); micro contracts are the same underlying. If a new instrument (e.g. a CME equity index future not yet in the library) is added, the parent cells are run unchanged on it.
Chance expectation for R2: each S family passes gates a-d with probability about 1.7% under zero edge, so 0.09 expected passes among the five; any pass is therefore informative, with the caveat that the S families share the sample window with their parents (they test mechanism robustness to clock/parameter, not new calendar time).

## PREREG block 'M2-MERGE' (owner ap_m2c, 2026-10-17, written BEFORE any merged family is scored)
Purpose: the 43 pair-modifier DELTA families registered `tiny: <10 cells` by ap_m2b (status in FAMILY_REGISTRY.csv) cannot be scored alone by the METHOD-2 harness. They are merged into super-families of >= 10 cells BY MECHANISM ONLY. No OOS or in-sample result, Sharpe, rank or report verdict was used to build the groups; the assignment below uses only the family mechanism sentence in the registry (what the overlay changes and, for size tilts, which observable it conditions on).
Merge rule (two steps, applied in this order): (1) LEVER = what the overlay changes on the live pair: (a) entry halt / throttle triggered by the day's own stop-outs or entry frequency -> MG_WHIP; (b) size or lock rule on account / P&L / open-risk state -> MG_RISKRULE; (c) exit or flatten rule on an open trade (in-trade state, clock flatten, event-linked flatten, profit-take timing) -> MG_EXIT; (d) bracket geometry (stop / target widths) -> MG_BRACKET; (e) entry filter, entry timing or entry order form -> MG_ENTRY; (f) size tilt or halt conditioned on an observable -> step (2). (2) OBSERVABLE for lever (f): calendar / clock / holiday -> MG_TILT_CAL; scheduled-event calendar (CPI/NFP/FOMC event day or window) -> MG_EVENT; market state (cross-asset composite or own-price run-up) -> MG_TILT_MKT.
Members (all 43 tiny families are assigned exactly once; 227 cells):
- MG_WHIP (23 cells): J5W_A, J5W_B, J5W_C, J5W_D, K21, K22, K25.
- MG_RISKRULE (20): H0C, K19, K20, K23, K24, K26.
- MG_EXIT (74): J0A, J0B, J0C, K11, K13, K14, K16, K17, K99, H10_A, G6C, J6C.
- MG_BRACKET (24): K12, K18, K18B.
- MG_ENTRY (18): J6A, J6B, K96, K96B, K100.
- MG_TILT_CAL (15): H6B, H6C, K27.
- MG_EVENT (26): G10_1, G10_2, H10_B, H10_C.
- MG_TILT_MKT (27): J7V, J9P_C, G10_3.
Ready (>= 10 cell) families (H11_B, PY9, G6A/B, H0A/B/D, H6A, H7_A-C, J9P_A/B, PA*/PX*/PY*/PZ*) are NOT merged and keep their own scoring (not double counted); the originals of the merged families are not deleted (registry rows stay `tiny`, matrices stay). The merged matrix for MG_x stacks the member families' cells (cell names keep their `<family>/<cell>` prefix), union day axis, 0.0 where a member has no row.
Registry: family_id MG_*, owner ap_m2c, order_form pair_overlay, pair_dependent 1, status ready, cluster_id = the MG id (8 new clusters; the 43 originals stay `tiny` and outside F). Test: ap_m2 exactly as accepted under POWER2 (12/3/3 tilt, sign-flip family null because delta series are two-sided P&L-only, 200 replicates, gates a-d) plus the a778 leverage-matched residual (ap_m2_resid logic, per-fold fit-window beta to the pair) in the shared schema; best-of-F rank computed by the harness owner over all effective clusters. Because lever groups are broad, a member-level result is read as plateau breadth, not as a separate test. Chance expectation: about 1.7-3% of zero-edge families pass gates a-d; with 8 merged families the expected number of chance passes is 0.14-0.24, so any pass is informative only after best-of-F.
Day-axis extension (same task): all pair-delta matrices are re-generated from the ORIGINAL family harness scripts (unchanged copies run by ap_m2c_launch.py on the extended ROWS_corrected_engine to 2026-10-16, outputs to a scratch dir); 2023-02-10..2026-10-12 must equal the stored matrices (2026-10-13 is expected to differ because the 10-13 label day was partial in the old rows).

PASS = gates a-d on the whole OOS AND OOS Sharpe >= 0.5 x 2.10 = 1.05 (I9's own OOS Sharpe under the same harness; sensitivity: also reported against 0.5 x 1.39 = 0.69, the merged family's posted value).
Frozen outputs for the FORWARD.md bands: cell lists, last-fold tilt weights, OOS weekly mean and SD per passing replication AND for the parents (the parents' bands are the ones that would be registered if the replication passes).

## BLOCK 'M2-REPL2' (worker intraday-other, 2026-10-17; coordinator follow-up (2): second instrument or never-read clock/parameter set for BM13 (+BM11/BM12 members), B7, B1 / B1leg_BTC, CL 240 (C10S1_CL:240_lr and :macd), WT432 (ATR30_m4, ATR20_m4, ATR10_m3), C11DIP; registered BEFORE any replication cell below was run or read)
Test for each replication family (run exactly once): METHOD-2 harness (ap_m2) with fit 12 / read 3 / step 3 months, rule 'tilt', 200-replicate family null, gates a-d (OOS Sharpe and net > 0; null rank >= 0.95; 4-week block bootstrap lower bound > 0; both OOS halves net > 0); PASS = gates a-d AND OOS Sharpe >= 0.5 x the parent's. Null kind: sign-flip (one random sign per ISO week on all cells) with the POWER2 section-7 net-exposure check (corr of the OOS daily series with the instrument's own daily change; if > 0.15 the sign-flip is invalid and the family is reported as 'BETA: needs drift-matched null', not PASS); the custom drift-matched null is used only where a simulator already exists (C11DIP: random-date long signals with identical exits). Labels: a new INSTRUMENT is a replication (different market, same calendar window); a new CLOCK/PARAMETER set on the same instrument and window is ROBUSTNESS only. The independent test for every family remains the forward period (frozen weights from the last fold, evaluator ap_m2_repl_forward.py, rule: PASS at 52 weeks z >= 1.64, early PASS at 26 weeks z >= 2.33, KILL at 26 weeks z <= -1.0).
UNREAD CHECKS performed (file listings of result directories and grep of the reports before registration): (a) BM12/BM13 result files exist only for ES, NQ, RTY, YM (ls b/bm12_co/real_*.pkl b/bm13_co/real_*.pkl); BM1 entry clocks 15:20/15:30/15:40 (grid T in 920/930/940), BM11/BL3 close-60/-50/-40/-30 minutes (15:00-15:30) on 28 instruments with the PRIOR-CLOSE reference only, M2-REPL 15:10/15:30 -> the entry clocks 14:40 and 14:50 were never an entry clock (the only 14:50 mention in REPORT_BL3 is a diagnostic volume-share window measured before entry) and the VWAP references (vwap30, sess_vwap) were never run on any instrument except the four equity indices. (b) REPORT_B7 states instruments ES, RTY, GC, CL, ZN, YM, BTC (b7/b7_co result files identical); REPORT_B1 states ES, RTY, GC, CL, ZN, BTC, ETH, YM; neither was ever run on NQ, HO, RB, NG, SI, HG, 6E or ZB (the Q-series use different generators; SW1 is regime-gated and a different grid). (c) C10-S1 result files exist for 6E, 6J, CL, ES, GC, NQ, ZN (driver.log); never ZB; the parameter sets lr(40), lr(60), macd(8,17), macd(19,39) are not in the C10-S1 rule list (RULES in ap_c10_s1_core.py). (d) WT grid (ap_wt_grid.py) = ATR {10,14,20,30} x m {2,3,4} x EMA {0,100,200} x tf {1,3,5,10} x exits {X1,X2,X3} = 432 cells; the (ATR, m) pairs below are not in it. (e) C11-DIP grid = RSI(2) < {5,10} x 4 exits x stop {2,3} ATR x SMA 200 on NQ/ES/RTY/YM; RSI thresholds 3, 15, 20 and SMA 150/250 were not run (SD batch used the identical rule on other markets: those instruments are READ).
REPLICATION FAMILIES (exact cells; code ap_m2_repl2.py; matrices outputs/methods2/fam/<id>.npz; parents and their OOS Sharpe -> pass threshold):
R-BM13N [robustness, NQ, never-read clocks]: BM13 grid (vwap30, sess_vwap) x |dist| {0.10, 0.15, 0.25} ADR x entry clock {14:40, 14:50} (exit 15:55, against) = 12 cells; parent BM13_NQ 1.77 -> >= 0.88.
