R-C10P-lr / R-C10P-macd [robustness, CL, unread parameter sets]: tf = 240 on CL with lr windows {40, 60} (40 cells) respectively macd {(8,17), (19,39)} (40 cells), same exits/sessions/EMA-filter variants as the parent; same thresholds.
R-WTR30 / R-WTR20 / R-WTR10 [robustness, NQ harness, unread (ATR, m) pairs; same EMA/tf/exit axes (36 cells per pair)]: R-WTR30 = (25,4), (40,4), (30,3.5), (30,4.5) (144 cells; parent WT432:ATR30_m4 1.26 -> >= 0.63); R-WTR20 = (17,4), (25,4), (20,3.5), (20,4.5) (144 cells; parent ATR20_m4 1.19 -> >= 0.60; (25,4) is shared with R-WTR30 and counted in both); R-WTR10 = (8,3), (12,3), (10,2.5), (10,3.5) (144 cells; parent ATR10_m3 1.00 -> >= 0.50).
R-C11R [robustness, NQ/ES/RTY/YM, unread parameters]: RSI(2) < {3, 15} x SMA {150, 250} (and 200 with RSI < {3, 15}) x the 4 registered exits x stop {2, 3} ATR = 6 x 4 x 2 = 48 cells per instrument, 192 cells; parent C11DIP 1.43 -> >= 0.72. Long-only: the sign-flip null is invalid for it (POWER2 section 7), so the result is reported with its beta check and cannot PASS without the drift-matched random-date null; the forward test is the decision.
Chance expectation: 11 replication families at about 1.7% false-positive each under no edge = 0.19 expected passes; a pass is therefore informative, a fail is not a refutation (peaked-family power is low).

## PREREG line 'DAYRULES G1b' (registered 2026-10-18, before scoring): combined day cap at 4R and 5R, each with the give-back ON (arm 2R / keep 33% = give back 67%, as live) and OFF = 4 cells: `G1 cap=4 gb`, `G1 cap=4 nogb`, `G1 cap=5 gb`, `G1 cap=5 nogb`. Same engine, base (TH-off live config, asserted 1.46/2.83/2.48/1.90), conventions, metrics, qualification (list A / list B), plateau (adjacent G1 points: cap 6R and the other new cap, same give-back setting), matched random-halt null (200 replicates at the cell's firing rate), block bootstrap and verdict rule as REPORT_DAYRULES.md; appended there as section G1b. Interaction to state: with arm 2R the give-back lock arms 2-3R below a 4-5R cap, so the cap is reached only on days that rise through the arm level without giving back 67%.

## BLOCK 'SMOOTH' (owner ap_sm, registered 2026-10-18, BEFORE any SMOOTH cell was scored; only the live-baseline assertion 1.46/2.83/2.48/1.90 had been run)
- Engine/base: clean process, ap_dr_core -> b11_nt8 first, ROWS_corrected_engine = TH-OFF rows (live chart has ST TH ON, HM TH OFF; no TH-on pair rows exist; deltas are vs the TH-off live cell). Live config asserted 1.46/2.83/2.48/1.90 (clean/H1/H2/all-in). Dollars at 8/8; 6/6 = 0.75x linear (no lot rounding). R = $9,600 per book (combined limits are compared with A x $9,600, as the live locks).
- Account-layer order (unchanged): pass 1 per-book rules on realised closed P&L [per-book daily target T, per-book daily floor F (own value per book), per-book week loss rule (realised book week P&L <= -6R blocks the book until Monday), max trades per book per session (label day of the entry; further entries skipped)] -> combined DAY lock [cap, give-back arm A with give-back g% (keep = 1-g), combined day loss floor, give-back active from lock start] -> combined WEEK stage [week floor, week give-back (arm 5R keep 50% / arm 6R keep 67%)]. Locks act on 1-minute mark-to-market equity on the union grid, open rows closed at the next 1-minute open, later entries removed (the existing lock_stage semantics). Lock start 09:30 ET gates the give-back arm only (the engine's gb_start semantics: active 09:30-18:00); cap/floors act from 00:00 in both settings. Order of effects = ap_dr_core / b11_nt8; the week give-back is variant (a) (halt until Monday), identical to lock_stage(WKO, arm, keep, floor).
- Grid (exactly the directive): per-book daily target ST x HM {off,3,4,5,6,8}R; per-book daily floor ST x HM {off,1,1.5,2,3}R; combined cap {off,4,5,6,8,10,12}R; give-back arm {off,1,1.5,2,3,4}R x give-back {33,50,67,80}% (give-back irrelevant when arm off); lock start {00:00, 09:30}; combined day loss floor {off,2,3,4}R; combined week floor {off,6,8,10}R; per-book week rule {off,6}R; week give-back {off, 5R/keep50%, 6R/keep67%}; max trades per book per session {off,6,10}. 13 axes. Live cell = ST/HM target 5/5, floor 2/2, cap 8, arm 2, give-back 67%, start 00:00, day floor off, week floor 8, per-book week off, week GB off, max trades off. Plain = no per-book rules, no day lock, week floor 8.
- Search: 6,000-cell seeded Latin hypercube (numpy default_rng seed 20261018; every axis level index = random permutation of floor(arange(N)*L/N), so every level appears >= floor(N/L) times), cells normalised and de-duplicated (arm off => give-back ignored); then ONE refinement round: +-1 step on every axis (ordered lists as printed with 'off' at the loose end of target/floor/cap/arm/day-floor/week-floor/max-trades and the first slot of the week-GB list) around the 50 best cells on weekly Sharpe and the 50 best on CVaR5 (all-in span); references: live, plain, every DAYRULES cell (G1,G2,G3,G4,G5 series from dr_scores.pkl) and every WEEKGB cell (wg_scores.pkl) enter the library as stored daily series (their parameters outside the grid are not neighbours), plus the live and plain cells re-scored by the new evaluator.
- Objectives (all-in span 2023-08-01..2026-10-16, weekly P&L of ISO weeks of the label day): PRIMARY weekly Sharpe (mean/SD x sqrt52); SECONDARY CVaR5 (mean of worst 5% of weeks), % negative weeks, max DD (daily equity), worst week, longest DD (weeks under water, weekly equity). Income = all-in net. Every Sharpe figure is printed with income (net, % of live) and CVaR5 beside it. Pareto: for income levels 100/95/90/80/70% of live all-in net, among cells with net >= level x live net: the cell with the best weekly Sharpe and the cell with the best CVaR5, each with all secondaries, in-sample and from the walk-forward procedure side by side.
- Validity (a) walk-forward on the CELL CHOICE: folds = 13-week test blocks from the first ISO week of 2024-02 to 2026-10-16 (11 folds, last partial); for each fold the cell is chosen on the trailing 52 weeks (weeks of the 52 before the fold, data from 2023-02-10) from the whole library (the search is not re-run per fold: the library is the fixed 6,000+refinement set; this slightly flatters OOS because refinement picks used all-in data, stated), objective = weekly Sharpe or CVaR5, unconstrained and with a trailing-52-week income constraint (net >= level x live trailing net for the 5 levels); the chosen cell's weekly P&L over the next 13 weeks is stitched; reported: OOS Sharpe, CVaR5, net, max DD, % neg weeks, worst week, vs live over the same weeks; and the in-sample best beside it. Walk-forward gain > 0 for a cell = the procedure at that cell's income band (the highest level L <= cell net/live net, or 70% if lower) has OOS Sharpe above live's OOS Sharpe.
  (b) matched random-halt null, 200 replicates, per shortlisted cell: base = PLAIN (no per-book, no day lock, week floor 8, i.e. the DAYRULES null base) with random halts at the cell's firing rates: day-level rate = share of trading days on which any per-book rule, trade cap or combined day lock fired in the cell, week-level rate = share of weeks in which the week stage fired; a random halt = lock at a uniformly random grid minute of the group with that probability. Null rank = share of replicates whose weekly Sharpe (resp. CVaR5 for the CVaR list) is below the cell's. Plus a BEST-OF-N null: a null world = N random-halt cells (N = number of cells in the library) with (day rate, week rate) drawn from the empirical rates of the library cells, statistic = the best Sharpe (resp. best CVaR5) among the N; 200 null worlds (or fewer if time-bound, stated); the chosen cell's rank = share of null worlds whose best is below the chosen cell's value. Needs >= 0.95 on both nulls.
  (c) plateau: the +-1-step neighbours of the cell on every axis (up to 26); gain = cell objective - live objective (all-in); a neighbour 'keeps' if its gain >= 75% of the cell's gain; plateau met if >= 60% of neighbours keep (gain must be > 0).
  (d) per-span sign consistency: objective improvement vs live in clean, H1, H2 (spans as DAYRULES); strict = positive in all three.
- Evaluator: numba re-implementation of pass1 + lock_stage (+ week stage) on the stored trade rows; validated against the python engine (ap_dr_core.run_cell / b11_nt8.lock_stage chain, extended in plain python for the new axes) on the live cell, plain, 20 random cells; max daily difference reported (must be $0.00 to proceed). No re-simulation of entries.
- Recommended cell: among cells passing ALL of walk-forward gain > 0, both nulls >= 0.95, plateau >= 60%, strict sign consistency on the objective, AND an all-in weekly Sharpe gain >= +0.10 over live: the one with the highest all-in net (smallest income cost); also list the highest-Sharpe robust cell. If none passes, state 'no robust cell' plainly. Weekly Sharpe gains bought by shaving winners are flagged by showing net and CVaR5 beside them. Shortlist for the nulls/plateau = top 10 on Sharpe + top 10 on CVaR5 + the best cell at each Pareto level (all in-sample), plus the recommended candidates.
- Chance expectation stated in the report: of ~6,500 correlated cells, the best in-sample Sharpe is selection-biased; the best-of-N null and the walk-forward are the two controls. No live change, no UNTOUCHED, no key use, DualStrat/Rithmic config never read (field semantics only).

## BLOCK 'DDMIN' (registered 2026-10-18, before any B/C table cell was scored): lever B (Trade Health) and lever C (bracket geometry) trade-set library
- Output: per-book PRE-NETTING event streams (one row per exit leg; columns entry_id, bar_in, side, entry_px, leg, qfrac, bar_out, ks_out, rs, exit_px) in outputs/autopilot/ddmin/tables/<BOOK>_<cfgid>.parquet with manifest.csv, for BOOK in ST, HM, WT (WT = the WT-10 live cell); the search framework (a33825) recombines them through the lot-netting account layer. Entries: the live signals of each book (nt8sim own_signals / WT-10 flips) filtered by BLOCKED, market at the next 1-minute open, per-book busy gate; exits resolved on the 1-second rows (stop first, gap fill = worse of stop and previous second close, target at the level, session flat at the block-bar close).
- Geometry grid C per book (TH-off): stop S in {45, 60, 75} points x target T in {0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0} R (R = S) x partial in {none, half at +1R then trail} x time stop in {none, 60, 120} minutes. The partial is only distinct from 'none' for T >= 1.5R (for T <= 1R the half-at-1R coincides with the target), so the TH-off grid has 3 x (4 + 2) x 3 = 54 distinct configs per book; ALL are run (fewer than the 400-config sample size). Partial: half the position exits at +1R, the other half trails 1R behind the best price after the partial (initial trail stop at entry) and keeps the target T. Time stop: exit at the open of the first 1-minute bar whose start is >= N minutes after entry.
- Lever B (TH) grid per book: TH off/on x MinPoints {10, 15, 20} x Required {2, 3} = 1 + 6 = 7 states; crossed with the 54 geometries = 378 configs per book (<= 400: ALL run, no sampling needed); the 400-per-book stratified sample of the directive is therefore the full enumeration where TH is available. TH-on states are PROVISIONAL (the DualStrat.cs ManageLoss source is not on disk): the rule is reconstructed from the live log (STStrat/HMStrat/WTStrat LossExit patterns) and every TH row is labelled PROVISIONAL below a 90% exit-type match (exit within 2 minutes) against the live log.
- The live cfg per book (TH off, S60, T1.0R, no partial, no time stop) must reproduce the per-book exits of acct_se/natural_se exactly (asserted) and, through the lot-netting layer, ROWS_corrected_engine (a33825 reports the daily difference).
- No cell is selected here; selection, nulls and walk-forward are in the search (REPORT_DDMIN).

## BLOCK 'DDMIN' (owner ap_dd / a33825, registered 2026-10-18, BEFORE any DDMIN search cell was scored; only evaluator validation (live-cell reproduction, netting at 8/8 vs ROWS_corrected_engine) had been run)
- Question: which joint setting of levers A (book allocation incl. WT-10 as third book), B (Trade Health), C (bracket geometry), D (SMOOTH risk-rule grid), E (sleeves in own accounts), F (sizing) minimises the live pair's drawdown at a given income, judged in-sample AND walk-forward. Assume live is not optimal; the search must be able to find a planted improvement (below).
- Basis. Engine: stored NT8-faithful pre-netting event streams (nt8sim.acct order semantics, 1-second exit resolution) re-netted with VARIABLE contracts by a numba lot-netting replay (opposite entry closes oldest opposite lots FIFO up to its quantity, residual opens a same-side lot; bracket exit closes oldest same-side lots up to its quantity, residual opens an orphan opposite lot (ORPH_NET off, as ROWS_corrected_engine); session flat closes all). Slippage 1 tick/side, $34.88 per 8 lots (linear in contracts). Fractional contracts allowed in the evaluator (half legs); integer rounding reported for the recommended cell. Skipped rows stay in the netting history (convention of all pair-conditioning families). Gate: reproduction of ROWS_corrected_engine at 8/8 and of the single-book engine runs, max daily difference reported (target $0.00).
- Units. R per book = $1,200 x contracts of that book (= $9,600 at 8); per-book rules (daily target/floor, per-book week, trade cap) scale with the book's contracts; WT-10 rules fixed 5R/-2R of its own contracts. Combined-lock R = $1,200 x (ST+HM contracts)/2 ($9,600 at 8/8; WT-10 contracts do NOT scale the combined lock). All other D axes as SMOOTH. Live = ST 8 / HM 8, D = live cell, B/C = live (TH off, 60/60 1.0R, no partial, no time stop), no sleeves. 6/6 = linear 0.75x of every pair-side and sleeve dollar (SMOOTH convention), also reported with contracts 6/6 directly.
- Scoring window. All objectives on the weekly path of the 142 ISO weeks 2024-02-01..2026-10-16 (the METHOD-2 OOS window; pair 6/6 DD52 $183.7k, 8/8 $245k). Objectives: (1) DD52 = max over rolling 52-week windows of the weekly-path drawdown; (2) P(DD>$200k) from the 52-week block bootstrap (4-week blocks, 20,000 draws, common random numbers across cells, seed fixed); (3) CVaR5 of weekly P&L (mean of worst ceil(5%) weeks); (4) worst week; (5) longest drawdown (weeks under water on the weekly path). Each at income floors 100/90/80/70% of live net over the same weeks (and the 6/6 equivalent). Composite headline: lowest DD52 at net >= 90% of live. Income, CVaR5 and DD are shown beside every figure.
- Levers and grids. A: ST contracts 0..16 x HM contracts 0..16 (incl. ST-only, HM-only), WT-10 contracts 0..8 (0 = absent). B: Trade Health per book from a3a74's tables (TH off/on per book x MinPoints {10,15,20} x Required {2,3}); TH rows PROVISIONAL (DualStrat.cs absent; reconstruction from the live log). C: stop {45,60,75} x target {0.75,1,1.5,2}R x partial {none, half at 1R then trail} x time stop {none,60,120} per book (a3a74 stratified tables; B and C are drawn ONLY from available tables, each cell draws one table per book). D: the 13 SMOOTH axes exactly as in SMOOTH (ap_sm_grid.AXES). E: 9 sleeves (K4 hedged, K4 unhedged, WT432, WT-10 separate account, ES dip-buy C11DIP, CL 240, B7XC (52 cells ex-crypto), BM13, BTC WT) each at risk {0,10,20,30}% of the pair-6/6 weekly SD (METHOD-2 12/3/3-tilt OOS series, outputs/methods2/oos), caps: <=10 NQ-eq contracts per sleeve (PF packs), <=30% risk share per sleeve, <=3 active sleeves per cell and total sleeve risk <=60% of pair risk. Sleeve edge scenarios 100% / 50% / 0% (series demeaned toward 0 by (1-lambda)): SEARCH AND RANKING USE THE 50% SCENARIO for any cell with a sleeve (sleeve series are NON-VIRGIN: selected on this window, optimistic); 100% and 0% reported for all shortlisted cells. F: each shortlisted cell at its own size and scaled linearly to the pair-6/6 DD52 ($183.7k) and a vol-targeted variant (weekly risk budget held constant: scale = target SD / trailing-26-week SD of the cell, lagged one week, clipped 0.5..2, target = live SD at the same size); F is a post-hoc transformation, not an LHS axis (A already spans size). Planted rule axis P (test only, see below) is NOT in the real search.
- Search. 30,000-cell Latin hypercube over A x B x C x D x E (B,C level = index into the available table list per book; WT table only if WT contracts>0), then local refinement (+-1 step on every axis; table index neighbours = tables differing in one geometry/TH field) around the 150 best on EACH of the 5 objectives (at the 90% floor for objectives 2-5, plus composite), plus references: live, plain, every DAYRULES (214), WEEKGB (42), SMOOTH cell, the PORTFOLIO sleeve cells (pair + one sleeve at 10/20/30%). Placeholder B,C = live until a3a74's tables land; the full search is re-run when they do.
- Validity on every shortlisted cell (shortlist = top 20 on the composite + the best cell per (objective, floor) + recommended candidates): (a) walk-forward on the CELL CHOICE: best cell per objective and floor on the trailing 52 weeks, applied to the next 13, rolled over 2024-02..2026-10 (WF-A: library cells without sleeves, 142 weeks, 11 folds); with sleeves in the library the first 52 trailing weeks lack sleeve data, so WF-B (all cells) covers 2025-02..2026-10 (90 weeks, 7 folds); OOS DD, income, Sharpe, CVaR5 reported beside the in-sample best and beside live over the same weeks; the library is fixed (not re-searched per fold). (b) matched null, 200 replicates: random-halt null for the D part (PLAIN D at the cell's own A,B,C, random halts at the cell's day-level and week-level firing rates) and random-weight null for E (each sleeve series circularly shifted by a random offset, same risk levels); statistic = parity-scaled objective (objective x live_net/net, i.e. the objective at live income by linear size scaling); rank = share of replicates with a worse (higher DD / lower CVaR) statistic. (c) best-of-N null over the whole search: a null world = N random cells (A,B,C drawn from the library, PLAIN D with random halts at rates drawn from the library cells' empirical rates, sleeves shifted) and the statistic = the best parity-scaled DD52 among cells with net >= floor; 200 worlds or fewer if time-bound (stated); rank of the in-sample best among worlds. (d) plateau: >= 60% of the +-1-step neighbours (up to the neighbours existing in the tables) keep >= 75% of the cell's DD gain over live (gain at income parity; gain must be > 0). (e) per-span sign consistency: DD52-parity improvement over live in the clean, H1, H2 sub-spans of the weekly window (spans as DAYRULES mapped to weeks); strict = positive in all three. (f) 52-week block bootstrap 20,000 draws, common random numbers (the same index matrix for every cell and for live), reporting P(DD>150k/200k/280k), median and p95 DD.
- Recommended cell: passes ALL of walk-forward gain > 0 (WF of the objective at the cell's floor vs live over the same OOS weeks), both nulls >= 0.95, plateau met, strict signs, and gain at the stated income floor; the recommended cell is the lowest-DD such cell at >= 90% of live net. If none, state 'no robust cell' plainly. DD gains bought by shaving winners are flagged by showing net/CVaR5 beside them.
- PLANTED-IMPROVEMENT TEST (recall of the search): a synthetic rule P is injected into the evaluator as an extra D-axis {off, P25, P50}: in the weeks whose weekly P&L is in the worst 10% of the cell's own weekly distribution, P25/P50 remove 25%/50% of the loss; in all other weeks it shaves 3% of the positive weeks' P&L (known income cost). The standard pipeline (LHS + refinement + WF + nulls + best-of-N) is run on a pair-side library with the planted axis, 20 independent seeds with 3,000-cell LHS each. Recall = share of seeds in which the best composite cell carries P50 (or P25) AND passes the walk-forward and null gates; the stated acceptance level is recall >= 0.8 for P50 and the planted cell must clear best-of-N >= 0.95; also reported: recall for a planted improvement of half the size (P25). If recall is below the stated level the search power, not the nature, is the finding and the verdict language says 'no robust cell found at this power'.
- Chance expectation: with ~30,000 correlated cells and a 3.7-year (pair) / 142-week (sleeve) window the best in-sample DD is selection-biased (SMOOTH: in-sample best Sharpe ranked 0.28 against best-of-N); the best-of-N null and the walk-forward are the two controls. No live change, no UNTOUCHED, no key use, DualStrat/Rithmic config never read (field semantics only; DualStrat.cs absent => lever B PROVISIONAL).
- DDMIN AMENDMENTS (logged 2026-10-18/19, before the final report, none after seeing a recommended cell): (1) walk-forward gain is operationalised as the parity-scaled objective (objective at live income by linear size scaling) of the cell-choice procedure minus live over the same OOS weeks; OOS income share is shown beside it but is not a separate gate. (2) Lever B uses a3a74's TICK-level Trade Health tables (ST/HM, 192 cells + tk_off control; validated 95.9% type+time) instead of the 1-second TH tables, which were in the first full run and then superseded; B,C tables are a3a74's 54-point TH-off geometry sample per book. (3) Best-of-N null: 20 search-replicating worlds (not 200; time-bound), built from the preceding library version (1-second TH rows) because regenerating them after the tick-table swap would have taken ~2 hours; the matched null uses 200 replicates per cell. (4) Planted test: 20 seeds per size, planted acceptance recall >= 0.8 on all gates at f = 50% was NOT met (0.75) because the walk-forward gate is noisy; the in-sample search found the planted rule in 20/20 seeds at both sizes. (5) Base = the SMOOTH live cell (user-confirmed equal to the live chart).
